  The natural inference from this additive structure is that the role prompt can be compressed into a single cached residual vector. \emph{We show it cannot.} Injecting the cached additive prediction -- or even the oracle clean residual $h_{XY}$ -- into a baseline host prompt with the persona text removed does not approach the clean long-persona target, at one site or at many layers. Persona-conditioned multi-token generation flows through attention back to the persona-text positions throughout the prompt, which no residual at one site reproduces.
  Local additivity in the residual stream does not imply prompt compressibility. The additive structure at the prompt-to-answer transition supports interpretability and fine-grained steering of persona or task contributions; persona-conditioned behavior across the full continuation depends on a distributed prompt/KV mechanism that local activation arithmetic does not displace.

</details>


### [29] [Same Model, Different Weakness: How Language and Modality Reshape the Jailbreak Attack Surface in Frontier MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23157)
*Casey Ford,Madison Van Doren,Sicheng Jin,Emily Dix*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: First systematic cross-lingual multimodal red-teaming study reveals that language-dependent vulnerability patterns dissociate linguistic from visual alignment failures, with role-play attacks less effective in Spanish but multimodal attacks more effective. Safety rankings invert across languages, demonstrating that current evaluation frameworks fundamentally misspecify the attack surface of globally deployed MLLMs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The attack surface of multimodal large language models exhibits language-dependent vulnerabilities that may reveal the mechanistic structure of alignment failures, but systematic cross-lingual and multimodal safety evaluation remains underexplored.

Method: Bayesian mixed-effects analyses of 52,272 harm ratings and binary attack success judgments from matched panels of nine native-speaker annotators per language group, evaluating 363 diverse adversarial prompt scenarios across text-only and multimodal conditions.

Result: Language does not scale vulnerability uniformly: linguistic framing attacks (e.g., role-play) are substantially less effective under Spanish prompting, while visually explicit multimodal attacks become more effective. Safety rankings are not preserved across languages, and absolute attack success rates have declined across model generations without closing gaps between them.

Conclusion: Safety evaluation frameworks treating language and modality as independent dimensions fundamentally misspecify the attack surface of globally deployed MLLMs, necessitating redesign. Language switching alone can expose dissociations between linguistic and visual alignment failure mechanisms, and safety rankings are not preserved across languages—Qwen Omni overtakes Pixtral Large as most vulnerable among es-MX participants.

Abstract: The attack surface of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) is language-dependent in ways that reveal the mechanistic structure of alignment failures. We present the first systematic cross-lingual, multimodal red-teaming study comparing jailbreak vulnerability in US English (en-US) and Mexican Spanish (es-MX) across four frontier MLLMs: Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5, Pixtral Large, and Qwen Omni. Using a fixed adversarial benchmark of 363 diverse prompt scenarios administered in text-only and multimodal conditions, we collected 52,272 harm ratings and binary attack success judgements from matched panels of nine native-speaker annotators per language group. Our central finding is that language does not scale vulnerability uniformly. Bayesian mixed-effects analyses reveal that linguistic framing attacks such as role-play become substantially less effective under Spanish prompting, while visually explicit multimodal attacks become more effective, which directly implicates the prompt-language interface rather than global annotator leniency. This dissociation indicates that linguistic and visual alignment failures operate through distinct mechanisms, and that switching language is sufficient to expose that separation. The practical consequence is that safety rankings are not preserved across languages. Qwen Omni overtakes Pixtral Large as the most vulnerable model among es-MX participants, a rank reversal no scalar correction of English-condition scores could recover, and absolute attack success rates have declined across model generations without closing the gaps between them. These findings demonstrate that safety evaluation frameworks treating language and modality as independent dimensions fundamentally misspecify the attack surface of globally deployed MLLMs, and must be redesigned accordingly.

</details>


### [30] [Fast-dDrive: Efficient Block-Diffusion VLM for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23163)
*Kewei Zhang,Jin Wang,Sensen Gao,Chengyue Wu,Yulong Cao,Songyang Han,Boris Ivanovic,Langechuan Liu,Marco Pavone,Song Han,Daquan Zhou,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Fast-dDrive 是一种面向端到端自动驾驶的块扩散（block-diffusion）视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型：在语义段落内部做双向去噪、在段落之间保持严格因果顺序；并把固定 JSON schema 的结构性 token 冻结为「脚手架（scaffold）」，只对数值 token 做扩散，同时引入 Scaffold Speculative Decoding（自投机验证）与共享前缀多轨迹 rollout 的测试时扩展。基于 Qwen2.5-VL-3B，在 WOD-E2E 上取得扩散类 VLA 中最佳的 ADE@3s/ADE@5s 与最高 RFS，nuScenes 平均 L2 降至 0.32 m（提升 22%），配合 SGLang 相对 AR 基线实现 11.8×（约 12×）端到端吞吐加速，单卡 H100 上达 210–608 TPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶的 VLA 模型必须同时满足两点：轨迹精度高且与推理过程一致；推理足够高效以便在车端以 batch=1 实时运行。但现有范式难以兼得：(1) 自回归（AR）VLA 逐 token 解码，在 batch=1 时受显存带宽限制（每步只提交 1 token，GPU 并行算力闲置），难以车端部署；同时存在曝光偏差（exposure bias），5 s 规划中早期坐标的小误差会被逐级放大成物理上不可行的机动。(2) 全序列扩散（如 dVLM-AD 的掩码扩散 MDM）通过双向注意力迭代去噪提供全局上下文，但两个结构性代价：全序列双向注意力禁止 KV-cache 复用、每个去噪步需全量重算，延迟远高于 AR；把整段响应当作单一双向单元忽略了其内在因果结构（感知→解释→元行为决策→轨迹），产生「逻辑泄漏（logical leakage）」——已规划的轨迹可反向影响模型陈述的感知，违反「先感知后规划」的基本因果性。此外，AR 的自回归分解与扩散范式都无法自然利用驾驶 VLA 输出固有的 JSON 结构以进一步降低开销。

Method: 整体构建于 Fast-dVLM 的块因果掩码扩散架构（backbone 为 Qwen2.5-VL-3B），核心技术有三层：

(1) Section-Aware Structured Diffusion (SASD) 训练方案。观察到驾驶 VLA 输出为 schema 决定的结构化 JSON（四个语义段：critical_objects 12 项二值检测、explanation 自由推理、future_meta_behavior 类别行为、trajectory 5 个 5 s 路点），因此把 keys/括号/标点等确定性 token 预填为冻结脚手架 x̂_T，仅对数值 token 集合 E 做扩散（总解码 token 中约 30% 为脚手架）。块边界与段边界对齐（每段按 d 划分块，段序 CO→Expl→FMB→Traj 严格因果，段内双向；变长段用 NULL 补齐后剥离），保证 100% 结构正确性。训练时引入两项零推理开销的机制：段加权交叉熵（权重 trajectory 3.0 / future_meta_behavior 2.0 / critical_objects 1.5 / explanation 1.0）与段自适应 Beta 噪声调度（(α,β) 分别为 (2,1)/(1,2)/(1,1.5)/(1,1)）；并与 token 级因果 LM 损失联合训练（L = 0.5·L_diff + 0.5·L_AR），使同一权重同时具备扩散头与 AR 头。

(2) 两种推理模式。Section Diffusion（SD）：沿用训练时的按段迭代解掩，利用脚手架与已解码段的 KV cache；Scaffold Speculative Decoding (SS)：每块先零前向自动接受脚手架 token，MDM 头一次前向并行起草全部数值 token，AR 头因果前向逐 token 验证，命中则接受、首个不匹配处以 AR token 替换并丢弃后续草稿，额外接受 1 个 bonus token。每块恰好 2 次前向，且段对齐块提供完整语义上下文从而提升草稿接受率，输出与纯 AR 等价。

(3) 共享前缀的测试时推理扩展。前三段（感知/解释/元行为）后验尖锐，保持 AR 验证器贪心、确定性解码一次并复用其 KV cache；仅在 trajectory 段启用带温度的采样，从同一 KV cache fork 出 N 条随机 rollout，各自用 Jerk-Minimizing Trajectory (JMT) 拟合插值到 20 个路点后等权平均输出轨迹，利用均值方差递减（残差方差降为 1/N，确定性偏差不变）抑制预测方差；因 trajectory 段很短，每次额外 rollout 仅增加分数级的计算开销。训练细节：8×H100，WOD-E2E 微调 3 epoch（30k CoT 标注样本 + 60k 仅轨迹样本），nuScenes 单独用 23k 样本。

Result: WOD-E2E 测试集：Scaffold Spec 取得全表最低 ADE@3s 1.254 与 ADE@5s 2.907，RFS 7.823（超过扩散基线 dVLM-AD 的 7.633，与最强 AR 基线 Poutine-Base 7.909 相当，且未用 GRPO 或更大轨迹池）；加 N=4 推理扩展后 ADE 降至 1.240/2.821（耗时约为单次 SS 的 2 倍以下）。吞吐 210.4 TPS、Tok/Step 4.90，约为 dVLM-AD（35.2 TPS）与 AR 基线（51.2 TPS）的 4–6 倍。nuScenes val：1s/2s/3s L2 为 0.12/0.33/0.50，平均 0.32 m（较 dVLM-AD 的 0.41 提升约 22%），优于 UniAD/VAD/BEV-Planner 等专用策略。

效率消融（WOD-E2E val，单 H100，batch=1）：AR 基线 7855 ms / 51.6 TPS / Tok-Step 1，ADE 0.839/2.083；dVLM-AD 9575 ms、0.8×；Self-Spec 3714 ms（2.1×）；Section Diffusion 3006 ms（2.6×，但 ADE 略差）；+Scaffold Spec 1919 ms（4.1×）；+SGLang 665 ms（11.8×，608.5 TPS），说明算法收益与系统级优化（kernel、CUDA graph）可叠加。SASD 消融：IWL 为主因（ADE5s 2.028→2.003，RFS 7.735→7.855），SNS 单独小幅改善（2.050/7.807），两者结合最佳（2.034/7.916）。测试时扩展中 ADE@5s 随 N 单调下降，默认取 N=4。

Conclusion: Fast-dDrive 表明，当模型输出具有已知结构时，把该结构编码进扩散过程可同时提升质量与速度，二者并非冲突目标：冻结 schema 脚手架 + 段对齐块扩散 + 安全关键 token 优先训练，使其以全序列扩散 6 倍的吞吐达到 SOTA 轨迹精度；共享前缀多轨迹 rollout 展示了块扩散天然具备而 AR 模型缺失的低成本测试时扩展维度。论文给出的更广泛原则是「结构化生成与高效解码互为补充」。局限性：依赖固定 JSON schema，任务定义变化需人工调整模板；推理扩展仍有额外计算，在极低延迟车端环境可能受限；评估仅限开环基准，需闭环仿真验证动态环境中的反应能力。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving via Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demands a precarious balance between high-fidelity trajectory planning and efficient inference. Existing paradigms typically fall short: autoregressive (AR) VLAs are memory-bandwidth-bound on edge hardware and prone to exposure-bias drift, while full-sequence diffusion models preclude KV-cache reuse and suffer from "logical leakage" that violates the fundamental perceive-then-plan causality. We present Fast-dDrive, a block-diffusion VLA that performs bidirectional refinement within semantic units while enforcing strict causal ordering across them. Leveraging the observation that driving VLAs often emit structured JSON-like outputs, Fast-dDrive freezes structural tokens into a section scaffold and employs a section-aware training recipe that prioritizes safety-critical planning. We further introduce Scaffold Speculative Decoding to achieve AR-equivalent quality at significantly higher throughput. Finally, we propose a low-overhead test-time scaling scheme: by forking $N$ stochastic trajectory rollouts from a single shared-prefix KV cache and averaging them, we effectively suppress prediction variance at a fractional computational cost. Empirical results demonstrate that Fast-dDrive redefines the speed-accuracy frontier for driving agents. On the WOD-E2E test set, Fast-dDrive achieves SOTA ADE@3s and ADE@5s, alongside the highest RFS among diffusion-based VLAs; on nuScenes, it reduces average L2 error to $0.32$m (a $22\%$ improvement). When integrated with SGLang, our framework delivers $12\times$ throughput speedup over the AR baseline, narrowing the gap between high-capacity VLAs and the efficiency demands of real-time on-vehicle deployment.

</details>


### [31] [Positional Failures in Long-Context LLMs: A Blind Spot in Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23170)
*Chuyifei Zhang,Hongyu Cui,Xiaowen Huang,Jitao Sang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现行长上下文推理基准（NIAH/RULER 之外的 SCROLLS、LongBench、L-Eval 等）普遍不控制目标任务在上下文中的位置，四个厂商旗舰长上下文发布（DeepSeek-V4-Pro、MiMo-V2.5-Pro、Kimi-K2.6、GLM-5.1）的主结果表中也完全缺失位置受控的长上下文基准条目。为此作者提出 CRE（Context Rot Evaluation）：以「任务位置 × 填充内容 × 上下文长度」3×3×3 全因子设计，在 GSM8K 与 ARC-Challenge 上评测 9 个 LLM。结果显示位置敏感度高度依赖模型：MiMo-v2-Flash 在 64K、with_solutions 填充下端位准确率 96%、中位仅 8%（-88pp），且跌幅随长度扩大；新版模型在 ws 下降幅收窄（MiMo-V2.5-Pro -32pp），但 questions_only_v2 填充下四个新模型跌幅依旧普遍（-16pp 至 -56pp）；8K 下「尾部重复副本」诊断探针使中位准确率回到端位 ±4pp 以内，76% 的中位错误答案直接复制填充文本中的答案，证实「填充答案干扰」是主要失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 普遍宣称支持 128K 以上窗口，但现有评测方式无法验证其「位置鲁棒性」。检索类基准（NIAH、RULER）已将任务位置作为一等受控变量，而主流长文推理基准（SCROLLS、LongBench、L-Eval、LooGLE、100-LongBench、ZeroSCROLLS）依赖自然文档，任务信息位置由数据源继承而非实验设定，导致「因位置导致的失败」被聚合平均掩盖。此外，厂商自身披露也存在同样缺口：审计 4 个旗舰发布 × 7 个长上下文基准 = 28 个单元格中，0 个出现在主结果表（NIAH/RULER 仅出现在 9B 代理模型消融中），而 agent/编码类基准 28 格中有 20 格进入主表。因此需要一个联合控制位置、填充内容与上下文长度的推理评测框架。

Method: 1）基准审计：将 11 个长上下文基准按位置控制程度分为三层——Tier 1（位置为一等变量：NIAH、RULER）、Tier 2（位置可配置但次要：BABILong、LongReason、InfiniteBench 混合）、Tier 3（位置完全不受控：LongBench、SCROLLS、ZeroSCROLLS、L-Eval、LooGLE、100-LongBench）；并对 4 个厂商发布做 19 个基准的披露矩阵审计（M/A/— 三档）。2）CRE 框架：把目标问题嵌入指定位置的填充内容中，3×3×3 全因子（填充类型 with_solutions / questions_only_v2 / neutral_text × 长度 8K/32K/64K × 位置 end/middle），每格 N=50、seed=42，drop = 中位准确率 − 端位准确率；诊断条件 middle_twice（中部重复两次）与 middle_dup（中部 + 尾部各一份，仅 8K 主测、64K 部分补测）。3）任务与模型：GSM8K、ARC-Challenge；初始 5 模型（Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct、MiMo-v2-Flash、GLM-4.7-FlashX、DeepSeek-V3.2 推理模式、Kimi k2.5）+ 4 个新一代厂商模型（DeepSeek-V4-Pro、MiMo-V2.5-Pro、Kimi-K2.6、GLM-5.1），覆盖 5 家 API 栈。4）诊断工具：填充答案匹配打分器（错误答案是否命中填充题目的标准答案）、尾部副本探针；辅以 Fisher 精确检验 + Bonferroni 校正（27/9 次比较，α=0.01）、max_gen=4096 重跑、CoT 复述率、seed42/seed100 稳定性等鲁棒性检查。

Result: GSM8K + with_solutions 填充下：Qwen 2.5-7B 端位 90/86/94%，中位跌幅 -86/-84/-94pp（最严重）；MiMo-v2-Flash -12/-24/-88pp（64K 中位仅 8%）；GLM-4.7-FlashX -12/-20/-34pp；DeepSeek-V3.2 全档 0pp；Kimi k2.5 0～-6pp。27 项比较中 6 项 Bonferroni 显著。填充内容效应高度异质：questions_only_v2 在 32K/64K 使全部 5 个初始模型下跌（-22～-94pp），连 ws 下免疫的 DeepSeek-V3.2 也跌 -22pp；neutral_text 在 8K 近零，但 Qwen 仍 -29pp(32K)/-30pp(64K) 且双 seed 显著，MiMo-v2-Flash 在 64K neutral 下反而 +2pp。错误归因：76% 的中位错误命中填充题答案，而端位仅 22%。诊断探针：8K 下 middle_dup 使 9 个模型全部回到端位 ±4pp 内（Qwen 4%→94%），middle_twice 改善有限（Qwen 4%→4%，MiMo 22%→70%，Kimi 40%→92%）；64K 下仅 2/4 达端位水平，说明探针本身受长度制约。ARC-Challenge 8K：9 模型全部呈方向性下跌（-6～-40pp），仅 Qwen 显著（p<0.0001）；另发现 qo_v2 填充在端位即造成格式混淆（Qwen -44pp、MiMo -46pp、FlashX -28pp），属任务格式特异现象。第 2 轮新模型：64K ws 下 GLM-5.1 +2pp、DeepSeek-V4-Pro -4pp、Kimi-K2.6 +2pp、MiMo-V2.5-Pro -32pp；但 qo_v2 下四模型 12 格全部为负（-16～-56pp，DeepSeek-V4-Pro 呈非单调）。

Conclusion: 当前长上下文评测存在结构性缺口：位置不受控的基准与厂商披露表都无法测量「随长度加剧的位置脆弱性」，这既是基准设计问题也是厂商评测实践问题。作者建议：(1) 除聚合分外报告分位置准确率；(2) 至少测试一个内部（非边界）位置，避免仅做首尾二元消融；(3) 完整披露推理栈（提供方、端点、温度、最大生成长度、随机种子）；(4) 将零结果作为一等结论报告（如 DeepSeek-V3.2 在 ws 下的 0pp 掩盖了 qo_v2 下的脆弱性）；(5) 附带诊断探针条件以区分位置效应。论文明确声明探针仅为一致性检验而非机制隔离或实用修复，且承认 13 项局限：N=50 带来的 ±14pp 置信区间、多数 64K 仅单 seed、五家 API 栈异构导致跨模型绝对幅度不可比、ARC 显著性不足（仅 Qwen 显著）、Round 2 未采集 64K 探针数据、厂商升级并非在所有任务上一致收窄跌幅，以及新一代模型采用稀疏注意力架构这一未受控混淆。总体而言，长上下文推理失败具有模型依赖性且随上下文变长而加剧，CRE 提供了此前缺失的评测层；作者承诺公开 CRE 代码、诊断条件与 11 基准审计。

Abstract: Position-controlled evaluation is standard for retrieval tasks such as Needle-in-a-Haystack and RULER, but mainstream reasoning benchmarks do not control positional placement of target tasks in long contexts. We audit 11 long-context benchmarks and find none jointly controls task position, filler content, and context length for reasoning. An audit of four flagship long-context releases finds no main result-table entry for NIAH, RULER, or LongBench-family benchmarks, while agentic and coding benchmarks appear in main result-tables across all four. We propose Context Rot Evaluation (CRE), a controlled framework varying all three factors, and evaluate nine LLMs on GSM8K and ARC-Challenge across two rounds: an initial five-model set and four newer vendor releases. Models can drop sharply when the target task moves from end to middle, and the drop grows worse with context length for vulnerable models. MiMo-v2-Flash drops 88pp at 64K under with_solutions filler (middle accuracy 8%). Newer releases show smaller drops: at 64K, three of four stay within +/-6pp of end-position accuracy; MiMo-V2.5-Pro narrows the MiMo-v2-Flash 88pp drop to 32pp. Under questions_only_v2 filler, middle-position drops persist across all four (range -16pp to -56pp across 8K, 32K, 64K). At 8K, a diagnostic probe adding a target-task copy at the end brings middle accuracy within +/-4pp of end baseline across all nine models, consistent with a positional explanation. In the initial five-model set, 76% of middle-position errors match surrounding filler text versus 22% at the end position, consistent with filler-answer interference as a dominant error mode. These results expose a structural evaluation gap in current reasoning benchmark design and vendor evaluation practice: positional vulnerabilities that grow with context length cannot be measured when task position is not controlled.

</details>


### [32] [Self-Improving In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23180)
*Baturay Saglam,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种在测试时通过零阶（zeroth-order）优化固定 few-shot 提示的连续嵌入来改进上下文学习（ICL）的方法。核心思想是：模型在自身演示输出上给出的对数概率（单次前向传播即可获得，无需生成 token）可作为"模型是否已正确推断任务"的自监督信号，被形式化为有界置信度代理 f(X)，并对其做嵌入空间上升优化，从而无需微调、无需标签集、无需外部数据即可对分类与开放式生成任务统一校准提示表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）虽已成为部署 LLM 的主流范式，但极为脆弱：仅重排语义相同的演示即可使准确率从接近 SOTA 跌至接近随机；对示例选择与格式同样敏感。已有的测试时改进方法分三类——演示选择（需候选池/有限标签集）、演示排序（需有限标签集，且排列搜索随演示数组合爆炸）、输出校准（需已知标签集，部分还需测试批次的直推访问或隐藏状态）——均作用于离散的提示级决策或事后概率调整，结构性地局限于分类任务，无法推广到自由形式生成（ICLEval 中 74.5% 样本为生成任务）。作者因此转向一个互补方向：直接干预模型实际条件化的连续提示嵌入矩阵，在不修改人类可读输入的前提下重塑输出分布，从而获得一个任务无关、无需标签集、可插拔组合的测试时校准模块。

Method: 方法建模为在固定离散提示的嵌入空间上做测试时优化。核心是一个标量、有界、连续、自监督的 ICL 置信度代理 f(X)=α·C̄(X)+β·R(X)+γ·G(X)∈[0,1]（α=0.6, β=0.3, γ=0.1），三个分量均只依赖单次 teacher-forced 前向传播在演示输出跨段位置上的对数概率：①逐演示绝对置信度——对每个演示输出跨段做长度归一化平均对数概率后取指数得几何均值置信度 c_i，再跨演示平均得 C̄；②池化稳健性——把所有演示输出 token 的真实 token 概率汇集，取分位 q=0.1 作为 Value-at-Risk 型尾部风险度量 R，惩罚"均值高但个别 token 概率极低"的脆弱性；③跨演示信息增益——按演示顺序计算 c_i 相对 c_{i-1} 的正向增量并平均得 G（T=1 时置 0），刻画随上下文变长而逐步推断任务的过程。优化采用 Nesterov-Spokoiny 高斯平滑框架下的零阶有限差分梯度估计：N 次高斯扰动（每行独立、按 token 分别扰动）下的 (f(X+μU)−f(X))/μ·U 蒙特卡洛平均，按步长 η 做上升更新 X_{k+1}=X_k+η·ĝ_k；因因果注意力下查询 token 不影响演示位置的对数概率，扰动仅施加于演示区域（查询位置噪声置零）。为防止嵌入漂移出分布，引入三种正则：逐 token 梯度裁剪、与初始嵌入的余弦相似度投影约束（阈值 κ=0.2）、以及初始代理门控 τ=0.05（f(X_0) 低于阈值时直接返回原嵌入，因此时代理曲面指数平坦、梯度无方向信号）；连续 5 步无改进则早停，最终返回历次迭代中代理值最高的嵌入 X*，再据此生成查询输出。方法不修改离散提示、不需微调/标签集/外部数据/模型参数/隐藏状态，成本不随演示数增长，可与选择、排序、校准方法自由组合。

Result: 在 ICLEval（12 任务、2040 样本，覆盖精确复制与规则学习，事实内容被替换为哈希串以剥离预训练知识混淆）与 Llama 3.1-8B、Qwen3-4B、Gemma 2-2B 上评测（贪心解码、精确匹配）：所有任务上准确率与基线持平或提升，从不下降；平均相对提升 Llama +6.0%、Qwen +10.8%、Gemma +6.7%，单侧 McNemar 检验在 2040 样本上均显著（p=0.001 / 0.038 / <0.001）。规则学习类任务收益最明显（Format Check 最高 +122.2%，Relation Analysis 在 Gemma 上 0.03→0.12 即 +300%），而近饱和的复制类任务（String Completion、Dict. Search）准确率持平——代理提升不必然迁移到查询准确率。与基线比较（分类子集）：在 Llama 与 Qwen 上优于 CC、DC-PMI、DEmO；Gemma 上 DEmO 在 Format Check（0.85 vs 0.07）更强，因其逐样本独立处理绕过了长演示序列的组合困难；CC/DC-PMI 在 Duplication Check 上因过度校准而退化。代理提升与准确率提升之间存在统计显著正相关：跨模型合并的单侧 Spearman 检验在 Fisher 合并 p=0.03（χ²=13.95）；Qwen 单模型相关性不显著，主因 Order Check 已满分、Duplication Check 标签仅 True/False 两 token 导致置信度膨胀而判别力未提升（作者视为可由更严格早停缓解的局限）。消融显示置信度分量 C̄ 是主要驱动力（移除后优化有效样本比例从 >90% 降至约一半，平均代理提升下降约 40%），稳健性分量 R 次之，信息增益分量 G 影响最小（因权重小）；将扰动扩展到全序列（含查询位置）会降低准确率，印证查询位置噪声无代理收益却造成漂移。N 与 μ 的敏感性呈非单调变化，符合有限差分估计的偏差-方差权衡；单样本优化约 10–60 秒，平均迭代步数 Gemma ≈34、Llama ≈48、Qwen ≈60（上限 250 步、耐心 5）。

Conclusion: 演示输出的对数概率构成一个可靠且可优化的"优化曲面"：其上升方向与下游性能一致，而不仅是用于提示打分/排序的评分函数。据此，作者给出一种无微调、无 token 生成、无需辅助数据、无需预定义标签集的测试时校准方法，在 2B–8B 模型上从不降低基线准确率、统计显著地提升，并在多数任务上优于分类专用基线；该方法对分类与自由形式生成一视同仁，可作为即插即用模块与现有演示选择/排序策略叠加组合。局限包括：需已知输出跨段位置且示例标签必须正确，未在少于 3 个演示的情形验证；代理非平稳（每次更新都改变其计算依据的对数概率）要求保守学习率、可能增加迭代步数，但实测额外开销有限。作者亦开源代码。

Abstract: We propose to improve in-context learning (ICL) by optimizing the continuous embeddings of a fixed few-shot prompt at test time. The key observation is that the log-probabilities a model assigns to its demonstrated outputs$\unicode{x2013}$available from a single forward pass without generating any tokens$\unicode{x2013}$provide a meaningful signal for how well the model has inferred the task from its demonstrations. We formalize this signal as a bounded, self-supervised confidence proxy and maximize it via zeroth-order optimization over the prompt embeddings, yielding a test-time calibration procedure. The approach requires no finetuning, no token generation, no predefined label set, and no external data, making it equally applicable to both classification and free-form generation tasks. Across a comprehensive suite of ICL tasks, the proposed calibration consistently matches or improves upon the base model and outperforms classification-specific baselines on most tasks. The statistically significant correlation between proxy improvement and downstream accuracy gain confirms that the proposed proxy encodes a reliable optimization signal for in-context learning.

</details>


### [33] [Hidden Human-Like Nature of Machine-Generated Texts: Theory and Detection Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23190)
*Chenwang Wu,Yiu-ming Cheung,Bo Han,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了机器生成文本中存在"隐藏人类化片段"的现象，理论分析表明这些片段增加了句子复杂度，使检测更加困难。为解决此问题，提出了一种模型无关的堆叠增强框架，通过hard-EM优化迭代过滤人类化片段并细化检测器，在多种场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有段落级MGT检测方法将机器生成文本视为完全机器化的文本，忽略了其可能包含高度符合人类写作风格的隐藏人类化片段，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 将片段级保留决策建模为潜在变量问题，使用hard-EM启发式程序进行优化，迭代过滤置信度高的人类化子序列并对剩余文本进行Detector细化。

Result: 该框架在多种LLM和实际场景中能持续提升现有检测器的性能，且支持免训练部署，兼具灵活性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种模型无关的堆叠增强框架，通过hard-EM优化过程迭代过滤人类化片段并在剩余文本上微调检测器，在多种LLM和实际场景中验证了有效性，且支持免训练部署。

Abstract: Machine-generated texts (MGTs) produced by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly prevalent across various applications, while their potential misuse in fake news propagation and phishing has raised serious concerns, highlighting the need for MGT detection. Existing paragraph-level detection methods commonly treat MGTs as entirely machine-like, overlooking the hidden human-like nature of machine-generated texts: even fully machine-generated texts may contain spans that are highly consistent with human writing. To this end, we first reveal the existence of such hidden human-like spans, and then theoretically analyze their impact on detection. Our analysis shows that these spans increase the sentence complexity for detection, thereby making MGT detection intrinsically harder. Based on this finding, we propose a model-agnostic stacked enhancement framework that improves existing detectors by reducing the influence of hidden human-like spans. Specifically, we model span-level retention decisions as a latent-variable problem and instantiate the optimization with a hard-EM-inspired procedure, where the detector iteratively filters confidently human-like subsequences and refines itself on the remaining text. Extensive experiments across various LLMs and practical scenarios demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently enhances existing detectors. Notably, the framework can also work in a training-free manner, offering flexibility and scalability for practical deployment.

</details>


### [34] [When Is Next-Token Prediction Useful? Marginalization, Ergodicity, Mixture Identifiability, Local Sufficiency, RAG, Tools, and Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23278)
*Francesco Corielli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面澄清了语言模型训练中常被混淆的三个核心概念：完整条件语言过程（包含潜在情境）、边缘文本过程（积分掉情境后的结果），以及从有限语料库中学习到的模型分布。作者论证了将模型训练解读为估计"边缘文本法则"需要满足平稳性、代表性、遍历性等强假设，而这些假设在异质性语言语料中难以成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语言模型训练理论中的概念混淆问题。传统观点将语言模型训练描述为"学习给定前文的下一个token的条件分布"，但作者指出这一描述仅在特定条件下成立。实际训练面临两个关键挑战：模型观察到的是采样延续而非完整条件分布；真实语言生成还受事实、意图、目标、信念、社会语境等非文本因素的 conditioning。

Method: 采用信息论框架进行分析，引入条件互信息（conditional mutual information）作为核心分析工具。具体论证路径：（1）严格区分三种概率对象；（2）检验平稳性、代表性、遍历性假设的适用性；（3）提出"近似充分统计量"要求——观测前缀需充分捕获与延续相关的潜在情境信息；（4）将分析推广至异质性训练语料场景。

Result: 核心结论：只有在残差条件互信息——即给定观测文本后，下一个token与被忽略情境之间的条件互信息——足够小时，边缘文本法则才具有实际效用。此外，RAG和工具调用可被解读为增强条件充分性的技术手段。

Conclusion: 论文对语言模型训练的理论基础提供了重要的概念澄清，揭示了标准统计估计假设在非平稳、异质性语言数据上的局限性。其对RAG和工具使用的条件充分性解读为改进模型架构提供了新视角。关键词：条件互信息、近似充分统计量、条件充分性、边缘文本法则、语言模型理论基础。

Abstract: Language models trained on observed sequences are often described as learning the conditional distribution of the next token given previous tokens. This description is only conditionally correct. A model trained on realized token trajectories does not observe full conditional laws; it receives sampled continuations. Moreover, real language generation is conditioned not only on previous words but also on non-textual circumstances: facts, events, intentions, goals, beliefs, social context, and task-specific constraints. This paper distinguishes three objects that are often conflated: the full conditional language process conditioned on latent circumstances, the marginal text-only process obtained by integrating those circumstances out, and the model-induced distribution learned from finite observed corpora.
  The paper argues that interpreting model training as estimating the marginal text-only law requires strong assumptions of stationarity, representativeness, and ergodicity, assumptions that are standard in statistical estimation but problematic when applied to heterogeneous language corpora. Even if these assumptions hold, the marginal text-only law is useful only when the observed prefix is an approximately sufficient statistic for the latent circumstances relevant to continuation. In information-theoretic terms, usefulness requires that the residual conditional mutual information between the next token and the omitted circumstances, given the observed text, be small.
  The paper then extends this argument to heterogeneous training corpora.
  Finally, the paper interprets Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) and tool use as conditional sufficiency devices.

</details>


### [35] [Convergence Without Understanding: When Language Models Agree on Representations but Disagree on Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23315)
*Muhammad Usama,Dong Eui Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper tests whether the observed convergence of internal representations across large language models extends to their reasoning processes. By analyzing 16 models across 800 reasoning problems, the authors find that while models develop similar representations, this similarity does not translate to shared reasoning strategies—models converge more on failed problems, their pre-decision representations align but post-decision ones diverge, and while shared information can be decoded, it has minimal causal influence on predictions. This suggests representational convergence primarily reflects shared input processing rather than shared reasoning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis posits that LLMs develop similar internal representations. The researchers sought to test whether this convergence extends to the reasoning processes that operate over these shared representations.

Method: Evaluated 16 language models (1.5B to 72B parameters) from 8 families on 800 reasoning problems across mathematics, science, commonsense, and truthfulness categories, stratifying analysis by problem difficulty, computational stage, and causal relevance.

Result: Three key dissociations were identified: (1) Difficulty inversion: models converge more on problems they collectively fail (CKA=0.897) than on solved problems (CKA=0.830); (2) Generation gap: pre-decision representations align (CKA=0.875) while post-decision representations diverge (CKA=0.274); (3) Epiphenomenal correctness: shared information is decodable across models (66% transfer accuracy) but has minimal causal influence (1.5-5.5% flip rate in ablation tests).

Conclusion: representational convergence in language models primarily reflects shared input processing constraints rather than shared reasoning strategies, with implications for ensemble design, interpretability transfer, and model similarity evaluation.

Abstract: Large language models trained under diverse objectives and architectures have been shown to develop increasingly similar internal representations, an observation formalized as the Platonic Representation Hypothesis. Whether this representational convergence extends to the reasoning processes that operate over shared representations remains untested. We evaluate representational similarity across 16 language models from 8 families (1.5B to 72B parameters) on 800 reasoning problems spanning mathematics, science, commonsense, and truthfulness, stratifying by problem difficulty, computational stage, and causal relevance. Our analysis reveals three dissociations: a difficulty inversion, where models converge more on problems they collectively fail (Centered Kernel Alignment [CKA] = 0.897) than on those they solve (CKA = 0.830); a generation gap, where pre-decision representations align (CKA = 0.875) while post-decision representations diverge (CKA = 0.274); and epiphenomenal correctness, where shared information is decodable across models (66% transfer accuracy) but exerts minimal causal influence on predictions (1.5% to 5.5% flip rate across ablation protocols). These results indicate that representational convergence in language models reflects shared input processing constraints rather than shared reasoning strategies, with direct implications for ensemble design, interpretability transfer, and evaluations of model similarity. Code is available at https://github.com/Usama1002/convergence-without-understanding.

</details>


### [36] [AraHopeCorpus: Annotation Guidelines and Dataset for Hope Speech in Arabic Social Media Crisis Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23325)

TL;DR: 本文发布了AraHopeCorpus，首个阿拉伯语希望语音标注数据集，收集自加沙战争相关的1万条YouTube评论。研究发现建设性希望表达占主导地位（64%以上），以宗教鼓励、集体团结和对正义的乐观为主要形式。该数据集为阿拉伯语社交媒体中的韧性研究和危机传播分析提供了重要资源，同时揭示了方言和讽刺表达对自动标注的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注武装冲突中的仇恨言论和虚假信息，而促进韧性、团结和乐观的建设性表达，尤其是阿拉伯语语境下的相关研究严重不足，缺乏专门的语料库支持。

Method: 收集2023-2024年间与加沙战争相关的1万条YouTube评论，由人工标注者采用详细标注框架将评论分为三类：希望语音、非希望语音、中性或模糊话语，并比较人类标注者与ChatGPT的标注结果。

Result: 希望语音占比超过64%，主要表现为宗教鼓励、集体团结及对坚韧和正义的乐观；非希望语音约13%，反映绝望和幻灭；其余为中性或混合内容。标注一致性达到0.71（Cohen's Kappa），但方言变体、讽刺和隐含含义构成标注挑战。大语言模型可辅助标注，但在处理方言和文化嵌入表达方面仍有局限。

Conclusion: AraHopeCorpus将成为研究阿拉伯语社交媒体中建设性话语的重要资源，支持希望语音检测、危机传播和韧性研究，并开源以促进该领域发展。

Abstract: Social media has become a crucial arena for shaping public narratives during armed conflicts, providing space for both harmful and constructive communication. While hate speech and misinformation have been widely studied, expressions that promote resilience, solidarity, and optimism remain underexplored, particularly in Arabic contexts. This paper introduces AraHopeCorpus, the first annotated dataset of Arabic hope speech collected from ten thousand YouTube comments related to the war on Gaza between 2023 and 2024. Using a detailed annotation framework, comments were classified into three categories: hope speech, no hope speech, and neutral or unclear discourse. The dataset shows that hopeful language dominates, accounting for more than sixty four percent of all comments. These expressions of hope appear mainly as religious encouragement, collective solidarity, and optimism for endurance and justice. No hope speech, representing about thirteen percent, reflects despair and disillusionment, while the rest of the comments contain neutral or mixed content. Inter-Annotator Agreement reached substantial levels (Cohen's Kappa equals 0.71), though dialectal variation, sarcasm, and implicit meaning posed annotation challenges. A comparative analysis between human annotators and ChatGPT revealed that large language models can support annotation but remain limited in handling dialectal and culturally embedded expressions. AraHopeCorpus will be released for research purposes under an open and non commercial license. It provides a valuable resource for studying constructive digital discourse, enabling further research on hope speech detection, crisis communication, and resilience in Arabic social media.

</details>


### [37] [ClimateChat-300K: A Multi-Modal Facebook Dataset for Understanding Diverse Perspectives in Climate Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23326)
*Wajdi Zaghouani,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Mabrouka Bessghaier,Shimaa Ibrahim,George Mikros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClimateChat-300K是一个包含约30万条Facebook帖子的气候相关大规模数据集，收集自超过2.6万个全球页面，支持情感分析和主题建模研究，为数字气候话语研究提供开放资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数字气候话语研究缺乏大规模、公开可用、带丰富元数据的数据集，导致难以进行可重复的跨学科研究来理解气候变化相关的极化现象和错误信息传播动态。

Method: 通过CrowdTangle平台收集2020年5月至2024年5月期间的299,329条公开Facebook帖子，提取41个元数据特征（内容、参与度指标、页面属性等），覆盖超过26,000个全球页面。运用主题建模和情感分析方法进行数据分析。

Result: 识别出5大领域（政策、行动主义、合作、科学、保护）下的10个主要主题。研究发现情感基调、帖子形式和页面身份对受众参与度有显著影响，视觉丰富且情感强烈的内容获得最高互动量。数据集还揭示了线上讨论如何随国际气候峰会等重大事件演变。

Conclusion: ClimateChat-300K作为开放资源，为研究数字气候话语极化、错误信息和话语动态提供了重要工具。该数据集支持跨时间、地理和制度背景的透明数据驱动研究，有助于深化对公众气候议题参与发展的理解。

Abstract: We present ClimateChat-300K, a large-scale dataset of 299,329 public Facebook posts about climate change collected between May 2020 and May 2024 through the CrowdTangle platform. The dataset contains 41 metadata features including post content, engagement metrics, and page attributes, covering material from more than 26,000 global pages. Each post includes rich contextual information such as language, timestamp, page category, and interaction counts, enabling comprehensive analyses of public discourse around climate communication. Using topic modeling and sentiment analysis, we identify ten main themes grouped into five domains: policy, activism, cooperation, science, and conservation. The results reveal that emotional tone, post format, and page identity strongly influence audience engagement, with visually rich and emotionally charged content receiving the highest levels of interaction. The dataset also demonstrates how online discussions evolved in response to major events such as international climate summits and the COVID-19 pandemic period. ClimateChat-300K provides an open resource for reproducible and interdisciplinary research on polarization, misinformation, and the dynamics of digital climate discourse. By releasing this dataset, we aim to support transparent, data-driven research and contribute to a deeper un-derstanding of how public engagement with climate issues develops across time, geography, and institutional contexts.

</details>


### [38] [Emotion Recognition in Sign Language Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23328)
*Yusong Wang,Keyu Mao,Takao Obi,Minghao Shao,Kotaro Funakoshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将情感识别对话(ERC)任务正式引入手语视频分析，定义了基于多轮对话历史的手语情感识别任务形式化框架，并构建发布 eJSL Dialog 数据集(1,920 个手语句子视频、480 段四轮对话、日语手语、4 类情绪)，同时用 EmoAffectNet、EANwH、EmoTrans、MMGCN、TelME 五个基线进行系统评测。结果显示：纯视觉模型性能最差(加权 F1 29.06 / 33.31)，文本对话模型 55.40，多模态对话模型最佳(TelME 70.22、MMGCN 60.17)，且各架构对模态的依赖模式差异显著，证明上下文与视觉线索具有互补性，同时暴露出通用多模态 ERC 框架迁移到手语时存在明显域差距与视觉表征不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语情感数据集(如 eJSL Solo、EmoSign)均由孤立句子或单次表达构成，脱离真实双向对话语境，缺乏多轮对话历史，导致模型无法建模情绪在对话中的动态演化与共情迁移，在真实场景中性能显著退化。同时，手语中面部表情同时承担语法功能(如疑问句)与情感表达(如惊讶)两种角色，存在显著歧义，而现有面向口语、非手语人群优化的 ERC 框架视觉表征与手语存在域不匹配。此外，手语情感研究本身在主流文献中极少被涉及，缺乏客观评测基准。

Method: 1) 任务定义：给定对话序列 D={u1..uN}，每个话语 u_i 含视觉特征 V_i(手语视频)与文本特征 T_i(日语口语翻译或逐词 gloss)，学习映射 y_t = f(V_t, T_t, C)，其中 C={u1..u_{t-1}} 为历史上下文；并形式化其三种受限变体 f(V_t)、f(T_t,C)、f(V_t,T_t)。2) 数据构建：从 STUDIES 日语共情对话语料短对话子集中选取 480 段四轮对话(教师-学生辅导场景)，按 4 类情绪与二元性别平衡选取；由 1 名男性与 1 名女性聋人专业演员以 JSL 表演录制，纯白背景、单 RGB 摄像头、1440×1080@30fps，得到 1,920 个视频片段(约 4.65 小时，平均 8.73 秒)，情绪标注 A/J/N/S；文件命名编码场景、对话、轮次与标签。3) 预处理：视频按 2 fps 均匀采样帧；RetinaFace 检测裁剪人脸并缩放至 224×224；rtmlib Wholebody landmarker 提取左右手各 21 关键点，按腕部归一化拼接为 84 维特征。4) 基线：EmoAffectNet(纯视觉人脸帧级)、EANwH(人脸+手部骨架+LSTM)；两者用 BOBSL 英手语字幕经 DistilRoBERTa 情绪分类器生成弱标签做弱监督后训练。文本/多模态模型(EmoTrans、MMGCN、TelME)在 MELD 上仅保留 4 类重训，无音频时音频特征置零；文本输入为 Google 翻译的日语真值转写，属 oracle 信息。5) 评测：以加权 F1 为主指标并报告各类别 F1，另做 text-only / visual-only / text+visual 模态消融与两个案例分析；标签质量由 3 名非手语者对 40 个样本判定验证(macro-F1 69.41%，Fleiss κ=0.580)。

Result: 加权 F1：EmoAffectNet 29.06、EANwH 33.31(纯视觉，Sad 类极差，仅 17.03/13.24)；EmoTrans 55.40(纯文本)；MMGCN 60.17、TelME 70.22(文本+视觉)，均为最佳。消融：TelME 高度依赖文本(text+visual 70.22 vs text-only 69.47 vs visual-only 19.67，视觉单独时 Angry/Joy/Sad 全为 0)；MMGCN 则在两模态融合时增益明显(text-only 52.03、visual-only 46.94、融合 60.17)。视觉-only 的 MMGCN 优于无上下文的纯视觉基线，说明对话上下文对视觉情感识别有补充作用。案例显示上下文可捕捉隐性的情绪转移(教师积极反馈→学生 Joy)，而视觉线索可纠正纯文本对中性事实陈述的误判(EANwH 与 TelME 正确识别 Joy，EmoTrans 误判 Neutral)。此外，文本侧性能显著高于对应口语四类 ERC 任务，说明手语视觉表征仍存在域差距与不足。

Conclusion: 本文首次将 ERC 任务引入手语视频分析并发布 eJSL Dialog 基准，填补了手语多轮对话情感数据集的空白。结论是：现有基于语音的 ERC 框架可迁移至手语对话任务，多模态对话模型显著优于孤立视觉模型，对话上下文与手语视觉线索（面部表情、上身动作）具有互补价值；但不同架构的模态依赖差异明显，且现有视觉表征难以充分捕捉手语特有的情感线索，跨域适配仍有明显性能差距。因此后续必须开发面向手语的上下文感知视觉提取器、扩大手语对话数据规模以支持大规模预训练、丰富标注(区分情感面部线索与语言性非手部标记)、扩大手语者多样性并纳入自发对话，最终用自动手语识别/gloss 取代 oracle 转写以实现端到端评测。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversation is a core component of affective computing, while current resources of sign language emotion datasets primarily focus on isolated sentences and lack conversational context. Models trained exclusively on these isolated utterances demonstrate degraded performance in real world scenarios because they cannot utilize historical dialogue flow. To address this structural limitation, we introduce the ERC task to sign language video analysis and propose the eJSL Dialog dataset. Constructed using the scripts from the STUDIES corpus, the dataset contains 1,920 video samples organized into 480 unique dialogues. We conduct systematic benchmarking on this dataset using models ranging from isolated visual networks to multimodal conversational architectures. The results reveal a domain gap when applying generic multimodal conversational emotion recognition models to sign language. These findings demonstrate the explicit need for context aware visual extractors specific to sign language and indicate that expanding the scale of conversational datasets to support large scale pre-training is a necessary next step for future research.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型仍受英语主导数据、不均衡的多语言覆盖、有限政治制度假设的影响，在跨文化应用时产生系统性错误，存在民主问责制的实质性风险

Conclusion: 需要在治理约束和范围条件下才可支持民主合法性；文化适应性政治NLP需在特定条件限制内谨慎部署以维护民主问责制

Abstract: The integration of large language models into political discourse analysis creates new opportunities for comparative research, policy analysis, and civic technology, while introducing material risks for democratic accountability. This paper argues that cultural adaptation is a prerequisite for trustworthy deployment of large language models in political communication across diverse linguistic and institutional contexts. Current systems remain shaped by English dominant data, uneven multilingual coverage, and assumptions grounded in a narrow range of political institutions and discourse conventions, producing systematic errors when applied across cultures. We formalize cultural adaptation across translation, discourse, and ontology levels, identify recurring cultural failure modes in political NLP, and propose an operational evaluation matrix grounded in cultural fidelity, calibration, and democratic safety. Building on political text analysis, sociotechnical auditing, and cross cultural pragmatics, we outline methodological pathways including participatory dataset development, culturally aware transfer learning, and benchmark design that makes cultural adaptation empirically measurable. We conclude by clarifying governance constraints and scope conditions under which culturally adaptive political NLP can support democratic legitimacy.

</details>


### [40] [From Correctness to Preference: A Framework for Personalized Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23382)
*Ranxu zhang,zeyang li,Jiacheng Huang,Rui Zhang,Xiaozhou Xu,sun zhe,Yanyong Zhang,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一个性化Agentic RL框架，通过PARPO解耦任务奖励与个性化偏好奖励，PSGM实现偏好对齐技能检索，解决用户异质偏好、行为一致性纠偏和个性化技能检索三大挑战，在多个数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic RL在需要用户条件化行为的场景中面临三大挑战：通用奖励无法捕捉异质用户偏好、观察行为受一致性效应干扰、扁平记忆无法支持个性化技能检索。

Method: 提出统一个性化Agentic RL框架，核心包括：1) PARPO：解耦任务质量奖励与个性化偏好奖励，使用用户特定锚点稳定异质奖励尺度下的学习；2) 两阶段偏好解耦奖励模型；3) PSGM：偏好对齐技能演化图记忆，用于个性化监督和偏好对齐技能检索。形成偏好识别→策略优化→结构化技能积累的闭环。

Result: 在ETAPP、ETAPP-Hard和SJAgent三个数据集上的实验表明，本框架一致性地优于强基线的记忆和RL方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了用户异质偏好建模、行为一致性纠偏和个性化技能检索问题，为个性化Agent系统提供了有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (Agentic RL) has achieved strong progress in tasks with clear success signals. However, many real-world agent applications require user-conditioned behavior: the same query may call for different planning strategies and tool-use decisions across users. This setting raises key challenges: generic rewards cannot capture heterogeneous user preferences, observed behaviors are entangled with conformity effects, and flat memories cannot support personalized skill retrieval. To this end, we propose a unified personalized Agentic RL framework that embeds personalization into training-time optimization. At its core is \emph{Personalized Anchor Reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization} (\textbf{PARPO}), which decouples generic task-quality rewards from personalized preference rewards and uses user-specific anchors to stabilize learning under heterogeneous reward scales. We further introduce a two-stage preference-disentangled reward model and \emph{Preference-Aligned Skill Evolution Graph Memory} (\textbf{PSGM}) for personalized supervision and preference-aligned skill retrieval. Together, they form a closed loop of preference identification, policy optimization, and structured skill accumulation. Experiments on ETAPP, ETAPP-Hard, and SJAgent show that our framework consistently outperforms strong memory and RL baselines. Code and data are included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [41] [Metacognition as Reward: Reinforcing LLM Reasoning via Knowledge and Regulation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23384)
*Sirui Chen,Lei Xu,Yuying Zhao,Yutian Chen,Yu Wang,Beier Zhu,Hanwang Zhang,Shengjie Zhao,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MaR是一种受元认知启发的RL框架，通过元认知知识和调节两个维度为LLM推理提供过程级奖励指导，无需手工设计实例特定评分标准。在22个基准上验证有效，Qwen3.5-9B+MaR可超越GPT-OSS-120B，并具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL奖励设计存在局限：RLVR仅提供最终答案的验证信号，对中间推理行为指导有限；RaR虽能评估推理质量和任务合规性，但需要实例特定评分标准且设计成本高。需要一种能提供过程级奖励指导、且无需人工设计实例特定规则的方法。

Method: Metacognition-as-Reward (MaR)框架，通过两个通用过程维度引导LLM推理：1) 元认知知识，无需手工实例特定评分标准即可识别任务相关信息；2) 元认知调节，规划并调整推理过程。将模型rollout构建为显式元认知组件，使用轨迹级奖励优化任务知识覆盖率、调节保真度和最终答案正确性。

Result: 在22个基准测试中一致提升模型性能，较基线模型提升最高7.7%，较DAPO提升最高11.0%。Qwen3.5-9B+MaR缩小了与前沿模型的差距，在整体平均上超越GPT-OSS-120B，并在多个单项基准上优于更强模型。过程级分析显示推理质量显著改善，且具有良好的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: MaR extends reward feedback to reasoning trajectories while grounding signals in general metacognitive dimensions, achieving up to 7.7% improvement over base models and 11.0% over vanilla DAPO, with strong out-of-domain generalization.

Abstract: Recent RL methods have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of LLMs. Existing reward designs mainly follow two paradigms: (1) Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) derives outcome signals from executable checks or ground-truth answers, but provides limited guidance for intermediate reasoning behaviors. (2) Rubrics-as-reward (RaR) goes beyond final-answer checking by using natural-language rubrics to assess reasoning quality and task compliance, but often requires instance-specific rubrics and substantial design effort. To address these issues, we introduce Metacognition-as-Reward (MaR), a metacognition-inspired RL framework that guides LLM reasoning through two general process dimensions: i) metacognitive knowledge, which identifies task-relevant information without hand-crafted instance-specific rubrics, and ii) metacognitive regulation, which plans and adjusts the reasoning process to provide reward guidance beyond final-answer outcomes. MaR scaffolds model rollouts into explicit metacognitive components and optimizes them with a trajectory-level reward over task knowledge coverage, regulation fidelity, and final-answer correctness. In this way, MaR extends reward feedback to reasoning trajectories while grounding the reward signals in general metacognitive dimensions. Experiments on 22 benchmarks show that MaR consistently improves model performance, achieving up to a 7.7% gain over the base model and up to an 11.0% gain over vanilla DAPO. Notably, Qwen3.5-9B + MaR narrows the gap to frontier models, surpassing GPT-OSS-120B on overall average and outperforming stronger models on several individual benchmarks. Process-level analysis further shows substantial improvements in reasoning process quality. MaR also generalizes to out-of-domain datasets, where MaR-trained models improve over their corresponding base models on average.

</details>
