Motivation: Conitzer and Sandholm [2005] provided O(n (log m)²) upper bound for IRV communication complexity, but could only prove Ω(n log m) lower bound, leaving a gap to be resolved.

Method: Fooling set technique (communication complexity lower bound method)

Result: Lower bound raised from Ω(n log m) to Ω(n (log m)²), exactly matching the existing upper bound. Under single-peakedness restriction, complexity reduces to Θ(n log m). Both IRV-Average and STV maintain the same asymptotic complexity as IRV.

Conclusion: Communication complexity of IRV is Θ(n (log m)²). Under single-peakedness, complexity reduces to Θ(n log m). IRV-Average and STV share the same asymptotic complexity as IRV.

Abstract: The communication complexity of a voting rule is the worst-case number of bits that n voters must transmit to a central authority under the most efficient elicitation protocol in an election with m candidates. We study the communication complexity of Instant-Runoff Voting (IRV). Conitzer and Sandholm [2005] established an upper bound of O(n (log m)${}^2$), but did not provide a matching lower bound beyond $Ω$(n log m). We resolve this open problem by raising the lower bound to $Ω$(n (log m)${}^2$) using the fooling set technique, thereby showing that the communication complexity of IRV is $Θ$(n (log m)${}^2$). We further show that this complexity drops to $Θ$(n log m) under the single-peakedness restriction, and that both the IRV-Average variant and Single Transferable Vote (STV), the multiwinner extension of IRV, have the same asymptotic communication complexity as IRV.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [BOHM: Zero-Cost Hierarchical Attribution for Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22866)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BOHM是一种从复合AI系统已有路由权重中提取层次归因树的新方法，在零边际成本下提供多分辨率归因，在智能体和LLM实验中与昂贵Shapley方法高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley值方法(SHAP)需要评估任意组件子集的联盟值函数，这在第三方API、不透明端点和智能编排器场景中不可行——因为这些系统将路由集中在少数工具上，导致大多数联盟无法从已部署的编排器进行评估。

Method: BOHM从AI系统已有的路由权重中直接提取层次归因树。核心计算：(1)叶节点归因=根到叶路径的路由权重乘积；(2)第k层归因=深度k节点的诱导分布。该方法提供零边际成本、无需访问组件内部、多分辨率归因同时输出的能力。

Result: (1)18个LLM×880道LiveCodeBench问题：BOHM达到tau=0.928，SHAP需9000倍评估量才达0.980；(2)5驱动×7基准智能体研究：驱动将路由集中在单一工具(中位top-share=0.65)，BOHM与SHAP的细胞级tau由驱动首选是否为实证最佳工具决定(均值+0.22 vs ~+0.01)；(3)美国人口普查层次结构(475叶节点、4层)：各层恢复地面真实排名(tau最高0.722)。

Conclusion: BOHM是Shapley方法的互补原语：一种多分辨率分解方法，可在与路由状态共存任何地方计算，与Shapley的分歧本身具有诊断价值。

Abstract: Compound AI systems route tasks through hierarchies of specialised components. Attribution is dominated by Shapley-based methods (SHAP), which decompose a coalition value function into per-component marginal contributions and require evaluation of the system on arbitrary component subsets. That requirement fails for third-party APIs, opaque endpoints, and agentic orchestrators that concentrate routing on a few tools, leaving most coalitions un-evaluable from the deployed orchestrator. We introduce BOHM, which extracts a hierarchical attribution tree directly from the routing weights such systems already maintain: leaf attribution is the path product of root-to-leaf routing weights; level-k attribution is the induced distribution over depth-k nodes. The method has zero marginal cost, requires no access to component internals, and provides multi-resolution attribution at every level simultaneously, which flat methods cannot offer at any evaluation budget. BOHM and SHAP answer different questions and converge when the deployed router routes near-optimally. On 18 LLMs in a 3-level hierarchy over 880 LiveCodeBench problems, BOHM yields Kendall tau=0.928; SHAP reaches tau=0.980 at 9,000x more coalition evaluations per seed. On a 5-driver, 7-benchmark agentic study (35 cells, complete coverage), drivers concentrate routing on a single tool (top-share median 0.65), and cell-level tau(BOHM,SHAP) is predicted by whether the driver's top pick is the empirically best tool (mean +0.22 vs ~+0.01). On a US Census hierarchy (475 leaves, 4 levels), BOHM recovers ground-truth rankings at every level (tau up to 0.722). BOHM satisfies efficiency, monotonicity, symmetry, and weak suppression but not Shapley's additivity. It is best understood as a complementary primitive: a multi-resolution decomposition computable wherever routing state exists, whose disagreement with Shapley is itself diagnostic.

</details>


### [76] [NeuroNL2LTL: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Natural Language Translation of Linear Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22874)
*Paapa Kwesi Quansah,Ernest Bonnah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroNL2LTL是一种神经符号架构，通过中间表示和验证器参与的强化学习，将形式验证集成到神经翻译流程中，在10+领域实现28%语义等价率和86%满足性验证率，兼具正确性保证与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言与线性时序逻辑(LTL)之间的翻译需要专业技能，限制了形式验证在安全关键开发中的应用；现有模板方法牺牲表达力换取可靠性，神经方法虽达到流畅度但无正确性保证。

Method: 提出NeuroNL2LTL神经符号架构，通过结构保持的中间表示路由翻译；引入验证器参与的强化学习训练范式；实现满足性和非平凡性检查与最小编辑修复机制；生成上下文锚定的LTL解释以辅助领域专家验证。

Result: 在200,000+需求实例上，28%达到语义等价，86%输出满足可满足性验证，并具备为领域专家生成上下文锚定解释的能力。

Conclusion: NeuroNL2LTL证明形式验证可同时作为训练目标和运行时过滤器，使神经规范系统的可靠性来源于逻辑保证而非统计置信度，为安全关键系统中的形式化验证民主化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Effectively translating between natural language (NL) and formal logics like Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) requires expertise that limits formal verification's reach in safety-critical development. Template-based approaches sacrifice expressiveness for reliability; neural methods achieve fluency but provide no correctness guarantees. We present NeuroNL2LTL, a neurosymbolic architecture unifying learned translation with formal verification. NeuroNL2LTL routes translation through an intermediate representation whose mapping to LTL is structure-preserving by construction. Generated specifications undergo satisfiability and non-triviality checking; a minimal-edit repair mechanism corrects near-miss outputs before they reach downstream tools. The central innovation is verifier-in-the-loop training: verification outcomes serve as reward signals for reinforcement learning, producing neural components that optimize directly for formal correctness. On 200,000+ requirements spanning aerospace, robotics, autonomous vehicles, and ten additional domains, NeuroNL2LTL achieves 28\% semantic equivalence with reference specifications while ensuring 86\% of outputs are verified satisfiable. The system also generates contextually grounded explanations from LTL, enabling domain experts to validate specifications without specialized training. This work demonstrates that formal verification can function as both training objective and runtime filter for neural specification systems, allowing us to build neural-based tools whose reliability derives from logical guarantees rather than statistical confidence.

</details>


### [77] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RMA是一个用于解决研究级数学问题的智能体框架，通过专业模块分工、多智能体协作和迭代验证，在包含10道研究级难题的First Proof基准上取得领先性能，成功解决8道问题，优于现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于竞赛数学或形式化定理证明，缺乏针对研究级数学问题的有效解决方案。这类问题需要长程推理、文献检索和迭代证明优化，现有方法难以应对。

Method: RMA将研究级证明问题分解为问题分析、文献检索与理解、公平比较、知识库构建和证明验证等专业模块；通过初始化器、提议器和验证器三类智能体在共享结构化记忆的协调下，以多角色多轮工作流方式运行，通过迭代反馈协同生成、细化和验证候选证明。

Result: 在First Proof基准测试（包含10道跨领域专家贡献的研究级问题）上，RMA表现优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线，成功解决8道问题，生成逻辑更严谨、可读性更高的证明。消融实验表明性能提升来自各组件的协同作用。

Conclusion: 性能提升源于结构化推理模块、迭代优化和验证器反馈的协同作用，而非任何单一组件的贡献。代码将公开发布。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [78] [SciAtlas: A Large-Scale Knowledge Graph for Automated Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22878)
*Shuofei Qiao,Yunxiang Wei,Jiazheng Fan,Bin Wu,Busheng Zhang,Mengru Wang,Yuqi Zhu,Ningyu Zhang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciAtlas是一个大规模、多学科、异构的学术知识图谱，整合了来自26个学科的4300万篇论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组，旨在解决学术检索中浅层关键词匹配和向量语义检索缺乏拓扑推理能力的问题。该系统通过神经符号检索算法（三路径协同召回和图重排序）实现从语义匹配到确定性关联发现的转变，可服务于文献综述、研究趋势合成、创意定位和学术轨迹探索等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球学术产出呈指数级增长，导致"信息爆炸"问题。当前学术检索工具主要依赖浅层关键词匹配或向量空间语义检索，缺乏复杂逻辑关联的拓扑推理能力。基于Agent的深度研究框架易产生逻辑幻觉且推理成本高昂。

Method: 构建SciAtlas大规模学术知识图谱，整合4300万篇论文（覆盖26个学科）、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组，开发神经符号检索算法（包含三路径协同召回和图重排序机制）。

Result: 实现从简单语义匹配到确定性关联发现的无缝过渡，显著降低推理成本，为AI Agent提供全局视角和拓扑认知基底。

Conclusion: SciAtlas可作为有效"认知地图"，赋能自动化科学研究全流程（文献综述、研究趋势合成、创意定位、学术轨迹探索），同时大幅降低推理开销。

Abstract: The exponential growth of global academic output has confronted researchers and AI agents with an unprecedented ``information explosion,'' where fragmented and unstructured knowledge organization impedes deep interdisciplinary integration. Current academic retrieval tools predominantly rely on superficial keyword matching or vector-space semantic retrieval, which lack the topological reasoning capabilities required to navigate complex logical connections. Agentic deep-research-based frameworks are often prone to logical hallucinations and consuming high inference costs. To bridge this gap, in this report, we introduce SciAtlas, a large-scale, multi-disciplinary, heterogeneous academic resource knowledge graph designed as a panoramic scientific evolution network. By integrating over 43M papers from 26 disciplines, and a total of 157M entities and 3B triplets, SciAtlas provides a structured topological cognitive substrate that dismantles disciplinary barriers and furnishes AI agents with a global perspective. Furthermore, we develop a neuro-symbolic retrieval algorithm featuring tri-path collaborative recall and graph reranking, achieving a seamless transition from simple semantic matching to deterministic association discovery. We also present key application directions of SciAtlas, including literature review, automated research trend synthesis, idea positioning, and academic trajectory exploration, to demonstrate that SciAtlas can serve as an effective ``cognitive map'' to empower the full loop of automated scientific research while significantly reducing reasoning costs. We have released the interfaces for KG retrieval and various downstream tasks in our GitHub repo.

</details>


### [79] [Energy per Successful Goal: Goal-Level Energy Accounting for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22883)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A-LEMS提出将AI能量核算单位从每次推理能耗重新定义为每次成功目标能耗（EpG），并通过五层观察管道量化编排开销（OOI），发现agentic工作流能量开销是线性基线的4.33倍，但工具增强任务中agentic执行反而更便宜，证明编排结构是能量成本的主要决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI能量基准以单次模型调用或训练运行作为测量单位，但对于agentic系统，单次用户目标可能触发多步编排、工具调用、重试和故障恢复周期，此时调用计数是实现工件而非任务属性，推理级归一化会错误表示目标完成的能量成本。

Method: 提出A-LEMS框架，通过时间边界模型和五层观察管道将RAPL信号映射到工作流级能量，并使用可重现性协议绑定硬件和运行时配置；定义编排开销指数（OOI）以隔离编排相对于线性执行的能量成本。

Result: 在五个推理任务族和三个工具增强任务族中，agentic工作流每成功目标的平均能量消耗是线性基线的4.33倍（888.1 J vs 205.3 J）；该开销由编排结构而非推理计算驱动；对于工具增强任务，OOI反转低于1.0x，证明agentic执行比线性执行更便宜，验证了该指标捕获的是编排结构而非固定的上行偏差。

Conclusion: Energy-per-inference基准不足以评估agentic AI系统；EpG和OOI提供了准确的基准测试基础，其中编排结构是决定能量成本的主要因素。

Abstract: Current AI energy benchmarks measure consumption at the granularity of a single model invocation or training run. For classical single-turn workloads this unit remains coherent. For agentic systems - where a single user goal may trigger multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure-recovery cycles - the invocation count is an implementation artifact rather than a task property, and inference-level normalization misrepresents the energy cost of goal completion. We present A-LEMS (Agentic LLM Energy Measurement System), a cross-layer measurement framework that redefines the unit of AI energy accounting from energy per inference to Energy per Successful Goal (EpG). EpG aggregates total workflow energy across all execution attempts, including failures and retries, normalized by successfully completed goals. A-LEMS formalizes energy attribution through a temporal boundary model, a five-layer observation pipeline mapping RAPL signals to workflow-level energy, and a reproducibility protocol binding every measurement to hardware and runtime configuration. Building on EpG, we define the Orchestration Overhead Index (OOI), isolating the energy cost of orchestration relative to linear execution under identical task criteria.
  Across five reasoning and three tool-augmented task families, agentic workflows consume 4.33x higher mean energy per successful goal than linear baselines (888.1 J vs 205.3 J). This overhead is driven by orchestration structure, not inference compute. For tool-augmented tasks, OOI inverts below 1.0x: agentic execution is cheaper than linear, confirming the metric captures orchestration structure rather than a fixed upward bias.
  These findings establish that energy-per-inference is insufficient for agentic AI. EpG and OOI provide the measurement foundation for accurate benchmarking, where orchestration structure is the primary determinant of energy cost.

</details>


### [80] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ImProver 2 是一个用于 Lean 4 自动化证明优化的神经符号框架，通过专家迭代和结构化脚手架使小型模型（7B参数）能够匹敌大型模型，确立了证明优化作为可扩展学习任务的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式数学库快速扩展，对验证证明的重构和维护需求增长，同时对神经证明器训练数据质量要求提高。然而，现有的可扩展证明优化面临异构和启发式指定目标、数据稀缺、训练和推理成本高等挑战。

Method: ImProver 2 采用数据高效专家迭代训练管道，结合暴露形式化结构与轻量级非正式抽象的脚手架框架，并引入结构化证明属性度量套件进行评估。

Result: 7B参数模型在同一模型家族中性能超越规模大得多的模型，并与中型前沿模型具有竞争力；神经符号脚手架显著提升小型和前沿模型性能；小型模型在正确脚手架和训练下能有效重构研究级复杂证明。

Conclusion: ImProver 2 成功证明了通过神经符号框架和专家迭代训练，小型模型（7B参数）能够匹敌甚至超越大型模型，优化研究级别证明，建立证明优化作为可扩展、可学习任务的可行性。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [81] [Mediative Fuzzy Logic: From Type-1 Foundations to Type-2, Type-3 and Quantum Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22900)
*Oscar Montiel Ross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了中介模糊逻辑(Mediative Fuzzy Logic)的统一理论框架，涵盖type-1、区间type-2、粒type-3和量子扩展，将中介算子表征为受犹豫度和矛盾控制的凸聚合，通过真-假独立对建模中介真值，并在标准t范数模糊逻辑基础上引入中介连接词构建命题系统。通过自主制动传感器融合示例验证了框架在不完全、异构和轻度矛盾证据下支持透明、保守、安全优先决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中介模糊逻辑旨在解决模糊控制与决策中的犹豫或冲突评估问题，但其逻辑和语义基础在type-1设置之外尚未充分发展。本文旨在建立统一的理论框架，弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 将中介算子表征为受犹豫度和矛盾控制的凸聚合；2. 将中介真值建模为连续双格结构中的独立真-假对；3. 在标准t范数模糊逻辑基础上引入中介连接词构建命题系统；4. 建立从type-1到区间type-2、粒type-3及量子扩展的一致语义框架。

Result: 证明了公式（无中介时）的可靠性、析onk悖论性和对底层模糊逻辑的保守性；各高级形式化在适当假设下可归约到type-1情形，保证跨层次一致性；自主制动传感器融合示例验证了框架处理不完全、异构和轻度矛盾证据的能力。

Conclusion: 中介模糊逻辑已建立从type-1到量子扩展的完整统一理论框架，各层次间具有数学一致性。该框架为智能决策系统提供了透明、保守、安全优先的决策支持基础，具有重要的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: Mediative Fuzzy Logic was conceived as a practical scheme for reconciling hesitant or conflicting assessments in fuzzy control and decision-making. However, its logical and semantic foundations remain underdeveloped, especially beyond operational type-1 settings. This article develops a unified account of the type-1 core together with interval type-2, granular type-3, and quantum extensions. We characterize the mediative operator as a convex aggregation controlled by hesitation and contradiction, model mediative truth values as independent truth-falsity pairs in a continuous bilattice-like structure, and introduce a propositional system extending a standard t-norm-based fuzzy logic with a mediative connective. We establish soundness, paraconsistency, and conservativity over the underlying fuzzy base for formulas without mediation, and formulate coherent semantic extensions to interval type-2 truth values, granule-indexed local evaluations, and effects and density operators on Hilbert spaces. An autonomous-braking sensor-fusion example illustrates how the framework supports transparent, conservative, and safety-first decisions under incomplete, heterogeneous, and mildly contradictory evidence. Under suitable assumptions, the higher-level formulations reduce to the type-1 case, clarifying coherence across levels and reliably supporting future work in intelligent decision systems.

</details>


### [82] [EVE-Agent: Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22905)
*Yamato Arai,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVE-Agent introduces evidence verifiability as a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution in search agents. By rewarding evidence spans based on marginal accuracy gain, it creates an auditable, self-generated curriculum without requiring oracle answers, human labels, or external annotations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data-free self-evolving search agents risk rewarding fluent but unsupported examples, as their self-generated curriculum lacks verifiability—turning it into an opaque and potentially unreliable training signal. The authors argue that evidence verifiability is a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution.

Method: EVE-Agent modifies the proposer-solver framework by adding an evidence verifier that rewards verbatim evidence spans based on marginal accuracy gain when the evidence is provided. This produces a training signal favoring evidence that genuinely helps answer the question.

Result: EVE-Agent substantially improves evidence-grounded correctness over prior self-evolving search agents. The resulting curriculum is not merely self-generated but auditable by construction.

Conclusion: EVE-Agent provides a scalable, label-free approach to self-evolution that produces not just effective but also auditable AI agents—each training example carries an inspectable source span that explains why it should be trusted.

Abstract: Self-evolving agents should not train on examples they cannot justify. Data-free self-evolving search agents offer a scalable route to systems that generate their own questions, answer them, and improve from their own feedback without human annotations. Yet, without verifiable evidence, this loop can reward fluent but unsupported examples, turning the self-generated curriculum into an opaque and potentially unreliable training signal. We argue that evidence verifiability is a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution in search agents: each generated instance should include not only an answer but also a source-grounded span whose contribution to that answer can be measured. We introduce EVE-Agent, an Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agent that operationalizes this principle through a modification to the proposer--solver framework. The proposer generates a question, an answer, and a verbatim evidence span. An evidence verifier then rewards the span according to the marginal accuracy gain when the evidence is provided. This produces a training signal that favors evidence that genuinely helps answer the question, without requiring oracle answers, human labels, or external annotations. EVE-Agent leaves the backbone model, retriever, search tool, and optimization framework unchanged. Experiments show that EVE-Agent substantially improves evidence-grounded correctness over prior self-evolving search agents. The resulting curriculum is not merely self-generated but auditable by construction: each training example carries an inspectable source span that explains why it should be trusted.

</details>


### [83] [GENSTRAT: Toward a Science of Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23238)
*Vartan Shadarevian,Kia Ghods,Alex Kenich,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GENSTRAT是一个基于程序化生成战略环境的大语言模型评估框架，通过生成两人零和不完全信息卡牌游戏分布解决现有基准饱和和泛化问题。创新点包括：六维能力画像分解模型能力层次，以及粗糙度指标检测模型在相似游戏间的表现突变。实验评估9个前沿模型、36,000+场比赛，结果显示能力画像能揭示表面实力相近模型的根本差异，为LLM在复杂战略环境中的实际部署提供诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)日益作为经济主体参与市场、拍卖和投标等场景，但很难预测其在特定部署环境中的行为表现。现有战略推理基准测试在固定规范游戏上评估，可能饱和且无法泛化到实际部署的复杂多变环境。需要一种能持续评估且抗污染的测试方法。

Method: 1) 程序化生成两人零和不完全信息卡牌游戏分布；2) 构建能力画像方法论，从状态空间、时间深度、信息敏感性、对手建模、风险和脆弱性六个维度分解模型能力；3) 提出粗糙度(jaggedness)指标，测量策略相似游戏中模型优势的不可预测跳跃程度。采用50游戏样本、2000游戏池、大规模 tournament 评估（36,000+场比赛）。

Result: 1) 排名前三的模型(gpt-5、claude、gemini-3.1-pro)总体实力接近但能力画像差异显著；2) gpt-5和claude的局部粗糙度更高，表现不稳定；3) 能力画像与粗糙度测量能揭示整体排名无法展示的模型差异，为部署决策提供更精细的参考。

Conclusion: 能力画像和粗糙度测量提供了一种整体排名无法提供的部署相关诊断工具。GENSTRAT通过程序生成和细粒度能力分解，为LLM在战略环境中的实际部署提供了更可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents in marketplaces, auctions, and bidding settings. Anticipating their behavior in any specific deployment is hard. Existing strategic-reasoning benchmarks evaluate models on fixed canonical games. These benchmarks may saturate as the frontier improves, and they do not allow evaluators to generalize with confidence from benchmark performance to the varied and messy strategic environments that actual deployments involve. We introduce GENSTRAT, which uses procedurally generated strategic environments to address these challenges. Concretely, we generate a distribution of two-player zero-sum imperfect-information card games. The generator can draw fresh games on demand, allowing for evergreen evaluation and resistance to contamination. We pair the game distribution with a capability-profile methodology that decomposes model competence across six axes (state space, temporal depth, information sensitivity, opponent modeling, risk, and brittleness). We also introduce a jaggedness measure of within-distribution smoothness that detects when a model's advantage jumps unpredictably between strategically similar games. We sample 50 benchmark games from a 2,000-game generated pool and evaluate nine frontier and open-weight LLMs in a head-to-head tournament with over 36,000 matches. Newer frontier-tier models score higher on average. Beyond that average, models with near-identical overall strength show qualitatively different capability profiles, and two of the top three leaderboard models (gpt-5 and claude) are noticeably more locally volatile than the third (gemini-3.1-pro), despite being close in overall strength. Together, the capability profile and the jaggedness measure give a deployment-relevant diagnostic that the overall ranking alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [84] [The Deterministic Horizon: Impossibility Results as Design Specifications for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23024)
*Dongxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This thesis transforms classical computational impossibility results into a practical design methodology for AI systems. Its central contribution is the "Deterministic Horizon" — an architecture-dependent accuracy ceiling that cannot be exceeded through training — alongside a catalog of 16 impossibility specifications, each providing computable boundaries, violation costs, and constructive design rules for trustworthy AI development.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fundamental computational limits (Turing, Arrow, No Free Lunch theorems) have traditionally been viewed as curiosities. This thesis argues they should instead serve as practical design rules for building trustworthy AI systems that must function in real-world deployment scenarios.

Method: The methodology involves: (1) deriving computable boundaries from fundamental limits, (2) quantifying violation costs, and (3) generating constructive design rules. Specific techniques include information-theoretic analysis for accuracy decay modeling, circuit-complexity lower bounds for cryptographic tasks, and discontinuity analysis for sample complexity jumps in preference learning.

Result: The flagship result establishes a "Deterministic Horizon" — an accuracy ceiling determined solely by transformer architecture (layer count and embedding width), invariant to training efforts (adapter rank, sample size, loss function). This horizon ranges from 19 to 31 across 12 architectures, with fine-tuning on optimal-length traces recovering under 4 percentage points. Additional results include: super-exponential accuracy decay past the horizon; constant-depth circuit lower bounds for modular exponentiation; discontinuous sample complexity jumps in preference learning under misspecification; requirements for independent metrics in multi-stage retrieval; failure of standard truthful auctions for prompt-dependent valuations; and 110-190x overhead for zero-knowledge neural inference verification. The catalogue contains 16 specifications, with 2 compositions proved, 1 honest obstruction, and 4 open problems.

Conclusion: The thesis proposes an "impossibility-specification methodology" for trustworthy AI: every fundamental limit of AI is also a design rule. This reframes impossibility results from theoretical curiosities into actionable design constraints for the generative research programme.

Abstract: Large language models now write software, draft legal documents, and produce clinical notes, yet fundamental limits, from Turing and Arrow to the No Free Lunch theorems, shape what computation can do. This thesis turns such impossibility results from curiosities into design rules. Its flagship result proves an accuracy ceiling set by architecture alone: past a critical reasoning depth, no amount of training moves it, at any adapter rank, sample size, or loss function. Computable before deployment from layer count and embedding width, this Deterministic Horizon is measured between nineteen and thirty-one across twelve transformer architectures, and fine-tuning on optimal-length traces recovers under four percentage points. The mechanism is a capacity invariant of the residual stream, and an information-theoretic conversion yields super-exponential accuracy decay past the horizon. An unconditional circuit-complexity lower bound for modular exponentiation against constant-depth prime-modulus circuits complements this result. The same argument recasts across subfields: preference learning under any misspecified model jumps discontinuously in sample complexity; multi-stage retrieval pipelines require at least as many independent metrics as stages; standard truthful auctions fail for agents with prompt-dependent valuations; and zero-knowledge verification of neural inference pays a measured overhead of one hundred ten to one hundred ninety times per non-linear activation. Together these form a catalogue of sixteen specifications, each pairing a computable boundary, a quantified violation cost, and a constructive design rule: two compositions are proved, one pairing is an honest obstruction, and four remain open. The impossibility-specification methodology is offered for the generative research programme that trustworthy AI may need. Every fundamental limit of AI is also a design rule.

</details>


### [85] [PathCal: State-Aware Reflection-Marker Calibration for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23074)
*Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Tsubasa Takahashi,Dengzhe Hou,Zhengzhong Tu,Kazunori Yamada,Fangzhou Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了大型推理语言模型中不同反思标记（如"wait"、"but"、"alternatively"）的功能差异和影响时机差异，提出PathCal控制器通过区分标记类型并仅在局部不确定状态干预来校准推理路径，实现推理效率与性能的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前测试时控制方法将反思标记视为单一粗粒度类别，未能区分其功能差异；论文发现不同标记类型不仅功能角色不同，影响最大的时机也不同，且标记选择在模型进入稳定推理轨迹之前最为关键。

Method: 提出了PathCal，一个无需训练的解码控制器，通过区分标记类型并在局部不确定状态进行干预来校准推理路径。在每个解码步，利用反思标记的分布估计当前推理轨迹与竞争分支之间的局部竞争，并在竞争分支证据过多时软性重新平衡标记logits。

Result: 实验表明PathCal在6个推理基准上实现了更好的效率-性能权衡，能够在减少生成长度的同时保持或提升准确率。

Conclusion: PathCal通过训练无关的方式，在6个推理基准上实现了更好的效率-性能权衡，在保持或提升准确率的同时减少了生成长度。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Language Models (LRMs) has paved the way for tackling complex reasoning tasks through test-time scaling by generating long-form Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories during inference. Meanwhile, these trajectories often contain explicit reflection markers such as ``wait'', ``but'', and ``alternatively'', signaling hesitation, revision, and the consideration of alternative explorations, respectively. Recent studies on test-time control leverage such markers as lightweight handles for steering reasoning, typically treating them as a single coarse-grained category rather than distinguishing their distinct functional roles. In this paper, we conduct type-wise suppression and fixed-prefix intervention, revealing that reflection markers differ not only in their functional roles but also in when they exert the greatest influence. Specifically, different marker classes affect accuracy and generation length in distinct ways, and marker choices are most consequential before the model settles into a stable reasoning trajectory. Motivated by these findings, we introduce PathCal, a novel training-free decoding controller that calibrates reasoning paths by distinguishing marker types and intervening only at locally uncertain states. At each decoding step, PathCal utilizes the distribution over reflection-markers to estimate local competition between maintaining the current reasoning trajectory and initiating a competing branch, and softly rebalances marker logits when competing-branch evidence becomes excessive. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PathCal achieves a better efficiency--performance trade-off, improving or preserving accuracy while reducing generation length, without relying on external verifiers or additional sampling.

</details>


### [86] [Inductive Deductive Synthesis: Enabling AI to Generate Formally Verified Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23109)
*Shubham Agarwal,Alexander Krentsel,Shu Liu,Mert Cemri,Audrey Cheng,Rui Meng,Tomas Pfister,Chun-Liang Li,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Aditya Parameswaran,Matei Zaharia,Ion Stoica,Mohsen Lesani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出归纳-演绎综合（Inductive Deductive Synthesis, IDS）：让 LLM 智能体在证明助手（Rocq/Coq）类型检查器的"部分证明预言机"反馈下，联合且增量地合成实现与机器可检查证明，并由失败经验驱动策略切换（ISA 的 proposer/reloader）与性能反馈优化。在 7 个分布式 KV 一致性规范上，IDS 达到 7/7、平均约 6.8 小时、$106/规范（约为专家人工的 200 倍加速、比 SOTA 智能体便宜 17%），吞吐最高达已发布已验证系统的 3 倍；同时在 Dafny/Verus/Lean/Coq 四个跨语言验证基准与三个代码+证明基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体（Codex、Claude Code 等）擅长生成、测试、迭代代码，但只能覆盖测试所触达的路径，无法给出"对所有输入、所有消息交错与并发更新都成立"的保证；分布式系统的读-写一致性（如因果一致性、read-your-writes）正属于此类组合爆炸的正确性属性，测试无法穷尽。机械化形式验证可提供这种保证，但代价是数月至数年的专家工时（Chapar 的 KV 存储证明约需 9–12 个月专家投入）。作者的关键观察是：直接给智能体一份规范加验证工具并要求其一次性写出"实现+完整证明"必然失败——Codex(GPT-5.4) 与 Claude Code(Opus 4.6) 在同 prompt、同预算下仅 2/7 规范成功，失败模式是结构性的：智能体沿用人类习惯，把验证当作写完代码之后的下游检查，既要求一次性完成整篇证明，又把全部正确性反馈推迟到最后。此外，即便只让 Codex 生成实现（best-of-100），给出形式规范也仅 1/4 属性通过，仅给自然语言则 0/4，说明瓶颈不在采样能力而在"缺少可验证的中间信号"。

Method: 方法核心是"在部分证明预言机下联合合成代码与证明"（jointly synthesizing code and proof under a partial-proof oracle），实现为多智能体系统：
(1) Rocq 类型检查器作为唯一预言机：Rocq 对含 Admitted 洞的部分证明文件同样给出接受/拒绝判定且无假阳性/假阴性；作者以此驱动演绎式合成，每步扩展部分实现与部分证明后询问 Rocq 是否仍 type-check，通过则存入搜索树形成"良型部分实现"分支，失败则该设计路径被提前排除。
(2) Deductive Synthesis Agent (DSA)：受严格约束的通用 LLM 编码智能体（实现须先良型、规范不可改、终态无未证假设、状态须有界），在搜索树上以前向动作 Define（补全实现，留 admit 洞）、Prove（以 Qed 闭合 Admitted 洞）、Decompose（声明辅助引理为 Admitted 以推进主证明）推进；出错则 Repair（换策略重试）或 Revert（回退到更早节点）。
(3) Inductive Synthesis Agent (ISA)：无状态、从失败中学习的外层循环。proposer 为战术角色，在 DSA 停滞（连续 10 轮无进展）时基于 Admitted 引理上下文与 make 错误输出，提供具体辅助引理签名与 tactic 序列；reloader 为战略角色，按 20/40/60… 轮升级，读取 DESIGN_LOG/STATUS 与历次 META_GUIDANCE，产出全新的高层设计蓝图（数据类型、抽象函数、不变量、引理分解）并重启 DSA。
(4) 协调器：并行管理 DSA 池，监控进展、记录失败策略、调用 ISA；候选实现一通过即提取为 OCaml 并在 5 台 VM 的 GCE 集群分布式运行时上基准测试，性能测量回灌给 ISA 引导搜索；证明闭合后执行确定性 audit（零 Admitted/Axiom/Hypothesis/Parameter、编译通过、并匹配四类"空证明/平凡解"模式），只保留通过审计的解，最终返回预算内最高效的已验证实现。
(5) 证明形式：AlgDef 模块提供七方法接口（getReq/getGuard/get/getRes/putReq/putGuard/put），Refinement 模块给出前向模拟（inductive simulation）证明，由此导出 TraceInclusion 定理（实现的可观察轨迹是规范轨迹的子集）。

Result: 【分布式 KV（主结果）】7/7 规范成功（pass rate ≥2/3），平均 6.8 小时、$106/规范（最大 11h、$155），总墙钟 48h、$744；Codex 与 Claude Code 各仅 2/7（分别 67h/$880、56h/$885）；在两者均成功的 RYW、MW 上 IDS 快 1.6 倍、便宜 17%。Chapar 因果一致性证明 10 小时 $148 完成（约 200× 于 9–12 个月专家工时），关键在于放弃全局单对象副本状态、改为按 key 分条目存储，使交付引理可按 per-message/per-store-entry/per-replica-clock 拆分。【性能】匹配或超越全部手写参考：Chapar 向量时钟基线吞吐 3×、IDS suite CC 与 MR 1.4×、RYW 相当（±20%）、MW/RYW+MW 上参考实现全部超时而 IDS 达 152k/139k ops/s；差距机制是 IDS 状态表示有界（关联列表/平衡树）而参考实现的 function-as-map 提取后形成随 put 增长的闭包链，Get 代价线性上升（MR 斜率 0.21µs/层，R²=0.95）；Chapar 上向量化基线输在网络字节（+5.2 B/put → +47µs），列表基线输在 CPU（γ=+21.6µs）。【跨语言证明基准（SOTA）】DafnyBench 88/100、miniCodeProps 100/100、Verus-Bench 149/150、CoqStoq 97/100；较同 prompt 同模型的 Codex 高 +10%～+51%。【代码+证明基准】VERINA 176/189（前 SOTA 38）、AlgoVeri 65/77（前 SOTA 31，2×）、CloverBench 62/62。【消融】Rocq 结构化诊断是最关键单一组件：退化为 accept/reject 后所有规范 ≤1/3 成功、VERINA 降至 58%；−J（去掉代码-证明联合生成）使四个最难规范归零、VERINA 160；−P 150（−14%）、−R 167（−5%）；audit 步骤曾捕获一个 put 恒返回 false 的平凡解；−PF（无性能反馈）使六个规范平均性能下降 1.42×。所有七个产物通过 Rocq 内核且无 Admitted/Axiom/Hypothesis/Parameter，三个因果一致性规范的证明规模为会话规范的 4–6 倍（3037–3924 行、79–128 条引理）。

Conclusion: 结论：形式化验证的工程瓶颈可由人力转为算力——通过为 LLM 智能体提供快速且无假阳性的正确性反馈（部分证明预言机），代码与证明的联合构造从"一次性交付"变成可搜索的自动化问题，把 vibe coding 推进为 verified coding；IDS 技术不依赖具体验证后端（Lean、Verus 同样满足"支持部分证明机械检查 + 可编程接口 + 可执行提取"三条件），也不依赖问题域（只需存在可机检规范与可基准测试的实现），可望迁移至 OS 内核、编译器、密码协议与硬件。论文同时明确三点局限：仍需人工给出完整 Rocq 规范（规范瓶颈被认为是最大遗留问题，作者提出"对抗式规范合成"与"从既有系统抽取规范"两个方向）；七个规范未覆盖生产 KV 的完整运维语义（动态扩缩容、重配置、故障恢复、可观测性）；实证仅限分布式 KV，其他领域仍属推测。总体判断：IDS 把"已验证软件生成"从人力密集型任务转变为计算驱动的自动化搜索范式，但其有效性与规范质量强绑定，mis-specification 仍是残余风险。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly excel at generating, testing, and refining code. However, they fall short on tasks requiring formal guarantees of full coverage that testing alone cannot provide. Distributed systems are a prime example: properties such as consistency between reads and writes must hold under every possible interleaving of events. Mechanized formal verification can guarantee such correctness, but typically demands months to years of expert effort. As evidence, even SOTA coding agents (Codex with GPT-5.4 and Claude Code with Opus 4.6) succeed on only 2/7 distributed key-value-store specifications. In this paper, we present the first effective approach to addressing this gap, Inductive Deductive Synthesis (IDS), which jointly and incrementally synthesizes implementation and proof, and learns from failed attempts to systematically try promising strategies. Built as an agentic LLM system, IDS achieves 7/7 in about 6.8 hours and $106 per spec on average, roughly 200x faster than expert effort and 17% cheaper than SOTA agents. IDS further incorporates performance feedback into the same loop, yielding implementations up to 3x faster than published verified systems.

</details>


### [87] [Agentic Proving for Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23772)
*Alessandro Sosso,Akhil Arora,Bas Spitters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在CLEVER Lean 4基准上评估Claude Code的agentic证明能力，发现现代agentic provers已能以极高成功率完成程序验证任务，现有基准难度不足，需要更严格的评估方法和替代同构评分的新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估agentic系统能力是否已扩展至程序验证领域，解决现有程序验证基准与modern provers能力之间日益扩大的差距。

Method: 使用Claude Code在agentic证明框架中评估CLEVER（Lean 4可验证代码生成基准）。方法涵盖规格生成、实现验证及端到端程序生成与验证流程。

Result: Claude生成有效规格达98.8%（81.3%通过同构评分），实现验证通过率87.5%，端到端成功率达98.1%；Claude能提供高质量自我反馈，手动审查确认其可识别失败根本原因。

Conclusion: 现代agentic provers的能力已超出当前程序验证基准的难度，建议采用更严格、抗bug的评估方法，特别是替代基于同构的评分方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.

</details>


### [88] [Redrawing the AI Map: A Theory of Accountability Boundaries in Agentic Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23179)
*Muhammad Zia Hydari,Farooq Muzaffar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Develops a capability-level theory of accountability-boundary placement in agentic AI ecosystems, proposing that verification cost and responsibility transferability determine whether execution and accountability boundaries move together, with three boundary strategies emerging from this tension.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI orchestrators appear to accelerate modularization and organizational disaggregation by reducing interface/assembly costs, yet capabilities requiring evidence, review, signoff, or assignable responsibility may retain integrated accountability boundaries despite modular technical interfaces — creating a theoretical gap about boundary placement.

Method: Integrates digital innovation, transaction cost, complementary-assets, digital platform governance, and IS control perspectives to develop seven propositions linking agentic assembly-cost reductions, accountability assets, appropriability, orchestrator intent capture, and boundary misconfiguration to boundary strategy, value appropriation, and rule debt.

Result: Identifies three boundary strategies (component, integrated, dual-track); introduces accountability assets and rule debt as key constructs; provides structured illustrations across document processing, legal services, audit, clinical decision support, and procurement.

Conclusion: The theory explains when digital modularization extends to organizational disaggregation and when accountability keeps capabilities integrated, offering a framework for understanding boundary logic across document processing, legal services, audit, clinical decision support, and procurement.

Abstract: Agentic AI orchestrators reduce the interface and assembly costs of composing information systems capabilities across organizational boundaries, seemingly accelerating modularization and organizational disaggregation. Yet AI-enabled capabilities whose outputs require evidence, review, signoff, or assignable responsibility may retain integrated accountability boundaries even when their technical interfaces become modular. We develop a capability-level theory of accountability-boundary placement in agentic ecosystems. We introduce accountability assets: complementary assets that make AI-supported outputs legitimate, auditable, reviewable, and assignable to a responsible party. We argue that verification cost and responsibility transferability determine whether the execution and accountability boundaries can move together. The theory identifies three boundary strategies: component, integrated, and dual-track. It also introduces rule debt, the governance burden that accrues when organizational decision rules migrate from formal information systems into ungoverned agentic execution environments. Integrating digital innovation, transaction cost, complementary-assets, digital platform governance, and IS control perspectives, we develop seven propositions linking agentic assembly-cost reductions, accountability assets, appropriability, orchestrator intent capture, and boundary misconfiguration to boundary strategy, value appropriation, and rule debt. The theory explains when digital modularization extends to organizational disaggregation and when accountability keeps capabilities integrated. Structured illustrations across document processing, legal services, audit, clinical decision support, and procurement discipline the boundary logic.

</details>


### [89] [AutoResearch AI: Towards AI-Powered Research Automation for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23204)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Dingjie Song,Yixiao Huang,Ziji Sheng,Xueyang Zhou,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Yongchao Chen,Ran Xu,Lifang He,Qingsong Wen,Manling Li,Cong Lu,Shuai Li,Pengtao Xie,Yixuan Yuan,Rui Meng,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Caiming Xiong,Philip S. Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科研工作流自动化（AutoResearch）的发展现状，从人类辅助的"Vibe Research"到AI主导的自主研究系统，分析了五类核心工作流条件，评估了现有系统的能力边界，并提出五维评估标准，强调AI科研自主性在结构化、可验证场景中更具可行性，但在需要具身交互、长期验证或伦理问责的场景中仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI科研系统仍处于碎片化状态，各系统在自主性、领域范围、执行环境、验证机制和人类监督方面存在显著差异；且面临证据保存、可复现性、弱方向拒绝、溯源追踪、跨域鲁棒性和问责性科学闭环等挑战。

Method: 提出AutoResearch框架定义AI驱动科研工作流自动化的发展谱系，涵盖从Vibe Research（人类主导）到AI主导系统的连续体；基于五个工作流条件组织分析（文献 grounding、假设形成、实验工具使用、反馈验证、报告知识传播）；综合调研AI科学家系统、人机协同框架、基准测试及开源基础设施。

Result: 提出五维评估框架（新颖性、有效性、影响力、可靠性、溯源性）；揭示研究系统在控制权、证据、执行、验证和问责等方面的分配模式；证明AutoResearch的自主性高度依赖领域特性。

Conclusion: AutoResearch的自主性具有领域依赖性：在结构化、可执行、快速验证的场景中更具可信度；但在具身化、延迟性、异构性、伦理约束或制度问责场景中能力受限。

Abstract: Scientific research is being reshaped by AI systems that move beyond isolated assistance toward longer-horizon workflows spanning literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experimentation, validation, reporting, and revision. This shift marks a transition from task-level AI for science to workflow-level research automation. Yet current systems remain fragmented, differing in autonomy, domain scope, execution environment, validation mechanism, and human oversight, while still struggling with evidence preservation, reproducibility, weak-direction rejection, provenance tracking, cross-domain robustness, and accountable scientific closure. This survey examines these developments through AutoResearch, defined as the developmental spectrum of AI-powered scientific workflow automation. Within it, Vibe Research denotes the human-steered region of prompt-based assistance and human-verified execution, whereas emerging AI-led systems coordinate larger portions of the discovery loop without achieving robust autonomy. We analyze how research systems redistribute control, evidence, execution, validation, and accountability across workflows and organize the field around five workflow conditions: literature and research grounding; hypothesis formation and planning; experimentation and tool use; feedback, validation, and review; and reporting and knowledge communication. We further synthesize AI scientist systems, mixed-initiative co-research frameworks, benchmarks, domain deployments, and open-source infrastructures. Finally, we propose five evaluation dimensions--novelty, validity, impact, reliability, and provenance--and show that AutoResearch autonomy is domain-conditioned, being more credible in structured, executable, and rapidly verifiable settings but limited in embodied, delayed, heterogeneous, ethical, or institutionally accountable contexts.

</details>


### [90] [Foundation Protocol: A Coordination Layer for Agentic Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23218)
*Bang Liu,Yongfeng Gu,Jiayi Zhang,Zhaoyang Yu,Sirui Hong,Maojia Song,Xiaoqiang Wang,Mingyi Deng,Zijie Zhuang,Ronghao Wang,Mingzhe Cao,Yutong Zhu,Xingjian Li,Yifan Wu,Jianhao Ruan,Yiran Peng,Shuangrui Chen,Jinlin Wang,Yizhang Lin,Dongjie Zhang,Dekun Wu,Chen Ma,Lizi Liao,Han Yu,Jian Pei,Heng Ji,Qiang Yang,Yuyu Luo,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Foundation Protocol (FP) 是一个面向人机共生社会的图优先协调层，旨在通过统一异构实体、支持多参与方协作、提供经济原语和强化的政策与审计机制，解决大规模自主智能体间的协调问题，同时保持问责制不可妥协。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体从工具演进为社会基础设施层，其瓶颈正从模型能力转向协调能力。智能体需要建立可靠关系、组织多智能体协作、交换价值、支持AI经济，并在现实监管下保持安全和问责。

Method: 提出Foundation Protocol (FP)，一种图优先的协调层，统一异构实体（包括智能体、工具、资源、人类、机构和组织），支持原生多参与方组织和基于事件的协作，并提供计量、凭证和结算等经济原语，将政策、血统追踪和审计作为一等公民。

Result: FP实现了一种可扩展的协调框架，能够封装和桥接现有协议，支持增量采用，降低集成和治理开销。

Conclusion: FP旨在保持自主智能体的可组合性同时确保问责制不可妥协，使协调本身成为开放、多元、可治理的人机社会共享基础设施。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are moving from tools into a layer of social infrastructure: they browse, purchase, deploy software, manage systems, and increasingly interact with one another. As these systems scale, the bottleneck shifts away from raw model capability toward coordination. Agents need to form reliable relationships, organize multi-agent work, exchange value, support an AI economy, and stay safe and accountable under real-world oversight. This paper introduces the Foundation Protocol (FP), a graph-first coordination layer for an emerging human-AI society. FP unifies heterogeneous entities, including agents, tools, resources, humans, institutions, and organizations, and supports native multi-party organization and event-based collaboration. It also provides economic primitives for metering, receipts, and settlement, and treats policy, provenance, and audit as first-class concerns. FP is designed to wrap and bridge existing protocols rather than replace them, enabling incremental adoption while reducing integration and governance overhead. The aim is to keep autonomous agency composable while keeping accountability non-negotiable, so that coordination itself can become shared infrastructure for a human-AI society that is open, pluralistic, and governable.

</details>


### [91] [Design and Report Benchmarks for Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23262)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper addresses the gap between LLM agent benchmark performance and real-world knowledge work capability by proposing a three-step approach (defining work activity, specifying tested setting, scoring work product) to make benchmark-to-work claims explicit. They derive 18 work activities from O*NET and demonstrate the approach through three benchmark case studies.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current knowledge-work evaluation for LLM agents follows traditional NLP task logic, leading to a disconnect where higher benchmark performance does not reliably indicate real-world deployment capability. Existing benchmarks fail to capture how knowledge work is actually organized through roles, responsibilities, local materials/tools, and downstream-usable artifacts.

Method: The paper proposes a three-step approach: (1) defining the work activity under evaluation, (2) specifying the tested setting with materials, tools, roles, and constraints, and (3) scoring the appropriate work product. They derive 18 work activities from the O*NET occupational database, then validate the approach through three benchmark case studies: GDPval, OfficeQA Pro, and APEX-SWE.

Result: The three case analyses demonstrate how benchmark design choices shape work claims—revealing gaps between benchmarked tasks, tested settings, scored products, and broader work claims. GDPval evaluates non-code occupational deliverables; OfficeQA Pro scores grounded document analysis by final answers; APEX-SWE assesses software engineering with executable scored products.

Conclusion: The paper contributes a systematic three-step approach for benchmark design that explicitly connects benchmark scores to real-world work claims. It provides practical guidance for benchmark designers and demonstrates its utility through three case analyses, offering an inventory of 18 work activities derived from O*NET to help name and distinguish work activities from common benchmark tasks.

Abstract: The development of LLM agents has led to a growing body of work on knowledge-work AI, including coding, research, and healthcare. However, current knowledge-work evaluation and benchmark design still largely follow the logic of traditional NLP tasks. As a result, higher benchmark performance does not reliably show that a system can carry out knowledge work in real-world deployment settings. This paper contributes a three-step approach for making explicit how benchmarked tasks represent the work claims attached to their scores: defining the work activity under evaluation, specifying the tested setting, and scoring the appropriate work product. We review work studies showing that knowledge work is organized through roles and responsibilities, local materials and tools, and artifacts that must remain usable in downstream workflows. We then translate these concerns into benchmark design and reporting guidance, covering how tasks should be mapped to work activities, how tested settings should specify materials, tools, roles, and constraints, and how scoring should focus on the work product left by the system. To name the work activity being evaluated and distinguish it from common benchmark tasks, we derive an inventory of 18 work activities from the O{*}NET occupational task database. We demonstrate the approach through three benchmark case analyses: GDPval, a non-code occupational deliverable benchmark; OfficeQA Pro, a grounded document-analysis benchmark scored by final answers; and APEX-SWE, a software-engineering benchmark with executable scored products. These cases show how benchmark design choices shape the strongest work claim a score can support, and where gaps arise between the benchmarked task, tested setting, scored product, and broader work claim.

</details>


### [92] [Parallel Context Compaction for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23296)
*Musa Cim,Burak Topcu,Chita Das,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长时域LLM智能体中对话历史超出上下文窗口导致的摘要压缩瓶颈，提出并行压缩方法替代传统顺序同步摘要，在多种模型架构上验证其能在控制摘要量的同时提升效率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域LLM智能体会积累不断增长的对话历史，最终超出模型上下文窗口。现有基于LLM的摘要压缩方法存在固有缺陷：摘要本身是有损的，阻塞式调用会使智能体推理停滞数十秒，且操作员无法对摘要量进行细粒度控制。

Method: 提出并行压缩（parallel compaction）方法用于长时域agentic流程，并在4种不同规模（8B至120B参数）、混合dense和MoE架构、涵盖推理和非推理模型的backbone上，对比顺序同步基线进行系统评估，测试基准包括HotpotQA多跳问答和LoCoMo长上下文对话数据集。

Result: 并行压缩使操作员能够对摘要量进行细粒度、可预测的控制，并实现更精准的分块级提示工程；在匹配的压缩解码量条件下，减少了端到端wall time并提升了压缩吞吐量。

Conclusion: 并行压缩方法在保持匹配压缩解码量的同时，有效缩短端到端wall time并提升压缩吞吐量，同时为操作员提供细粒度、可预测的摘要量控制能力，证明了其相对于传统顺序基线的优越性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate growing conversation histories that eventually exceed the model's context window. Context compaction via LLM-based summarization keeps the conversation bounded, but summarization is inherently lossy and the blocking call stalls agent inference for tens of seconds. Moreover, the operator has no fine-grained control over summary volume since prompt instructions are largely ignored, and as context grows, both the amount of output tokens the model produces and the information it retains fluctuate substantially from run to run, making the agent's retained knowledge unpredictable across runs. We introduce \textbf{parallel compaction} for long-horizon agentic flows and characterize it against the sequential synchronous baseline across four backbones spanning 8B to 120B parameters, mixing dense and MoE architectures with reasoning and non-reasoning models, on the HotpotQA multi-hop QA and LoCoMo long-context dialogue benchmarks. Parallel compaction gives the operator fine-grained, predictable control over summary volume and enables more targeted prompt engineering per block. At matched compaction decode volume, it reduces end-to-end wall time and improves compaction throughput over the sequential baseline.

</details>


### [93] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs)，一种可编程的治理基础设施，将监管义务编译为可在结构化证据图上进行检查的机器可验证约束。通过确定性监管编译器将结构化中间表示记录转换为可组合的知识库模块，实现基于配置文件的治理重构，无需修改服务代码。在AI辅助HPC资源分配场景中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI治理合规方式以文档为中心（ prose描述、静态检查清单、人工审查），无法扩展至自动化AI系统。需要一种可扩展、自动化的治理方法来解决AI赋能关键数字基础设施中的透明度、问责制、公平性和可追溯性义务。

Method: 将OKB形式化为5元组，绑定规范义务与RDF/OWL概念模式、可执行的SHACL验证规则、明确的证据要求及PROV-O溯源链接。开发确定性监管编译器，将结构化IR记录翻译为可组合KB模块，支持基于配置文件的治理重配置而不修改服务代码。

Result: 实现两个原型，在AI辅助HPC资源分配场景中完成24次验证运行和四个治理配置文件测试。结果显示：配置文件敏感的验证能力、严格加性的违规累积、SHACL验证延迟12.6ms至100.3ms、以及Combined配置文件被确认为最全面。

Conclusion: OKB框架提供了一种有效的可编程治理基础设施，实现AI系统的可扩展自动化合规验证。所有工件以开源方式发布。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [94] [DART: Semantic Recoverability for Structured Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23311)
*Ke Yang,Panpan Li,Zonghan Wu,Kejin Xu,Huaxi Huang,Xiaoshui Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DART: A modular runtime system that solves the semantic recoverability problem in structured tool agents by formally defining admissible restore points that preserve downstream committed work, validated across multiple LLM domains and LangGraph.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: When a structured tool agent fails mid-execution, runtime faces a dilemma: safe full replay is wasteful, while local checkpoint restore is efficient but risks leaving downstream committed work tied to non-existent upstream history. This is critical in commitment-sensitive settings where rollback targets a failed instance yet downstream consumers have already acted on its output. Existing approaches provide mechanical rollback but no criterion for semantic validity after downstream commitment.

Method: DART: a modular runtime that (1) localizes the failed instance, (2) certifies semantically recoverable boundaries, (3) aligns checkpoints to those boundaries, and (4) selects an admissible restore point preserving committed downstream work under dependency and effect constraints—or blocks otherwise.

Result: Across three LLM-driven domains and external validation on LangGraph, DART correctly recovers all evaluated commitment-sensitive cases where baseline local recovery fails, with a five-domain safety audit finding no unsafe admitted rollbacks.

Conclusion: Controller legality does not imply semantic validity; sound local recovery requires an explicit admissibility check. DART successfully addresses the semantic recoverability gap in commitment-sensitive settings.

Abstract: When a structured tool agent fails mid-execution, the runtime faces a dilemma: replaying the entire task is safe but wasteful, while restoring from a local checkpoint is efficient but can leave committed downstream work tied to an upstream history that no longer exists. This tension is acute in commitment-sensitive settings, where rollback targets a single failed instance yet downstream consumers have already acted on its output. Existing recovery approaches provide mechanical rollback but no criterion for whether a local restore remains semantically valid after downstream commitment. We formalize this gap as semantic recoverability and address it in DART, a modular runtime that localizes the failed instance, certifies semantically recoverable boundaries of that instance, aligns checkpoints to those boundaries, and selects an admissible restore point that preserves committed downstream work under dependency and effect constraints-or blocks otherwise. Across three LLM-driven domains and external validation on a LangGraph-based substrate, DART correctly recovers all evaluated commitment-sensitive cases where baseline local recovery fails, and a five-domain safety audit finds no unsafe admitted rollbacks. These results show that controller legality does not imply semantic validity, and that sound local recovery requires an explicit admissibility check.

</details>


### [95] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VDSS是一个人在环多智能体框架，通过合同驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件，用于呼吸机决策支持。系统使用上下文老虎机进行在线偏好适应，并通过结构化拒绝反馈触发针对性重新规划，减少无效迭代。回顾性ICU轨迹回放表明该系统具有更高的建议可接受性和更少的交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前呼吸机决策支持面临挑战：1）规则方法难以实现个性化泛化；2）端到端强化学习或单一大型语言模型系统难以控制和审计；3）需要追踪不断演变的生理状态和疾病轨迹，同时尊重安全边界和临床医生的调优风格。

Method: 1）多智能体框架：协调模块化决策组件，通过合同驱动的结构化接口连接；2）上下文老虎机：在线偏好适应，从每次调整周期中临床医生最终接受的决策更新偏好；3）结构化拒绝反馈：触发针对性重新规划，减少无效迭代；4）可追溯证据：为审查生成可追溯的决策依据。

Result: 回顾性ICU轨迹回放结合专家审查表明：更高的推荐可接受性，更少的交互轮次达到可接受方案。

Conclusion: VDSS支持临床可部署的人机协同合作，通过模块化架构和人在环设计在实现个性化支持的同时保持可控性和可审计性。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [96] [When Planning Fails Despite Correct Execution: On Epistemic Calibration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23414)
*Zehao Wang,Shilong Jin,Zhao Cao,Lanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM多智能体系统中智能体误判自身知识而导致规划失败的问题，提出EPC-AW框架。该框架通过信息一致性评估选择稳定计划，并利用历史偏差信息持续校准认知状态，无需直接验证可行性即可有效改善系统规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在行动计划执行正确时仍可能失败，因为智能体在评估计划可行性时会误判自身知识，这种现象被称为规划中的认知偏差（epistemic miscalibration）。该问题具有隐蔽性（计划自洽可执行但存在隐性错误）和动态性（新信息会改变可行性评估，使过去的偏差信号被掩盖）。

Method: 提出Epistemic Planning Calibration Agentic Workflow (EPC-AW)，采用两种策略：1) Information-consistency-based Plan Selection，选择评估在智能体间保持稳定的计划；2) Consistency-guided Epistemic State Refinement，利用历史差异来引导未来规划，实现动态校准。

Result: 实验表明EPC-AW将系统级成功率平均提升9.75%。

Conclusion: EPC-AW通过信息一致性评估而非直接可行性验证来选择计划，并通过一致性引导的认知状态优化来持续校准多智能体系统的规划能力，最终实现了平均9.75%的系统成功率提升。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when planned actions are executed correctly because agents may misjudge their knowledge when evaluating plan feasibility, a phenomenon we term epistemic miscalibration in planning. Unlike execution errors, epistemic miscalibration is latent during planning, as generated plans can remain self-consistent and executable without observable errors; the miscalibration is also dynamic, as new information can alter feasibility assessments, potentially obscuring past miscalibration signals and causing them to recur over time. To address this, we propose the Epistemic Planning Calibration Agentic Workflow (EPC-AW), which assesses whether plans remain supported under varying information conditions rather than directly verifying feasibility. EPC-AW employs Information-consistency-based Plan Selection, selecting plans whose evaluations are stable across agents, together with Consistency-guided Epistemic State Refinement to adapt calibration over time by leveraging past discrepancies to guide future planning. Experiments show that EPC-AW improves system-level success by an average of 9.75%.

</details>


### [97] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究OPSD中特权信息导致意外副作用的问题，提出EDGE-OPD方法通过引导式展开和证据掩码双重机制解决该问题，实验证明该方法能使模型成功学习稀有目标身份，同时保护通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD面临核心挑战：特权信息可能导致模型行为变化超出预期，包括修改推理能力、降低通用能力、影响响应长度/风格/局部token偏好等指标。这使得学生模型可能学习到副作用而非期望的可迁移行为。

Method: 提出EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD)方法，包含两个核心机制：a) 引导式展开(guided rollouts)在采样时注入特权上下文行为，确保稀有目标行为出现在on-policy数据中；b) 证据掩码(evidence mask)仅在特权上下文支持采样token的位置更新学生模型，而非对展开中的每个token进行更新。

Result: 实验表明，标准OPSD及其变体RLSD（无论是否使用验证器）完全无法学习目标身份，而引导式展开的引入使学习成功成为可能。掩码区域消融实验进一步揭示，角色信号集中于正向证据尾部，证明该方法可实现高效知识迁移并保护通用能力。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD通过引导式展开和证据掩码的双重机制，有效解决了OPSD中特权信息导致意外副作用的问题，为高效知识迁移和通用能力保护提供了有价值的洞察。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has gained wide attraction as an LLM post-training paradigm due to its effectiveness in improving capabilities without introducing model distribution drift, and consequently, regression in general tasks. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is an efficient use-case of OPD, which is appealing as it requires only a single model as a student and teacher, and it also has the benefit of providing privileged context that is a absent at inference time (e.g. a persona, a private fact, or a worked solution) to the teacher during the training process. The challenge in this approach is that the privileged information can change model behavior more than intended: it can modify reasoning, degrade general capabilities, and affect performance indicators like response length, style, or local token preferences. Consequently, OPSD may train the student on side effects rather than a desired, transferable behavior. In this paper, we study this problem in a rare-token/identity setting and propose EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD), a modification of OPSD with two distinct characteristics: a) it uses guided rollouts to inject privileged-context behavior to the student at sampling time, so that the rare target behavior is actually present in the on-policy data, and b) it applies an evidence mask: the student is updated only at token positions where the privileged context supports the sampled token, rather than on every token in the rollout. We empirically show that OPSD (and its variant RLSD, with and without a verifier) completely fail to learn a target identity, while the integration of guided rollouts allows them to succeed. Additionally, mask-region ablations show that the persona signal is localized to the positive-evidence tail, allows us to draw valuable insights about efficient knowledge transfer and preservation of general purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [98] [CP or DP? Why Not Both: A Case Study in the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23569)
*Emma Legrand,Roger Kameugne,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将动态规划(DP)与约束规划(CP)混合求解偏序车间调度问题(PSSP)的方法。以DP为主搜索框架，CP作为子程序提供全局约束传播能力。该方法灵活支持任意层搜索策略，便于纳入任意优先级约束，并能自然扩展为大邻域搜索。虽非最优方案，但证明了DP-CP混合集成的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划(DP)和约束规划(CP)是解决组合优化问题的两大经典范式，但通常各自独立使用。该论文认为两者可以有效且优雅地结合，从而充分发挥各自优势。

Method: 提出一种DP与CP混合的方法：DP作为主搜索框架，CP作为子程序利用全局约束传播。具体包括：(1) 将CP过滤算法嵌入DP框架；(2) 支持任意层搜索(Anytime Column Search)等灵活策略；(3) 通过CP建模方便地纳入任意优先级约束；(4) 设计大邻域搜索(LNS)方案，在重启时复用DP模型并施加偏序调度以改进当前解。

Result: 该混合方法能够自然处理任意优先级图，甚至支持设计大邻域搜索(LNS)方案。然而，在PSSP问题上该方法的性能不如最先进的纯CP求解器。

Conclusion: 本论文的主要贡献是证明了DP与CP混合集成的可行性。这种混合方法能够处理任意优先级图，并支持设计LNS方案。虽然在PSSP问题上不如纯CP求解器有竞争力，但为组合优化领域提供了一种新的解决思路。

Abstract: Dynamic Programming (DP) and Constraint Programming (CP) are well-established paradigms for solving combinatorial optimization problems. Usually, these two approaches are used separately. This paper aims to show that the two can be combined effectively and elegantly, with DP serving as the primary search framework and CP used as a subroutine to leverage global constraint propagation. This paper presents such an approach for the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem (PSSP), for which a pure DP method has previously been proposed, and efficient CP filtering algorithms are available. The PSSP is a general scheduling problem where each job consists of a set of operations with arbitrary precedence constraints. The approach is flexible enough to accommodate anytime DP strategies, such as anytime column search, whereas the original DP algorithm operated in a strictly layer-wise manner. Moreover, the flexibility of the CP modeling makes it straightforward to incorporate arbitrary precedence constraints. As a result, the model naturally handles any precedence graph and even enables the design of a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) scheme, in which the DP model is reused, and partial-order schedules are imposed across restarts to improve the incumbent solution. While not competitive with state-of-the-art pure CP solvers for this specific problem, our primary contribution is demonstrating the viability of this hybrid integration.

</details>


### [99] [Co-ReAct: Rubrics as Step-Level Collaborators for ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23590)
*Jiazheng Kang,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiangwang Chen,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-ReAct提出了一种利用评分 rubric 在推理阶段指导 ReAct 风格 agent 进行动作选择的框架，通过 GRPO 训练 list-wise 的 rubric 生成器，在 DeepResearchBench 和 SQA-CS-V2 上取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 风格 agent 在搜索密集型多步推理任务中，主要依赖内部判断决定下一步行动，导致推理轨迹浅显、冗余或目标不明确。现有 rubric 应用多停留在训练时的评估信号或事后评价，缺乏细粒度的步骤级动作指导。

Method: 1) 提出 Co-ReAct 框架，在每个决策步骤将 rubric 注入 agent 上下文以指导 Reason-or-Act 决策；2) 采用 GRPO 训练 rubric 生成器，优化 list-wise Spearman 等级相关reward，对齐多裁判专家共识排名，而非传统的 pairwise 或 binary 偏好。

Result: Co-ReAct 在 DeepResearchBench 和 SQA-CS-V2 上，在 8B/14B 开源模型和前沿闭源模型上均一致优于 ReAct 及代表性测试时计算基线。训练好的 rubric 生成器可作为即插即用组件独立提升基线性能。

Conclusion: Rubric 可作为可靠的步骤级动作引导信号，list-wise 偏好优化优于 pairwise/binary 偏好建模，为提升 agent 推理质量提供了新范式。

Abstract: ReAct-style agents for search-intensive, multi-step reasoning tasks rely largely on their own internal judgment to decide what evidence to seek, which reasoning or action step to take next, and when to stop, often producing shallow, redundant, or poorly targeted trajectories. Prior work has explored rubrics as external quality signals, but existing uses are mostly evaluative rather than action-guiding: rubrics typically serve as training-time rewards or post-hoc evaluators of completed outputs, and in deep-research settings they are often coarse-grained and report-level rather than step-level. We introduce Co-ReAct, a rubric-guided action-selection framework that uses rubrics as step-level guidance during inference. At each decision step, Co-ReAct injects a rubric into the agent's context to guide the next Reason-or-Act decision, specifying what the agent should target in evidence seeking, search, reasoning, or self-evaluation. To make this guidance reliable, we train a dedicated rubric generator with GRPO. Unlike prior pairwise or binary preference formulations, our objective optimizes a list-wise Spearman rank-correlation reward against multi-judge expert consensus rankings, encouraging rubrics that are discriminative rather than merely plausible. On DeepResearchBench and SQA-CS-V2, Co-ReAct consistently improves over ReAct and representative test-time compute baselines across search agents built on both 8B/14B open-source and frontier closed-source base models. The trained rubric generator can also serve as a drop-in component that improves these baselines without changing their underlying decision mechanisms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/Co-ReAct.

</details>


### [100] [Solving the Aircraft Disassembly Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23592)
*Charles Thomas,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对飞机生命末期拆解的调度优化问题，考虑了技术人员资质、设备需求、操作优先级、飞机平衡和空间限制等多重约束，提出并比较了约束编程和混合整数规划两种求解方法，在基于真实数据的多规模测试实例（最多1450个任务）上验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机生命末期拆解是实现可持续性的必要操作，但航空公司的利润率较低。因此，高效的拆解调度对于确保该过程的盈利能力和激励相关实践至关重要。该问题涉及数千个任务和多种复杂约束，需要专门的优化方法来求解。

Method: 论文提出了两种方法来求解飞机拆解调度问题：(1) 约束编程（Constraint Programming, CP）模型，利用CP的约束传播和搜索机制处理复杂约束关系；(2) 混合整数规划（MIP）模型，通过数学规划求解大规模优化问题。两种方法均使用基于真实工业数据生成的测试实例进行验证。

Result: 实验测试了包含最多1450个任务的多种规模实例，验证了两种模型的有效性和实用性。基于工业合作伙伴提供的真实运营数据，证明了所提方法能够处理实际生产环境中的复杂调度需求。

Conclusion: Both CP and MIP models effectively solve the aircraft disassembly scheduling problem, with the CP approach likely handling complex constraints more naturally while MIP provides strong optimization guarantees. The problem formulation captures the real-world operational complexities and the proposed methods can handle large-scale instances with thousands of tasks.

Abstract: Dismantling aircrafts reaching their end of life is a complex endeavour that is necessary in terms of sustainability but yields small income margins for air transport companies. An efficient scheduling of the disassembly procedure is thus crucial to ensure the profitability of the process and incentivize practice. This is a large scheduling problem that involves thousands of tasks and many different constraints: Extracting parts that are destined to be reused requires technicians with specific certifications and equipment. Extraction operations might be subject to precedence relations. Furthermore, the aircraft must be kept balanced during the whole process. Finally, some of the locations of the aircraft have a limited space that caps the number of technicians able to work there concurrently. This article presents the problem in details and proposes two approaches to solve the problem: a Constraint Programming model and a MIP model. The models are tested on instances of varying sizes involving up to 1450 tasks, which are based on real operational data provided by an industrial partner.

</details>


### [101] [One Policy, Infinite NPCs: Persona-Traceable Shared RL Policies for Scalable Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23652)
*Yoosung Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出pcsp（Persona Conditioned Shared Policy），一种基于LLM嵌入的条件强化学习策略，用于游戏中NPC的个性控制。在300人角色生活模拟基准上实现零样本角色识别率超过随机基线17倍，语义-行为对齐Spearman相关系数约0.73，推理速度比LLM-as-policy基线快22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生活模拟游戏需要数百至数千个NPC同时保持个性一致性和可控性，现有方法在角色一致性、可控性或实时推理方面存在不足，无法满足实际部署需求。

Method: pcsp将冻结LLM嵌入与共享强化学习策略结合，采用一次性角色编码、低秩角色投影、神经角色条件化，以及PPO + InfoNCE一致性+KL多样性的训练目标。InfoNCE轨迹一致性目标为关键组件。

Result: 消融实验表明InfoNCE目标不可或缺，移除后零样本角色识别降至随机水平。外部验证（Melting Pot 2.4.0）确认多智能体策略环境中产生角色驱动的行为差异。UE5部署在64个智能体上以低故障率复现结果。

Conclusion: 共享强化学习策略可支持可扩展、实时的角色条件化NPC控制，为商业游戏引擎中的大规模智能NPC系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: On a 300-persona life-simulation benchmark, pcsp achieves compositional zero-shot persona identification up to 17x above chance, Spearman rho approx 0.73 semantic-behavioral alignment, and 22x faster inference than an LLM-as-policy baseline. Life simulation games require hundreds to thousands of non-player characters (NPCs) that behave consistently with distinct personalities while remaining controllable through designer-authored natural language. Existing methods fail on constraints like persona consistency, controllability, or real-time inference. We introduce pcsp (Persona Conditioned Shared Policy), a single reinforcement learning policy conditioned on frozen LLM embeddings of free-form persona descriptions. pcsp combines once-per-NPC persona encoding, low-rank persona projection, neural persona conditioning, and a PPO + InfoNCE consistency + KL diversity training objective. Across three experimental settings, ablations show that the InfoNCE trajectory-consistency objective is load bearing: removing it collapses zero-shot persona identification to chance. External validation on Melting Pot 2.4.0 substrates confirms that our method produces persona-conditioned behavioral divergence in multi-agent strategic environments. We distinguish two senses of held-out evaluation: compositional zero-shot and vocabulary-expansion held-out. Finally, a UE5 deployment reproduces the in-engine persona-conditioning ablation at 64 agents with a low failure rate, showing that the sub-frame inference profile survives in a commercial game engine. These results prove that shared RL policies can support scalable, real-time, persona-conditioned NPC control.

</details>


### [102] [MemAudit: Post-hoc Auditing of Poisoned Agent Memory via Causal Attribution and Structural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23723)
*Zhewen Tan,Yilun Yao,Huiyan Jin,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Mengyuan Fan,liang lu,Feng Liu,Xiangzheng Zhang,Duohe Ma,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemAudit 提出了一种事后（post-hoc）因果记忆审计框架，通过"反事实记忆影响分数（CMIS）+ 记忆一致性图（MCG）"双信号融合对被投毒的智能体记忆进行排序并定向删除，在 MINJA 的 QA 与 RAP 设置上将攻击成功率分别从 70%/83.3% 降至 0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强型 LLM 智能体通过外部持久记忆积累跨会话经验，但恶意用户可通过普通交互将恶意记录写入记忆（MINJA 的 query-only 注入攻击），使投毒在后续检索中被反复激活、造成延迟且持续的行为操纵。现有防御（A-MemGuard、信任打分、贝叶斯信任记忆等）均为在线预防式，未回答"有害行为已发生后，究竟是哪些记忆条目应被追责与移除"这一事后归因问题。真实部署中的失效模式往往是先发生异常行为（日志、用户举报），事后才需诊断，因此需要一种无需 oracle 投毒标签的事后记忆审计与修复机制。

Method: 形式化设定：记忆库 M，检索子集 R(q,M)，生成策略 f，输出 y=f(q,R(q,M))；有害事件 e=(q*,y*,R*)；审计者仅可访问 M、有害事件集合 E 与重放接口，目标是选出移除集 S 使 min_S HarmAfter(M\S)。方法包含两个互补信号：(1) CMIS(m_i)=h(q*,y*)−h(q*,f(q*,R(q*,M\{m_i})))，即移除单条记忆后重放并用 harm 函数度量有害度下降，反映事件级因果贡献；(2) 以记忆图为节点、以语义相似度定邻域、以 DeBERTa-v3 自然语言推理检测蕴含/矛盾并定义不一致权重 w(m_i,m_j)，结构异常分数 CAS(m_i)=Σ w·sim，以 μ+2σ 判异常，反映全局结构不一致。归一化后融合为解毒分数 DS(m_i)=α·CMIS~+(1−α)·CAS~（默认 α=0.6），按 DS 排名取 Top-k 删除。审计采用批量（batch）方式：所有有害事件在同一未修改的原始记忆状态上归因，最终排名确定后再统一移除，避免顺序效应。实验遵循三阶段事后协议（重放收集有害事件 → 在原始记忆库上审计 → 删除 S 后在净化记忆库 M'=M\S 上重评），指标为 ASR 与污染率 ρ=poison/(benign+poison)。

Result: 主结果（QA ρ=0.20、RAP ρ=0.15）：MemAudit 在 QA 上将 GPT-4o 的 ASR 从 70.0% 降至 0.0%、GPT-4o-mini 从 50.0% 降至 0.0%、DeepSeek 从 70.0% 降至 10.0%；RAP 上三种骨干均降至 0.0%（GPT-4o 从 83.3%）。同等删除量下，随机删除(RD)、检索频次删除(RF)、近邻矛盾过滤(NNC)仅取得部分下降（如 QA GPT-4o 分别为 67.0%、58.0%）。消融：单用 CMIS 或单用 MCG 均明显劣于双信号（QA GPT-4o 为 32.0%/46.0% vs 0.0%）；融合权重在 α=0.6 最优，α=0.8 反而回升。污染率分析揭示明确的失效边界：QA 在 ρ≤0.20、RAP 在 ρ≤0.15 可完全净化，ρ=0.25/0.24 时 ASR 骤升至 60%~70%/80%~87%，ρ=0.50/0.63 时几乎完全失效（90%~100%）。

Conclusion: MemAudit 首次将"事后因果记忆审计"形式化为记忆增强型 LLM 智能体的安全问题，在无需投毒标签的前提下通过因果重放与结构异常检测的融合实现可疑记忆的定向移除，证明事后记忆修复在短文本 QA 与长程推理智能体两类场景均可行；作者主张持久记忆应被视为 LLM 智能体的一等安全攻击面。但方法仅适用于事后诊断且依赖可观测失效信号与重放质量，存在误删良性记忆的风险；在投毒密度超过临界阈值（QA≈0.25、RAP≈0.24）时失效，需配合回滚、全局清洗或更强的在线防御；评估仅覆盖 MINJA 的 QA/RAP 两个基准，代码与数据尚未开源。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on persistent memory to store past interactions, retrieve relevant demonstrations, and improve long-horizon task execution. However, this memory mechanism also creates a practical security vulnerability: an adversarial user may inject malicious records into the agent's memory through ordinary interaction, and these records can later be retrieved to steer the agent's reasoning and actions. Existing defenses primarily focus on online intervention, such as prompt filtering or output blocking, but they do not address the post-hoc question of which stored memories are responsible after harmful behavior has already been observed. We propose \textbf{MemAudit}, a post-hoc causal memory auditing framework for memory-augmented LLM agents. The framework combines two complementary signals: (1) a counterfactual memory influence score that measures each memory's causal contribution to harmful outputs, and (2) a memory consistency graph that identifies structurally anomalous memories within the broader memory store. We evaluate MemAudit against MINJA, a query-only memory injection attack in which malicious records are generated and stored through normal agent interactions rather than direct memory-bank modification. Across both QA and reasoning-agent settings, MemAudit substantially reduces attack success rates under realistic post-hoc auditing scenarios. The results show that QA attack success is reduced from $70\%$ to $0\%$, while RAP attack success drops from $83.3\%$ to $0\%$.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23780)
*Haoyuan Wang,Xiaohao Liu,Jiajie Su,Jianmao Xiao,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI
