TL;DR: 本文提出面向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的鲁棒知识编辑框架 ASAM（Adversarial Subspace Alignment），核心是将编辑从"单样本记忆"转向"语义概念级内化"：通过 LAR 在联合视觉-语言隐空间生成对抗式语义等价变体，用 RCSL 以奇异值驱动的低秩对齐目标在编辑层强制这些变体收敛到同一语义主轴，并以 Asymmetric Gradient Flow 只更新编辑层参数而 detach 原始样本。在 MMEdit 基准（E-VQA/E-IC）上作为即插即用模块接入 WISE 与 MEND，Generality 显著提升（如 BLIP2 E-VQA 84.80→93.97），Reliability 与 Locality 基本保持，顺序编辑抗干扰能力明显增强，开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在式多模态知识编辑虽然具备高可靠性（Rel.）和良好局部性（Loc.），但泛化性（Gen.）不足：编辑无法传播到语义等价的视觉/语言改写变体上。作者指出三大根因——(CH1) 高维多模态空间中缺乏可扩展的显式语义监督，简单的有监督目标无法覆盖语义等价输入所在的弯曲流形；(CH2) 现有方法将样本成对划分为"域内/域外"的二元编辑范围，隐含静态边界，忽略语义相关性的连续性，且参数更新会强化捷径相关、干扰边界判断；(CH3) 参数更新锚定于单个有偏样本，吸收了偶然的视觉模式与语言伪迹（sample-centric anchoring），导致过拟合与泛化边界受限。因此需要一种能在保持可靠性和局部性的同时显式提升泛化性、对分布偏移鲁棒的编辑机制。

Method: 问题形式化：定义"知识单元" K_i 为语义等价多模态输入对集合，K = {K_1..K_n}；鲁棒编辑要求对 K_i 内所有输入输出 y_i* 且在 K\K_i 上保持原预测（定义3.2），以 Rel./Loc./Gen. 三指标评估。方法为三组件：①LAR（Latent Adversarial Robustification）：将视觉与文本编码拼接为联合表示 z=[e_v,e_t]，以交叉熵损失的梯度 g_z=∇_z L_ce 极大化求解 L_p 球内的投影梯度上升扰动 δ*，随机初始化单步近似生成 n 个对抗潜变体（梯度-知识耦合下界 + ‖δ‖≤ε 的受控偏差上界；并用独立预训练语义判别器 D + 二分搜索测定最大语义保持扰动预算 ε* = sup{‖δ‖: D(M(x),M(x+δ)) ≥ τ}）。②RCSL（Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning）：在编辑层 L 提取 ℓ2 归一化隐状态 ĥ_i，构成 H_s 与 Gram 矩阵 G=H_s H_s^T；命题4.2 证明"所有变体表征完全对齐 ⟺ G_ij=1 ⟺ rank(G)=1"，因此以 SVD(H_s)=UΣV^T 的奇异值为无归一化 logits，构造温度缩放 softmax 目标 L_align = -log(exp(σ1/τ)/Σexp(σj/τ))，抑制次级奇异方向迫使表征塌缩到共享语义轴。③Asymmetric Gradient Flow：原始样本 ĥ_0 作为语义锚点从 L_align 中 detach 以保留其计算图供 Rel./Loc. 目标使用，仅对抗变体的梯度流向编辑层参数 θ_L，避免扭曲原始表征。总目标 L_total = L_rel + L_loc + β·L_align。超参：ε=1e-3、n=4、τ=4、β=10；Adam，单卡 48GB vGPU，EasyEdit 框架，每实验 5 个随机种子取平均。

Result: 1) 单次编辑：ASAM_WISE 在 BLIP2 上 E-VQA Gen 84.80→93.97、E-IC 82.54→86.22，Rel. 保持 99.00/100.0，T-Loc./M-Loc. 同步上升；ASAM_MEND E-VQA Gen 94.17→94.65、T-Loc. 92.35→93.80，E-IC Gen 79.16→87.64。在 MiniGPT-4 上同样在所有指标保持或提升（WISE E-VQA Gen 98.86→99.00，E-IC 89.80→88.49 略降但 Loc. 提升）。2) 顺序编辑：n=100 时 E-VQA Gen 由 34.90→50.90，Loc. 同步维持/提升（E-IC Gen 56.30→67.99），显示更强抗编辑间干扰与抗灾难漂移能力。3) 跨骨干验证：LLaVA（Gen 64.00→64.67，T-Loc. 53.33→54.29）、Qwen2VL（Gen 68.80→69.97，Rel. 87.15→87.99），增益幅度较小但方向一致，说明方法具模型/任务无关性。4) 消融：SimCSE 改写仅带来边际提升，而 LAR 变体带来主要增益（E-VQA 92.00→93.97，E-IC 84.93→86.22）；余弦对齐与 ℓ2 范数正则均损害泛化（87.00/87.67）与局部性，RCSL 最优（93.97/86.22），验证低秩对齐假设。5) 分析：t-SNE 显示 ε≈1e-3 为表征由环形结构转向主方向集中的临界点，与超参敏感性的 Gen 峰值一致；隐状态相似度热图呈对角优势与全局非对角相似度升高，符合低秩子空间假设；扰动案例中 WISE 10 例中 3 例丢失目标概念，ASAM 全部保持。6) 开销：BLIP2 显存 10688→10774MB（+0.8%）、耗时 +5%；MiniGPT-4 显存 18838→18950MB、耗时 595.73s→790.78s。

Conclusion: ASAM 通过"隐空间对抗变体生成 + 编辑层低秩子空间对齐 + 非对称梯度流"的紧耦合设计，把编辑从实例级记忆转为抽象语义概念的内化，在不牺牲可靠性与局部性的前提下显著提升多模态编辑的泛化性与抗分布偏移鲁棒性，并可作为即插即用模块增强记忆式（WISE）与梯度式（MEND）编辑器，在顺序编辑场景下抑制误差累积。局限：未考虑编辑带来的公平性、偏见等下游伦理影响；核心增益在部分骨干上幅度有限；未来将扩展到视频-语言模型与具身智能体等更复杂动态环境。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.

</details>


### [104] [SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23898)
*Jianshu Zhang,Yijiang Li,Huifeixin Chen,Haoran Lu,Letian Xue,Bingyang Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过SpaceNum框架系统评估VLMs的空间数值理解能力，发现当前模型在动态转换和静态布局任务中均表现不佳，性能接近随机猜测，主要依赖浅层空间线索而非真正的数值-空间映射。通过微调可部分改善但迁移性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在具身环境中被广泛部署，需产生数值输出（如动作幅度、空间坐标），但这些数值输出是否真正基于空间感知尚不清晰。

Method: 提出SpaceNum统一框架，包含两个互补设置：动态转换（空间探索）和静态布局（空间推理）；设计Num2Space和Space2Num双向任务评估VLMs映射能力；并进行误差分析、推理追踪分析和对照干预实验。

Result: 当前VLMs在空间数值理解上存在严重不足：性能接近随机猜测；过度依赖浅层空间线索；难以构建稳定的坐标感知表示；无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化空间布局。

Conclusion: 显式推理仅带来边际收益，而微调可以部分改善空间数值理解能力，并可迁移到外部空间推理基准测试。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [From Raw Experience to Skill Consumption: A Systematic Study of Model-Generated Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23899)
*Zisu Huang,Jingwen Xu,Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Qihao Yang,Muzhao Tian,Xiaohua Wang,Changze Lv,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Xue Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对「模型生成的领域级 Agent 技能」做了首个覆盖完整技能生命周期（经验生成—技能抽取—技能消费）的系统性实证研究。作者在五个智能体任务领域（具身规划、办公软件、软件工程、网页搜索、工具调用）上构建「效用导向（utility-grounded）」评测框架：以目标智能体自身执行轨迹为经验池，用统一最小化抽取框架蒸馏出单一领域技能，再回灌同一模型在留出测试集上评测相对无技能基线的增益。为区分抽取者与消费者的角色，作者提出 Extraction Efficacy (EE) 与 Target Evolvability (TE) 两个指标。核心发现：模型生成技能平均有效，但存在显著的负迁移（negative transfer）；不同抽取器与目标模型表现高度不统一，一个模型可能是强抽取器却是弱消费者，反之亦然；技能效用与模型规模、基线任务能力均无明显关联。作者进一步逐阶段解构影响因素，并将经验转化为一条「元技能（meta-skill）」，用以指导技能抽取对准与真实效用相关的特征，从而跨域一致提升技能质量并大幅减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent 技能研究呈两极分化：一类方法（如 Trace2Skill、AutoRefine、PRAXIS、MemP、ProcMem、CoEvoSkills）不断提出新的轨迹到技能抽取技术，但各自在自己的设定下评测，缺少贯穿全生命周期的系统性理解；另一类基准（如 SkillsBench、SWE-Skills-Bench、Skills-in-the-Wild）只测「技能消费」阶段，抽取环节在闭环之外，SkillsBench 使用人工撰写技能，Skills-in-the-Wild 直接取自公开技能库；最接近的 SkillCraft 又把技能限定为原子工具的可执行函数组合，且任务本身为适配这种组合而设计，难以推广到更广泛的领域。因此，领域级、模型生成的技能究竟是否有效、何时有效、成败动因为何，仍是黑箱。本文的动机正是要弥合这一缺口，把「经验启发式」的实践推向「效用导向」的纪律化研究，并最终反哺抽取方法本身。

Method: 研究框架：将技能生命周期形式化为三阶段。设目标模型 M（既生成经验又消费技能）与可不同的抽取器 E。阶段一经验生成：M 在领域 D 的训练划分 Q^train 上执行任务，产生含成功与失败轨迹的经验池 T_{M,D}={(task_i, trajectory_i, outcome_i)}。阶段二技能抽取：E 用统一最小化抽取框架将经验池蒸馏为固定 schema 与预算约束下的技能集 S_{E,M,D}。该框架仅保留 Trace2Skill 的两层结构：逐轨迹分析（每条轨迹独立并行抽取至多 K 个成功模式与失败模式）与层次化归并（以可配置组大小 G 做树形归并，去重、泛化、调和重叠直至单一模式集），最后通过支持技能创建/更新/删除的结构化工具调用生成技能集；刻意不含领域启发式、过滤规则、验证器、子智能体群或冲突消解，以确保差异归因于抽取器能力而非流水线工程。技能 schema 遵循 Agent Skills 开放标准（name、description、body 等字段）。阶段三技能消费：同一 M 加载技能后在留出测试划分 Q^test 上评测，以相对无技能基线的性能变化作为技能效用的代理，对应部署真实的「抽取器辅助单步演化」设定：固定 M、只变 E，从而受控比较不同抽取程序把经验转化为下游增益的能力。实验在五个领域（具身规划、生产力软件、软件工程、网页搜索、工具调用）上实例化，系统变化抽取器与目标模型，并提出两个解耦指标：Extraction Efficacy (EE)（固定抽取器跨目标产出有用技能的可靠性）与 Target Evolvability (TE)（固定目标从不同抽取器抽取的技能中获益的程度）。RQ1 考察技能是否可靠有效；RQ2 逐阶段分析（经验组成如何塑造技能质量、何种性质刻画有用技能、同一技能如何跨消费者迁移）其效用驱动因素；RQ3 将发现转化为对抽取的即插即用改进——一条引导抽取朝向与实际效用相关特征的元技能。

Result: 1) 模型生成的领域级技能平均而言对下游智能体有益，但存在不可忽视的负迁移：技能有时反而拉低下游表现，说明「经验自动蒸馏成技能」并非单调增益。2) 抽取器与目标模型的行为都不一致（不均匀）：不存在普遍最优的抽取器或消费者，同一模型可能是强抽取器却是弱消费者，反之亦然，即「会造技能」与「会用技能」是两种可分离能力。3) 技能效用与模型规模无关，也与目标模型的基线任务强度无关——更大的模型或更强的基线并不自动带来更好的技能利用收益。4) 逐阶段解构表明：经验池的组成（成功/失败轨迹的构成与质量）显著影响技能质量；有用技能具有可刻画的结构性性质；同一技能在不同消费者之间的迁移效果差异明显，负迁移主要源自技能内容与消费者既有行为/失效模式的不匹配。5) 基于这些规律提出的元技能（meta-skill）可作为抽取阶段的即插即用增强，在多个领域上一致提升技能质量，并实质性地降低负迁移发生率。

Conclusion: 本文把 Agent 技能研究从「经验驱动的启发式实践」推进为「效用导向的系统性学科」：确立经验生成—技能抽取—技能消费三阶段的全生命周期分析范式，用 EE 与 TE 指标分离抽取与消费两侧的角色能力，并实证否定「更大模型 / 更强基线 → 更好技能效用」的朴素预期，揭示非平凡的负迁移是当前抽取方法的核心瓶颈。其最大贡献在于闭环：实证发现被转化为可落地的抽取准则（元技能），并验证其跨域一致改善技能质量与减少负迁移。局限方面，抽取框架被刻意最小化（无子智能体群、无冲突消解、无验证器），结论主要针对「单技能、领域级、单步演化」协议，作者以该协议模拟部署真实场景；技能 schema 受 Agent Skills 开放标准与固定预算约束，且仅覆盖五个领域。更广的启示是：技能工程的评估必须以实际下游效用为准绳，抽取方法的设计也应以「消费者实际能吸收什么」为优化目标，而非仅追求抽取产物的形式完备与文本质量；同时，技能库在不同模型与领域间扩散时，需要显式的负迁移检测与筛选机制。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly improve by reusing \emph{skills} -- structured procedural artifacts distilled from past experience. In particular, \emph{domain-level} and \emph{model-generated} skills are especially promising. They offer fast adaptation within a domain by encoding domain-specific recurring procedures, and they scale beyond labor-intensive hand-crafting. However, while extraction methods continue to proliferate, understanding remains limited, with no comprehensive study spanning the full skill lifecycle -- \textbf{experience generation}, \textbf{skill extraction}, and \textbf{skill consumption} -- to ask whether such skills actually work, when they work, and what makes them succeed or fail. To close this gap, we build a utility-grounded evaluation framework that provides systematic experimental results across extractors and target agents, covering five diverse agentic task domains. We find that model-generated skills are beneficial on average but exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and that neither extractors nor targets behave uniformly. A model can be a strong extractor yet a weak consumer, or vice versa, with skill utility independent of model scale or baseline task strength. To explain these patterns, we then dissect each lifecycle stage in depth, analyzing how experience composition shapes skill quality, what properties characterize useful skills, and how the same skill transfers across different consumers. Finally, we translate these findings into a concrete \emph{meta-skill} that guides skill extraction toward the features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality across domains and substantially reduces negative transfer.

</details>


### [106] [SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23904)
*Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Weiquan Huang,Qihao Yang,Ziwei Zhou,Zisu Huang,Yan Li,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Yuqing Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xue Yang,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOpt提出了一种系统化的文本空间优化器，用于训练AI智能体的技能。与之前的手工设计或松散的自修订方法不同，SkillOpt将技能作为冻结智能体的外部状态进行训练，采用类似深度学习优化器的规范化方法。在6个基准测试、7个目标模型和3种执行环境中，SkillOpt在全部52个评估单元中达到最佳或持平性能，相比基线平均提升约20个百分点以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current agent skills are either hand-crafted, one-shot generated, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision. None of these approaches behaves like a deep-learning optimizer, and none reliably improves over the starting point under feedback. There is a need for a disciplined, reproducible skill training paradigm similar to weight-space optimization.

Method: SkillOpt uses a separate optimizer model to transform scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document. Key mechanisms include: 1) textual learning-rate budget to control edit scope, 2) rejected-edit buffer to prevent regression, and 3) epoch-wise slow/meta update for stability. Critically, it adds zero inference-time model calls at deployment since the agent remains frozen.

Result: Across 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells, SkillOpt achieves best or tied performance on all of them, outperforming competitors including human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill. On GPT-5.5, it improves average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, +24.8 in Codex agentic loop, and +19.1 in Claude Code. Optimized skills also transfer across model scales, execution environments, and to related benchmarks.

Conclusion: SkillOpt is the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills. It establishes a new paradigm where skills are trained with the same reproducibility discipline as weight-space optimization, achieving state-of-the-art results across 52 evaluated cells. The approach demonstrates strong transferability, with optimized skills retaining value across model scales, execution environments, and related benchmarks. This work opens up a new research direction for systematic skill optimization in LLM agents.

Abstract: Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization.

</details>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [LFRAG: Layout-oriented Fine-grained Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Multimodal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22829)
*Yifan Zhu,Yu Mi,Yue Lu,Yanchu Guan,Zhixuan Chu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LFRAG通过布局分割和语义-布局融合编码器，将多模态RAG从页面级推进到块级检索，配合后期交互检索机制，显著提升了检索精度和生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Multimodal RAG系统主要依赖粗粒度的页面级检索，无法有效捕捉视觉富文档中细粒度的语义和布局结构，导致检索精度下降并引入冗余上下文。

Method: 提出LFRAG框架，实现从页面级到块级的检索升级。核心包括：1) 布局分割构建语义连贯的细粒度检索单元；2) 设计语义-布局融合编码器，通过交叉注意力整合局部语义与全局上下文；3) 采用块级后期交互检索实现精确的查询-内容对齐。

Result: 在LFDocQA基准上，LFRAG在检索任务上达到最优性能，答案准确率提升7.20%，生成任务token消耗降低73.07%。

Conclusion: LFRAG是面向视觉富文本的高精度、高效率的多模态RAG框架，通过块级检索实现了精确的查询-内容对齐，有效减少了生成任务中的token消耗。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as an effective paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, existing multimodal RAG systems predominantly rely on coarse-grained page-level retrieval, which fails to capture fine-grained semantic and layout structures in visually rich documents, thereby compromising retrieval accuracy and leading to redundant context in downstream tasks. To address these issues, we propose Layout-oriented Fine-grained Retrieval-Augmented Generation (LFRAG), a novel framework that advances multimodal RAG from page-level to block-level retrieval. We perform layout segmentation to construct semantically coherent fine-grained retrieval units and design a semantic-layout fusion encoder that integrates local semantics with global context via cross-attention. With block-level late interaction retrieval, LFRAG enables precise query-content alignment and reduces irrelevant content for downstream generation. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct LFDocQA, a large-scale benchmark with block-level annotations spanning diverse document types, designed to assess both multimodal document retrieval and question answering with greater granularity than existing datasets. Extensive experiments on LFDocQA demonstrate that LFRAG achieves state-of-the-art performance on retrieval tasks, outperforms the best baseline by 7.20% in answer accuracy, and reduces token consumption by 73.07% in generation tasks, confirming LFRAG as an accurate and efficient framework for multimodal RAG over visually rich documents. Our code and datasets will be released soon.

</details>


### [108] [RAG4Outcome: A Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Framework for Prognostic Prediction in Chronic Osteomyelitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22833)
*Daqian Shi,Pei Han,Jishizhan Chen,Yang Wang,Xiaolei Diao,Xianyou Zheng,Pengfei Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG4Outcome，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）技术的慢性骨髓炎预后预测框架。该框架通过整合PET-CT影像、手术记录和随访笔记等多模态临床数据，利用领域特定检索语料库和专家引导的提示工程，实现可解释且证据支撑的预后评估。初步实验验证了该方法的有效性和临床适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性骨髓炎预后预测面临三大核心挑战：
1. 疾病特性：高复发率和复杂的术后恢复轨迹对预后评估提出严峻挑战
2. 评估局限：传统手动评分系统缺乏可扩展性、效率低下且一致性不足
3. 数据异构性：临床数据的多模态、异构性特征使得现有的多模态学习方法面临挑战，这些方法通常需要对齐的输入和大规模的标注数据集

Method: 提出RAG4Outcome检索增强生成框架，采用以下关键技术：
1. 统一预测管道：整合PET-CT影像报告、结构化手术/诊断记录、非结构化随访笔记等多模态临床数据
2. 领域特定检索语料库：构建针对慢性骨髓炎的专业知识库，支持精准检索
3. 专家引导提示工程：结合临床专家知识设计提示模板，引导生成过程
4. 证据锚定机制：确保预测结果基于检索到的临床证据，增强可解释性

Result: 在真实世界病例上的初步实验结果表明：
1. RAG4Outcome展现出良好的预测有效性
2. 预测结果与临床实际高度对齐
3. 框架能够提供可解释的、基于证据的预后评估

Conclusion: RAG4Outcome框架展现了应用于AI辅助感染管理和术后决策支持的潜力，能够实现更具可解释性、证据支撑性和临床可靠性的预后评估。

主要创新点：
- 首个将检索增强生成技术应用于慢性骨髓炎预后预测的框架
- 有效整合异构多模态临床数据，无需大规模标注数据集
- 通过专家引导的提示设计增强预测的可解释性和临床适用性

Abstract: Chronic osteomyelitis presents substantial prognostic challenges due to its high recurrence risk and complex postoperative recovery trajectories. Traditional assessment often relies on manual scoring systems, which limit scalability, efficiency, and consistency in clinical practice. Furthermore, the heterogeneous nature of clinical data poses challenges for current multimodal learning approaches that require aligned inputs and large annotated datasets. In this work, we propose RAG4Outcome, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for prognostic prediction in chronic osteomyelitis. Our method integrates multimodal clinical data, including PET-CT imaging reports, structured surgical and diagnostic records, and unstructured follow-up notes, into a unified prediction pipeline. By combining a domain-specific retrieval corpus with expert-guided prompting, the framework enables more interpretable, evidence-grounded, and clinically reliable prognosis. Preliminary results on real-world cases demonstrate promising effectiveness and clinical alignment, highlighting the potential of RAG4Outcome for AI-assisted infection management and postoperative decision support.

</details>


### [109] [AI-Friendly LaTeX: Using LaTeX Code as a Knowledge Source for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22923)
*Tom Verhoeff*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 面向数学/技术类教材与讲义的 RAG，直接使用编译后的 PDF 通常会丢失结构、标签、代码格式与数学符号。本文提出一条以 LaTeX 源码为知识源的预处理流水线：把 .tex 源码、编译产生的 .aux 辅助文件与可选的作者语义标注，转换为可读 Markdown（.rag.md）、标签表（labels.json）与分块文件（chunks.jsonl），核心思想是"交叉引用解析 + 宏分类而非盲目展开 + 图形语义描述 + 术语表富化"，使嵌入文本（embedding_text）在语义上比展示文本（markdown）更丰富，从而适配向量检索而非排版复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 回答教材、讲义、编程练习类问题（如课程助教、研究者的论文助手）需要显式知识源作为依据，RAG 是主流方案。问题在于知识抽取层：PDF 是最终视觉产物而非语义来源，会遮蔽章节结构、破坏代码格式、丢失 label、把段落截断跨页、把数学符号变成难以解析的文本；LaTeX 源码则保留了 sectioning 命令、label、环境、宏名与模块化文件结构。但源码并非天然"AI 友好"：\ref/\eqref/\pageref 需解析、自定义宏需解释、练习与例题需识别、作者语义元数据需补充；而现有工具（LaTeXML、Pandoc）目标是忠实复现排版结果，均不以"面向 RAG 的分块、面包屑 heading_path、可读交叉引用与语义富化嵌入文本"为目标。因此需要一个面向 RAG 的 LaTeX 预处理方法。

Method: 输入：主 .tex、编译生成的 .aux、经 \input/\include 与 aux 中 \@input 递归发现的附属文件、可选 YAML 标注。输出：.rag.md、labels.json、chunks.jsonl（每行一个 chunk，字段含 id、kind、heading_path、source_file、start_line/end_line、labels、markdown、embedding_text、metadata）。流水线 11 步：读源码→递归包含→解析 \newlabel 建标签表→载入 YAML→LaTeX 转 Markdown→解析 \ref/\eqref/\pageref→按注册表解释宏→保留语义记号并富化嵌入文本→按章节/定义/例题/练习分块→写出三种输出。关键机制：(1) 宏分四类——排版型（可安全展开）、语义记号型（如 \splt 不展开而附加"Notation note"说明）、结构型（如 \Exercise 转成带 exercise_id 的标题与 metadata）、未知型（保留并告警）；核心原则是"展示给学生的表示与用于检索嵌入的表示不必相同"。(2) 图形被视为独立知识对象，抽取 label、caption、上下文、图片文件或 TikZ 源码、页码与作者语义描述；引入 ai-annotation 包提供的 no-op 宏 \AIDescription、\AIDeclareNotation、\AINote、\AIChunkBreak（draft 选项下渲染为边注并记入 .log）以便图注之外的文本代理。(3) 标注两条互补路径：源码内声明宏（位置耦合紧、适合新文档）与外部 YAML（不改 .tex、便于审计与第三方标注）；YAML 在冲突时优先，并含 macros、pedagogy.visibility、suppress_macros 三个顶层键；带标签的 TeX 注释被证明不可行，因为注释在源加载阶段即被剥离。(4) 自动生成 kind="glossary" 术语表 chunk，并在含已注册宏的 chunk 中追加术语说明与别名。(5) 人机分工：YAML 用于人类撰写语义意图（可注释、多行），JSON/JSONL 用于机器生成与交换。(6) 结构性环境（exercise/solution/definition/theorem/lemma/proof/example/remark）单独成块并带 visibility 字段，供检索时过滤。cleveref 通过 aux 中 @cref 解析，遗留三处缺口（\crefrange、逗号列表、\crefname 自定义名词）；分块粒度提供 --min-heading-level、\AIChunkBreak 与 token 预算兜底。实现为开源 Python 包 latex-rag-preprocessor（PyPI），配 ChromaDB 摄取/检索脚本。

Result: 给出可运行的完整流水线与三类输出实例：Section~\ref 解析为可读的 "Section 4.3"、"page 71"；figure chunk 含 caption、image_file、语义描述与 section/page 元数据；exercise 与 solution 分为 kind=exercise/solution 并附 visibility=student/teacher_only；chunk 元数据支持回溯到 source_file、start/end_line、section、title、page_start/page_end 以便答案引用原文位置。附带 ChromaDB 摄取脚本（本地 sentence-transformer，幂等 upsert；--openai 可切换 text-embedding-3-small）、检索脚本与位置浏览模式（按 heading_path 子串过滤，如 "Chapter 4 > Stability"），可由 Claude Code 通过 Bash 工具直接调用，无需占用上下文窗口。局限与未来工作已明确：不做完整 TeX 解释、不执行条件分支、不保证不泄露解答的行为（需提示设计、检索过滤、答案校验、访问控制与对抗性评测等额外层）；版权与许可问题；cleveref 三项待补；缺少"不可拆分段落/定理与证明同块"的机制；多模态图像嵌入尚未支持；目前仅在单一伴随文档上做演示，尚未在真实课程教材与真实学生查询上评估检索精度、召回率与下游答案质量。

Conclusion: LaTeX 源码因包含 PDF 中常缺失的结构与作者意图，是 AI 系统的高价值知识源，但必须被谨慎预处理：交叉引用应被解析为人类可读文本，宏应被分类处理而非一律展开，仅靠排版无法表达的概念需补充语义标注。管道以 YAML 承载人工撰写的语义意图、以 JSON/JSONL 承载机器生成的分块，产出既保留人类可读记号、又附带检索所需含义的检索单元；与 LaTeXML、Pandoc 等面向排版复现的工具不同，其目标是语义富化、可检索的 chunk。论文据此提出"AI-friendly LaTeX"写作约定（有意义 label、语义宏、区分排版与语义宏、结构化标记练习/解答/定义、标注可见性、维护术语表、不依赖纯视觉记号、可识别环境中的代码示例），使源码在保持可出版质量的同时，把语义与教学结构暴露给 AI 系统。

Abstract: Large language models can answer questions about textbooks, lecture notes, and programming exercises more reliably when their answers are grounded in an explicit knowledge source. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a common approach: relevant fragments of a document are retrieved and inserted into the model context before answering. For mathematical and technical material, the original LaTeX source can be a better starting point than a PDF, because it contains structural information, labels, sectioning commands, macros, and authorial intent that are often lost or distorted in PDF extraction. However, LaTeX source is not automatically AI-friendly. Cross-references must be resolved, custom macros must be interpreted, exercises and examples must be identified, and author-supplied semantic metadata may be needed. This article describes a focused preprocessing approach for turning LaTeX source, together with its compiled auxiliary files and optional author annotations, into Markdown and JSONL chunks suitable for indexing in a vector database.

</details>


### [110] [From Head to Tail: Asymmetric Knowledge Transfer in Long-tail Recommendation with Generative Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23310)
*Chenyi Yan,Ruocong Tang,Xing Fang,Yang Huang,He Guo,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AKT-Rec proposes using LLM-generated semantic IDs with asymmetric knowledge transfer to address long-tail recommendation. It employs multimodal LLMs and RQ-VAE for semantic ID generation, with cluster-guided adaptive embedding directing knowledge flow from head to tail items, and hierarchical feature aggregation for adaptive prediction across activity levels. Validated on industrial-scale data and production deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-tail recommendation in e-commerce suffers from severe data imbalance. Existing methods fail to effectively combine multimodal content features with collaborative signals, and crucially ignore the asymmetric nature of knowledge transfer between head and tail IDs—where noisy tail signals can harm head ID representation learning.

Method: The framework uses Multimodal LLMs with supervised fine-tuning to align content representations with collaborative information, then discretizes these into semantic IDs via Residual-Quantized VAE (RQ-VAE). Two core components: (1) Cluster-Guided Adaptive Embedding decomposes ID representations into cluster-level and individual embeddings, using asymmetric contrastive learning and activity-aware gating to direct knowledge transfer from head to tail; (2) Hierarchical Feature Aggregation builds parallel feature views and adaptively fuses them for varying activity levels.

Result: Offline: 0.35% AUC improvement and 1.53% GAUC improvement over competitive baselines. Online A/B testing: 2.76% CTR increase and 3.47% GMV increase on Alibaba Tmall platform.

Conclusion: AKT-Rec effectively addresses long-tail recommendation by leveraging LLM-generated semantic IDs and asymmetric knowledge transfer. The framework demonstrates significant improvements in both offline metrics (0.35% AUC, 1.53% GAUC) and online business metrics (2.76% CTR, 3.47% GMV) on Alibaba Tmall platform, validating its practical utility in production environments.

Abstract: Long-tail recommendation in real-world e-commerce platforms remains challenging due to severe data imbalance. Existing methods often struggle to combine content-based multimodal features with collaborative signals. Many of these methods also ignore an important asymmetry in knowledge transfer between head and tail IDs: noisy signals from tail IDs can hurt representation learning for head IDs. This paper presents AKT-Rec, a framework for Asymmetric Knowledge Transfer in long-tail Recommendation that uses LLM-generated semantic IDs. AKT-Rec uses Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) with supervised fine-tuning to align content representations with collaborative information for both items and users, producing semantic representations. It then discretizes these representations into semantic IDs with a Residual-Quantized VAE (RQ-VAE), which yields semantic clusters of similar entities. AKT-Rec has two main components: (1) Cluster-Guided Adaptive Embedding, which decomposes each ID representation into a cluster-level embedding that captures shared semantics and an individual embedding. Through an asymmetric contrastive objective and an activity-aware gating mechanism, this module directs knowledge transfer from head to tail IDs. (2) Hierarchical Feature Aggregation, which builds parallel feature views and adaptively fuses them to optimize predictions for samples with varying activity levels. Extensive experiments on a large-scale industrial dataset and online A/B testing on the Alibaba Tmall platform demonstrate the effectiveness of AKT-Rec. AKT-Rec improves offline performance by 0.35% in AUC and 1.53% in GAUC, outperforming several competitive baselines. In online A/B testing, AKT-Rec achieves a 2.76% increase in CTR and a 3.47% increase in GMV, validating its utility in real-world production environments.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Generalizable and Efficient Large-Scale Generative Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23312)
*Qiuling Xu,Ko-Jen Hsiao,Moumita Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式推荐模型从2M扩展到1B参数的工业实践经验。研究发现任务依赖的缩放行为，通过偏移缩放定律诊断模型规模需求，并针对性提出多token预测、采样softmax、语义item towers等生产级解决方案，最终在100万用户为期一周的生产级shadow评估中验证了1B模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练收益在生产环境中无法自动转化为下游应用改进，存在任务上限、重复训练成本、服务延迟和物品新鲜度等挑战。

Method: 采用偏移缩放定律（offset scaling-law）拟合作为诊断工具；提出多token预测对齐服务延迟、采样softmax与投影解码头提升训练效率、语义item towers结合协同嵌入掩码应对冷启动问题。

Result: 1B参数 backbone 模型在所有下游任务上均取得比2M baseline 更高的MRR；观察到任务相关的缩放行为——部分任务存在性能上限，而其他任务持续受益于更大规模。

Conclusion: 模型规模应被视为生产迁移问题的一个组成部分，需与其他因素（任务上限、解码成本、服务延迟对齐、物品泛化）综合考量。

Abstract: Generative recommendation models can model user behavior as sequences of events and provide a shared backbone for multiple recommendation tasks. In production, however, pre-training gains do not automatically translate into downstream application improvements: task headroom, repeated-training cost, serving latency, and item freshness all affect transfer. We describe our experience scaling a generative recommender from 2M to 1B backbone parameters, excluding embedding and decoding layers, in a production-scale title recommendation setting. Across multiple downstream tasks, we observe task-dependent scaling behavior: some tasks approach an empirical ceiling within the observed scale range, while others continue to benefit from additional capacity. This motivates using offset scaling-law fits as a diagnostic for where additional model scale may be more or less useful.
  We then study production constraints that arise when applying the model in practice. Frequent retraining over trillions of behavior tokens makes training and decoding efficiency important; cached serving can make the immediate next-token target stale; and newly launched titles may need to be scored from semantic metadata before collaborative ID embeddings are reliable. We address these issues with multi-token prediction for serving-latency alignment, sampled softmax and a projected decoding head for efficient repeated training, and semantic item towers with collaborative-embedding masking for cold-start adaptation. In a one-week production-shadow evaluation over 1M users, the 1B-backbone model achieves higher MRR than the 2M-backbone baseline across all reported tasks. Overall, the results support treating model scale as one component of a production transfer problem, alongside task headroom, decoding cost, serving-latency alignment, and item generalization.

</details>


### [112] [TPMM-DPO: Trajectory-aware Preference-guided Model Merging for Iterative Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23398)
*Lingling Fu,Yongfu Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对迭代DPO中噪声累积导致性能退化的问题，提出TPMM-DPO方法，通过轨迹感知的偏好引导模型融合，利用可学习权重自适应整合历史策略模型，构建更稳健的参考模型，实验证明该方法在域内外评估中均显著优于传统迭代DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在迭代DPO中，前一轮迭代的策略模型被重复用作后续轮次的参考模型时，偏好数据中的噪声和参考模型的误差会随时间累积，导致后期过度优化、性能波动和泛化能力下降。

Method: 提出TPMM-DPO方法，将迭代DPO过程中产生的策略模型序列视为优化轨迹，利用学习到的融合权重自适应整合多个历史模型，构建更平滑、更鲁棒的参考模型，从而替代传统方法中仅依赖单一前序模型的参考模型。

Result: 在域内和域外评估中，TPMM-DPO均实现了更高的胜率和奖励分数，且有效缓解了迭代DPO中后期阶段的性能退化问题。

Conclusion: TPMM-DPO通过可学习权重的模型融合策略，有效缓解了传统迭代DPO中由于偏好噪声累积导致的性能退化问题，在域内和域外评估中均取得了更高的生成质量和奖励分数，证明了轨迹感知融合方法相比简单平均融合的优越性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has been widely adopted for large language model alignment due to its simple training procedure and lack of an explicit reward model. However, in iterative DPO, when the policy model from the previous iteration is repeatedly used as the reference model for subsequent rounds, noise in preference data and errors in the reference model accumulate over time. This accumulation can lead to late-stage over-optimization, performance fluctuations, and degraded generalization.
  To address these issues, we propose TPMM-DPO, a trajectory-aware preference-guided model merging method. The method treats the sequence of policy models generated during iterative DPO as an optimization trajectory and adaptively integrates them using learned fusion weights, thereby constructing a smoother and more robust reference model. In contrast to conventional iterative DPO, which relies solely on a single previous model, TPMM-DPO effectively mitigates error accumulation induced by noisy preferences and improves training stability.
  Experimental results show that standard iterative DPO often suffers from performance degradation in the middle and later stages of training, whereas TPMM-DPO consistently improves generation quality and achieves higher win rates and reward scores on both in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations. Further ablation studies and robustness analyses demonstrate that, compared with simple averaging, learnable-weight fusion more effectively alleviates late-stage performance degradation caused by noisy preferences.

</details>


### [113] [HARNESS-LM: A Three-Phase Training Recipe for Harnessing SLMs in Sponsored Search Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23572)
*Vipul Gupta,Shikhar Mohan,Lakshya Kumar,Pranjal Chitale,Nikit Begwani,Amit Singh,Manik Varma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HARNESS-LM (HLM) 是一个三阶段训练框架，旨在将大型检索模型的能力蒸馏到紧凑模型中，以解决赞助搜索中检索质量与生产延迟之间的平衡问题。在必应广告真实评估基准上，HLM恢复了超过98%的参考检索器精度，同时在A100 GPU上实现了27倍更低的延迟和20倍更高的吞吐量，在线A/B测试显示收入提升1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型检索模型（如Qwen3-Embedding-4B/8B）在公开基准测试上表现优异，但在高吞吐量、低延迟敏感的生产环境中部署不切实际。需要一种方法在保持检索质量的同时大幅降低延迟和计算成本。

Method: 三阶段训练框架：(1) 微调十亿参数规模的SLM构建高性能"教师"检索器；(2) 通过L2目标对齐查询表示，将知识蒸馏到600M参数以下的"学生"编码器；(3) 应用最终对比微调阶段优化学生模型的检索性能。

Result: 在真实世界必应广告评估基准上，HLM在多种设置下恢复了超过98%的参考检索器精度；在NVIDIA A100 GPU上实现最高27倍更低的在线查询编码延迟和20倍更高的吞吐量；在线A/B测试显示收入+1%、展示量+0.6%、点击量+0.4%的提升。

Conclusion: HLM方法在真实赞助搜索环境中展现了实用性，190M参数的学生模型能够替代生产中运行的大型集成检索器，在显著降低计算成本的同时提升业务指标。

Abstract: In the competitive landscape of sponsored search, balancing retrieval quality with production latency is a critical challenge. While large retrieval models based on Small Language Models (SLMs) such as Qwen3-Embedding-4B/8B set strong upper bounds on public benchmarks, their deployment in high-throughput, latency-sensitive environments remains impractical. In this paper, we present HARNESS-LM (HLM), a three-phase training framework for transferring the capabilities of large-scale retrievers into compact, cost-efficient models. The approach comprises: (1) training a high-performance reference ("teacher") retriever by fine-tuning a billion-parameter-scale SLM; (2) aligning query representations via an L2 objective to distill knowledge into a sub-600M parameter student encoder; and (3) applying a final contrastive refinement stage to optimize the student for retrieval performance. We also present a comprehensive empirical study of key design choices, including alignment objectives, embedding dimensionality, model scale, architecture, and optimization strategies, to identify configurations that are most effective in production settings. On a real-world Bing Ads evaluation benchmark, HLM recovers over 98% of the reference retriever's precision across multiple settings, while delivering up to 27x lower online query-encoder latency and 20x higher throughput on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Online A/B testing on Bing Ads further shows a +1% Revenue, +0.6% Impression, and +0.4% Click uplift over the current ensemble of retrievers running in production with the deployed 190M parameter model, clearly highlighting the practical efficacy of the HLM recipe in a real-world sponsored search setting.

</details>


### [114] [Synthetic Sources?: Auditing Generative Search Engine Citations for Evidence of AI-Generated Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23684)
*Mowafak Allaham,Nicholas Diakopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对四款生成式搜索引擎（ChatGPT、Copilot、Gemini、Perplexity）在政治、健康、环境三个公共重要性议题上的引用来源进行审计，使用 712 条真实用户查询，抓取 26,266 个唯一来源 URL，并借助 AI 文本检测器 Pangram 判定其中约 16%（3,056 篇）为 AI 生成内容；同时发现这些引擎的引用分布高度不均衡（Gini=0.68），大量来源集中在长尾域名，且 AI 生成内容多来自长尾与部分政府/社交媒体站点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的生成式搜索引擎（GSE）取代传统搜索成为主要信息获取入口，其"综合式回答+内嵌引用"模式把来源选择权交给了黑箱系统。而网络空间中 AI 生成内容（AI slop）激增，LLM 本身存在偏见、幻觉与人工监督缺失等问题；若 GSE 无法识别并回避引用合成来源，用户可能把低质量、可能虚假的信息误当作权威官方信息消费，在健康、政治等高利害领域造成实际伤害。此外，来源选择机制的不透明还可能被用于品牌自我推广排名、SEO 操纵、知识注入/投毒攻击，从而放大低质量 AI 内容的影响。此前对 GSE 的审计（可验证性、事实性、引用归属、政治偏见等）尚未涉及"被引用来源本身是否为 AI 生成"这一问题，因此本文将其作为第一步的实证审计。

Method: 1) 查询构建：从 Search Arena 数据集（24,069 条，2025.3–5 收集）出发，剔除 Creative Generation/Other 意图，拆分多轮为单轮、去重，得 11,185 条英文查询；用 BERTopic（沿用 Search Arena 开源流程）划分 76 个主题，人工审阅每个主题 5 条样例，识别出政治与健康相关的 8 个主题共 1,325 条，再按"与美国语境相关"人工剔除，保留 432 条（政治 257、健康 175）。环境议题因 Search Arena 缺失，改用 Climate Q&A 数据集（3,425 条），经 langdetect 过滤得 2,668 条英文，随机抽 20%（534 条），再剔除非美区域查询，保留 280 条并保持原类别分布。合计 N=712（政治 175 / 健康 257 / 环境 280）。
2) 引用抓取：通过各引擎 UI（而非 API）提交全部查询，用 Playwright 编写爬虫模拟用户行为（输入、开启联网搜索、监测 DOM 变化至答案稳定、点击展开引用列表），抓取标题/URL/摘要，得 26,266 个唯一来源、7,675 个唯一域名。
3) 正文抓取：使用 newspaper3k 访问 26,266 个 URL，成功提取 19,154 条（72.9%）正文，其余为 PDF/图片/视频或失效链接。
4) 检测器选型：比较 Pangram 与 GPTZero。以 RAID 基准 200 条人写文本（研究摘要、维基、Reddit、新闻各 50）测假阳性，两者均 100% 正确；再用 GPT-5.3/Gemini-3/Claude-Haiku-4.5 基于人写文本前四词生成 200 条 AI 文本，两者均 100% 检出。另用"AI slop"报道涉及的 7 个新闻类站点随机抽取 105 篇，估算野外假阴性率（Pangram 31.4% vs GPTZero ~30.4%），二者一致率 82%。为保守（取下界）最终选用假阴性率较高的 Pangram。
5) 分析：将 19,154 条正文送入 Pangram API，按 Highly Likely AI / Likely AI / Possibly AI / Unlikely AI 四类统计（排除含义模糊的 Possibly AI），并从域名层面用 Gini 指数衡量引用集中度、分析长尾分布，跨提供方与主题交叉比较。

Result: 共获得 2,848 条响应，91.2% 含内嵌引用；8.8%（252 条）无引用，其中健康类占 11.6%、环境 8.2%、政治 5.7%，说明部分回答依赖模型内部知识；有引用的响应平均 9 条引用。ChatGPT 引用最多（平均 14.7 条/响应、10,453 个唯一来源），其后为 Copilot 8.4、Perplexity 7.9、Gemini 7.3；主题层面环境类引用最多（10.9 条）。
引用分布高度集中：总体 Gini=0.68，ChatGPT 最高（0.648），Gemini 0.595、Perplexity 0.563、Copilot 0.492；Top 25 域名仅占全部引用的 23.8%，59.1% 的域名只被引 1 次、16.5% 被引 2 次，呈显著长尾。Top 25 中维基百科占 15.2%，政府网站合计 33.0%，社交媒体（Reddit、YouTube）占 8.8%，学术/科研出版商占 14.6%，新闻媒体代表性很低。
AI 生成来源比例：19,154 条成功抓取内容中，Unlikely AI 82.5%（15,815）、Highly Likely AI 15.2%（2,916）、Possibly AI 1.4%（283）、Likely AI 0.7%（140）；合并后约 16%（3,056）被判定为 AI 生成，构成下界。AI 生成来源极集中于长尾：Top 25 域名仅占 2.9%，97.1% 散布于其余域名；Top 35 域名占 AI 生成来源的 24.7%。
提供方差异：Copilot 最高（1,614 条，占其引用的 27.8%），其后 Gemini 14.7%、Perplexity 9.4%、ChatGPT 7.3%；Copilot 在三个主题上均最高（环境 607、健康 655、政治 352）。域名层面 6,258 个域名中 28.0%（1,754 个）至少含一条 AI 生成来源，其中 66.2% 仅 1 条、17.7% 为 2 条、16.0% 为 3 条以上。高 AI 占比站点包括 iere.org（100%）、biologyinsights.com、climate.sustainability-directory.com、greenpacks.org、maweb.org、allaboutamerica.com 等（多为 100%），以及 factually.co（自述"AI-powered research tool"，100%）、timesofindia.indiatimes.com（45%）、m.economictimes.com（60%）、greenly.earth（53%）；Reddit 61 条、政府域名 pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov 44 条（4.1%）、earth.gov 11 条。作者还抽样 200 篇判定为 AI 的来源，均未发现 AI 使用披露声明。主题层面：环境类 AI 生成来源最多（13.3%），健康 16.3%、政治 11.1%（原文表述与排序存在不一致）。
值得注意的机制性发现：GSE 一方面依赖窄的头部权威源（政府 33%、维基、社媒），另一方面主体是长尾中仅被引 1–2 次的域名，而 AI 生成内容恰恰集中在这条长尾上——形成"来源多样性"与"来源质量可核查性"之间的系统性权衡。

Conclusion: 研究以"算法审计 + 社会技术"视角首次实证确认四家主流生成式搜索引擎确实在引用 AI 生成的合成来源，占比约 16%（作者在讨论中表述为"平均约五分之一"），且这些 AI 内容多来自被引一两次的长尾域名，少数头部域名为纯 AI 农场式站点；健康类回答中有 1/9 完全不带引用，也提示引擎会回退到模型内部知识。作者据此主张：开发者与风险评估方应承担部分责任，建立引用来源的透明化与治理机制（如标注来源类型、识别并可能过滤 AI 生成内容、审计政府/社交媒体/专业出版商站点上的 AI 使用情况），并建议后续研究将结论作为下界报告、或采用多检测器集成，以提升该新兴领域结论的可复现性。作者明确列出局限：仅限英文与美国语境、单轮查询、样本来自 Search Arena 存在地域与主题偏差、27% 非文本来源（PDF/图片/视频）无法分析、检测器评估基准仅覆盖新闻类内容且在线内容普遍缺乏 AI 披露导致真值不可验证。

Abstract: The growing accessibility of Large Language Models via conversational interfaces capable of responding to users' questions by drawing on, synthesizing, and citing information from the web (i.e., Generative Search Engines) has simplified the information-seeking process for users. However, with the proliferation of AI-generated content on the web, it is unclear whether these engines can reliably omit citing synthetic sources (i.e., AI-generated sources). Should these engines be unable to do so, this puts users at risk of harm by treating information from AI-generated sources synthesized in responses of generative search engines as equivalent to information from authoritative or official sources. In a step towards identifying whether AI-generated sources are being cited by these engines, this work presents an audit of four generative search engines (ChatGPT, Copilot, Gemini, Perplexity) using a total of 712 real-world human-generated queries spanning domains of public importance: politics, health, and the environment. Our findings show evidence of AI-generated sources being cited across all four generative search engines (~16% of cited sources) and identifies key source web domains these sources belong to that are frequently cited across these engines and topics. In addition, we observed that generative search engines include a somewhat narrow set of repeatedly cited domains while predominantly surfacing a large number of minimally cited domains in responses to users' queries. These findings contribute to the growing body of work on assessing the risks of generative search engines with the objective of increasing public awareness of their limitations and encouraging appropriate measures to improve information quality and governance of these systems.

</details>


### [115] [TubiFM: Unified Item, Carousel, and Search Ranking for Streaming Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23702)
*Alexandre Salle,Chenglei Niu,Suchismit Mahapatra,Xiaoxiao Chen,Suvash Sedhain,Yaqi Wang,Shervin Shahryari,Saurabh Agrawal,Qiang Chen,Michael Tamir*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TubiFM使用"user story"统一表示用户跨表面历史，基于Llama 3.2 1B构建单一模型处理item ranking、carousel ranking和search，无需任务特定架构。离线超越专家baseline，在线显著提升search和carousel的观看时间，并大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化发现系统为item ranking、carousel ranking和search分别训练独立模型，但实际上这些任务暴露了同一用户旅程中的互补信号（如watches影响carousel和item ranking，search queries揭示意图，watch history帮助解释search意图）。

Method: 提出"user story"序列化表示方法：将用户跨surface历史（包括attributes、sessions、watch events with surface/carousel context、search events）转换为单一token序列。通过交错预训练语言tokens与领域特定event tokens，使异构推荐和搜索任务可表达为prompted next-token prediction。TubiFM是基于Llama 3.2 1B的模型，训练于user stories，可对items、carousels或search results进行ranking，无需任务特定架构。

Result: 离线评估中，单一模型在item、carousel和search ranking上优于专家baseline。在线A/B测试中，search TVT提升+3.9%，carousel TVT提升+0.30%，item ranking TVT为+0.14%（统计中性）。

Conclusion: TubiFM serves on L40S GPUs并降低p99排名延迟从500ms至200ms，证明共享用户故事representation可简化发现系统同时提升性能。

Abstract: Personalized discovery systems often train separate models for item ranking, carousel ranking, and search, even though these tasks expose complementary signals from the same viewer journey: watches shape carousel and item ranking, search queries reveal intent even when they do not lead to a catalog match, and watch history helps interpret search as rewatching, continuation, or new discovery. We introduce the user story, a serialized representation that turns a user's cross-surface history - attributes, sessions, watch events with surface and carousel context, and search events - into a single token sequence. By interleaving pretrained language tokens with domain-specific event tokens, user stories let heterogeneous recommendation and search tasks be expressed as prompted next-token prediction over a shared grammar. TubiFM is one instantiation of this approach: a Llama 3.2 1B-based model trained on user stories and prompted to rank items, carousels, or search results without task-specific architectures. In offline evaluation, this single model outperforms specialist baselines across item, carousel, and search ranking. In online A/B tests, TubiFM significantly improves search total viewing time (TVT) by $+3.9\%$ and carousel TVT by $+0.30\%$. Item ranking is statistically neutral on TVT ($+0.14\%$), but matches a mature production stack; across all three tasks, TubiFM serves on L40S GPUs and reduces p99 ranking latency from 500ms to 200ms. These results show that shared user stories can improve discovery while simplifying ranking systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Expressive Power of Deep Homomorphism Networks over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22852)
*Moritz Schönherr,Balder ten Cate,Maurice Funk,Benny Kimelfeld,Carsten Lutz,Arie Soeteman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: The paper establishes the theoretical expressive power of Deep Homomorphism Networks (DHNs) by relating them to various fragments of first-order logic, including UNFO, counting quantifiers, and ratio quantifiers, thereby illuminating DHN-SQL connections and enabling decidability analysis of static analysis problems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Message-passing GNNs have inherent expressive limitations, necessitating more powerful graph learning architectures; DHNs are particularly suited for relational databases due to connections to SQL and conjunctive queries.

Method: Systematic mapping between DHN architectures (with max/sum/mean aggregations) and formal logic fragments by establishing precise correspondences to UNFO, counting quantifiers, ratio quantifiers, and FO with expressive counting.

Result: Established precise connections: DHNs with max/sum/mean ↔ UNFO and its extensions (counting/ratio quantifiers); sum-aggregation DHNs ↔ unary quantifier alternation fragment of FO; also connected to FO with expressive counting.

Conclusion: Results enable studying decidability of emptiness and subsumption problems for DHNs; experiments validate theoretical differences in expressive power through benchmark tasks.

Abstract: The expressive limitations of message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have motivated a wide range of more powerful graph learning architectures. We advocate Deep Homomorphism Networks (DHNs) as a model particularly well-suited for learning over relational databases, due to their close connection to important fragments of SQL such as conjunctive queries. We study the precise expressive power of DHNs by relating them to various natural fragments and extensions of first-order logic (FO). For DHNs with max, sum, and mean aggregations, we establish connections to the unary negation fragment (UNFO) and to the extensions of UNFO with counting quantifiers and with ratio quantifiers. We further relate sum-aggregation DHNs to the unary quantifier alternation fragment of FO and to an extension of FO with expressive counting. Through the classical correspondence between FO and SQL, these results also illuminate the relation between DHNs and SQL. They also enable us to study the decidability of two fundamental static analysis problems for DHNs, the emptiness problem and the subsumption problem. Finally, we confirm through experiments that the established differences in expressive power are reflected in the performance on suitable prediction tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Measuring Database Unfairness via Dependency Quantification Under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22952)
*Mariia Vologdin,Yuchao Tao,Amir Gilad*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私环境下数据公平性评估困难的问题，提出了一个形式化的不公平度量框架。该框架包含三个互补的度量：基于互信息的方法、基于加权MaxSAT约简的修复方法和top-k元组贡献方法。所有方法均满足差分隐私可计算性需求，并在真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）已成为保护敏感数据的事实标准，但其引入的隐私噪声和受限的数据访问使得评估私有数据集的公平性和可靠性变得困难。现有方法缺乏对DP约束下数据不公平进行量化的正式框架。

Method: 1. 提出满足差分隐私的可计算不公平度量（mutual information-based measure）；2. 通过加权MaxSAT约简近似数据修复度量；3. 设计top-k元组贡献度量以隔离最具影响力的记录；4. 开发隐私保护算法并分析其敏感性、准确性和效率。

Result: 1. 提出了满足 positivity、monotonicity 和 DP computability 三个核心需求的正式不公平度量框架；2. 通过三个互补的度量（互信息、MaxSAT约简、top-k贡献）全面刻画数据不公平；3. 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明所提度量能有效近似非私有对应物、准确量化隐私约束下的偏差，并为数据管理提供洞察。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一个正式的不公平度量框架，包含三个满足差分隐私的度量，通过算法设计在多个真实数据集上验证了有效性，为隐私约束下的数据公平性评估提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) has become the de facto standard for protecting sensitive data, providing strong guarantees that published statistics or models reveal limited information about any individual. However, privacy noise and restricted data access make it increasingly difficult to assess the fairness and reliability of private datasets. In this paper, we propose a formal framework for quantifying data unfairness under DP. We identify three core desiderata for unfairness measures based on previous work: positivity, monotonicity, and DP computability. We further instantiate them through three complementary measures: (1) a mutual information-based measure with a total variation distance proxy suitable for DP, (2) a data repair-based measure approximated via a reduction to weighted MaxSAT, and (3) a top-$k$ tuple contribution measure that isolates the most influential records in fairness violations. We design privacy-preserving algorithms and analyze their sensitivity, accuracy, and efficiency. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that our proposed measures faithfully approximate their non-private counterparts, effectively quantify bias under privacy constraints, and provide insights for data management.

</details>


### [118] [Conceptual Schema Inference for Tabular Datasets using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23105)
*Zhenyu Wu,Jiaoyan Chen,Norman W. Paton*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究表格数据的概念模式推断问题，提出GeSI和EmSI两种基于LLM的方法，前者利用生成式LLM推断层次类型并构建全局模式，后者通过表格嵌入聚类后构建层次结构。实验验证了方法的有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖、Web表格、开放数据门户中的大规模表格数据来源于异构来源，导致表示不一致。现有工作主要关注数据集发现与探索，缺乏对概念模式推断的研究——即如何直接从原始表格中提取实体类型、属性及类型间关系。

Method: GeSI利用生成式LLM从表格和列级语义中推断层次类型及属性，整合为捕获类型间关系的全局模式；EmSI采用基于LLM的表格嵌入按列级语义对表格进行聚类，在各组内推断属性并从共享属性模式构建层次结构。两方法仅依赖列标题和单元格值。

Result: 实验分析表明方法在推断模式的简洁性、结构质量、大规模仓储可扩展性方面有效，案例研究验证了端到端模式推断的可行性。

Conclusion: 两项方法均能有效实现大规模仓储的概念模式推断，在模式简洁性、结构质量、可扩展性方面表现良好，末端到末端案例研究验证了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Large collections of tabular data from data lakes, web tables and open data portals often originate from heterogeneous sources, leading to representational inconsistencies. Understanding and organizing such repositories therefore remains a major challenge. While prior work has primarily focused on dataset discovery and exploration, this paper addresses the complementary problem of conceptual schema inference: automatically deriving a conceptual schema that captures entity types, attributes and inter-type relationships directly from raw tables. We propose two large language model (LLM)-based approaches that use only column headers and cell values: GeSI uses generative LLMs to infer hierarchical types and their attributes from table- and column-level semantics, and to integrate them into a global schema that also captures relationships across types; EmSI employs LLM-based table embeddings to group tables by column-level semantics, infer attributes within each group, and construct hierarchical structures from shared attribute patterns. Finally, we report an experimental analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of our approaches in terms of the conciseness and structural quality of the inferred schema components, their scalability to large repositories, and a case study illustrating end-to-end schema inference.

</details>


### [119] [BCTuner: LLM-Guided Monte Carlo Tree Search for Efficient Blockchain Knob Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23280)
*Yaoyi Deng,Chongyang Tao,Mingxuan Li,Xuelian Lin,Han Sun,Mingchao Wan,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BCTuner addresses the difficult knob tuning problem in permissioned blockchains by combining LLM-guided reasoning with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). It organizes multi-source tuning knowledge, incrementally constructs and validates configurations, and uses adaptive pruning to reduce exploration costs. Evaluated on Hyperledger Fabric and ChainMaker, it achieves significant performance gains (211% throughput improvement) while reducing system interactions by 8x.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knob tuning in permissioned blockchains is challenging due to architectural complexity, semantic gap between knob logic and optimization requirements, coupled configurations across transaction pipeline stages, and the high cost of deployment and distributed benchmarking for each trial.

Method: BCTuner employs LLM-based reasoning over organized tuning knowledge, formulates tuning as an MCTS process with structured action trajectories, and applies adaptive pruning for branch exploration. Configurations are incrementally constructed, validated, evaluated, and refined rather than generated in one step.

Result: BCTuner achieves up to 211.38% throughput improvement over defaults, outperforms state-of-the-art by up to 20%, and requires up to 8x fewer blockchain interactions.

Conclusion: BCTuner successfully bridges the semantic gap between knob-specific logic and numerical optimization through LLM-guided reasoning, achieving significant performance improvements with reduced evaluation cost.

Abstract: Knob tuning plays a critical role in improving the performance of permissioned blockchains. However, efficient tuning remains challenging due to the architectural complexity of blockchains and the semantic gap between knob-specific logic and the numerical optimization requirements of tuning tools. In addition, configuration changes are often coupled across different stages of the transaction pipeline, making their performance impact difficult to isolate and predict. Since each trial requires deployment and distributed benchmarking, ineffective exploration incurs substantial cost. These challenges motivate BCTuner, a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided framework that combines knowledge-guided reasoning with structured search. BCTuner organizes multi-source tuning knowledge to support LLM-based reasoning over knob semantics, constraints, and deployment context. It formulates tuning as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process over structured action trajectories, where configurations are incrementally constructed, validated, evaluated, and refined rather than generated in one step. BCTuner further applies adaptive pruning to discard infeasible or low-potential branches before system evaluation. We evaluate BCTuner on Hyperledger Fabric and ChainMaker under diverse workloads and network settings. Experimental results show that BCTuner achieves up to 211.38% throughput improvement over default configurations and outperforms the state-of-the-art blockchain tuning method by up to 20% in performance, while requiring up to 8x fewer interactions with the blockchain system.

</details>


### [120] [A Pragmatic Approach to Learned Indexing in RocksDB: Targeted Optimizations with Minimal System Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23815)
*Shubham Vashisth,Olivier Michaud,Bettina Kemme,Oana Balmau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探索将现成学习型索引以最小改造方式集成到RocksDB生产数据库中。通过利用RocksDB的MemTable和持久化层分离架构，提出MemTable重用机制和块感知学习型索引，实现写吞吐提升1.5倍、读吞吐提升2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型索引在并发和持久化支持方面存在不足，缺乏类似B+-Tree的成熟实现，同时研究原型通常复杂度较高，导致生产系统采用受限。

Method: 利用RocksDB的MemTable与持久化层的架构分离，在内存层引入重用机制保留模型知识，在存储层采用块感知的只读学习型索引替换原有B+-Tree索引。

Result: 相比SOTA系统，写吞吐提升1.5倍，读吞吐提升2.1倍，在大规模多样化数据分布和访问模式测试中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: MountDB通过最小化存储引擎改造，成功将学习型索引部署于生产数据库，在大规模真实负载下实现了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Learned indexes have emerged as a promising alternative to traditional index structures, offering higher throughput and lower memory usage by approximating the cumulative key distribution function with lightweight models. Despite these benefits, adoption in production systems remains limited, partly because learned indexes that support concurrency and persistence as effectively as, e.g., the B+-Tree, do not yet exist, while many research prototypes introduce substantial complexity. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf learned indexes can be integrated into a production database with minimal storage-engine redesign. Using RocksDB as a case study, we exploit its separation between in-memory Memtables and immutable on-disk files to deploy specialized indexes at each level. We show that directly applying existing learned indexes is insufficient under write-heavy workloads because frequent Memtable replacement prevents models from fully adapting. To address this, we introduce a reuse mechanism that preserves structural knowledge across Memtable instances. At the storage level, we replace RocksDB's disk index with a learned index without modifying the storage layer or read path. We further adapt a read-only learned index to be block-aware, enabling worst-case single-I/O lookups. We implement these techniques in MountDB, an extension of RocksDB. Experiments on large-scale workloads with diverse data distributions and access patterns show up to 1.5X higher write throughput and 2.1X higher read throughput than state-of-the-art systems, demonstrating that established learned indexes can be integrated into production systems with minimal overhead and substantial performance benefits.

</details>


### [121] [CHRONOS: Temporally-Aware Multi-Agent Coordination for Evolving Data Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23887)
*Joydeep Chandra*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CHRONOS是一个用于时序知识图谱数据市场的三层架构，通过神经ODE时序衰减解决索引陈旧问题、基于变点检测的条件Shapley定价解决价值误归因问题、以及EXP3-IX算法解决差分隐私预算过度消耗问题，在500卖家规模下实现0.937召回率、2.74 QPS和161ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱数据市场面临三重耦合失效：(1) 静态设计下混合索引快捷方式随边演化而失效，导致召回率下降；(2) 平稳Shapley定价在分布漂移后错误归因价值；(3) 无协调的智能体过度消耗共享的差分隐私预算。这些问题需要统一的时序市场机制设计。

Method: 提出CHRONOS三层架构：(1) 第一层采用神经ODE时序衰减处理快捷边，提供O(Pq·λ·Δt)的查询期望召回损失上界，带单调包络保证将松弛度降至1.8-3.2倍观测损失；(2) 第二层在检测到的变点条件下进行Shapley估值，提供噪声下的有限样本误差保证；(3) 第三层使用EXP3-IX算法实现O(√T·log T)的遗憾界，通过矩会计法实现ε-δ差分隐私。每轮发布私有化亲和矩阵（高斯机制），检索和排序作为后处理无额外隐私开销。

Result: 在四个基准测试上的表现：(1) 召回率@10达到0.937；(2) 吞吐量2.74 QPS；(3) 延迟161ms；(4) zCDP组合下总隐私预算ε=4.25（δ=10⁻⁶）。支持500卖家的多轮结算，具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: CHRONOS在时序知识图谱市场场景下实现了竞争性的性能点。核心限制是：在当前隐私保护水平下，发布的估值仍以噪声为主导；实用价值主要来自公共索引路由和低敏感度统计驱动的自适应调度。这表明隐私-效用权衡仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge-graph data marketplaces face three coupled failures in static designs: stale hybrid index shortcuts reduce recall as edges evolve, stationary Shapley pricing misattributes value after distribution shifts, and uncoordinated agents over-consume a shared differential-privacy budget. We present CHRONOS, a three-layer architecture providing a unified treatment of these challenges with explicit public and private separation. Layer one applies neural-ODE temporal decay to shortcut edges, providing a per-query expected recall-loss bound of Big-O of Pq lambda delta t, with a monotone-envelope guarantee reducing bound looseness to 1.8 to 3.2 times observed loss. Layer two conditions Shapley valuation on detected changepoints and provides finite-sample error guarantees under noise. Layer three uses EXP3-IX to achieve Big-O of the square root of T log T regret while enforcing epsilon and delta differential privacy via moments accounting. CHRONOS releases a privatized affinity matrix per epoch using the Gaussian mechanism; all retrieval and ranking are post-processing, incurring no extra privacy cost. We provide multi-epoch settlement, scalability analysis for 500 sellers, and comparisons against accelerated baselines. Across four benchmarks, CHRONOS shows 0.937 recall at ten, 2.74 queries per second, 161 ms latency, and total epsilon of 4.25 at delta of 10 to the power of negative 6 under zCDP composition. These results indicate a competitive operating point. A limitation is that at this privacy level, released valuations remain noise-dominated; utility derives primarily from public index routing and adaptive scheduling driven by low-sensitivity statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Latent Cache Flow: Model-to-Model Communication Without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22863)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LCF introduces a compact, efficient KV cache transfer mechanism for LLM agent communication, reducing adapter size to ~4% of prior work while enabling effective cross-model knowledge sharing even when contexts differ.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-based LLM agent communication suffers from high latency and information loss due to sequential decoding and encoding; existing KV cache transfer methods like C2C require large expensive adapters and assume identical context, making them unsuitable for real-world multi-agent scenarios where models have differing contexts.

Method: LCF uses jointly translated and compressed key-value representations to reduce adapter size to ~4% of C2C, and designs the adapter to transmit summaries of new information unfamiliar to the target model, enabling cross-model communication with differing contexts.

Result: LCF with a 13 MB adapter matches or exceeds a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for differing contexts, LCF achieves 23% higher accuracy and 8.5x faster speed than text-based baselines.

Conclusion: LCF achieves comparable or superior accuracy compared to C2C with dramatically smaller adapters (13 MB vs 956 MB), and outperforms text-based communication by 23% in accuracy and 8.5x in speed for different-context scenarios.

Abstract: LLM agents today communicate via text, which incurs considerable latency and information loss due to the need to autoregressively decode the sharer model's state and encode at the receiver model. Recent work such as Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026) seeks to exchange KV caches by learning adapters that translate sharer KV matrices to the receiver model. However, the adapters are large and expensive to train, and translate individual tokens, which requires the target context to be identical. This is unsuitable for agent communication, where the LLMs have differing context.
  We introduce Latent Cache Flow (LCF). To address efficiency, we observe that keys and values can be jointly translated and compressed, reducing the adapter to about 4% of C2C's size. To address differing context, we design the adapter to transmit a summary of new information that the target model does not have. Our early experiments show that a 13 MB LCF adapter can be more accurate than a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for different contexts, LCF is 23% more accurate and 8.5x faster than text-based communication.

</details>


### [123] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于层间几何特征的不确定性量化方法，通过追踪MLP更新的累积轨迹提取11个尺度不变特征，利用稀疏线性探针预测模型不确定性，在选择性预测任务中大幅超越传统最大softmax概率方法，并揭示了错误在哪些层开始形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于最大softmax概率(MSP)的不确定性量化方法虽然计算成本低，但通常存在校准不良的问题；而基于隐藏状态探针的方法将激活状态视为静态快照，忽略了表征在层间形成过程中的轨迹信息。

Method: 从语言模型各层MLP更新中提取11个尺度不变的几何特征，追踪表征形成过程中的累积路径，并将其输入稀疏线性探针进行预测。

Result: 所提探针在选择性弃权任务中显著优于MSP基准，且性能提升幅度与基准方法的校准不良程度正相关，最高可获得21个AURC百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 该方法通过追踪MLP层间更新的累积路径，提取11个尺度不变的几何特征，结合稀疏线性探针，在选择性弃权场景下显著优于传统MSP方法，AUROC提升高达21个百分点，同时揭示了哪些层导致错误形成。

Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

</details>


### [124] [Learning-Augmented Online Scheduling with Parsimonious Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23255)
*Mugen Blue,Sungjin Im,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个系统研究学习增强调度中延迟与抢占复杂度权衡的论文，在保持理论延迟界的同时限制抢占次数，为非相关和可变形机器提供首个有界抢占保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习增强算法主要优化任务延迟，但依赖频繁的"盲目"抢占，忽视了算法性能与抢占复杂度之间的根本性权衡，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 提出首个系统性地限制抢占次数同时优化延迟的学习增强调度算法，设计了O(1)竞争比的算法，在准确预测下每个任务仅需O(1)次抢占，预测误差以对数尺度影响开销。

Result: 单台机器和非相关并行机器上实现O(1)竞争比的算法，准确预测下每个任务仅需O(1)次抢占，开销随预测误差对数增长；为非相关机器和可变形机器提供首个有界抢占保证。

Conclusion: 首次为非相关机器和可变形机器提供有界抢占保证，将学习增强框架的理论适用范围扩展到更具约束性和现实性的场景，并通过实验验证了算法的有效性。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have emerged as a powerful paradigm to surpass traditional worst-case lower bounds by integrating potentially noisy predictions. While this framework has seen success in online scheduling, existing work primarily optimizes job latency while relying on frequent, ``blind'' preemptions. This ignores the fundamental trade-off between algorithmic performance and preemption complexity. We provide the first systematic study of learning-augmented scheduling that curbs preemption while optimizing latency. We establish that the gap between theoretical latency bounds and preemption overhead can be bridged with solid analytical foundations. Our results include $O(1)$-competitive algorithms for single and unrelated parallel machines with only $O(1)$ preemptions per job under accurate predictions, with overhead scaling logarithmically with the prediction error. By providing the first bounded-preemption guarantees for unrelated and malleable machines, we extend the theoretical reach of the learning-augmented framework to more constrained and realistic settings. Finally, our algorithms are validated through experiments.

</details>


### [125] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionSense是一种面向能源受限自主边缘系统的融合感知智能传感框架。通过三步训练流程（服务器融合学习→模态必要性量化→紧凑边缘融合）生成运行时决策层，在RGB+Depth/LiDAR双模态测试中实现高达33倍能耗降低和92.3%质量损失减少，同时线性扩展支持多传感器场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统计算分布在近传感器、边缘和云端，面临严格的能耗、延迟和可靠性约束。多模态传感器套件日益普及，但现有方法存在缺陷：要么在强大服务器上融合模态，要么应用忽略跨模态依赖的单模态近传感器滤波器，导致冗余传输或遗漏事件。

Method: FusionSense采用三步训练流程：(1)服务器端融合模型学习下游任务；(2)通过Filter-out-Safe(FoS)标签量化各模态对融合决策的必要性；(3)通过将近传感器预测作为辅助信号注入，紧凑化边缘侧融合模型。运行时决策层可联合减少计算和通信开销，且随传感器数量线性扩展。

Result: 在RGB+Depth/LiDAR双模态SynDrone设置中，FusionSense在更高数据降采样率下维持任务质量。关键指标：(1)1%特征-of-Interest流行率下能耗降低33倍；(2)10%流行率下降低11倍；(3)固定30%数据降采样下质量损失减少92.3%；(4)相比最佳先前滤波基线，能耗节省提升约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense成功实现了一种融合感知的智能传感框架，能够在能源受限的自主边缘系统中有效平衡任务质量与能效。其三步训练流程和运行时决策层设计，使得系统能够在大幅降低数据传输和计算能耗的同时，维持较高的任务性能。该方法为多模态边缘计算环境下的自适应传感提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

</details>


### [126] [Optimal Dimension-Free Sampling for Regularized Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23726)
*Meysam Alishahi,Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Lipschitz分类损失函数（logistic、hinge、ReLU等）建立了各种正则化项下的最优采样复杂度边界，通过高阶矩分析和经验过程理论改进了传统敏感性采样框架。相比先前$k^3/\varepsilon^2$结果，对$\|\cdot\|_2/k$正则化达到$k^2/\varepsilon^2$，对$\|\cdot\|_1/k$正则化达到$k/\varepsilon^2$，对满足导数有界条件的$\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$正则化达到线性复杂度$k$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为广泛的Lipschitz连续分类损失函数（如logistic、sigmoid、hinge、ReLU损失）建立最优采样边界，以改进机器学习中核方法等算法在大规模数据集上的采样效率。

Method: 采用精细的高阶矩界分析和经验过程理论，避免了VC维数和敏感性框架中的过度计数问题。研究使用简单的均匀采样或（平方）范数采样，替代了先前工作中更复杂的敏感性采样方法。

Result: 证明了不同正则化项下的最优采样复杂度：(1) $\|\cdot\|_2/k$正则化：$k^2/\varepsilon^2$上下界；(2) $\|\cdot\|_1/k$正则化：$k/\varepsilon^2$上下界；(3) $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$正则化：满足$|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$、$g(0)>0$且单调或凸时为线性复杂度$O(k)$，否则为$k^2/\varepsilon^2$。若$g(0)=0$，则不存在维度无关边界，甚至排除亚线性边界。所有上界均有匹配的下界（至多多对数因子）。相比ICML'24的$k^3/\varepsilon^2$敏感性采样边界有显著改进。

Conclusion: 本研究为Lipschitz连续分类损失函数提供了最优采样边界，改进了先前的立方复杂度结果。核心贡献在于使用高阶矩界和经验过程分析的精细论证，绕过了VC维数和敏感性框架中的重复计数问题。通过简单的均匀或范数采样，对多种正则化项（$\|\cdot\|_2/k$、$\|\cdot\|_1/k$、$\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$）建立了上下界匹配的最优采样复杂度。对于满足导数有界条件$g'(x)\leq g(x)$且$g(0)>0$的正则化器，可达线性采样复杂度；否则为二次复杂度。当$g(0)=0$时，证明了不存在与维度无关的边界，甚至亚线性边界也不可能存在。

Abstract: We prove optimal sampling bounds achieving $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-relative error for a broad class of Lipschitz continuous classification loss functions under various regularization terms. This includes important functions such as logistic and sigmoid loss, hinge loss, and ReLU loss, as prominent and popular representative examples. In particular, we prove $k^2/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_2/k$ regularization, and $k/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_1/k$ regularization. For $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$ regularization, the sampling complexity depends mainly on a bounded derivative property: if $|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$, and $g(0)>0$, and $g$ is monotonic or convex, then it admits linear in $k$ sampling complexity; otherwise the general bound is $k^2/\varepsilon^2$. However, if $g(0)=0$, our results indicate that no dimension-free bounds are possible, and even sublinear bounds are ruled out. All upper bounds are complemented by matching lower bounds up to polylogarithmic terms. Moreover, our work relies conceptually and algorithmically on simple uniform or (squared) norm sampling and hereby improves over recent cubic $k^3/\varepsilon^2$ sensitivity sampling bounds of (Alishahi and Phillips, ICML'24). This is achieved by refined arguments involving higher moment bounds and empirical process analyses to avoid overcounting that appears in the de-facto standard VC-dimension and sensitivity framework.

</details>


### [127] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuRA通过块张量-火车分解结合SVD谱预条件化，在保持与LoRA相当的效率的同时，显著优于全量微调，并为LLM微调、强化学习和视觉语言模型指令调优提供了更优的适配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient methods like LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. Noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features.

Method: FuRA reparameterizes weight matrices via block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core tensor L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and block-wise singular values S are optimized.

Result: FuRA outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count, achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA, and its quantized variant QFuRA surpasses QLoRA.

Conclusion: FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings (LLM fine-tuning, LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs), and QFuRA surpasses QLoRA.

Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [128] [The Readout Shortcut: Positional Number Copying Dominates Arithmetic CoT Readout in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22870)
*Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定位了 1–3B 小语言模型 CoT 答案读出阶段的一条"位置捷径"：模型直接复制答案分隔符前最后一个数字（答案上下文中的尾位数字），而非依赖中间推理；该机制可解释为何打乱 CoT 步骤仍保留大部分准确率，也意味着 step-level CoT 监控会误把"位置化答案搬运"当作"真实计算"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张力：CoT 对算术是必要的（去掉即崩），但打乱步骤顺序几乎不损失性能——若 CoT 的贡献不在逻辑序列，那它到底在贡献什么？此前工作（Turpin 2023、Lanham 2023、Arcuschin 2025、Pfau 2024、Dutta 2024、Bogdan 2025）只证明 CoT 可能"不忠实"，未回答读出到底用什么信号、该信号在网络何处、以及它是否主动压制本可利用的计算。本文针对 1–3B 指令微调模型在算术任务上同时给出这三点的答案。

Method: 三架构（Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct、Gemma-2-2B-it），GSM8K 前 500 题，基线 CoT 准确率 67.0/45.6/66.2%。核心方法为教师强制前缀注入（prefix completion）以隔离"生成"与"读出"两个阶段。四条收敛证据线：(1) 数字腐化分解 A(全腐化)/B(保金)/C(干净) 分离 Δcopy 与 Δoff-copy，加七条件因果阶梯 D_rep/D_trunc/D_blank/No-CoT 检验复制通道与保留上下文补全的优先级；(2) 答案位置扫描（0/0.25/0.5/0.75/1.0）、干扰项注入（相邻/随机/中间结果）、框架解离（模板/裸数字/非答案/内联）区分位置复制 vs 内容选择 vs 平滑近因；(3) 七级打乱层级（ordered/within-step/step-shuffle/word-shuffle/reverse/token-shuffle/no-CoT）以答案 token 可达性为自变量；(4) 头级零消融、均值消融、激活修补与归纳头重叠分析。统计：Wilson 95% CI、McNemar 精确检验、预声明 23 个对比族内 Holm–Bonferroni、配对 bootstrap（10,000 次）、置换检验；辅助分析为探索性。外部效度：GSM-Symbolic、SVAMP、基座模型、7–8B 扩展、RL 蒸馏推理模型、BBH 非算术任务。

Result: ① 金答案存在性主导读出：Δcopy=0.921/0.875/0.543（54–92 pp），上限归一化后 88–92% 跨架构收敛；Δoff-copy≈0.000/0.004/0.024，说明中间步骤几乎不携带额外信息。② 自由生成（无前缀注入）复现同一签名：答案=最后一个 CoT 数字 0.960–0.974，即使答错项也达 0.905–0.957；准确率几乎由"金答案是否位于末位"决定（0.991–0.997 vs 0.004–0.030）。③ 复制通道优先于补全：D_rep（正确中间步+错误尾数）准确率崩塌至 0.076/0.084/0.010，与全腐化 A 无异；D_trunc（去掉尾数）回升至 0.399/0.183/0.058（+32/+10/+5 pp），D_blank≈D_trunc 排除格式因素；D_trunc 远高于 No-CoT（+29/+16/+4 pp），且 74–77% 的恢复项仅需 1 步运算，说明该捷径甚至压制模型本可完成的单步算术。④ 选择性随架构/规模变化：Qwen/Llama 复制新干扰数 0.87–0.95，Gemma 仅 0.12–0.19（内容门控 gate≈0.85）；7–8B 时 Qwen-7B P(distractor)=0.095、Llama-8B 0.045–0.317 且 Δoff-copy=0.196；R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B 上限归一化 Δcopy 仍达 96%。⑤ 位置曲线呈"缓坡+末端跳变"（末位 +31/+20 pp，Spearman r=1.00/0.90），keep_end 恢复 99%/93% 的打乱损失；打乱层级随答案 token 可达性单调（步级保留 68–87%，token 级塌至 no-CoT）。⑥ 机制层面：Llama 局部化（L10–L15，top-5 消融 −20 pp，随机对照 0 pp）、Gemma 集中（L14H4 单头 −19.4 pp）、Qwen 分布式（K50=17，均值消融 −33.5 pp）；激活修补在 Qwen 恢复 61% 的打乱差距。⑦ 边界验证：GSM-Symbolic/SVAMP 复制；基座模型已有该机制（指令微调只是放大）；BBH 逻辑演绎保留率降至 44%/21%，shuffled-objects 机会校正后 45.5%/6.8%，与非算术任务上"无可复制尾数"的预测一致。⑧ 十项替代解释各有专属对照（干扰项、位置扫描、框架解离、新分隔符、GSM-Symbolic、基座探针、均值消融+修补、Gemma/7–8B 门控、No-CoT 地板、D_blank）。

Conclusion: 读出可分解为两个独立变化的成分：普遍稳健的"金答案存在性依赖"（全部 5 个规模×模型单元）与差异巨大的"内容门"（Qwen 无、Llama-1B 部分、Gemma-2B 强、7–8B 主导）。因此 CoT 的顺序不敏感性来自读出的不变层，而非推理过程。核心警示：在 1–3B 规模下，step-level 过程奖励/监控所依赖的"步骤质量→答案"耦合极弱（耦合量仅 4–29 pp），且被部分腐化的 CoT 比截断的 CoT 更具欺骗性（D_rep≪D_trunc），故步级忠实性评估会把位置化答案搬运误判为真实计算，构成 CoT 监督失效模式。实践建议：1–3B 部署优先采用输出侧答案校验而非步级过程奖励；剥离答案模板框架可部分破坏捷径；监控"答案是否等于最后一个 CoT 数字"是简易的复制主导诊断；随规模增大内容选择性出现，监控性应部分恢复。局限：主要证据限于 1–3B 指令微调算术模型；Gemma 部分实验因教师强制保真度不足被排除（子集上模式仍复现）；7–8B 仅 N=2、推理蒸馏模型 N=1、非算术任务 N=2；头级定位为敏感性画像而非完整电路；D_trunc 只给出保留上下文贡献的下界，且不含"生成期是否真的计算"的结论。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting is necessary for arithmetic in small language models, yet shuffling its steps preserves most performance. What does CoT contribute if not logical sequencing? In three 1-3B instruction-tuned LMs on GSM8K, we isolate the answer-readout stage via prefix completion and identify a positional shortcut: the model copies whichever number occupies the trailing position before the answer delimiter, regardless of intermediate reasoning. Gold-answer presence accounts for 54-92 pp of accuracy (89-92% of each model's teacher-forcing ceiling); even on incorrect items, the final answer matches the last CoT number 95-96% of the time. The copy channel takes precedence over retained-context completion: replacing the trailing number with a wrong value collapses accuracy to near-zero despite correct intermediates, yet removing it recovers 5-32 pp above that floor--even single-step arithmetic the model can otherwise perform is suppressed when a copyable number is present. Qwen and Llama copy novel distractors 87-95% of the time; Gemma gates selectively. Head-level ablation implicates architecture-specific head sets; the effect replicates on GSM-Symbolic. On non-arithmetic BBH tasks, shuffle retention drops sharply; at 7-8B, content-selective gating emerges. Step-level faithfulness evaluations risk conflating positional answer transport with genuine computation--a failure mode for CoT-based oversight.

</details>


### [129] [Approximate Machine Unlearning through Manifold Representation Forgetting Guided by Self Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22871)
*Weiqi Wang,Zhiyi Tian,Chenhan Zhang,Luoyu Chen,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ManiF-SMC（Manifold Forgetting with Self Mode Connectivity），一种完全在表征空间内运行、无需任务标签的近似机器遗忘方法。其核心观察是：重训练后的模型会把被删除样本映射到保留集中语义最相近的样本附近。据此，作者把近似遗忘重述为"把被删样本的表征推离其原始位置、拉向其 top-k 近邻保留样本的质心"，并用带间隔（margin）的三元组损失统一"推"与"拉"两个目标。由于目标质心与合适间隔都难以先验确定，方法引入自模式连通性（SMC）模块：在原始模型与遗忘模型之间用二次 Bézier 曲线并学习控制点，仅用保留邻域数据训练，得到一个"快速重训练"代理模型，用来估计质心并生成逐样本自适应间隔。实验在 MNIST/CIFAR10/CelebA/Tiny-ImageNet 上表明其遗忘效果与 SOTA 近似方法相当（MIA 59%、RA 99.04%、TA 81.75%，运行时间约 2.4 秒 vs 重训练约 1783 秒），且是唯一可在无解码器/无标签场景（语义通信）下工作的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘是落实 GDPR 等法规"被遗忘权"的关键机制；从头重训练（gold standard）计算与存储开销过高，故需近似遗忘方法。作者指出现有近似遗忘的两大缺陷：(1) 基于标签操纵（如 SalUn）或任务梯度反转（如 GA、VBU）的方法与原始学习目标冲突，破坏了模型效用，且无法保证与"重训练式遗忘"等价；(2) 这些方法的遗忘损失严重依赖标签访问，在语义通信、联邦学习等场景中遗忘方可能只能拿到模型或已学习表征。为此提出的研究问题是：如何在与重训练行为保持一致的前提下，在表征空间中实现有效且标签无关的近似遗忘？灵感来自两点观察：(a) 图 1 显示重训练模型会把被删样本映射到语义最相近的保留样本附近（而非像梯度上升那样打散类簇）；(b) 自监督学习中的紧致性/均匀性与 MMCR（最大流形容量表征）理论表明，调控表征几何结构即可引导模型行为，无需依赖标签或任务梯度。

Method: **问题重述**：设训练集划分为遗忘集 S_u 与保留集 S_r。对每个被删样本 x_i，用原始表征在 S_r 中检索 top-k 近邻 S_k^i，并定义局部质心 c_{i,u}=mean f_{θ_u}(x_j)。据此给出"推"项 max Σ||f_{θ_u}(x_i)-z_{i,o}||²（推离原始表征）与"拉"项 min Σ||f_{θ_u}(x_i)-c_{i,u}||²（拉向语义近邻质心）。

**ManiF（流形对比遗忘）**：将推-拉统一为间隔式三元组铰链损失 L_triplet=Σ[dist(f_θ_u(x_i), c_{i,u}) − dist(f_θ_u(x_i), z_{i,o}) + α]_+，欧氏距离为默认度量。给出"遗忘距离下界"命题：当 L=0 时，被删表征到近邻质心的距离至少比到原始表征的距离小 α。
