**SMC（自模式连通性）**：以 θ_u、θ_o 为端点参数化二次 Bézier 曲线 φ_w(t)=(1−t)²θ_u+2t(1−t)w+t²θ_o，控制点 w 可学习；仅用保留邻域并集 S_k 上的损失 L_{S_k}(φ_w(t))（t~U[0,1]）优化 w（对 w 求梯度，θ_u 固定），得到低损失连通路径；在路径上取 t*=0.5 采样代理模型 θ̃=φ_w(t*)，据此计算 c_{i,θ̃} 与逐样本自适应间隔 α_i=[dist(f_θ̃(x_i), z_{i,o}) − dist(f_θ̃(x_i), c_{i,θ̃})]_+，代入三元组损失。命题 1 给出 logit drift 的 Lipschitz 上界，说明沿路径表征连续变化，故代理模型能稳定估计保留邻域几何。算法 1 交替执行 (A) 学习 Bézier 控制点、(B) 采样代理模型、(C) 用自适应间隔更新 θ_u。

**额外设计**：训练时引入 MMCR 正则提升类间可分性；邻居集 S_k^i 在原始表征空间一次性构建后固定，原型 z_{i,o} 缓存；全部流程不使用标签。

Result: 1) 总体效果：在 MNIST/CIFAR10/Tiny-ImageNet/CelebA 上，ManiF-SMC 是唯一"无标签"遗忘方法；其 MIA 随遗忘样本数 USS（200–1200）上升而上升，RA/TA 相比 VBU 退化更小。在 CIFAR10（w/ MMCR）上取得最佳 MIA 59.00%、RA 99.04%、TA 81.75%（重训练为 MIA 61%、RA 99.28%），整体与 SOTA 近似方法相当。表征可视化显示其几何结构最接近重训练与 SalUn，保持类簇紧致并将遗忘样本移向语义最近邻簇，而 GA/VBU 因梯度反转增大类内距离。
2) 效率：ManiF-SMC 约 1.5–11.8 秒（随 USS 增长），重训练需 10²–10⁴ 秒（MNIST 464.9→470.4 s；CIFAR10 1742→1783 s），效率优势达 2–3 个数量级。
3) 组件消融：SMC 自适应间隔相比固定间隔（纯 ManiF，α=0.01）在 MIA 上提升约 1%–5%，且在 USS=800/1000 时 RA/TA 保持明显更好（CIFAR10 USS=1000：RA 89.80→94.91%，TA 72.34→77.00%）；代价是 RT 增加约 0.35–1.6 s。距离度量上 L2 范数遗忘效果最好（NT-Xent 余弦相似度 MIA 较低、RT 更高）。k 增大可提升效用但削弱遗忘（CelebA：k=5→10 时 RA 96.34→96.48%，MIA 54.5→53.0%）；t*=0.5 在路径采样点中最优。
4) MMCR 的普适收益：几乎所有方法（重训练、GA、VBU、RFU、SalUn、ManiF-SMC）在加入 MMCR 后 MIA/RA/TA 全面提升（ManiF-SMC 在 CIFAR10 上 MIA 55→59%，TA 80.58→81.75%），仅略微增加原训练开销。
5) 扩展性：可用于 VAE 生成模型（USS 增大时 MIA 由 49.4%→65.5%(MNIST)、53.8%→67.0%(CIFAR10)，R-MSE/T-MSE 仅轻微上升，生成样本质量保持）；在 CelebA 语义通信场景中，Retrain/GA/VBU/RFU/SalUn 在无解码器时均不可行，仅 ManiF-SMC 可用且效果接近有解码器的重训练；亦可迁移到 InfoNCE、信息瓶颈与 ViT 架构。
6) 局限：无标签约束导致 USS 较大时效用损失大于使用剩余数据微调的 SalUn；补加微调可缓解 RA/TA 退化，但会重新依赖标签，削弱方法的标签无关优势。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是把近似机器遗忘从"数据/标签中心"重构为"表征空间的流形迁移"问题：将遗忘操作化定义为"推离原始表征 + 拉向语义最近邻保留样本质心"，从而与重训练行为一致并摆脱对任务标签和任务梯度的依赖。ManiF-SMC 用带间隔的三元组损失同时承载遗忘与表征保持目标，并通过自模式连通性（Bézier 低损失路径上的代理模型）快速估计质心、生成自适应间隔，弥补了缺少重训练模型的缺口。实验证明其在遗忘效果、模型效用与效率上与 SOTA 近似方法可比，且是唯一可用于无标签/仅有编码器的语义通信等复杂场景的方案；同时发现 MMCR 表征正则对近似与精确遗忘均有稳定增益，提示"表征几何调控"是提升遗忘效能的通用设计原则。未来方向包括：进一步刻画表征空间重述的理论基础、构建对重训练一致性的更严格度量、以及在预训练大模型、多下游任务共享编码器等场景下的扩展。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is a fundamental mechanism that enforces the right to be forgotten. Existing unlearning studies that rely on label manipulation or task-gradient reversal often deliver limited unlearning effectiveness. Moreover, they can undermine the original learning objective and typically do not guarantee equivalence to standard unlearning by retraining.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{ManiF-SMC} (\textbf{Mani}fold \textbf{F}orgetting with \textbf{S}elf \textbf{M}ode \textbf{C}onnectivity), motivated by the observation that a model retrained on the remaining data tends to classify erased samples by their semantic similarity to the retained data. We begin with systematically recasting the approximate unlearning as pushing each erased sample away from its original learned manifold representation centroid toward its nearest semantic neighbors in the retained data. This reformulation aligns unlearning with retraining behavior and operates purely in representation space, reducing reliance on labels and task-specific gradients. To tackle the manifold representation-based unlearning problem, ManiF-SMC encapsulates the unlearning and representation preservation goals in a margin-based triplet loss. Because finding a suitable margin for unlearning is challenging, we propose a self-mode-connectivity module that rapidly reconstructs the local manifold to guide the adaptive margins generation for each unlearning case. Extensive experiments on four representative datasets show that ManiF-SMC achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to state-of-the-art approximate methods while operating solely within the model's representation space.

</details>


### [130] [MedExpMem: Adapting Experience Memory for Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22872)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Yannian Gu,Winnie Chiu Wing Chu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MedExpMem是一个让医学视觉-语言模型通过"经验记忆"机制持续学习诊断专长的框架，通过两阶段学习从诊断失败中提炼成对鉴别笔记，在11个子专业影像学测试中最高提升准确率7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉-语言模型(VLM)缺乏诊断经验的动态积累能力，其参数编码的是静态知识，无法像经验丰富的医师那样通过临床实践持续提升对易混淆疾病的鉴别诊断能力。

Method: 提出MedExpMem框架，采用两阶段构建过程模拟医师学习：(1)初始实践暴露知识缺口；(2)反思性再诊断深化理解。框架核心包括：从诊断失败中提取判别性经验，编码为包含关键鉴别指标、可操作决策规则和推理错误模式的成对鉴别笔记；新病例采用经验记忆检索引导鉴别推理。

Result: 在涵盖11个子专业的影像学基准测试中，MedExpMem实现了一致的准确率提升，最高可达7.0%，且在不同模型和规模上均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedExpMem作为一个竞争性方法，有效解决了医学适应性问题，超越了参数化学习的能力范围，为VLM诊断智能体提供了一种持续积累专业经验的新范式。

Abstract: Experienced physicians develop diagnostic expertise through clinical practice, acquiring not only disease knowledge but also the ability to differentiate confusable conditions. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) lack this capability -- their parameters encode static knowledge that does not evolve across diagnostic encounters. We propose MedExpMem, an experience memory framework enabling VLM-based diagnostic agents to accumulate differential diagnosis expertise. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation, which retrieves encyclopedic disease descriptions, MedExpMem memorizes discriminative experience derived from the agent's own diagnostic failures and organizes them as pairwise differential notes encoding key discriminators, actionable decision rules and reasoning error patterns. The framework adopts a two-phase construction process mirroring physician learning: initial practice exposes knowledge gaps, and reflective re-diagnosis refines understanding. When encountering new cases, the agent retrieves experience memory to guide differential reasoning. We evaluate MedExpMem on a radiology benchmark spanning 11 subspecialties. Results demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements, maximum 7.0%, across diverse models and scales. Analytical experiments validate experience quality and robustness, demonstrating MedExpMem as a competitive method addresses medical adaptation needs beyond the reach of parameteric learning.

</details>


### [131] [MARGIN: Runtime Confidence Calibration for Multi-Agent Foundation Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22949)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARGIN是一种在线校准方法，通过对称指数加权移动平均与贝叶斯收缩融合，在无需模型访问和预留数据的情况下，为每个智能体动态学习校准因子，有效解决了大语言模型置信度在困难任务上与准确率负相关的核心问题，显著提升多智能体系统的信任决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（Foundation Model）智能体在多智能体部署中依赖自我报告的置信度进行信任决策，但模型置信度系统性失调——在困难任务上甚至与准确率呈负相关。设计时校准方法（温度缩放、 Platt缩放、直方图分箱）在分布偏移下性能退化，无法应对此问题。

Method: MARGIN采用对称指数加权移动平均（EWMA）结合贝叶斯收缩融合，从任务流中实时学习每个智能体在每个置信度带下的校准因子。方法具备三个超参数，默认值稳健，无需模型访问、预留数据或模型重训练。

Result: 在19个模型、8个基准、超过50000个观测值的实验验证中，MARGIN在分布偏移下实现比最佳设计时基线方法低3-6倍的校准误差。在多智能体选择中，原始置信度在困难基准上导致成对决策准确率低于随机（45-56%），而MARGIN将其纠正至70-89%，并在三个基准上超越最佳模型甲骨文。

Conclusion: MARGIN提出了一种无需模型访问、无需预留数据、无需重训练的在线校准方法，通过对称指数加权移动平均与贝叶斯收缩融合实现对每个智能体、每个置信度带的动态校准。该方法在分布偏移下表现出色，将困难基准上的成对决策准确率从45-56%提升至70-89%，并在四个基准中的三个上超越了"总是选择最佳模型"的甲骨文。论文提供了六个形式化命题支撑理论分析。

Abstract: Foundation model agents increasingly operate in multi-agent deployments where a coordinator must decide which agent's response to trust. The standard approach weights agents by their self-reported confidence, but recent evidence shows that foundation model confidence is systematically mis-calibrated and, on hard tasks, inversely correlated with accuracy. Design-time calibration methods (temperature scaling, Platt scaling, histogram binning) cannot address this problem because they fit a fixed correction to held-out data and degrade under distribution shift.
  We present MARGIN (Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization), an online calibration method that learns per-agent, per-confidence-band calibration factors from the task stream itself, requiring no model access, no held-out data, and no retraining. MARGIN uses symmetric exponentially weighted moving averages with Bayesian shrinkage blending, and has three hyperparameters with robust defaults. Across 19 foundation models, 8 benchmarks, and over 50,000 observations, MARGIN achieves 3-6x lower calibration error than the best design-time baseline under distribution shift. In multi-agent selection, raw verbalized confidence produces pairwise resolution worse than random (45-56%) on hard benchmarks. MARGIN corrects this completely, raising pairwise resolution to 70-89% and surpassing the always-best-model oracle on three of four benchmarks. Six formal propositions characterize convergence, tracking speed, and the optimality of symmetric updates for non-strategic agents, with all predictions illustrated empirically.

</details>


### [132] [When Do LLMs Reason? A Dynamical Systems View via Entropy Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22873)
*Wei Xia,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDRM通过分析解码阶段早期熵动态来判断是否需要链式思维推理，在15个基准和4个LLM上实现41-55% token节省并提升准确率。该方法将熵轨迹嵌入紧致流形表示，实现零样本部署和细粒度实例级自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-thought reasoning often provides marginal or negative gains on factual/open-ended tasks while increasing token consumption. The key question: when is explicit reasoning actually beneficial?

Method: EDRM embeds entropy trajectories into a compact manifold representation for zero-shot deployment and instance-level adaptation. The framework uses early decoding entropy dynamics to adaptively select inference strategies, identifying reasoning modes via entropy reduction patterns (structured reasoning) versus unstable/increasing patterns (non-beneficial tasks).

Result: EDRM consistently outperforms static baselines across 15 benchmarks and 4 LLMs. Dataset-level: 41-55% token reduction with improved accuracy (50 calibration samples). Instance-level: up to 4.7% accuracy improvement with 27-45% token savings.

Conclusion: EDRM achieves 41-55% token reduction with improved accuracy using only 50 calibration samples; instance-level gains reach 4.7% with 27-45% token savings. Reasoning should be selective rather than default; entropy-driven decoding control enables efficient and adaptive LLM inference.

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become the default strategy for enhancing LLM capabilities, yet its application raises a fundamental question: when is explicit reasoning actually beneficial? Empirical evidence reveals a striking paradox: CoT often provides marginal or even negative gains on factual and open-ended tasks while multiplying token consumption. In this work, we show that LLM reasoning is not a static property of tasks or models, but a \emph{dynamic decoding state} that emerges during generation. Through systematic analysis, we find early-stage entropy dynamics provide a reliable signal of this state: tasks benefiting from CoT exhibit consistent entropy reduction, while others display unstable or increasing patterns. This behavior can be interpreted as a phase-transition-like shift from a high-entropy exploratory regime to a low-entropy structured reasoning regime. Based on these insights, we propose \textbf{EDRM} (Entropy Dynamics-based Reasoning Manifold), a lightweight and training-free routing framework that leverages early decoding entropy to adaptively select inference strategies. EDRM embeds entropy trajectories into a compact and interpretable manifold representation, enabling both zero-shot deployment and fine-grained instance-level adaptation. Across 15 benchmarks and 4 LLMs of varying scales and architectures, EDRM consistently outperforms static baselines. At the dataset level, EDRM achieves \textbf{41--55\%} token reduction while improving accuracy with as few as 50 calibration samples. At the instance level, it further improves accuracy by up to \textbf{4.7\%} while maintaining \textbf{27--45\%} token savings. These results suggest that reasoning should be invoked selectively rather than by default, and demonstrate the effectiveness of entropy-driven decoding control for efficient and adaptive LLM inference.

</details>


### [133] [WeCon: An Efficient Weight-Conditioned Neural Solver for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22876)
*Xuan Wu,Jinbiao Chen,Yang Li,Lijie Wen,Chunguo Wu,Yuanshu Li,Yubin Xiao,Chunyan Miao,You Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeCon 是一种面向多目标组合优化问题（MOCOP）的"权重条件化"单模型神经求解器。它通过带门控残差融合（GRF）的编码器生成信息丰富的权重条件上下文、用可插拔的残差融合（RF）模块在解码器中缓解权重信号稀释，并提出高效偏好优化（EPO）以构造高质量偏好对。在 Bi-TSP、Bi-CVRP、Bi-KP、Tri-TSP 四类问题上，WeCon 的 HV 与 SOTA 求解器 POCCO-W 相当，但推理时间减少约 40%；变体 WeCon-CCO 在所有问题上取得最佳整体 HV，代价是推理时间增加约 1.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MOCOP 神经求解器普遍采用基于分解（Weighted-Sum 标量化）的策略，用单一模型泛化到不同权重向量，但存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 权重的注入方式过于粗糙——如 PMOCO 仅在解码器注入权重，无法提供信息丰富的权重条件上下文；WE-CA 主要在编码器注入权重，导致解码阶段的权重信号被稀释；而 POCCO-W 依赖 MoE/CCO 的门控路由机制，虽有效但带来额外运行时开销，限制了交通信号控制等时间敏感场景的应用。(2) 偏好优化（PO）方法依赖纯随机采样构造解对，难以采到足够数量的（近）最优解作为"较优解"，导致偏好对信息量低、训练效率差（BOPO、VAGPO 等仅改进了偏好对筛选而忽略了采样过程本身）。作者由此提出核心研究问题：能否在不显著增加运行时间的前提下，通过有效产生权重条件上下文并避免解码中的权重信号稀释，达到高水平性能？

Method: 整体架构由编码器、解码器与训练策略三部分组成。

编码器：先用两个 FCN 分别嵌入实例特征得 H⁰、权重向量得 A⁰；每层依次执行 (a) MHSA 自注意力更新实例嵌入（采用 RMSNorm + SwiGLU FFN）；(b) 两个交叉 MHA 块实现实例—权重双向交互：A 以 (A, H₁) 为 Q、K/V 更新，H 以 (H₁, A) 为 Q、K/V 更新；(c) 提出的 Gated Residual Fusion（GRF）块将更新后的权重嵌入 Aˡ 沿节点维广播并以门控残差方式注入实例嵌入：Hˡ = H₂ˡ + gˡ ⊙ (AˡW₃)，门控 gˡ 由 [H₂ˡ‖A→ˡ] 经 GeLU 与两层线性映射再 sigmoid 得到，从而自适应控制权重特征注入强度，输出信息丰富的权重条件上下文。

解码器：先用单层 MHA（Q 为问题特定的上下文嵌入 h_q，K/V 为 [H‖A]）得到 Ĥ₁ᵗ，随后紧接即插即用的 Residual Fusion（RF）块：q_cᵗ = Ĥ₁ᵗ + ((ReLU([Ĥ₁ᵗ‖A→ˡ]W₆+b₆))W₇+b₇)，即把广播后的权重嵌入再次显式注入决策向量 q_cᵗ，再经 tanh 裁剪注意力打分与 softmax 得到选择概率 p_iᵗ。RF 提供了从 A 到 q_cᵗ 的额外直接梯度路径（非退化假设下局部非消失），理论上缓解权重信号稀释。为展示 RF 的通用性，还提出变体 WeCon-CCO：在 POCCO-W 解码器的 MHA 层与 MoE/CCO 模块之间插入 RF（并把 CCO 中的 InstanceNorm 换成 RMSNorm 以与编码器一致）。

训练：提出 Efficient Preference Optimization（EPO）。对每个实例采样 r 个解，其中 ⌈r/c⌉ 个采用引导采样——每一步只在概率最高的 top-k 可行节点中选点（若可行节点少于 k 则取全部），其余 r−⌈r/c⌉ 个随机采样；再按目标值两两比较构造 r(r−1)/2 个偏好对。以平均对数似然作为隐式奖励 f_θ = log p_θ(π|G,λ)/|π|，用 g_θ(π_w≺π_l) = σ(β(f_θ(π_w) − f_θ(π_l))) 建模偏好概率，β 为温度（双目标 3.5、三目标 4.5），并最大化偏好标签 y 的对数似然。超参：k=5、c=8、r=64、C=10、权重数 N=101（双目标）/105（三目标）；训练 200 epoch，每 epoch 10 万实例，batch 64，Adam lr=3e-4、weight decay=1e-6；分解方法采用 Weighted-Sum。

Result: 在 Bi-TSP20/50/100/150/200、真实世界 TSPLIB 实例（KroAB100/150/200）、Bi-CVRP20/50/100、Bi-KP50/100/200/500/1000、Tri-TSP20/50/100 及大规模实例上与 5 种传统启发式（WS-LKH、MOEA/D、NSGA-II、MOGLS、PPLS/D-C）和 9 种神经求解器（DRL-MOA、MDRL、EMNH、PMOCO、CNH、WE-CA、PA-MoE-W、GF-MOCA、POCCO-W）比较。

主要结论：(1) WeCon-A 与 POCCO-W-A 的 HV 基本相当（Gap 差异在 −0.05%~+0.04%），但推理时间少 38%~55%；结论中表述为仅需 POCCO-W 60% 的推理时间。(2) 不做数据增强时 WeCon 反而优于 POCCO-W，Bi-TSP150/200 的 Gap 分别降低 0.08% 与 0.16%，归因于编码器提供的更丰富权重条件上下文。(3) WeCon-CCO 在所有 MOCOP 变体与规模上取得最佳整体 HV，但推理时间约为 WeCon 的 1.7 倍；优势随规模增大更明显（如 Bi-TSP500/1000、Bi-KP500/1000 上 WeCon/WeCon-CCO 明显优于基线）。(4) 效率：参数量 5.4M（POCCO-W 2.0M），但训练时间仅为 POCCO-W 的 37.1%、GPU 显存更低、推理更快；扩大 WE-CA/POCCO-W 的深度或宽度并不能达到 WeCon 的水平，说明增益来自结构设计而非模型规模。(5) 可视化：WeCon 的权重/实例嵌入余弦相似度矩阵显示更高的表示共振；解码第一步的节点选择分布随权重平滑变化（WE-CA 几乎不变，POCCO-W 变化不规则），证实 RF 缓解了权重信号稀释。(6) 消融：移除编码器、GRF、双向交叉（BC）、RF 或替换为 RL/PO/BOPO 均导致性能下降，EPO 优于 RL、PO、BOPO；RF 即插即用性得到验证（WE-CA+RF 性能提升）。(7) 超参 k、c 不敏感；WS 分解优于 TCH。

Conclusion: 论文给出研究问题的肯定答案：WeCon 通过"编码器—解码器双向深度利用权重"实现了 SOTA 级别的求解质量与高推理效率的统一，是首个同时在编码器与解码器中充分挖掘权重信号并兼顾性能的 MOCOP 神经求解器；WeCon-CCO 则证明 RF 模块可与 MoE/CCO 等重型解码器协同，在所有评测设置上取得最佳整体 HV。EPO 表明改进偏好优化的采样过程（而非仅筛选偏好对）能有效提升训练效果。局限性方面，作者指出当前跨规模统一模型在单一规模上的表现可能不及针对该规模单独训练的模型，未来可引入课程学习（Curriculum Learning）以进一步增强跨规模泛化。

Abstract: Existing neural solvers for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems (MOCOPs) commonly adopt decomposition-based strategies that scalarize an MOCOP into multiple subproblems associated with distinct weight vectors. However, they either inject weights only once during decoding, limiting weight-conditioned context modeling, or primarily during encoding, causing weight-signal dilution during decoding. Moreover, preference optimization methods rely on purely random sampling to construct solution pairs for training solvers, which often produces less informative pairs and thus leads to low training effectiveness. To better address these limitations, we propose an efficient Weight-Conditioned neural solver (WeCon). Specifically, we design an encoder layer with three attention blocks and our proposed Gated Residual Fusion (GRF) block to facilitate harmonious interaction between instance features and weights, thereby generating informative weight-conditioned context. We further introduce a plug-and-play Residual Fusion (RF) block in the decoder to alleviate weight-signal dilution. Finally, we propose Efficient Preference Optimization (EPO), which constructs high-quality solutions, thereby generating more informative pairs to improve training effectiveness. Experiments on four MOCOP variants across different problem scales and distribution patterns demonstrate that WeCon achieves HyperVolume (HV) values comparable to SOTA solver POCCO-W, while reducing inference time by 40%. Ablation studies validate the contributions of all designs.

</details>


### [134] [Tensor Cache: Eviction-conditioned Associative Memory for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22884)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tensor Cache 提出一种两层缓存架构：L1 为滑动窗口注意力，L2 为快速权重记忆专用于存储被驱逐的 KV 对。核心贡献是识别并修复了分块均值训练中的虚假外积问题，通过并行加权求和扫描实现精确的逐令牌写入，使被驱逐令牌信息可被后续查询有效访问，在长上下文任务中提升了内存-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有滑动窗口缓存虽能限制内存增长，但完全丢弃窗口外的令牌，导致被驱逐的相关证据信息永久丢失。快速权重记忆虽然可以压缩存储，但直接应用于 Transformer 会产生大量虚假外积干扰。如何在有限内存约束下保留并有效利用被驱逐令牌的信息，是一个尚未解决的关键问题。

Method: Tensor Cache 采用两层缓存架构：L1 为标准的滑动窗口 softmax 注意力；L2 为固定大小的外积型快速权重记忆，通过矩阵 A 存储被驱逐的 KV 对。L2 读取利用线性注意力恒等式 q_t(k_i ⊗ v_i) = ⟨q_t, k_i⟩ v_i，仅需单次矩阵乘法即可完成查询。每层维护独立的矩阵 A，通过学习的每头衰减率和写入率参数调控记忆压缩，并通过标量门控自适应融合 L1 和 L2 输出。训练方面，修正了分块均值更新中引入的 C²-C 个虚假跨令牌外积问题，采用与逐令牌写入等效的并行加权求和扫描。

Result: 在系统扩展性、联想检索、长上下文语言建模和记忆容量诊断等多项测试中，Tensor Cache 均显著提升了有界状态基线方法的内存-质量边界。

Conclusion: Tensor Cache 通过两阶段缓存架构有效解决了滑动窗口注意力带来的信息丢失问题，恢复了被驱逐令牌信息的可访问性。关键改进包括：识别并修复了分块均值训练中的 C²-C 虚假外积问题；通过端到端学习的每头衰减率和写入率参数自适应控制记忆压缩；标量门控机制实现 L1/L2 输出的动态融合。在系统扩展性、联想检索、长上下文语言建模和记忆容量诊断等多个维度上均优于有界状态基线方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformer KV caches grow linearly with context length; sliding-window caching bounds memory but discards evicted tokens entirely, so relevant evidence outside the window becomes inaccessible. We introduce \emph{Tensor Cache}, a two-level cache that pairs sliding-window softmax attention as a first-level cache (L1) with a fixed-size outer-product fast-weight memory as a second-level cache (L2) fed by KV pairs evicted from the window. Recent tokens remain in exact local attention; evicted pairs are compressed into a per-layer matrix $A$ and read by future queries through a single matrix multiplication, exploiting the linear-attention identity $q_t(k_i \otimes v_i)=\langle q_t,k_i\rangle v_i$. A learned scalar gate fuses the L1 and L2 outputs, and per-head decay and write-rate parameters are trained end-to-end. The outer-product memory and the read identity are well-known; our contribution is their use as an L2 cache fed exclusively by sliding-window evictions, plus identifying that the common chunked-mean training shortcut $A\!\leftarrow\!λA\!+\!η(\bar k\!\otimes\!\bar v)$ silently introduces $C^2{-}C$ spurious cross-token outer products per chunk, and closing the gap with a parallel weighted-sum scan equivalent to per-token writes within float32 epsilon. Across systems scaling, controlled associative recall, long-context language modeling, and memory-capacity diagnostics, Tensor Cache improves the memory--quality frontier over bounded-state baselines.

</details>


### [135] [Push Your Agent: Measuring and Enforcing Quantitative Goal Persistence in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23574)
*Yuandao Cai,Yuzhang Zhu,Liyou Gao,Wensheng Tang,Shengchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QGP问题并构建PushBench基准，揭示当前语言智能体在定量目标持续性方面的不足。状态追踪和后备队列控制器可显著提升性能，但即使前沿模型在长时域任务上仍有明显退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长时域语言智能体虽然能做出看似合理的局部工具调用，但无法持续工作直到达到要求的计数目标。这反映了智能体在定量目标达成方面的系统性缺陷。

Method: 提出了定量目标持久性(QGP)概念和PushBench基准测试，用于评估仓库工件收集和验证器支持的工作单元。测试了状态追踪检索控制器、后备队列工作单元控制器，以及黑盒前沿智能体(Claude Code和Codex CLI)。

Result: 状态追踪检索控制器达到69-78%成功率并消除重复提交；后备队列工作单元控制器在标准控制器完全失败的任务中达到25-50%成功率；Claude Code和Codex CLI在50个工件任务上表现良好，但在100个工件时降至每条件3/9成功率。

Conclusion: 定量目标强调了一种与局部任务能力不同的可靠性要求：智能体必须保持已验证的进度，仅在请求的工作完成时才停止。这揭示了当前语言智能体在持续性任务上的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents can make many plausible local tool calls yet fail to persist until a requested count is actually complete. We study this gap as Quantitative Goal Persistence (QGP): whether an agent keeps working until an external verifier confirms enough distinct valid items. PushBench turns this into a benchmark for repository-artifact collection and verifier-backed work units, so repeated work, duplicate submissions, false completion, and progress drift are measured directly rather than hidden behind a final success flag. In matched controller comparisons, a state-tracking retrieval controller reaches 69-78% success while eliminating duplicate submissions, and a backlog-tracking work-unit controller reaches 25-50% success in settings where standard and completion-gated controllers complete no task instances. Black-box frontier-agent evaluations with Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6) and Codex CLI (gpt-5.4) solve many 50-artifact tasks but drop to 3 out of 9 successes per condition at 100 artifacts. The results show that quantitative goals stress a different reliability requirement from local task competence: agents must maintain verified progress and stop only when the requested work is complete.

</details>


### [136] [Pointwise Metrics Mislead: An Evaluation Protocol for Multimodal Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22891)
*Mads H. Baattrup,Jörn Bach,Laurids Jeppe,Finn Labe,Alexander Grohsjean,Christian Schwanenberger,Peer Stelldinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 揭示了科学重建评估中逐点度量的根本性缺陷：当后验分布存在非零宽度时，最小化MSE/MAE的逐点估计器会产生系统性偏差，压缩分布尾部、模态和形状等关键特征，提出包含CRPS、谱保真度和覆盖率校准的三段式评估协议作为替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学重建评估以RMSE、MAE等逐点度量为主，默认"误差越低越好"。作者指出该假设在多模态后验的逆问题中结构性地失效。

Method: 提出三段式评估协议：（1）逐事件分布精度（CRPS）；（2）群体级边缘精度（谱保真度诊断）；（3）不确定性可信度（覆盖率校准）。在具有解析后验的合成基准和粒子物理中的多对一逆问题上验证，发现点度量与分布度量下的模型排名会发生逆转，且校准分析可进一步区分CRPS下无法区分的架构。

Result: 基于方差定律，均方误差或平均绝对误差最小化训练的逐点估计器，其边缘谱宽必然严格小于真实后验，导致偏差与架构、训练和数据集大小无关。该偏差恰好压缩了下游科学测量所依赖的尾部、模态和形状等谱特征。

Conclusion: 评估协议而非模型决定了科学结论。传统的逐点度量（RMSE、MAE）会系统性地压缩后验分布的宽度，扭曲尾部、模态和形状等关键谱特征，这对下游科学测量有严重影响。

Abstract: Evaluation in scientific reconstruction is dominated by pointwise metrics - RMSE, MAE, per-event resolution - under the implicit assumption that lower error means better reconstruction. We show that this assumption fails structurally for inverse problems with multimodal posteriors. By the law of total variance, point estimators trained to minimize MSE or MAE produce a marginal spectrum strictly narrower than the truth whenever the posterior has nonzero width. The resulting bias is independent of architecture, training, and dataset size, and it compresses precisely the spectral features - tails, modes, shapes - that downstream scientific measurements rely on. We propose a three-part evaluation protocol where each step targets a failure mode the others miss: per-event distributional accuracy via CRPS, population-level marginal accuracy via a spectrum-fidelity diagnostic, and uncertainty trustworthiness via coverage-based calibration. On a synthetic benchmark with an analytic posterior and on a realistic many-to-one inverse problem from particle physics, model rankings reverse between pointwise and distributional metrics, and calibration further separates architectures indistinguishable under CRPS. The evaluation protocol, not the model, determines the scientific conclusion.

</details>


### [137] [From Residuals to Reasons: LLM-Guided Mechanism Inference from Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22897)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARICL框架通过LLM智能体分析基础模型的预测残差，识别其缺失的结构化知识，生成显式校正项，从而在保持预测性能的同时提供可解释的修正理由。在9个基准数据集上持续提升，并在蛋白质数据集上证明其校正公式能够跨批次泛化至符合生化规律的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性方法本质上是"检查性"的，只能回答"哪些特征重要"，却无法阐明特征间的交互机制，且无法随人类理解迭代改进解释。直接让LLM预测会迫使它搜索整个输出空间，效率低下。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Residual In-Context Learning (MARICL)框架：LLM智能体锚定基础模型的预测，分析高残差样本，推测缺失结构，通过多轮文本梯度优化生成显式校正项。关键创新是将"预测什么"转变为"基础模型遗漏了什么"这一更窄的问题。

Result: 在9个跨科学、生物医学、社会经济及合成场景的基准测试中，MARICL均一致优于基础模型。在Cell-Free Protein数据集上，冻结的公式在同试剂协议内超过92%的案例中改善预测；跨不同协议则系统性失效——成功边界与生化机制对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提升预测性能，更重要的是实现了机制层面的泛化能力，而非仅拟合批量特异性噪声，提供了可检验、可质疑、随理解迭代的科学解释。

Abstract: A persistent challenge in machine learning for scientific applications is jointly achieving prediction and understanding. Statistical models excel on structured data but operate as black boxes, while existing interpretability methods are largely inspective: they answer "which features matter?" but do not articulate how features interact or refine explanations iteratively alongside human understanding. Asking an LLM to predict the target directly forces it to search the entire output space; we instead anchor predictions with a base model and ask the LLM the narrower question of what that model is missing. We introduce Multi-Agent Residual In-Context Learning (MARICL), an agentic framework in which LLM agents analyze where a base-model fails, hypothesize missing structure from high-residual examples provided in context, and produce explicit correction terms refined through multi-turn textual gradient optimization. Across nine benchmarks spanning scientific, biomedical, socioeconomic, and synthetic settings, MARICL improves consistently over its base model on all datasets. To test whether these corrections reflect real structure or batch-specific noise, we freeze formulas learned on one experimental batch of the Cell-Free Protein dataset and apply them (with no retraining and no further LLM calls) to held-out batches. Within the same reagent protocol, the frozen formulas improve predictions in over 92% of cases; across a different protocol, they fail systematically. The success boundary aligns with the biochemistry, not the batch count; direct evidence of mechanistic generalization.

</details>


### [138] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A privacy-preserving two-stage federated recommendation system that uses cloud-based collaborative filtering for candidate generation and on-device re-ranking with sensitive signals, avoiding centralization of private user data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional mobile content personalization requires centralizing sensitive user data on servers, conflicting with modern privacy expectations and regional regulations.

Method: Two-stage pipeline: (1) Cloud-based collaborative filtering on non-sensitive app-context data to generate candidate shortlist; (2) On-device re-ranking using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients transmitted off-device.

Result: Validated on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and proprietary pilot dataset; production-ready Kotlin Multiplatform library enables deployment across Android and iOS.

Conclusion: The system validates effectively on MovieLens, UCI HAR, and proprietary data, delivering a production-ready Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on both Android and iOS.

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [139] [FIRMA: FIbonacci Ring Model Aggregation for Privacy-preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22898)
*Rachid Hedjam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FIRMA提出了一种无服务器、环拓扑、永久隐私保护的联邦学习协议族，包含三个渐进增强版本，通过Fibonacci权重混合、精度门控抑制和2-opt环置换解决现有FL协议的结构性三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习协议存在结构三难困境：基于服务器的聚合存在单点故障和梯度反转风险；去中心化环状协议暴露分类头且使用均匀权重；个性化方法则重新引入中心聚合。现有方法无法同时实现：无服务器运行、永久隐私保护分类头、环拓扑和非对称邻居加权。

Method: 提出FIRMA协议族，包含三个渐进版本：(1) \fibfl基础版：服务器自由环聚合，Fibonacci加权邻居混合，永久隐私保护分类头；(2) \fibflp增强版：添加精度门控邻居抑制，选择性降低收敛差的节点权重；(3) \fibflpp完整版：2-opt环置换最大化相邻客户端类别多样性，K_g=⌈N/2⌉轮 gossip实现全局覆盖，余弦退火自保留校准。建立收敛速率界及三个命题（归一化、覆盖、保留、多样性最优性）。

Result: 在28种配置（4个基准×7个异构场景）实验中，\fibflpp在所有12种标签偏斜配置中均超越\fedavg，在CIFAR-10、K=1时达到峰值优势+20.7pp；在Dirichlet异构条件下，\fibflpp在28种配置中17种达到最高精度，为Pareto最优协议。

Conclusion: FIRMA协议族成功解决了联邦学习的结构三难困境，在无服务器条件下实现了隐私保护、高精度和高效覆盖的统一，是目前唯一同时满足服务器自由、永久隐私头、环拓扑和原则性非对称加权的方法。

Abstract: Federated learning protocols face a structural trilemma: canonical server-based aggregation~\cite{mcmahan2017} creates a single point of failure and gradient inversion risk; decentralised ring-gossip alternatives~\cite{hu2019segmented} expose classification heads to semi-honest peers via uninformed uniform weights; and personalised methods~\cite{collins2021exploiting} reintroduce central aggregation. No existing protocol simultaneously achieves server-free operation, permanently private heads, ring topology, and principled asymmetric neighbour weighting. We propose FIRMA (\textbf{FI}bonacci \textbf{R}ing \textbf{M}odel \textbf{A}ggregation), a family of three progressively enhanced federated learning protocols: 1) \fibfl\ establishes the foundation: server-free ring aggregation with Fibonacci-weighted neighbour blending and permanently private classification heads. 2) \fibflp\ augments this with accuracy-gated neighbour suppression, selectively down-weighting poorly-converged peers while preserving the Fibonacci directional bias. 3) \fibflpp, the full system, completes the family with a 2-opt ring permutation that maximises adjacent-client class diversity, global ring coverage via $K_g{=}\lceil N/2\rceil$ gossip passes, and cosine-annealed self-retention calibration. We establish a convergence rate bound and three supporting propositions governing normalisation, coverage, retention, and diversity optimality. Systematic experiments across 28 configurations -- four benchmarks crossed with seven heterogeneity regimes -- demonstrate that \fibflpp\ surpasses \fedavg\ in all 12 label-skew configurations, with a peak advantage of $+20.7$\,pp on CIFAR-10 at $K{=}1$. Under Dirichlet heterogeneity, \fibflpp\ is the Pareto-dominant method among all server-free protocols, achieving the highest accuracy in 17 of 28 configurations.

</details>


### [140] [Expand More, Shrink Less: Shaping Effective-Rank Dynamics for Dense Scaling in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23191)
*Guoming Li,Shangyu Zhang,Junwei Pan,Wentao Ning,Jin Chen,Gengsheng Xue,Chao Zhou,Shudong Huang,Haijie Gu,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对可扩展推荐模型 RankMixer 中的"嵌入坍缩"（embedding collapse）问题，提出 RankElastor 架构。作者用有效秩（effective rank / stable rank）作为谱度量，实证发现 RankMixer 的 token mixing 与 P-FFN 交替产生"阻尼振荡"的有效秩轨迹（混合微增秩、FFN 收缩秩），并给出理论解释：块转置混合的秩扩张有界（erank ≤ 2(k+μ)），而 GELU 型逐 token FFN 在同次齐次性下会确定/概率地收缩有效秩。RankElastor 用（i）参数化全混合（可学习全秩混合矩阵 W，取代固定置换 P 与粗粒度块结构）和（ii）GLU 改进的 P-FFN（门控乘性项 + 可学习残差）替换上述两个模块，从而在理论上获得"秩可恢复"保证。在 Criteo/Avazu 工业级基准上 AUC 相对最强基线提升约 0.001（0.81482 vs 0.81375 / 0.79323 vs 0.79270），有效秩更高、缩放行为更优、训练开销仅增 10%–15%，并在 KuaiVideo/TaobaoAd 行为序列任务上同样领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中的模型扩展（scaling）是提升性能的核心路径，但已有研究指出表示学习会陷入 embedding collapse（嵌入矩阵近似低秩、多奇异值近零），从而限制表达力并使扩展收益递减。RankMixer 作为新近高效的扩展型架构（tokenization + token mixing + per-token FFN 交替堆叠）虽在多个基准上达到 SOTA，但此前从未从有效秩/谱角度系统考察其坍缩行为：具体缺少（1）RankMixer 跨层有效秩动态的实证刻画；（2）对"为何 mixing 能增秩、FFN 却缩秩"的理论解释；（3）针对该架构族坍缩的结构性改进方案。已有的坍缩缓解工作多针对特定推荐器（DCNv2、xDeepFM 等），不具普适性。

Method: 诊断部分：定义有效秩 erank(X)=Σσ_i²/max σ_i²=‖X‖_F²/‖X‖_2²（stable rank）；在 FuxiCTR 上对比 RankMixer（2 mixing + 2 P-FFN）与等深度 DCNv2/xDeepFM（MLP、AutoInt 亦为基线），发现常规模型呈单调秩衰减，RankMixer 呈混合增秩/FFN 缩秩的锯齿状阻尼振荡，末层有效秩仅略高于原始嵌入（Avazu 上收缩占主导、坍缩重现）。理论部分：定理 2.1 给出块转置混合 M=X+𝒯(X) 的有效秩界 2kμ/(√k+√μ)² ≤ erank(M) ≤ 2(k+μ)，并证明 rank(𝒯(X)) ≤ min{T, rd}，说明扩张有界；定理 2.2 证明逐行 FFN φ(XA)B 的坍缩失效：正齐次激活下 rank-1 输入只能产生至多 2 个输出方向（符号齐同时为 1），且在"谱集中+响应间隙"假设下 erank 以高概率按常数因子 α<1 收缩。方法部分提出 RankElastor：(1) 参数化全混合——将 RankMixer 的固定置换 vec(Mᵀ)=LN((P⊗I)vec(Xᵀ)+vec(Xᵀ)) 推广为 vec(Mᵀ)=LN((W+I)vec(Xᵀ))，W∈R^{TD×TD} 可学习，网格尺度细化到 d*=1，解除 Kronecker 约束；定理 3.1/C.1 证明 d*=1 时可达集为全空间 R^{T×D}，而任意 d*>1 的块混合可达集严格偏小（低有效秩时块张成子空间维数不足，缺失细粒度高秩坐标交互）。(2) GLU 改进的 P-FFN——Z_t=(GELU(M_tW₁)⊙(M_tW₂))W₃+M_tW_r，含提升投影、压缩投影与可学习残差；定理 3.2/D 证明乘性项诱导二次（degree-2）交互，rank(φ(XA)⊙(XC)) ≥ min{D, k(k+1)/2}，且在 m ≥ C·k·log D 时以高概率 erank(𝒢(X)) ≥ erank(X)+δ（通过正交 Frobenius 能量下界与 Jacobian 迹下界给出 δ = Θ(k log D/σ_max²(XD))）。复杂度：全混合 O(T²D²) 计算与参数（原为 O(TD) 无参数），GLU-P-FFN 为 O(T(3rD²+D²))、参数 (3r+1)D²（原 O(TrD²)/O(rD²)）。实验设置：FuxiCTR 框架，Criteo/Avazu（(T,D)=(15,26)/(16,24)，P-FFN 隐层维 D，GLU 扩展比 r=3），嵌入维 20/16，最多 100 epoch 早停，batch 4096/10000，10 次随机初始化取均值；指标 AUC/LogLoss 与有效秩；消融（去全混合、ReLU/GELU/GLU 式 FFN）、效率（每 epoch 时间、GPU 显存）、宽/深/联合密集参数缩放、行为序列任务（KuaiVideo、TaobaoAd 的 AUC/gAUC）。

Result: RQ1 主结果：RankElastor 在两个数据集上 AUC 与 LogLoss 均最优，Criteo AUC 0.81482/LogLoss 0.43730（RankMixer 0.81375/0.43799；DCNv2 0.81365），Avazu AUC 0.79323/LogLoss 0.37196（RankMixer 0.79270/0.37218），AUC 增益约 0.001，按工业级 CTR 标准具统计意义。RQ2 坍缩缓解：RankElastor 的逐样本有效秩分布位移显著大于 RankMixer，层间仍保留"混合扩张/FFN 收缩"交替模式但扩张更强、收缩更弱，全阶段平均有效秩稳定更高；在 RankMixer 坍缩明显的 Avazu 上 RankElastor 保持持续增长。RQ3 缩放：宽度、深度和联合缩放下 RankElastor 的 AUC 增益更大、LogLoss 更低，联合缩放优于单维缩放，与语言模型及 RankMixer 的观察一致。消融表明两模块存在明显协同：去掉全混合 AUC 降至 0.81413（Criteo），单用 GLU/ReLU/GELU 式 FFN 均退化（0.81326/0.81349/0.81393），而把 GLU 式 FFN 单独套到 RankMixer 上仅带来边际提升（0.81393），说明 GLU 的收益依赖强化的 mixing 能力。效率：训练每 epoch 时间仅增加 10%–15%，GPU 显存与 RankMixer 相当，仍优于多数基线。RQ4 泛化：KuaiVideo gAUC 0.6731/AUC 0.7514、TaobaoAd gAUC 0.5778/AUC 0.6522，均优于 RankMixer（0.6691/0.7482、0.5763/0.6508）及其他基线，并呈现相似的谱动态改善。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是：以有效秩/谱视角系统诊断 RankMixer 的嵌入坍缩，刻画出"阻尼振荡"的跨层秩轨迹，并从理论上定位其成因（块转置混合扩张有界 + 正齐次 FFN 的秩收缩）；据此提出 RankElastor，通过参数化全混合与 GLU 改进 P-FFN 实现"Expand More, Shrink Less"，并给出表达力与有效秩恢复的（高概率）理论保证，在工业级 CTR 与行为序列任务上同时提升精度、表示多样性与缩放效率。可延伸方向包括：更有表达力的 token mixing 机制、保谱非线性模块、token-transformation 类架构谱动态的更深入研究。主要局限（作者自述）：所识别的秩动力学依赖特定架构族，理论分析仅覆盖该类模型；此外全混合的 O(T²D²) 参数与计算在 token 数增大时扩展性受限，且各定理依赖初始化谱不相干、激活正齐次、小方差预激活窗口等较强假设，GLU 有效秩增量 δ 的证明中若干步为启发式期望估计与一阶 Taylor 近似，尚缺严格的有限样本精细界；消融中的"GLU 提升有限"也提示该组件的实际增益部分依赖 full mixing 的配合。

Abstract: Scaling recommendation models is a central challenge in recommender systems. Recently, RankMixer has emerged as an effective solution, operating on a unified token representation and alternating between token mixing and per-token feedforward networks (P-FFNs) to achieve scalable performance. However, RankMixer suffers from \textit{embedding collapse}, where learned representations have low effective rank, limiting expressivity and underutilizing the expanded representation space. Through empirical analysis and theoretical insights, we identify rigid token mixing and P-FFN modules as the primary causes of this phenomenon, jointly inducing a \textbf{damped oscillatory trajectory} in effective-rank evolution across layers. To address it, we propose RankElastor, a novel architecture that produces spectrum-robust representations with provable collapse mitigation. RankElastor introduces two components: (i) \textbf{parameterized full mixing}, which enables expressive token mixing with improved spectral robustness; and (ii) \textbf{GLU-improved P-FFNs}, which stabilize representation spectra through GLU-style FFN modules. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that RankElastor consistently improves recommendation performance, mitigates embedding collapse, and exhibits robust scaling behavior. Code is available at this GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/RankElastor

</details>


### [141] [Transcoders Trace Visual Grounding and Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22902)
*Dimitrios Damianos,Leon Voukoutis,Georgios Skyrianos,Vassilis Katsouros,Georgios Paraskevopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以"功能为中心"的可解释性框架，用 Transcoder（MLP 子层的稀疏近似）替代稀疏自编码器（SAE）来分解 Gemma 3-4B-IT 的视觉语言计算过程，构建了从图像 patch 到生成 token 方向的可解释计算通路。相较 SAE，Transcoder 的归因在视觉基础 token 上作用更强、更稳定，且与语义相关图像区域对齐更好；通过"虚假视觉接地"反事实实验验证了通路的特异性。进一步从电路追踪中提取图结构特征训练逻辑回归分类器，对幻觉生成的预测 AUC 达 0.68。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视觉语言模型（VLM）在多模态推理上表现优异，但其内部如何将视觉输入转换为文本表征仍缺乏理解。已有的可解释性工作主要使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）对静态残差表示做分解，这类方法只能捕捉表征的静态成分，无法揭示驱动跨模态交互的"功能性更新"——即模型在层间计算中真正做出的动态变换。作者因此需要一种能够追踪层内计算因果过程的工具，用于定位视觉信息如何具体影响 token 生成。

Method: 采用 Transcoder 替代 SAE：Transcoder 是对 MLP 子层的稀疏近似，可作为层级计算的因果代理。整体流程为：(1) 在 Gemma 3-4B-IT 上部署 Transcoder，将模型分解为可解释的计算通路；(2) 用归因（attribution）方法评估 Transcoder 与 SAE 在视觉基础 token 上的因果效应强度与稳定性，并检验归因位置与语义相关图像区域的对齐程度；(3) 设计"False Visual Grounding"反事实分析（人为制造与图像不符的视觉条件）以验证所恢复通路是否特异于视觉-语言交互；(4) 从 Transcoder 产生的电路追踪（circuit traces）中抽取基于图的机制特征（如通路的拓扑/连接结构），训练逻辑回归分类器以预测幻觉生成。

Result: Transcoder 归因在视觉基础（visually grounded）token 上产生比 SAE 归因更强、更稳定的干预效应（patch ablation 条件下），且其归因的图像区域与语义相关区域对齐更佳；反事实分析表明恢复出的通路确实特异于视觉-语言交互，而非通用文本机制。基于机制图特征的逻辑回归分类器对幻觉生成达到 AUC 0.68，表明电路结构本身携带可用于检测幻觉的信号。

Conclusion: 以功能为中心的电路分解（function-centric circuit decomposition）能够为多模态计算提供可解释且具预测能力的刻画：Transcoder 相比 SAE 更适合定位跨模态的因果通路，结构化电路特征还可作为幻觉的机制性判据。局限在于分类器性能仅为中等水平（AUC 0.68），说明图特征虽有效但尚不充分，未来可结合更丰富的特征或更强基线与干预性实验进一步提升。

Abstract: Generative Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on multimodal reasoning, but how visual inputs are transformed to text remains poorly understood. Existing interpretability work on VLMs uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), which decompose static residual representations and miss the functional updates that drive cross-modal interaction. We adopt a function-centric framework based on Transcoders, sparse approximations of MLP sublayers that act as a causal proxy for layer-wise computation. Applied to Gemma 3-4B-IT, the framework decomposes the model into interpretable computational pathways linking image patches to directions in token generation. Transcoder attributions produce stronger and more stable effects on visually grounded tokens under patch ablation than SAE attributions, and align better with semantically relevant image regions. A False Visual Grounding counterfactual analysis confirms that the recovered pathways are specific to vision-language interaction.Finally, we perform a structural analysis of hallucinated generations, by extracting graph-based indicators from circuit traces produced by the transcoders. A logistic classifier over these mechanistic graph features predicts hallucinations at AUC $0.68$. These results show that function-centric circuit decomposition yields interpretable and predictive accounts of multimodal computation in VLMs.

</details>


### [142] [Is Dimensionality a Barrier for Retrieval Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23556)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Jonathan Kogan,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"最大间隔嵌入"（maximal-margin embedding）的理论角度解释了为什么低维嵌入（d≈1000）仍能支撑十亿级语料的检索：核心结论是"低秩饱和"——只要维度以对数速度随数据规模增长，即可在远低于 Weller et al. (2026) 所给维度的情形下达到与无限维几乎相同的最大间隔。具体地，对 k-sparse 关联矩阵 A=S_{n,k}，达到最优间隔 Θ(k^{-1/2}) 所需的维度恰为 Θ(k log(n/k))（上下界匹配，完全解决了 Weller et al. 的遗留问题）；此外给出 d=o(k log(n/k)) 时的显式构造，并在自由嵌入模型上实测发现 sigmoid 损失显著优于 InfoNCE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索系统普遍使用 d≤4096（典型 d=768）的稠密嵌入配合最近邻搜索：低维带来低成本缓存与快速检索，但低维（低秩）限制了可实现的几何构型，与"表示质量"存在张力。令人意外的是，如 DPR 在 d=768 下仍能在约 2×10^7 段落库上取得优异表现。作者提出核心问题 Q：低维嵌入为何能在超大规模数据上成功？文献中此前只有负向结果（Weller et al. 2026 给出维度与间隔的上界关系 d ≥ log C(n,k)/log(1+1/m)），缺少"同时兼顾大间隔（质量）与小维度（紧凑性）"的正向结果；Ben-David et al. (2002) 的 sign-rank 界 sr(A)=O(m^{rd}(+∞,A)^{-2} log(nN)) 仅保证存在正间隔嵌入，且对 S_{n,k} 给出 O(k² log n) 的维度依赖。作者以间隔作为"表示质量"的核心代理量（间隔控制对扰动的鲁棒性、量化容忍度、组合泛化能力），并借用 Weller et al. 的 top-k 检索模型来回答该问题。

Method: 模型与设定：关联矩阵 A∈{0,1}^{N×n} 编码查询-文档相关性；定义"margin-m、relative-bias-τ 嵌入"，并取 τ=0（可加一维无损化为相对偏置 τ，间隔按 1/(1+|τ|) 缩放）；定义受限维度最大间隔 m^rd(d,A)、无维度限制的间隔复杂度 m^rd(+∞,A)（与 discrepancy 等价，至多差常数因子 8），以及 sign-rank sr(A)。核心工具：(1) 关键引理 1——把"存在查询向量 U"刻画为凸包条件：⟨U,V_i⟩≥m（符号为 +）⟺ conv({σ_iV_i}) 中所有向量范数 ≥ m，从而可非构造性地给出查询嵌入；(2) Johnson–Lindenstrauss 引理的新用法：只对文档向量的凸组合（符号和的 T 项平均）保持范数近似，而非保持所有 Nn 个内积，配合 Maurey 引理（存在 T 项平均逼近凸包中任一点，误差 ≤T^{-1/2}）恢复出维度 O(ε^{-4}m^{-2} log n) 的近似最优嵌入；(3) 下界用体积/球打包论证，但与前人不同：不对 {U_j}、{V_i} 直接打包，而是构造由 {V_i} 线性组合而成的半径 Ω(m√k)、数量 exp(Ω(k log(n/k))) 的打包点集（基于二元 k-sparse 集合族与符号最小范数组合 Lemma 2/3）；(4) 谱界 m^rd(+∞,A) ≤ inf‖J‖_op（可凸优化计算），给出 S_{n,k} 的 (1+o_k(1))/(2√k) 上界并配以显式构造达下界；(5) 自 Khatri–Rao 提升构造：取 i.i.d. 球面向量 a_i，V_i=vec(a_i a_i^T)，U_j=vec(P_j)（P_j 为相关文档所张成 k 维子空间的投影矩阵），用 Beta 分布尾界 + 联合界控制负样本内积，最后加一维偏置归一化为 margin-±m；(6) 压缩感知/RIP 文献（Candès–Tao、Foucart 等）作为辅助工具与概念来源；(7) 实验：在自由嵌入模型上对 S_{n,k}（k=2,3）用 InfoNCE 与 sigmoid（SigLIP）损失训练 10 万步，比较达正间隔所需最小维度与所得最大间隔。

Result: 定理1.4（主结果）：对任意 ε>0，存在 C_ε=O(ε^{-4})，使 m^rd(C_ε·m^rd(+∞,A)^{-2}·log min(n,N), A) ≥ (1-ε)·m^rd(+∞,A)；对 k-sparse 行 A 给出维度 O(k log n)，改进了 Ben-David et al. (2002)（后者维度 O(k² log n) 且只保证 sign rank）。定理1.5（不可能性）：n≥3k 时，m^rd(d,S_{n,k})>0 蕴含 d ≥ C·k log(n/k)/log(1+2/(m√k))；结合定理1.4/Corollary 4 得：获得 margin Θ(k^{-1/2}) 的最小维度恰为 Θ(k log(n/k))，从而完全解决 Weller et al. (2026) 的 S_{n,k} 设定（并给出一般 k-sparse 矩阵的推广版 Theorem F.1，基于"shattered"支撑族）。定理1.6（更小维度的构造）：d ≥ (k²+5k+7)/2 且 r=⌊(√(8d−7)−1)/2⌋=√(2d)+O(1) 时，m^rd(d,A) ≥ Δ/(2(√k+1)−Δ)，其中 Δ=Θ((r−k)/r·((n-k)N)^{-2/(r-k)})；特例：d→∞ 时恢复 (1+o_k(1))/(2√k)；d=(k²/2)(1+α)² 时 margin ≥ (1/(2√k))·n^{-2(k+1)/(αk)}，即在 Θ(k²) 维度获得任意逆多项式间隔。Corollary 2：由 Alon et al. 的 2k+1 维 Vandermonde 多项式构造给出 m^rd(2k+1,A) ≥ (2n)^{-2k}(2k)^{-1/4}。Corollary 1：m^rd(+∞,S_{n,k}) = (1+o_k(1))/(2√k)；另得 k=1 时的精确上界 m^rd(+∞,I_n) ≤ n/(3n−4)。间隔意义方面：Proposition 4/5 表明 margin m 的嵌入可检索至多 k+2km/(1−m) 个对象的组合（向上泛化），且当 m ≥ (k−1)/(2n−k−1) 时可检索所有 1..k 大小的子集（向下泛化，联系 k-邻接多面体）；Proposition 6 给出 L-Lipschitz 编码器下扰动 < m/L 时检索不变；Proposition 8 表明 m/4-网量化后仍保持 m/2 间隔。实验：sigmoid 损失在远低维度（接近理论最优的 5 维）即可获得正间隔且几乎不随 n 增长，其所得间隔也一致高于 InfoNCE（InfoNCE 与 Weller et al. 的实验一致，需 d=Θ(n^{1/3})）；Proposition 9 说明当 A 的行稀疏度 >1 时 InfoNCE 损失无法收敛到 0（甚至发散），而 sigmoid 损失的全局极小点恰与 margin 嵌入重合（Proposition 7），这解释了两者差距。

Conclusion: 低维并非检索模型的障碍：最优质量的嵌入只需随数据规模对数增长的维度即可获得，因此可以给出编码器维度的精确建议，并区分"间隔"与其他"表示质量"概念的理论刻画。定理1.4与定理1.5共同表明在 S_{n,k} 模型中维度–间隔权衡的精确刻画，方法学上把问题与压缩感知/RIP、sign-rank、间隔复杂度等文献相连通。局限：在 d=o(k log(n/k)) 区间定理1.6的下界与定理1.5的上界仍不匹配；实验仅在自由嵌入模型（非真实数据集与架构）中进行，故 InfoNCE 在真实任务或检索之外性质上是否更有优势尚无定论——这被作者列为两个重要的未来方向。作者声明无直接负面社会影响，并说明LLM仅用于部分证明补全、文献检索、代码生成与润色（初始手稿无LLM参与，所有代码经人工校验）。

Abstract: Why does the low dimensionality of representations, typically $d\approx 1000$, not prevent modern embedding-based retrieval models from scaling to billions, or even trillions, of data points? To answer this question, we study maximal-margin embeddings in the following retrieval model, classically studied in communication complexity [PS86] and more recently in embedding-based retrieval [WBNL26]. Let $A\in \{0,1\}^{N\times n}$ be a matrix indicating whether each of $N$ queries is relevant to each of $n$ documents. We are interested in the largest margin $m>0,$ denoted by $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(d, A),$ for which there exist unit norm embeddings of the queries and documents $\{U_j\}_{j = 1}^N, \{V_i\}_{i = 1}^n$ with the following property. $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \ge m$ whenever $A_{ji} = 1$ and $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \le -m$ otherwise. A large margin is a key proxy for representation quality: it controls both robustness to perturbations and compositional generalization across queries. Our main theorem establishes that the best possible margin without a restriction on the dimension, $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A),$ can be nearly achieved in dimension $d = O(\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A)^{-2}\log n)$ which improves a theorem of [BDES02]. Together with a matching lower bound in Theorem 1.5, we conclude that when $A\in \{0,1\}^{\binom{n}{k}\times n}$ is the matrix containing all possible $k$-sparse rows once, dimension $d = O(k\log (n/k))$ is necessary and sufficient for the maximal possible margin $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A) = Θ(k^{-1/2})$ in this setting. This fully resolves the setup of [WBNL26]. We also give several constructions for large margins when $d = o(k\log (n/k)).$ Finally, we empirically test the InfoNCE and sigmoid losses for producing large margin embeddings and demonstrate a clear advantage of the sigmoid loss.

</details>


### [143] [Human-Centered Learning Mechanics: A Dynamical Framework for Entropy-Regulated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22940)
*Kim Phuc Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出「人类中心学习力学」(HCLM) 框架，把带熵正则的训练视为开放、受控的动力系统，而非封闭的优化问题。核心洞见：熵正则只有在所选熵代理沿优化轨迹产生「非退化信息力」时才有意义——梯度非零本身既不保证预测有效改变（可能因表征塌缩、尺度膨胀或「预测零空间」方向而无用），也不保证与任务/受保护风险约束相容。论文以噪声标定的对数行列式熵 H_ν(Z)=½logdet(I+C_Z/ν) 为对象，给出精确谱力公式（Thm 4.1）、协方差误差敏感性（Prop 4.2）、保函数缩放反例（Prop 4.3）、读出正则性下界（Prop 4.4）、可观测/预测零空间度量分解（Thm 5.2）、标量反馈的完整可行区间与冲突角锐界（Thm 6.1–6.3）、有界保任务干预的锐极限（Thm 6.4）、多受保护风险下的精确极小极大可行性证书与鲁棒版本（Thm 7.2–7.3）、受保护锥的熵容量（Thm 7.4）、屏障式阈值保持（Thm 7.5）、有限步与新数据审计证书（Prop 7.6、Thm 7.7），以及区分「新鲜输入表征信息」与「样本码信息」的泛化定理（Thm 8.2）。数值验证（有限差分、原对偶比对、最大值原理检查）误差在 1e-9~1e-16 量级；digits 小规模实验中「投影式熵控制」测试交叉熵略优于 ERM，而标量控制器虽逐步满足任务下降证书，却在 93.05% 步中熵请求不可行、最终熵比 1.727（目标 0.8），说明「遵守优先级约束」≠「达成目标」。注意：所给元数据摘要对应较早版本（含缩放律/Wasserstein 流表述），而 arXiv 全文为 2026-10-16 修订版，主题已转向「受保护风险下的可观测熵控制」，二者结论需分别对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习常被视为参数空间中的动力学过程，但既有理论多把训练当作封闭的优化系统，无法覆盖真实 AI 中的不确定性、资源约束、分布漂移、下游决策风险与人类反馈。论文指出三个具体障碍：(1) 表征几何无法仅由预测辨识——等比例放大编码器、逆向缩放读出层可保持预测不变，故熵代理的标量变化可能只反映尺度变化而非信息变化；(2) 任务梯度与熵梯度可能强烈冲突，使任何非负标量系数都无法同时满足两个速率要求（例如 a=c=1、b=−0.9、κ=1/2 时需 β≥0.9 与 β≤5/9，无解）；(3) 表征对新鲜输入的信息量与训练样本级信息量是不同通道，前者受控并不自动给出算法化泛化界。此外，作者强调「熵下降不是安全准则」：熵减少可能不改变预测，甚至伴随受保护群体风险上升。因此需要一个把「可观测性」「可行性」「受保护风险」「人类授权」显式化的条件化学习理论。

Method: 形式化：编码器 h_θ + 读出 r_θ，度量 M≻0 的自然梯度流 θ̇=−M^{-1}(g+βh)，记 a=gᵀM⁻¹g、b=gᵀM⁻¹h、c=hᵀM⁻¹h。主要技术链条：
1) 表示几何与「力」的定义：H_ν(Z)=½logdet(I_p+C_Z/ν)（噪声标定的对数体积），Thm 4.1 给出精确谱式 ∇_ZH_ν=Z_c(C_Z+νI)^{-1}/(m−1) 及 ‖∇_ZH_ν‖²_F=Σλ_j/(λ_j+ν)²≤r/(4(m−1)ν)；每个谱项在 λ_j=ν 处最大、在 λ→0 或 ∞ 时趋零，故「力小」是歧义信号（塌缩或尺度过大）。Prop 4.2 给出 |ΔH|≤pδ/(2ν) 与力误差 ≤‖Z_c‖_F δ/((m−1)ν²)。Prop 4.3：h→ah、W→W/a 保预测而 H→0，熵变化方向预测导数为零。Prop 4.4：B-Lipschitz 读出且输出方差 ≥v 时 H_ν≥½log(1+v/(B²ν))，排除任意体积塌缩。
2) 可观测性：Thm 5.2 定义 M-正交投影 P=M^{-1}J_Fᵀ(J_FM^{-1}J_Fᵀ)^†J_F，得到 u_obs/u_null 的 Pythagoras 分解（J_F u_null=0）、坐标不变性（度量随重参数化变换）。
3) 可行反馈控制：Thm 6.1 给出满足「任务下降比例 κ」与「熵速率请求 r」的精确标量区间 [β_lo, β_hi]；Cor 6.2 锐界 b²≤(1−κ)ac（角度形式 cos²ϑ≤1−κ）；Thm 6.3 将 c 换成 c_obs=|PM^{-1}h|_M² 即可观测限制版，c_obs=0 时任何正的熵下降请求不可行；Thm 6.4 给出保任务、范数预算 B₀ 下最大额外熵下降率 B₀√c_eff，c_eff=c−min(b,0)²/a，最优方向 q=h−(b/a)g（PCGrad 型投影）。
4) 有限步与误差：Thm 6.5 用下降引理给出步长限制 η≤κ/(ℓ_L C_v)、任务下降与 a_t→0；给出梯度误差下的鲁棒证书 ĝᵀv−δ_g‖v‖_M≥κ(‖ĝ‖+δ_g)²、ĥᵀv−δ_h‖v‖_M≥r，以及带噪更新的期望界。
5) 受保护风险与人类授权：Prop 7.1 给出「总体改善却伤害受保护群体」的精确反例（θ∈(ϖ,1) 时任务与熵同时下降但 R₁ 严格上升，且任意速度方向都无法兼顾）。Thm 7.2 将任务/熵/风险行统一为 d_iᵀu≤q_i，并用凸最小最大与支撑函数给出 ε*(U,B)=max_{λ∈Δ_N}{−Σλq̄_i−B‖P_U M^{-1}Σλd̄_i‖_M}，ε*≤0 当且仅当可行；正残差即不可行性证书（对偶乘子为冲突组合，非道德权重）。Thm 7.3 给出行误差不确定集下的鲁棒证书 ε_rob 与「误差支配则任何预算均不可行」的判据。Thm 7.4：受保护齐次锥 K 的熵容量 = B‖p_H‖_M（p_H 为 z_H=−M^{-1}h 在 K 上的度量投影），p_H=0 时无论预算多大均无容量。Thm 7.5：屏障型条件 k_jᵀθ̇+∂_tR_j−ḋ_j≤−γ_j e_j 导出 e_j(t)≤e_j(0)e^{−γ_jt}（含扰动项为 ω̄_j/γ_j）。Prop 7.6：有限步风险上界 U_j(s)，需同时检验 s=0 与 s=η 才能保证整段不越界（切向方向在曲边界处会失败，如 R=‖θ‖²、θ=(1,0)、w=(0,1)）。Thm 7.7：新鲜审计批 + Hoeffding + TV 漂移界 + 可数并界（α_jt=6α/(π²J_R(t+1)²)）给出同时检查点的部署风险证书。Cor 7.8 组合任务下降、有限步阈值与审计事件并要求授权标志有效。Prop 7.9：延迟响应需预留 m_j≥D_jΔ 的风险余量。
6) 泛化通道：Prop 8.1 反例——样本依赖编码器可使 I(X;h_S(X)+ξ|S)=0、协方差为 0，而 0-1 损失期望泛化差 ½−Φ(−A/√ν)>0（数值核对 A/√ν=3 时为 0.4986501）；Thm 8.2 改为约束「训练样本→学习器」的带噪样本码通道 S→(Q,V)→W，给出 |E[R−R̂]|≤√(2σ_ℓ²I(S;W|V)/n)≤√(σ_ℓ² E_V logdet(I+C_V/ν)/n)；Cor 8.3 给出多次顺序释放的信息预算 Σ_t E logdet(I+C_t/ν_t)，暗示若该和为 O(n^α)（α<1）则界为 O(n^{(α−1)/2})，但该条件本身仍待证明。
所用数学工具（高斯最大熵界、自然梯度、正交投影、下降引理、凸最小最大、支撑函数、Hoeffding、相对熵变分不等式）均为既有工具并逐一标注归属；论文明确不主张通用优化原理或普适安全/精度改进。

Result: 数值验证：有限差分检查任务梯度最大误差 2.78×10⁻¹⁰、归一化对数行列式梯度 7.74×10⁻¹¹；100 个随机矩阵上谱恒等式误差 2.78×10⁻¹⁶，度量变换下力范数一致到 5.16×10⁻¹³；独立约束优化器在 50 个随机例上验证干预速率公式到 4.97×10⁻¹³，另核对 404 个标量区间例；受保护风险扩展中 40 组原始—对偶比对 Eq.(37) 最大偏差 7.72×10⁻⁹、40 组锥容量比对 5.84×10⁻¹⁰、原始可行性残差 ≤4.95×10⁻⁹（其中一个 SLSQP 解报 line-search 状态而非 success，但残差与独立对偶间隙满足同一 10⁻⁷ 容差，原始状态被保留）；100 个二次风险例在每段 21 个点上未见上界违反；40 组鲁棒行误差公式比对最大偏差 3.31×10⁻¹¹。精确的零支撑与不确定性支配反例验证「不可行性可持续于任意预算」。真实数据实验（scikit-learn digits，1797 样本，10 类，64 维；固定分层划分 1077/360/360；16 个 tanh 隐单元 + 线性读出；300 步全批量；20 个公共初始化；H=H_0.1(Z)/16；Armijo 回溯）：ERM 测试 CE 0.2274±0.0032 / 准确率 94.33%；weight decay（λ=1e-4）0.2284 / 94.33%；固定熵正则（β=0.01）0.2277 / 94.32%；仅范数匹配 0.2394 / 94.24%（更差）；投影式熵控制 0.2232 / 94.57%（最优但幅度小，且其泛化间隙略大于 ERM，说明更低测试损失不能归因于熵本身改变了泛化）；标量反馈控制器（κ=1/2、k=0.1、H★=0.8H₀）测试 CE 0.3092 / 93.83%，熵请求在平均 93.05% 的步中不可行，最终熵比 1.727 远高于目标 0.8——尽管其任务下降证书在每步都满足，作者强调这不是「熵控制成功」。运行中未出现任务损失上升，但仅验证该设定下的实现性保护，不覆盖随机、延迟反馈或群体风险安全。

Conclusion: 结论要点：熵调节包含三个可分离要求——(i) 力必须存在；(ii) 其作用必须相对预测映射被辨识（可观测 vs 预测零空间）；(iii) 请求的干预必须与受保护目标相容。非零梯度只满足第一项。对数行列式力具有非单调谱响应，保函数缩放可在不改变确定性预测的情况下降低其标量值；标量反馈下任务下降与熵速率要求定义出可能为空的显式区间，且冲突角给出锐界；把干预限制在可观测方向会进一步收紧可行集（甚至为空），揭示「表面在控的熵控制可能无可行效果」。统计层面必须区分新鲜输入表征信息与训练样本级信息：前者可在记忆型预测器中归零而不阻止泛化失败；后者在带噪样本码与 Markov 条件下才支撑条件化泛化界。风险层面：总体任务损失与熵的同时下降可严格提升受保护群体风险，因此需要独立的、显式指定的受保护风险约束，其与可观测干预预算的交集具有精确可行性证书（原始—对偶/最小最大），受保护齐次锥具有精确熵容量；大规模未行误差下的可行集具有精确鲁棒证书。人类权威的形式化落点是「规格指定与发布/暂停授权」，而非把压缩等同于对齐或认证人类福祉；作者明确声明数学不识别压缩与对齐、不含人类反馈研究。局限：所有保证均为条件化（有限步需曲率与误差裕度、群体风险需新鲜审计与分布漂移界、延迟覆写需余量与已验证回退）；跨架构、随机反馈、分布漂移、联邦与缩放律等均列为待做的实验与理论工作（第 10 节给出分阶段研究纲领，含「缩放应作为预测问题而非假定幂律」）。综合评价：这是一个理论贡献清晰、自我约束良好的条件化可行性理论，价值主要在于把「熵正则是否真正起作用且可被授权」转化为可证伪的证书与反例；但所用凸几何与信息论工具均为标准工具，其新意在于学习问题的联合规格化表述与精确障碍刻画，且实验规模小（单一 digits 数据集、300 步、20 种子），不足以支撑性能或安全优势的经验结论。另需注意版本差异：所给元数据摘要（含收敛性、熵流、Wasserstein 梯度流、缩放律式条件解释的三项贡献）对应较早稿，而 arXiv 全文为修订版，主线已改为可观测熵控制 + 受保护风险，缩放律部分被改写为「未来研究纲领」，不应把两版结论混用。

Abstract: Deep learning is increasingly viewed as a dynamical process in parameter space, yet many existing theories still treat training as a closed optimization system. This view is limited for real-world AI, where models operate under uncertainty, resource constraints, distribution shift, downstream decision risks, and human feedback. We propose Human-Centered Learning Mechanics (HCLM), a dynamical and information-theoretic framework for open and controlled learning systems. The central idea is that entropy regularization is useful only when the chosen entropy surrogate generates a non-degenerate information force along the optimization trajectory. Otherwise, entropy terms may produce weak, unstable, or misaligned gradients, causing the dynamics to collapse toward ordinary loss minimization. We introduce the notion of effective entropy and study tractable geometric entropy surrogates, including variance-based and log-determinant covariance proxies. The paper makes three contributions. First, it formalizes entropy regularization through effective information force and characterizes degenerate entropy regimes. Second, it derives convergence, entropy-flow, Wasserstein-gradient-flow, and noisy-representation generalization results under explicit assumptions. Third, it offers a conditional dynamical interpretation of scaling-law-like behavior as a balance between information injection, entropy dissipation, and residual risk, without claiming an unconditional derivation of empirical neural scaling laws. Controlled representation-learning experiments support the hypothesis that geometric entropy surrogates, especially log-determinant covariance entropy, induce stronger and more stable information forces than softmax-normalized entropy.

</details>


### [144] [FederatedRSF : Federated Random Survival Forests for Partially Overlapping Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22954)
*Maryam Moradpour,Jonas Harriehausen,Amirreza Aleyasin,Lion Philipp Wolf,Youngjun Park,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FederatedRSF是一个联邦生存分析框架，通过仅交换特征兼容的生存树实现跨机构合作，同时保护数据隐私，解决了多中心场景下特征异质性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心生存预测可提高模型鲁棒性和泛化能力，但患者临床和基因组数据的隐私性限制了数据共享，且不同机构间的特征空间存在异质性（特征集仅部分重叠）。

Method: 通过聚合各中心本地训练的生存树，仅重分配特征兼容的树到各站点，实现基于部分特征重叠的联邦学习推理。

Result: 联邦模型在GBSG2乳腺癌队列上使用Harrell's C-Index评估，经重复交叉验证和站点分割测试，性能与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: FederatedRSF可实现与集中式训练相当的性能，为多中心生存预测提供了一种保护隐私的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-center survival prediction can improve robustness and generalizability, yet privacy regulations and institutional governance often prevent pooling patient-level clinical and genomic data across institutions. In practice, deployment is further complicated by feature-space heterogeneity, in which sites collect different covariates or use different sequencing panels, resulting in only partially overlapping feature sets. We present FederatedRSF, a Python package that implements federated random survival forests, aggregating locally trained survival trees and redistributing only feature-compatible trees to each site, enabling inference with partial overlap without sharing raw data. We evaluate FederatedRSF on the GBSG2 breast cancer cohort distributed with the scikit-survival package, simulating feature heterogeneity across clients by withholding subsets of features, and assessing discrimination using Harrell's concordance index (C-Index) under repeated cross-validation and site-splits. The results demonstrated that the federated model can achieve performance comparable to that of the centralized training setting.

</details>


### [145] [Certification from Examples is Hard for Circuits and Transformers under Minimal Overparametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22964)
*Artur Back de Luca,Kimon Fountoulakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"从样本认证"（exact certification）：仅凭带标签的输入-输出样例，最少需要多少条样例才能唯一确定学习到的假设等于目标函数。作者证明这种"证书规模"（certificate size）对假设类极度敏感——仅仅做常数级的过参数化（如阈值电路多加一个门、log-精度 Transformer 多加一个注意力头+6 个嵌入/残差坐标），就能把认证难度从多项式推到指数级：深度 ≥2 的 TC^0 电路中 cert(f*, TC^0_{d,s+1}) ≥ 2^t；Transformer 中同理；且这些下界是"逐目标"的（对原类中每个目标成立），并通过有限总体精确计算验证了构造电路与训练 Transformer 的实验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型在推理与算法任务上虽能取得高平均准确率，却仍存在不一致行为；仅凭平均准确率无法证明模型实现了预期计算。György 等人已提出 exact certification 是精确学习中的基础难题（以比特比较任务的最坏情形为例）。但此前缺少对"需要多少样例才能认证"的定量刻画，且已有结果（如 Servedio 对单调电路的证明）只给出单个最坏目标的界。本文目标：给出"最小过参数化即导致指数认证困难"的定量形式，并说明这种困难在被动（仅输入-输出）认证协议下无法用容差放宽来消除。

Method: 语义化的证书定义：cert(f*, H) = 使版本空间 VS(S) 中所有假设在全域上与 f* 一致的最小带标签样例集 S 的大小；另定义近似证书 cert_ε（统一误差 ≤ ε）与 cert_R（绝对错误数 ≤ R）。核心组合工具是"不交分歧原则"（Prop 4.1/A.1）：若类中存在 |T| 个假设，其与目标的分歧集 E_π 非空且两两不交，则 cert ≥ |T|，因为单个带标签样例至多排除一个"欺骗者"(deceiver)。具体构造采用"触发块"机制：取 t 个坐标 I，按模式 π 把输入域划分为 2^t 个两两不交的块 B_π，构造在 B_π 内覆盖输出、块外与目标一致的欺骗者。电路侧：额外一个阈值门 CHECK_{I,π} 检测 x_I = π，并把该位以足够大权重 ±M 注入顶层门，实现同深度"一门覆写"（AC^0 需 +1 深度、+2 门；受限扇入需 Θ(t) 门、Θ(log t) 深度）。Transformer 侧（projected-pre-norm、averaging hard attention、log 精度）：把残差流扩展到 m+6 维并用 4 维代码块 α/β/γ（均值 0、单位范数，是 layer_norm 的不动点）编码"位置是否在 I 内/是否匹配 π"；新增一个注意力头以常数 query q†=(2,-1,1,0) 与 value v=-c2-c3，利用 AHAT 的平均硬注意力取最大分数位置，输出触发位 z' = 1[x∈B_{I,π}]，写入两个辅助坐标；最终读出加上 ±F·z（F 为该精度最大可表示值）以饱和方式强制输出 σ，从而在同精度、同深度下实现块覆写。非空性（non-vacuity）用有限性论证：有限类必存在证书大小 ≤ ⌈log₂|H|⌉ 的目标（Lemma A.1），配合电路/Transformer 类的假设计数上界 2^{poly(n)}。近似情形由 Prop 4.2 统一给出：ε_n < 2^{-t-1} 或 R_n < 2^{n-t-1} 时证书仍 ≥ 2^t。实验部分用有限总体精确公式 p_h^Q(m)=C(|Q|-|E_h^Q|, m)/C(|Q|, m) 计算"存活欺骗者期望数"，不做独立性假设。

Result: 理论结果：(1) TC^0（Thm 4.1）固定深度 d≥2、任意 f*∈TC^0_{d,s}、任意 1≤t≤n，cert(f*, TC^0_{d,s+1}) ≥ 2^t；取 t=Ω(n) 得 2^{Ω(n)}，且存在同一目标 f_n 在原类中证书为 n^{O(1)}、在 +1 门类中 ≥2^{Ω(n)}，故下界非空且是"逐目标"的（区别于 Servedio 只针对单个最坏目标）。AC^0 有常数松弛版 cert(f*, AC^0_{d+1,s+2}) ≥ 2^t（若 NOT 也计入门数则需 s+t+2）；受限扇入/NC^1 版需 Θ(t) 门，额外 k 个门给出 2^{Ω(min{n,k/4})}。(2) Transformer（Thm 4.2）log-精度 AHAT：最后一层多加一个头 + 6 个残差/嵌入坐标，即对原架构可实现的每个目标都有 cert ≥ 2^t；同时 log 精度类至多 2^{poly(n)} 个假设，故存在目标在原类中证书为多项式、在扩大类中 ≥2^{Ω(n)}。(3) 近似认证（Prop 4.2）：容许 poly(n) 次绝对错误时，取 t=n−O(log n) 得 cert ≥ 2^n/poly(n)=2^{Ω(n)}；而固定相对容差 ε>0 只强制 t<log₂(1/ε)−1，下界仅为 O(1/ε) 量级，说明常数相对误差可掩盖高达 ⌊ε2^n⌋ 个绝对错误。实验结果：构造的深度-2、3 门加法阈值电路 + 2^n 个一门欺骗者（每个错误集 2^n(2^{n+1}−1) 且互不相交）表明：均匀采样候选证书需指数规模才有可见衰减，最优选取也需 2^n 个精选样例才能完全消除（剩余数恰为 max{2^n−m,0}）；训练的 10 位加法 Transformer（4 层、d_model 64、2 头、205,196 参数，带中间位/进位监督，通过正负类 99.9% 校验）中 150 个被接受模型有 87 个在 314 万条留出集上零错误，63 个仍有残余错误；即便聚焦到含 79.9% 错误质量的紧凑子集 Q_tgt（1,048,576 条），均匀采样证书仍须很大才能排除全部欺骗者，且错误不落在 Q_tgt 内的模型对任何 Q_tgt 采样都存活；后验错误并集仅 15,494 条样例即可排除所有已观察欺骗者（但属事后选取）。有限类枚举实验（深度-2/规模-2 的受限阈值类与 AC^0 类）显示：确定性"平分"策略每次都选出 OR_n，其在基类中的证书恰为 n+1（并给出证明），而在支持块欺骗者的扩大类中同一目标的下界为 2^n。

Conclusion: 核心结论：从样本做精确认证的难度本质上由"环境中的假设类"决定，而非仅由目标本身决定；只要常数级的架构扩张（一个阈值门、一个注意力头 + 常数宽度），就会在每个目标周围生成指数多个触发型欺骗者，使多项式数量的样例不足以证明模型实现了预期计算。并且这种障碍不能通过放宽为"近似正确"来绕过——除非允许指数规模的绝对错误量（固定相对容差会隐藏指数多的错误）。局限：Transformer 结论建立在理想化的 projected-pre-norm + AHAT + log 精度模型上；训练模型的实验只是有限集上的候选证书存活分析，而非全类上的渐近下界；触发建议（I, π）的位数成本（log C(n,t)+t）必须计入，不能视为免费。后续方向是"主动认证"：允许认证者以不同方式查询候选模型（成员查询、等价查询、反例搜索、对中间推理轨迹的查询）并与可信的目标 oracle/验证器比对，从而考察更强的交互协议能否突破被动认证的障碍，抑或类似障碍在更强协议下依然存在。

Abstract: As state-of-the-art neural networks are deployed on reasoning and algorithmic tasks, exactness guarantees become increasingly important. However, high average-case accuracy can still mask inconsistent behaviors. This motivates exact certification, which asks for the smallest set of labeled examples needed to certify that a learned hypothesis equals the target. We show that while some hypotheses are easy to certify, even minimal overparametrization can make certification exponentially hard across several hypothesis classes. For threshold circuits of depth $\ge 2$, adding a single extra gate can force certificate sizes exponential in the input dimension. We show an analogous hardness result for log-precision Transformers with only constant architectural overhead. We also characterize approximate certification, showing that allowing only polynomially many mistakes still requires exponentially large certificates, whereas constant relative-error guarantees can hide exponentially many mistakes. Empirically, we study certification for constructed circuits and trained Transformers for recognizing binary addition. While the constructed circuits instantiate the exponential barrier for certification, the trained Transformer analysis shows that imperfect models can evade detection by large uniformly sampled certificate candidates.

</details>


### [146] [Learned Relay Representations for Forward-Thinking Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22967)
*Benjamin Rozonoyer,Jacopo Minniti,Dhruvesh Patel,Neil Band,Avishek Joey Bose,Tim G. J. Rudner,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Relay是一种让掩码扩散模型跨去噪步骤传递潜在表征的方法，通过可学习通道和截断BPTT训练，实现推理加速和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型在迭代细化生成序列时，每轮去噪后丢弃内部计算，导致后续步骤需重新计算模型表征中的有价值信息，造成信息浪费和效率损失。

Method: 引入可微分的per-token通道，在前向传递中传递信息，并通过截断BPTT训练；可与block diffusion和KV缓存无缝兼容。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数独规划任务上验证了设计选择，在编码任务上超越监督微调基线，推理延迟降低32%。

Conclusion: Relay可以扩展到Fast-DLLM v2等SOTA扩散语言模型，在编码任务上超越标准监督微调，同时将推理延迟降低32%，推进了性能-延迟Pareto前沿。

Abstract: When Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) generate sequences through iterative refinement, the rich internal computation over masked positions is discarded, forcing every subsequent refinement step to recompute the valuable internal information stored as model representations. To avoid a hard reset between denoising rounds, we propose Learned Relay Representations (Relay), a method that allows MDMs to be forward-thinking when denoising by explicitly learning how to propagate latent information for the benefit of future denoising steps. Relay introduces a differentiable per-token channel that passes information between forward passes and is trained via truncated backpropagation through time (BPTT). We show that this framework can be scaled to state-of-the-art Diffusion Language Models (DLMs), and is seamlessly compatible with techniques like block diffusion and KV caching. We first provide a thorough justification of the design choices in Relay on a challenging Sudoku-based planning task. We then scale Relay to Fast-dLLM v2, a state-of-the-art DLM, outperforming standard supervised finetuning on coding tasks while reducing inference latency by up to 32%. Our empirical results demonstrate that state-of-the-art DLMs can be explicitly trained to relay latent information forward across decoding steps, advancing the performance-latency Pareto frontier. We provide code for all our experiments.

</details>


### [147] [A mathematical theory of balancing relational generalization and memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22972)
*Luke Cheng,Samuel Lippl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究学习系统如何在学习一般规则和记忆例外之间取得平衡。提出了一个新的任务范式来测试这一能力，并发现核岭回归模型虽然能平衡二者，但泛化成功与否高度依赖于表示几何结构。预训练语言模型验证了这些理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务范式缺乏对"学习一般规律与记忆例外"这一关键能力的有效测试。

Method: 提出了一个新的任务范式"带例外的传递推理"(transitive inference with exceptions)；使用核岭回归进行理论分析；在预训练语言模型上验证理论预测。

Result: 模型可以在关系泛化和记忆化之间取得平衡；但与不带例外的传递推理不同，成功的泛化对表示几何结构敏感；语言模型能够泛化传递规则，但也会产生理论预测的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，学习系统需要在关系泛化和记忆化之间取得平衡；成功泛化高度依赖于特定的表示几何结构；新的任务范式对于研究这一能力至关重要。

Abstract: Humans, animals, and modern machine learning models exhibit impressive abilities to learn complex behaviors and generalize these behaviors to unseen situations. This ability requires us to learn rules and regularities that allow for such generalizations. At the same time, in most complex environments, any rule will have its exceptions. How do learning systems balance between learning general regularities and memorizing exceptions? We argue that a lack of task paradigms has hindered the study of this essential ability. To address this gap, we introduce a novel task, transitive inference with exceptions, that tests for relational generalization and memorization of an exception to the relational rule. We then analytically characterize the behavior of a simple, theoretically tractable model of neural network learning (kernel ridge regression) across a broad family of representations and task parameters. We find that these models can balance between relational generalization and memorization, but unlike for transitive inference without an exception, successful generalization is sensitive to the specific representational geometry. We explain why this task is more challenging mechanistically by drawing on our analytical theory. Finally, we validate our theoretical insights in pretrained language models that are finetuned on ordered relations, finding that these models successfully generalize according to the transitive rule, but also make the kinds of systematic mistakes predicted by our theory. Overall, our theory shows how learning systems can balance between relational generalization and memorization, explains how this can go wrong, and emphasizes the need for new task paradigms designed to probe this ability.

</details>


### [148] [Assessing Predictive Models for Fairness Based on Movement Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23234)
*Francesco Lettich,Mario A. Nascimento,Chiara Pugliese,Chiara Renso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现有空间公平性研究仅考虑个体单一居住地假设的局限性，创新性地提出将公平性评估扩展至个体运动模式的新问题。方法通过多分辨率空间划分关联运动轨迹与地理区域，并利用空间扫描统计量进行公平性评估，在大规模合成数据集上验证了有效性，同时揭示了定位精度与分辨率之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间公平性研究假设每个个体仅归属于单一地理位置（如居住地），忽视了个体在多个区域的活动轨迹；由于公平性也应考虑个体在不同区域的活动模式，因此有必要将空间公平性概念从静态位置扩展至动态运动模式。

Method: 首先将个体运动轨迹关联至地理区域（考虑不同分辨率和对齐方式的多重空间划分），随后采用空间扫描统计量评估预测模型在运动模式层面的公平性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能有效检测新型不公平现象，准确检索被不公平对待的个体对象。

Conclusion: 研究通过数千个合成不公平数据集验证了方法的有效性，能够检测新型不公平性并检索被错误对待的个体，同时揭示了多分辨率下定位性能的一致性权衡关系。

Abstract: Assessing the spatial fairness of predictive models involves establishing whether they are statistically penalizing (favoring) individuals associated with certain geographical locations. Literature on this topic makes the fundamental assumption that each individual is assigned to a single geographical location (e.g., place of residence). However, fairness with respect to the set of locations where one has been, i.e., their movement patterns over different regions, also matters when fairness is considered. Consequently, we argue that it is necessary to generalize the notion of spatial fairness to also include movement patterns, leading to the novel problem of assessing predictive models for fairness relative to the movements of individuals. To deal with this problem, we propose an approach that first associates the movements of individuals to certain geographic regions, considering multiple spatial partitions with different resolutions and alignments, and then employs a suitable spatial scan statistic to assess whether a predictive model is fair based on movement patterns. In the experimental evaluation, we study the performance of our approach over thousands of synthetic unfair datasets, showing that it is effective at detecting this new type of unfairness and at retrieving the set of objects treated unfairly, while localization performance exhibits a consistent multi-resolution trade-off.

</details>


### [149] [Worse than Random: The Importance of a Baseline for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22973)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Michael E. Houle,Oussama Chelly,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper highlights a critical evaluation gap in unsupervised feature selection research—many proposed methods fail to outperform simple random feature selection. The authors advocate for mandatory baseline comparison against random selection to ensure genuine methodological contributions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lack of established evaluation baselines makes it difficult to determine the value added by novel unsupervised feature selection methods and the effectiveness of their underlying approaches.

Method: Proposing random feature selection as a baseline for evaluating unsupervised feature selection methods.

Result: Empirically demonstrate that many state-of-the-art unsupervised feature selection methods are outperformed by random feature selection in both performance and efficiency.

Conclusion: Mandating random feature selection as a required baseline in the development process of novel unsupervised feature selection methods to ensure genuine improvement over random selection.

Abstract: Many novel unsupervised feature selection methods are proposed each year, yet their empirical evaluation is limited to supervised and unsupervised evaluation metrics computed on selected datasets, along with comparisons to existing methods. However, in the absence of an established evaluation baseline, it is difficult to determine the value added to the existing literature by each of these methods, and how effective their underlying approaches are. We propose using random feature selection as a baseline for evaluating the unsupervised feature selection methods. We empirically show that many of the state-of-the-art methods in unsupervised feature selection are outperformed by random feature selection in both performance and efficiency. Accordingly, we emphasize on the strict requirement of considering random feature selection as a baseline in the development process of novel unsupervised feature selection methods to ensure a consistent improvement over random feature selection.

</details>


### [150] [Test-Time Training Undermines Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22984)
*Simone Antonelli,Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现TTT范式在提升模型适应性的同时引入了严重安全漏洞，可将攻击成功率提升至95%以上，且该漏洞可转移至生产环境。提出困惑度偏移检测方法作为初步防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT范式虽能提升少样本学习、检索增强生成和复杂推理等任务的性能，但动态参数适配会引入新的安全漏洞，被对手利用进行越狱攻击。

Method: 识别三种TTT威胁模型并演示攻击者如何利用它们绕过安全过滤器；提出有效性感知评估方法来纠正TTT诱导的过拟合导致的ASR膨胀问题。

Result: TTT显著提高攻击成功率（ASR）及ASR@10；在LoRA下，少样本和生成阶段威胁模型的平均ASR@10分别达到95%和93%；漏洞可转移至生产微调API；TTT诱导的过拟合会产生退化输出，导致标准评估器高估ASR。

Conclusion: TTT暴露了新的攻击面，可强化攻击并破坏现有安全护栏。建议使用轻量级检测器通过困惑度偏移来标记TTT请求，但稳健部署最终需要动态对齐。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) is an emerging paradigm that enables models to adapt their parameters during inference, improving performance on tasks such as few-shot learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and complex reasoning. However, this dynamic adaptation introduces new vulnerabilities that adversaries can exploit to jailbreak models. We identify three threat models for TTT and demonstrate how attackers can leverage them to bypass safety filters. Our results show that TTT can significantly increase the Attack Success Rate (ASR) and the ASR over 10 generation trials (ASR@10). For example, under LoRA, the few-shot and generation-phase threat models achieve an average ASR@10 of 95% and 93% respectively, across models from different families and scales. These vulnerabilities transfer to production fine-tuning APIs. We also show that TTT-induced overfitting can produce degenerate outputs that inflate ASR under standard judges, and propose a validity-aware evaluation to correct for this. Our findings suggest that TTT exposes a new attack surface, strengthens attacks, and undermines existing safety guardrails. As a first step toward defense, we propose a lightweight provider-side detector that flags TTT requests via the perplexity shift on a private harmful holdout, but robust deployment will ultimately require dynamic alignment.

</details>


### [151] [Smoothed Elicitation Complexity for Approximate $Γ$-calibration of Discrete Classification Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23017)
*Jessica Finocchiaro,Victor Ganson,Drona Khurana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对多分类概率预测器的校准问题，提出使用性质的条件分布（而非预测向量本身）来定义校准，从而将复杂度从指数级（类别数n）降为维度d（性质的表达维度）。本文进一步将研究范围从连续性质拓展到离散性质，通过Lipschitz连续性质作为中间桥梁，首次为强可排序离散性质提供了完整的近似校准刻画和可实现复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元校准定义直接扩展到概率多分类器时会面临指数级复杂度爆炸（随类别数n指数增长），需要一种更高效的校准框架；同时，现有近似性质校准研究局限于连续标量性质，而许多实际重要的性质（如众数、排名）是离散的。

Method: 使用Lipschitz连续性质作为中间工具，构建设计这些Lipschitz性质的算法，并通过后处理将其转化为原始离散性质，从而建立离散性质与连续性质之间的桥梁。

Result: 完全刻画了强可排序离散性质的Lipschitz可实现复杂度；首次为离散性质提供了近似校准结果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为离散性质提供了近似校准结果，并通过构造Lipschitz连续性质作为中间步骤，证明了可以后处理这些Lipschitz性质以获得原始离散性质，从而完全刻画了强可排序离散性质的可实现复杂度。

Abstract: One prominent method of evaluating machine learning model trustworthiness is the notion of calibration. In the binary outcome setting, a probabilistic predictor is calibrated if outcomes are realized according to a model's distributional prediction, conditioned on this prediction. Straightforward extensions of binary calibration definitions to probabilistic multiclass classifiers suffer from an exponential complexity blowup as the space of predictions grows exponentially in the number of classes $n$. As a remedy, Noarov and Roth (2023) propose multiclass calibration with predictions that are properties of the outcome distribution, reducing complexity from growing in the number of classes $n$ to the dimension $d$ of the property, called its elicitation complexity. Previous work on approximate property calibration is generally limited to continuous scalar properties, despite many relevant properties of interest being discrete, like the mode or rankings. We characterize the approximate property calibration of discrete properties which are strongly orderable by using Lipschitz continuous properties as an intermediary. This work is the first to our knowledge to provide approximate calibration results for discrete properties. Along the way, we characterize the Lipschitz elicitation complexity of strongly orderable discrete properties by constructing algorithms for designing these Lipschitz properties, which we prove can be post-processed to obtain the original discrete property.

</details>


### [152] [PACE: Two-Timescale Self-Evolution for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23019)
*Chen Ling,Pei Chen,Albert Guan,Jiaming Qu,Shayan Ali Akbar,Madhu Gopinathan,Erwin Cornejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE框架，让冻结的小语言模型（4B-14B参数）能够自主进化成为有效的AI智能体，无需依赖前沿模型或更新模型权重。通过双时间尺度机制协调低风险的提示优化与高风险的控制逻辑更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中部署语言模型智能体需要大量计算和人力来调优提示词、解析器、验证器等组件。现有自进化框架依赖前沿模型进行故障诊断、修订建议和更新判断，在资源受限场景下不可行。

Method: PACE采用双时间尺度框架：(1) 在固定控制逻辑下进化提示词直到收益饱和；(2) 在保持验证集上接受约束的控制逻辑更新。框架在冻结的SLM上运行，不更新模型权重。

Result: 在3个冻结SLM后端（4B-14B）和4个受控基准测试的12种组合中，PACE在全部组合上达到最佳性能。相较于 vanilla SLM智能体提升达+9.2%，相较于单模式进化基线提升达+5.4%。Tau-bench案例显示多轮工具调用成功率的改善。

Conclusion: 可靠的SLM智能体自进化可以在不更新模型权重或依赖前沿模型教师的情况下实现。核心贡献不是某个特定的最终求解模式，而是自主发现任务适配的推理策略这一能力本身。

Abstract: Deploying language-model agents in production often requires substantial compute and human effort to tune prompts, parsers, validators, and other components of the agent pipeline. Self-evolution offers a promising alternative, but most existing frameworks assume access to frontier models that can reliably diagnose failures, propose revisions, and judge their own updates. We study whether frozen small language models (SLMs) can serve as effective self-evolving agents under resource constraints. We propose PACE (Prompt And Control Logic Evolution), a two-timescale framework that coordinates low-risk prompt refinement with higher-risk control-logic updates. PACE evolves prompts under fixed control logic until prompt-level gains saturate, then considers constrained control-logic updates that are accepted through held-out validation. Across three frozen SLM backbones ranging from 4B to 14B parameters and four controlled benchmarks, PACE achieves the best performance on all 12 backbone--benchmark combinations, improving over vanilla SLM agents by up to +9.2% relative improvement and over the stronger single-mode evolution baseline by up to +5.4% relative improvement. A tau-bench case study further shows that PACE improves multi-turn tool-use success over vanilla and prompt-only evolution. These results suggest that reliable SLM agent self-evolution is possible without updating model weights or relying on frontier-model teachers, and that the key benefit is not any single final solver pattern but autonomous, validated discovery of task-appropriate inference strategies.

</details>


### [153] [World Machine: Towards Generative World Modeling for Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23025)
*Elton Cardoso do Nascimento,Alexandre da Silva Simões,Esther Luna Colombini,Ricardo Ribeiro Gudwin,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: World Machine proposes a latent-state transformer architecture for generative time series modeling that overcomes the quadratic complexity limitation of vanilla transformers, validated on synthetic experiments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional transformers face quadratic computational and memory costs that scale with context length, limiting their applicability for world modeling tasks requiring long-horizon prediction and simulation.

Method: Transformer-based architecture with latent states enabling adaptive context handling; training protocol with ablated components evaluated on Toy1D synthetic dataset.

Result: World Machine improves upon traditional transformers by achieving subquadratic scaling behavior, with validated performance on synthetic Toy1D dataset demonstrating component-wise contributions.

Conclusion: World Machine demonstrates feasibility for generative world modeling of time series, achieving capabilities beyond conventional transformers while maintaining practical computational and memory efficiency.

Abstract: World models represent a paradigm shift in generative AI, pursuing predictive understanding and controllable simulation of environments in a structured and generalizable way. We present World Machine, a generative world-modeling architecture for time series. It is a transformer-based architecture with latent states that enables adaptation to different amounts of observed data and contexts. This shows an improvement over traditional transformers, which have a computational and memory cost that scales quadratically with the context. Experiments on a proposed synthetic dataset, Toy1D, validate the approach's feasibility, demonstrate capabilities not found in conventional transformers, and highlight the contributions of each component of the training protocol.

</details>


### [154] [RADAR: Relative Angular Divergence Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23028)
*Xavier Cadet,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RADAR，一种基于几何的跨域迁移性度量方法。该方法通过分析模型层间位移轨迹的角度对齐和距离变化，以及比较领域内外的动力学分布来预测迁移效果。在文本和视觉多个基准测试中表现竞争力，尤其适用于平滑或清晰分离的领域转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习依赖数据，但数据获取常面临可用性约束、成本或领域专业知识需求的挑战。扩展数据集是常见应对策略，但这并不总是能提升下游性能，甚至可能导致性能下降（负迁移）。因此需要一种方法来预测不同领域间迁移的成功可能性。

Method: RADAR通过分析表征的逐层演化来估计跨域迁移性，具体包括：测量角度对齐和沿层间位移轨迹的距离相对变化，以及比较领域内和跨领域动力学的经验分布。该方法基于几何方法，通过轨迹分布的散度来量化迁移潜力。

Result: 在多种模态的实验中，RADAR与现有的迁移性度量方法相比表现出具有竞争力的预测性能。特别是在领域转换平滑或清晰分离的场景下效果显著。

Conclusion: 域迁移性估计的有效性取决于模型内部表征空间的几何结构，不同模态偏好不同的拓扑表述。RADAR在不同设置中提供具有竞争力的预测性能，尤其在领域转换平滑或清晰分离时表现突出。

Abstract: Machine learning methods rely on data. However, gathering suitable data can be challenging due to availability constraints, cost, or the need for domain expertise. Expanding datasets with additional sources is a common response to limited data, yet this practice does not always improve downstream performance and can sometimes lead to a loss of performance, known as negative transfer. We propose RADAR, a simple, geometrically grounded metric for estimating cross-domain transferability in foundation models. RADAR analyzes the layer-wise evolution of representations by measuring angular alignments and relative changes in distance along layer-to-layer displacement trajectories, and by comparing empirical distributions of within-domain and cross-domain dynamics. We hypothesize that domain transferability is related to the divergence between these trajectory distributions. We evaluate the metric across multiple modalities, including cross-lingual sentiment classification with text embedding models and cross-domain image classification with foundation vision models. Across several settings, RADAR provides competitive predictive performance relative to existing transferability metrics on several vision and text benchmarks, with particularly strong results when domain transitions are smooth or cleanly separated. Our ablations further suggest that the effectiveness of transferability estimation depends on the geometry of the model's internal representation space, with different modalities favoring different topological formulations.

</details>


### [155] [Uncovering the Latent Potential of Deep Intermediate Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23033)
*Arnesh Batra,Arush Gumber,Aniket Khandelwal,Jashn Khemani,Anubha Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了预训练基础模型中层向嵌入的几何结构和语义分布，发现任务相关信息在层间呈非单调分布，标准聚合方法无法有效捕获。通过提出 LOES 方法识别任务判别性子空间，并结合 GeoReg 损失稳定微调过程，在多种场景下取得一致的性能提升，证明了层向嵌入几何结构对深度模型的表示和迁移能力具有核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍做法仅使用最终层或浅层混合特征，忽视了任务相关信息在层间的非单调分布特征，且简单的聚合方式无法恢复这些信息。有效迁移依赖于识别哪些层编码了任务判别性结构及其嵌入的几何组织方式。

Method: 提出 LOES（层级最优嵌入选择）方法，通过构造性谱方法在正交性和各向同性约束下最小化残差，以识别任务判别性子空间；提出 GeoReg（几何正则化损失），在微调时对类流形施加单纯形结构约束，稳定表征几何。

Result: LOES 在多种架构、深度、模态和数据集规模下均一致超越标准基线，且性能增益随模型深度增加而增长；方法还支持跨语言和跨模态的可解释性分析。

Conclusion: LOES 在多种架构、深度、模态和数据条件下持续优于基线方法，且优势随模型深度增加而扩大；GeoReg 通过几何正则化稳定微调过程；语义因子在层间的分布规律可用于跨语言和跨模态可解释性分析；层向嵌入几何结构是深度模型表示和迁移知识的关键因素，而非偶发现象。

Abstract: Foundational Models pretrained on huge amount of data learn representations that evolve across depth, forming a hierarchy of embeddings with distinct semantic content and geometric structure. Contrary to the widespread practice of using only the final layer or shallow mixtures, we show that task-relevant information is distributed non-monotonically across layers and cannot be recovered by naïve aggregation. Through a geometric and empirical study across multiple modalities, we show that effective transfer depends on identifying which layers encode task-discriminative structure and how their embeddings are geometrically organized.
