Our open-source package is available at https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

</details>


### [182] [Self-supervised Adversarial Purification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23239)
*Woohyun Lee,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPR-GAE通过将净化器与分类器分离解决GNN对抗攻击问题。该方法使用多个广义PageRank过滤器捕捉多样化结构表示，以自监督策略训练，在多种攻击场景下实现了最先进的鲁棒性，可作为独立即插即用净化器应用于GNN分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional adversarial training methods intertwine accuracy and robustness within a single classifier, leading to a suboptimal trade-off between these conflicting objectives. Existing adversarial purification methods lack adaptability to diverse graph structures.

Method: GPR-GAE uses a self-supervised training strategy with multiple Generalized PageRank (GPR) filters to capture diverse structural representations for robust and effective purification. The framework implements a multi-step purification process to achieve precise graph recovery and robust defense against structural perturbations.

Result: Experiments across diverse datasets and attack scenarios demonstrate state-of-the-art robustness of GPR-GAE.

Conclusion: GPR-GAE achieves state-of-the-art robustness as an independent plug-and-play purifier for GNN classifiers. The separation of purification from classification provides a flexible solution that can adapt to diverse graph structures in a data-driven manner.

Abstract: Defending Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) against adversarial attacks requires balancing accuracy and robustness, a trade-off often mishandled by traditional methods like adversarial training that intertwine these conflicting objectives within a single classifier. To overcome this limitation, we propose a self-supervised adversarial purification framework. We separate robustness from the classifier by introducing a dedicated purifier, which cleanses the input data before classification. In contrast to prior adversarial purification methods, we propose GPR-GAE, a novel graph auto-encoder (GAE), as a specialized purifier trained with a self-supervised strategy, adapting to diverse graph structures in a data-driven manner. Utilizing multiple Generalized PageRank (GPR) filters, GPR-GAE captures diverse structural representations for robust and effective purification. Our multi-step purification process further facilitates GPR-GAE to achieve precise graph recovery and robust defense against structural perturbations. Experiments across diverse datasets and attack scenarios demonstrate the state-of-the-art robustness of GPR-GAE, showcasing it as an independent plug-and-play purifier for GNN classifiers. Our code can be found at https://github.com/woodavid31/GPR-GAE.

</details>


### [183] [RelPrism: A Multi-Faceted Pre-training Framework with Self-Generated Tasks for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23241)
*Jinyu Yang,Cheng Yang,Junze Chen,Zedi Liu,Muhan Zhang,Hanyang Peng,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RelPrism是一种针对关系数据库的多面自监督学习框架，通过构建内在、关系和混合属性并利用多粒度聚类形成伪任务池，解决了现有SSL方法单一视角监督信息不足的问题，在多种预测任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法通常从单一角度（如节点级内在属性或子图级关系结构）获取监督信息，面对关系数据库中多层面、多粒度的异构预测需求时适应性有限。

Method: 提出RelPrism框架，从内在属性、关系属性和混合属性三个角度构建信息，通过多粒度聚类形成伪任务池进行预训练，使表示学习能够覆盖更广泛视角和粒度级别。

Result: 在5个真实世界数据集的14个任务上，分类ROC-AUC提升4.15%，回归MAE降低10.75%，优于现有最优基线方法。

Conclusion: RelPrism通过多面自监督学习在14个任务、5个数据集上取得了最先进的性能，ROC-AUC提升4.15%，MAE降低10.75%，代码已开源。

Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the cornerstone of modern data systems and support diverse predictive tasks. Recent relational deep learning (RDL) methods enable end-to-end prediction by converting RDBs into graphs, where rows are represented as nodes and inter-table interactions are represented as edges, and then applying graph-based models for representation learning. Despite the strong capability of RDL, effective self-supervised pre-training for RDBs remains non-trivial. RDB tasks often require multi-faceted information across different perspectives and granularities. For example, user churn classification may rely more on interaction patterns, whereas consumption value prediction requires both user-item behaviors and intrinsic user attributes for fine-grained regression. Such heterogeneous needs challenge RDB representation learning, as pre-training objectives should cover comprehensive information for downstream adaptation. However, existing SSL methods typically derive supervision from a single facet, such as node-level intrinsic attributes or subgraph-level relational structures, providing limited adaptability. To this end, we propose RelPrism, a multi-faceted self-supervised learning framework for RDBs. RelPrism constructs intrinsic, relational, and hybrid attributes from distinct perspectives, and applies multi-granularity clustering to each perspective to form corresponding pseudo-task pools. Pre-training over these pools exposes representations to broader perspectives and granularity levels, yielding a stronger basis for downstream adaptation. Experiments on 14 tasks across 5 real-world datasets show that RelPrism improves ROC-AUC by 4.15% for classification and reduces MAE by 10.75% for regression over state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism.

</details>


### [184] [Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23244)
*Miria Feng,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COALA proposes a novel lightweight alignment algorithm for LLMs by reformulating preference learning as a convex optimization problem, eliminating the need for a reference model and achieving ~5.7× computational efficiency gain over DPO while maintaining competitive performance across multiple datasets and models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing alignment methods like RLHF are computationally expensive and complex, while alternatives such as DPO suffer from inconsistent ranking accuracy, high GPU resource demands, and expensive hyperparameter tuning.

Method: COALA leverages a convex optimization reformulation of neural networks to reframe preference learning without requiring a reference model, thereby reducing computational overhead. This approach enables efficient single-GPU training with minimal VRAM consumption.

Result: On four datasets (including a 26,621-sample synthetic Educational Feedback dataset) and six models (including Llama-3.1-8B), COALA achieves competitive performance while using only ~17.6% of DPO's total TFLOPs. It exhibits stable, monotonically increasing rewards and reaches peak margins in significantly shorter time compared to DPO and ORPO.

Conclusion: COALA is the first work to effectively apply convex optimization to preference fine-tuning of LLMs, offering a theoretically grounded, computationally efficient, and practically deployable alternative to existing alignment methods.

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) to align with human preferences has driven the success of systems such as Gemini and ChatGPT. However, approaches like Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) remain computationally expensive and complex. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a simpler alternative but has limitations such as inconsistent ranking accuracy, high dependence on GPU resources, and expensive hyperparameter tuning. We propose the Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning Algorithm (COALA): a novel lightweight strategy with strong theoretical guarantees. By leveraging the convex optimization reformulation of neural networks, COALA eliminates the need for a reference model and obtains significant reduction in both training time and VRAM consumption, thus enabling efficient training on a single GPU. Experiments across four datasets--including a 26621-sample synthetic Educational Feedback dataset--and six models (including Llama-3.1-8B) demonstrate COALA's competitive performance and efficiency while utilizing as little as ~17.6% of DPO's total TFLOPs. COALA exhibits stable, monotonically increasing rewards and reaches peak margins in significantly shorter time in comparison to traditional methods such as DPO and ORPO. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time convex optimization has been effectively applied to preference fine-tuning of LLMs.

</details>


### [185] [Accelerating Divisible Load Processing Through Machine Learning: A Practical Framework for Large-Scale Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23247)
*Bharadwaj Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper presents the first ML framework for predicting optimal processing times in Single-Level Tree Network architectures using Divisible Load Theory, achieving 97-99% accuracy with a feedforward neural network trained on 100,000 synthetic configurations and providing 10-100x speedup over traditional computation methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional DLT computation for SLTN architectures requires explicit mathematical formulation which can be computationally expensive, creating a need for faster prediction methods that can enable real-time scheduling, design space exploration, and cloud resource allocation applications.

Method: A feedforward neural network (FNN) with 16 engineered features trained on 100,000 synthetically generated configurations to predict optimal processing times without explicit DLT equation formulation.

Result: The model achieves 97-99% R-square accuracy with 1-5% mean absolute percentage error, with inference times under 1 millisecond providing 10-100x speedup over traditional DLT computation. Feature importance analysis shows the model implicitly captures DLT mathematical structure including load conservation and simultaneous finishing constraints.

Conclusion: The ML-based approach achieves 10-100x speedup over traditional DLT computation while maintaining 97-99% accuracy, with inference under 1ms enabling real-time applications. The method generalizes well across diverse configurations but shows slight degradation for very large or highly heterogeneous systems. This work establishes machine learning as a viable approach for accelerating distributed computing optimization.

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the first machine learning framework for predicting optimal processing times in Single-Level Tree Network (SLTN) architectures for the Divisible Load Theory (DLT) paradigm. Using a feedforward neural network(FNN) with 16 engineered features, we train a model on 100,000 synthetically generated configurations to predict optimal processing times without explicit formulation of DLT equations. The model achieves 97-99% accuracy (R-square factor) with mean absolute percentage error of 1-5%, demonstrating that neural networks can effectively learn complex load distribution relationships. Feature importance analysis reveals that the model implicitly captures DLT mathematical structure, including load conservation and simultaneous finishing constraints. With inference times under 1 millisecond, the approach provides 10-100x speedup over traditional DLT computation, enabling applications in real-time scheduling, design space exploration, and cloud resource allocation. The method generalizes well across diverse system configurations (n=3 to 20, load size =1 to 100 GB) with consistent accuracy, though performance degrades slightly for very large or highly heterogeneous systems. This work demonstrates the feasibility of using machine learning to accelerate distributed computing optimization while maintaining near-optimal accuracy.

</details>


### [186] [Enhancing Deep Neural Network Reliability with Refinement and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23249)
*Ramya Hebbalaguppe,Ajay Shastry,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Chetan Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对深度神经网络“校准提升但精化(refinement/sharpness)下降”的可靠性问题，提出：(1) 一个新的精化代理损失 L_ref（拉近最近同类样本、推远最近异类样本），并从理论上证明监督对比损失(SupCon)是它的上界，从而可直接用 SupCon 优化；(2) 两阶段训练框架 RefCal——先用 SupCon 预训练编码器，再冻结编码器、用分类+校准损失微调线性分类头。RefCal 可与任意校准方法（CE、CE+TS、LS、MMCE、MbLS、MDCA 等）组合，在 CIFAR-100-LT 上取得 (准确率, 精化AUC, ECE) = (58.81, 95.67, 0.08)，显著优于 CRL 的 (46.27, 93.70, 0.22)，并在 ImageNet-LT、TinyImageNet、CIFAR-100-C、OOD 检测等场景同时改善准确率/精化/校准。作者还澄清了 Brier 分数分解所暗示的“校准—精化冲突”只在常数错误率假设下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN 的置信度常不可靠（过度自信），因此需要“校准”(calibration)——预测置信度与经验正确率一致；但统计学家强调模型还应“精化”(refinement, 又称 sharpness)——对正确与错误预测给出显著不同的置信度。作者指出两个关键动机：(1) 校准指标可被后处理“作弊”式地改善——例如对所有恶性/良性预测赋予固定置信度向量 {0.7,0.3}/{0.2,0.8} 即可得到良好 ECE，却抹平了真/假阳性的置信度差异，削弱判别力；(2) 现有训练时校准方法（MMCE、FL+MDCA、AdaFocal、MixUp、LogitNorm、CE+TS 等）往往以牺牲精化为代价，CRL 虽能提升精化却损害校准与准确率。文献中由 Brier 分数分解推导出的“校准与精化存在权衡”也被作者重新审视：作者主张在非常数错误率下二者可同时改善。此外，既有精化/AUC 优化方法（AUCM 等）多限于二分类，多类扩展不自然。

Method: **1) 精化代理损失（Definition 2）**：对归一化到单位超球的嵌入 z_i，设正集 P_i（同标签）、负集 N_i（异标签），定义 L_ref = Σ_i [ min_{p∈P_i} ½‖z_i−z_p‖² − min_{n∈N_i} ½‖z_i−z_n‖² ]。几何含义：收紧类内簇（拉近最近正样本）+ 拉大类间间隔（推远最近负样本），最近负样本的间隔与分类性能相关，故可作为精化代理。**2) 理论分析**：Lemma 1 给出单位范数下 z₁·z₂ = ½(2−‖z₁−z₂‖²)；Lemma 2 通过 Jensen 不等式与 log-sum-exp 上下界证明 ℒ_SC > ℒ_ref，即监督对比损失是精化损失的紧上界/可优化代理（同时给出 τ>0 的推广），从而可复用已有 SupCon 训练框架做精化；SupCon 的内在 hard-positive/negative mining 特性被用来解释其精化增益。**3) RefCal 两阶段训练**：Stage 1 精化阶段——用 SupCon 预训练编码器（ResNet-50 / EfficientViT-M1，1000 epochs，batch 1024/512，τ=0.1~5，混合精度，A100 40GB）；Stage 2 校准阶段——冻结编码器，仅训练线性分类头 100 epochs，损失为标准分类损失 + 所选校准损失（CE、CE+TS、LS、MMCE、MixUp、CRL、AUCM、FL+MDCA、AdaFocal、MbLS、LogitNorm）。**4) 评测**：精化用 AUROC（正确/错误样本置信度可分性）刻画，校准用 ECE/SCE/ACE/smECE，并报告 Top-1 与 Top-1% 准确率；数据集覆盖 CIFAR-10/100 及其 -LT、TinyImageNet(-LT)、ImageNet-1K/LT、STL10（二分类）、CIFAR-100-C（腐蚀鲁棒性）、CIFAR10→SVHN（OOD 检测），含 Grad-CAM 可视化与 3 次运行的误差棒。

Result: CIFAR-100-LT（不平衡因子 0.1, ResNet-50）：RefCal+CE+TS 达到 Top-1 58.46、AUROC 96.85、ECE 7.62（SCE 0.41/ACE 0.46），CRL 为 46.27/93.70/22.03；摘要报告最优组合 (58.81, 95.67, 0.08) vs CRL (46.27, 93.70, 0.22)，即准确率提升逾 10%、精化 AUC 提升约 2–3 点、校准误差大幅下降。CIFAR10-LT：RefCal 取得 ECE 1.05、AUROC 99.04、准确率 88.11，优于 SOTA 基线 MbLS 的 7.05/98.59/87.82。STL10 二分类：AUROC 99.90，超过 AUCM 的 98.86。ImageNet-LT（ResNet-18）：ECE 16.11→10.82（AUC 98.30→98.10 基本持平）；EfficientViT-M1 在 CIFAR-100 上 Top-1 47.77→56.78、ECE 29.53→9.67，AUROC 95.07→96.00。OOD（CIFAR10→SVHN）：RefCal+AUCM 的 FPR@TPR95 降至 10.2、检测误差 6.6、AUROC 98.2，优于各单独方法。CIFAR-100-C（高斯噪声/亮度等腐蚀）：Top-1 24.81→32.97、AUROC 84.08→91.72，腐蚀严重度变化下方差更小。Grad-CAM 显示 RefCal 使模型聚焦前景显著物体而非背景。作者同时指出 ℒ_ref 与 AUCM/MixUp/AdaFocal 联用在部分数据集上联合优化较困难（多损失经 SGD 交互复杂），且训练成本高于后处理方法（但推理无额外开销）。

Conclusion: 论文主张并实证支持：校准与精化之间并非必然冲突，先前基于 Brier 分解的权衡结论依赖常数错误率假设；在表征层面通过监督对比学习引入的精化代理损失，可使准确率、校准度与精化度三者同时提升，且天然支持多类分类。作者给出的可复用方案是“SupCon 预训练 + 冻结特征上做校准微调”的通用流程 RefCal，可与现有校准方法即插即用组合，在多类/长尾/大规模/腐蚀/OOD 场景均验证有效。局限包括：训练开销显著大于后处理校准；与部分校准损失（AUCM、MixUp、AdaFocal）的联合优化不稳定，作者推测源于不同校准方法产生的表征与 ℒ_ref 兼容性差异，留待后续研究；未来工作包括在视觉语言模型上应用 RefCal 训练范式。

Abstract: Although deep neural networks (DNNs) achieve high predictive accuracy, their confidence estimates are often unreliable, potentially compromising user trust in their decisions. This has motivated research on calibrated models, where calibration measures how well a model's predicted confidence aligns with the empirical probability of correctness. However, calibration metrics can often be improved through post-processing techniques that merely mimic training-time uncertainty without genuinely improving the model's understanding. For this reason, statisticians recommend that models be not only calibrated but also refined. Intuitively, a model is considered more refined if it assigns significantly different confidence scores to correct and incorrect predictions, a property also referred to as sharpness. We observe that many existing calibration methods improve calibration at the cost of reduced refinement. To address this limitation, we propose: (1) a novel loss function that explicitly promotes refinement and can be optimized through supervised contrastive learning; and (2) a unified training framework, RefCal, that jointly optimizes calibration, refinement, and accuracy to improve DNN reliability. On the CIFAR-100-LT dataset with 10 percent class imbalance, RefCal achieves (accuracy, refinement, ECE) of (58.81, 95.67, 0.08), substantially outperforming the widely used Correctness Ranking Loss, which achieves (46.27, 93.7, 0.22).

</details>


### [187] [A Simple Plug-in for Improving Eviction-Based KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23258)
*Yuping Lin,Jiayuan Ding,Yue Xing,Pengfei He,Jiliang Tang,Subhabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VECTOR is a plug-and-play augmentation for KV cache eviction that uses three-way token routing (retention, approximation, eviction) by combining importance signals from a base scorer with reconstructability signals from regression-based value estimation, recovering useful value information that would otherwise be lost in binary eviction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache growth is a critical bottleneck for long-context inference. Existing methods relying on binary eviction or representation approximation may underutilize tokens that are not critically important for exact retention but are still reconstructable, leading to irreversible information loss.

Method: VECTOR introduces a three-way token routing mechanism that classifies tokens into retention, approximation, or eviction sets. It combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation to make routing decisions.

Result: VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression scenarios, with particularly clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

Conclusion: VECTOR achieves superior quality-memory trade-offs, especially under stricter compression budgets, and can be integrated as a plug-and-play module into existing eviction-based inference pipelines.

Abstract: KV cache growth is a major bottleneck for long-context inference in large language models. Existing methods are often dominated by binary eviction or representation approximation, which may underutilize tokens that are not critical for exact retention but are still reconstructable. We present VECTOR, a plug-and-play augmentation for eviction-based pipelines that introduces three-way token routing: retention, approximation, and eviction. VECTOR combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation. By leveraging reconstructability, VECTOR recovers useful value information that would otherwise be irreversibly lost under binary eviction, while preserving key vectors for attention routing stability. Experimental results show that VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression, with especially clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

</details>


### [188] [Multi-Gate Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23259)
*Zhizhan Zheng,Feiyun Zhang,Shuchun Liu,Tian Xia,Xi Liu,Dasheng Hu,Hongquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出「多门残差」（Multi-Gate Residuals, MGR）：一种把残差隐藏状态扩展为 n 条并行流的深度注意力结构，用轻量打分+门控机制更新各流、以 AttnPool 汇聚得到当前层输入，从而在几乎不增加跨设备通信的前提下稳定激活尺度并抑制 massive activation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PreNorm 范式下的标准加法残差把深度视为「均匀累加路径」，导致：(1) 信息逐层稀释，深层难以选择性关注早期表示；(2) 残差流幅值无界增长（massive activation / PreNorm dilution），深层需输出更大的值才能保持影响，造成数值不稳定与梯度分布失衡。已有深度注意力/多流方案各有代价：AttnRes（Full AttnRes）需聚合所有前层输出，通信与显存瓶颈显著，在分布式训练中难以扩展；Hyper-Connections / mHC 等多流方案虽 flops 常数级扩展，但残差路径上的操作过于复杂，工程复杂度高、实际性能与效率均不理想；而 MoDA 类深度注意力需要额外 KV 存储、索引与专用融合 kernel。作者希望融合两类范式的优点，在不引入通信瓶颈的前提下获得性能与可扩展性兼具的新结构。

Method: 结构：多流残差需要「聚合器（aggregator）」与「混合器（mixer）」。聚合器沿用 AttnRes 的深度方向 AttnPool：以层专属可学习向量 w_l^(α)∈R^d 作 query，与各流 s_i 经 RMSNorm 后的内积（除以 √d）得分数，softmax 归一化为 α_{i→l}，加权求和得 h_l = Σ α_{i→l} s_i。混合器（门控）从第 n 层起替代直接拼接，用门控系数 β_{i←l} 把各流与当前层输出 ℱ_l(h_l) 插值：s_i' = (1-β_i)⊙s_i + β_i⊙ℱ_l(h_l)。两种变体：独立变体 β 为 sigmoid 型（式2，饱和于 0 附近）；竞争变体 β 为 softmax 型且额外引入遗忘门分数 b_{l,0}^(β)（式3），使各流实际更新权重之和 <1。评分函数 φ(s_i, w_l^(β), b_{l,i}^(β)) = w_l^(β)·RMSNorm(s_i)/√d + b_{l,i}^(β)。稳定性分析：残差更新的 Jacobian 谱半径易 >1 导致方差爆炸；Highway 式凸组合给出逐层范数上界 ‖x_L‖ ≤ max(‖x_0‖, max_l‖ℱ_l(x_l)‖)，把「乘性累积爆炸」转化为「有界选择问题」，与 AttnPool 的凸组合叠加后前向全流程范数有界。门控偏置初始化：依据 Var(x_L) ≈ Var(x_0) + L·g_init²·Var(Δ) 推出 g_init ∝ 1/√L，进而 sigmoid 饱和区 b_l ≈ -½ln(L)+C（深度翻倍偏置约降 0.35）；给出按基准深度外推的公式 b_init^(L) = ln(√(L/L_base)(exp(-b_init_base)+1) - n)，竞争变体设 b_{l,0}=b_init、b_{l,i}=0，独立变体设 b_{l,i}=-b_init；混合器权重 w 初始化为 0。工程优化：将 lerp 与 AttnPool 融合为单 kernel，估算 I/O 约为 3nC 读、1nC 写；采用类 mHC 的选择性激活重计算，并利用 s_i = (s_i' - β_i⊙ℱ_l(h_l))⊙1/(1-β_i) 反解输入流，仅保留 top-p（p=0.01）β 值对应的输入流做「Fallback Inversion」，以省显存。

Result: 在 nanoGPT 框架、FineWeb-10BT 数据集（ctx 1024、batch 512、20K 步≈10B token、Muon+AdamW、ReLU²、RoPE）上，训练 S/M/L 三种规模（12/24/36 层，0.12B/0.35B/0.77B）。MGR 在所有规模与 n=4/n=8 配置下均优于 PreNorm、mHC-lite、Block AttnRes、Full AttnRes：S 规模验证损失 2.9020/2.9034（n=8）vs Full AttnRes 2.9066、PreNorm 2.9440；L 规模 2.5857 vs 2.5920/2.6213，且 n=8 下优势更明显。诊断上：MGR 大幅抑制 PreNorm 的逐块输出幅值膨胀与「梯度稀释」（深层梯度衰减、浅层过大），表现接近 Full AttnRes；PreNorm 第 7 层后出现 massive activation 并持续高位，而 MGR 与 Full AttnRes 无显著 massive activation（MGR 的 FFN 幅值略大于 AttnRes 但 attention 输出更小，整体相当）。深度分析：PreNorm 的层间角距离在深层单调下降（表示高度同质化），MGR 各流呈现异质的语义边界且边界位置随流不同；块剪枝性能损失曲线更均匀（最差 ΔPPL=13.67 出现在紧邻 LM head 的最后一块），无 PreNorm 式的早期块灾难性失效，也无「半休眠」块。门控值统计显示 β 平均很小（多数 <0.1，少数离群 0.8–0.9），验证反解重计算策略的可行性；Fallback Inversion 与标准反向传播的训练曲线和验证性能几乎一致。

Conclusion: MGR 以「多流 + 轻量门控 + AttnPool」的极简设计，在无跨设备通信开销的前提下同时获得稳定激活尺度、消除 massive activation 与梯度稀释，并在同等规模下超过 AttnRes、mHC-lite、PreNorm 等基线；配合 kernel 融合与反解式重计算，可在 GPU 集群上大规模训练与部署，训练开销可忽略、推理开销极小。作者倡导与 Slot Attention 相近的理念：各流作为并列的「记忆库」独立更新语义，避免复杂自上而下投影覆盖主干非线性层的表示。局限与展望：实验规模限于 0.12B–0.77B、10B token 规模，尚不能确认优势在更大规模是否保持；部分流出现近零角距离的「静态」现象究竟是数据集/规模不足还是流数校准得当仍待验证；作者推测 Full AttnRes 的全历史注意力可能因信息过载而削弱对真正显著特征的聚焦，该假设有待更严格验证。

Abstract: While Attention Residuals has shown some effectiveness in addressing the widespread issue of unbounded activation growth across deep residual layers, it inevitably incurs significant communication overhead. To circumvent this bottleneck, we propose Multi-Gate Residuals (MGR), which stabilizes activation scales without additional communication burden. It utilizes a straightforward scoring and gating mechanism to maintain multi-stream context, coupled with Attention Pooling to extract hidden states from the stream states. Empirical experiments demonstrate that MGR is practical for large-scale training and deployment, offering tangible performance improvements over existing architectures.

</details>


### [189] [When Good Equations Get Bad Scores: Improving Symbolic Regression Through Better Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23272)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Huang,Runxiang Wang,Ziwen Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代符号回归(SR)的双层优化框架中，内层参数拟合(普遍使用 BFGS/Nelder-Mead)因非线性算子造成的强非凸性而频繁陷入局部最优，导致"好结构、差分数(Good Structure, Bad Score)"瓶颈：约 22% 被丢弃的候选结构经高精度重拟合后本可胜过当前最优，极端情形高达 65.75%。作者提出即插即用的 SR 原生评估器 SAGE-Fit，由三模块组成：(1) 结构感知的变量投影(VarPro)降维——解析 AST 分离条件线性参数与非线性参数，线性参数用闭式正则化最小二乘消去；(2) 语义引导初始化——函数空间最远点采样(FS-FPS)，在输出行为而非参数坐标上度量多样性以避免"盆地塌缩"；(3) 投影 Gauss–Newton(TRF 阻尼)实现曲率感知的快速局部收敛。在 LLM-SRBench 的 LSR-Synth 上接入 PySR/uDSR/LaSR/LLM-SR，符号准确率与数值精度全面提升，候选级 lost rate 从 BFGS 的 70% 降至 9.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归(SR)主流方法均采用双层优化：外层搜索离散表达式结构，内层优化连续常数并把最小化损失作为适应度返回给外层。当前内层仍主要依赖 BFGS、Nelder-Mead 等通用求解器，但物理方程常含嵌套非线性算子(sin、exp、x^k 等)，使 MSE 损失面高度非凸、病态，形成"针中盆(Needle-in-a-Basin)"拓扑：单一初始化下局部梯度迅速衰减而满足停止条件，导致正确结构被赋予人为偏低的适应度；再叠加三角周期性与指数对称性带来的多模态，通用梯度法无法逃离次优吸引域。这造成大量"优化器诱发的假阴性"，使外层错误剪枝真方程，扭曲反馈信号、降低搜索效率。作者进一步用重拟合分析量化：被丢弃候选中平均约 22% 在高精度重拟合后优于当时的 incumbents，最严重轨迹达 65.75%，说明现代 SR 的内环评估保真度是被忽视的关键瓶颈。

Method: SAGE-Fit(Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator)作为可插拔内层评估器，由三阶段流水线组成：\n1) 结构感知降维(Module 1)：对候选结构的 AST 做仿射传播，判定参数是否"条件线性"(∂f/∂θ_k 与 θ_k 无关且 f|θ_k=0 不含 θ_k)，将其划入线性集 α∈R^{dα}，其余归入不可约非线性集 β∈R^{dβ}，d_f=d_α+d_β。表达式可分离为 f(X;α,β)=c_f(X;β)+Φ_f(X;β)α，对任意固定 β 有闭式解 α*(β)=(ΦᵀΦ)†Φᵀ(y−c)，秩亏时用 Tikhonov 正则伪逆；原 d_f 维问题投影为 d_β 维非线性流形上的 min_β L_proj。\n2) 语义引导初始化(Module 2)：Function-Space Farthest-Point Sampling(FS-FPS)。在投影后的 β 空间围绕 warm start 过采样 M=100 个候选，逐个计算函数指纹 f_m=f(X;α*(β_m),β_m)，在输出向量空间用归一化欧氏距离 d_sem=‖f_i−f_j‖/(‖y‖+ε) 贪心选出 K=8 个最远点(首点取训练损失最低者)，避免参数空间随机采样造成的"盆地塌缩"。\n3) 投影 Gauss–Newton(Module 3)：在 L_proj 上采用 Trust Region Reflective 阻尼的投影 LM 步 Δβ=−(J̃ᵀJ̃+λ_β(diag(J̃ᵀJ̃)+ηI))⁻¹J̃ᵀr̃；按 Golub–Pereyra 构造每次评估 β 时重解 α*(β)，使 r̃ 仅依赖 β；J̃ 用中心差分计算，每个差分探针均重解线性最小二乘；Hessian 规模降为 d_β×d_β 并以对角项做逐坐标尺度归一化(应对 sin(ωx) 与 x^k 之间的各向异性)。三模块整合为 Algorithm 1，输出忠实分数 Ŝ(f) 与拟合参数 (α̂,β̂)。超参：M=100、K=8；SPB 冷启动 θ_0 为全一向量，lost-rate 阈值 τ=3.0。

Result: 主实验(LSR-Synth,每题 1800s,配对同种子同硬件,仅替换内层优化器)：PySR+ 符号准确率 2.78%→8.33%(约3×)，uDSR+ 化学域 SA 达 13.89%(基线 11.11%)，LaSR+ 化学域 0%→16.67%，LLM-SR+ 首组 Acc_0.1 33.33%→60.61%、SA 8.33%→11.11%，各域 NMSE 亦普遍下降(部分数量级改善)。轨迹级诊断(LLM-SR,129 题):SAGE-Fit 使最终 NMSE 平均降低 3.22 个数量级(材料科学中位对数比 5.67)，平均 22.16% 的被丢弃候选经重拟合后优于对应 incumbent，最高 65.75%。候选级诊断 SPB(20 题 200 个含 2–11 个参数、中位 7 的候选,冷启动同初始化):lost rate 分别为 SAGE-Fit 9.5%、SAGE-Fit w/o FS-FPS 15.0%、BFGS_multi 54.5%、BFGS_single 70.0%;mean log NMSE 分别为 −3.863/−3.786/−2.470/−1.460;中位单候选耗时 0.57s/0.96s/0.37s/0.16s。消融表明 FS-FPS 贡献明确(去掉后 lost rate 升至 15%、误差上升),且多启动 BFGS 的改善远不及结构感知投影+语义初始化组合,说明失败不能仅用重启次数解释。

Conclusion: 本文将"好结构、差分数"确立为现代 SR 中被低估的关键瓶颈,主张从"生成假设"转向"忠实评估":结构感知降维、语义空间多样初始化与专用投影 Gauss–Newton 的组合能显著提高内环评估保真度,从而在完全不改动外层搜索逻辑的前提下普遍提升 PySR/uDSR/LaSR/LLM-SR 的符号准确率与数值精度,证实更优的参数优化是释放现代 SR 系统发现能力的关键催化因素。同时应关注以下局限:(1) 绝对 SA 数值仍很低(多在 2–14% 区间),增益主要体现在相对倍数与部分域;(2) 与"真实 oracle 分数 S*"相比仍存在评估差距 Δ(f)>0,SPB 采用四者最优作为经验参照而非真最优,故 lost rate 为相对指标;(3) SAGE-Fit 单候选中位耗时(0.57s)高于 BFGS(0.16s),在大规模候选评估中成本更高,需在时间预算内权衡;(4) 数据集规模表述不一致(正文 129 题、附录 128 题);(5) 基线行采用 SAGE-Fit 后处理重拟合后计算,虽意在公平对比最终拟合与搜索引导的贡献,但解读需谨慎;(6) 结论为"重估内环评估"的倡议,方法本身不改变外层搜索能力上限。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) plays a central role in scientific knowledge discovery by distilling mathematical equations from observational data. Most existing SR methods function within a bi-level optimization framework: an outer loop that searches for the discrete equation structure, and an inner loop that optimizes the continuous parameters of that structure. Crucially, parameter-fitting quality directly determines a structure's score and thus the outer-loop search. However, nonlinear operators make the inner loop highly non-convex, and budget-driven reliance on fast local solvers (e.g., BFGS) often yields poor local minima and underestimated scores for correct structures. This ``Good Structure, Bad Score'' phenomenon becomes a key bottleneck, degrading efficiency and misguiding the search away from the true equation. To resolve this, we propose SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator for Symbolic Regression), an SR-native fitting framework that exploits the dual native priors of symbolic expressions. By capitalizing on the structural and semantic priors unique to SR, we design tailored modules for each property, thereby effectively mitigating this optimization bottleneck. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach, as a plug-and-play module, significantly enhances evaluation fidelity and universally improves the performance of various SR systems.

</details>


### [190] [Diffusion Domain Expansion: Learning to Coordinate Pre-trained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23275)
*Egor Lifar,Semyon Savkin,Timur Garipov,Shangyuan Tong,Tommi Jaakkola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散域扩展（DDE）：用一个小规模可训练的 ViT「协调器」网络来编排多个预训练扩散模型的去噪输出，从而把预训练模型扩展到生成更大的对象（更长的音乐、更大的图像）和更复杂的条件（多条件点的 CLEVR、多块条件地图），而无需重新训练大模型。协调器在规模 L_train 上训练后可外推到 L_test ≥ L_train。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型训练成本高（图像、音乐、机器人轨迹、分子结构），社区普遍关注复用与后训练控制。已有「扩展生成域」的方案主要是固定算法的协调机制：MultiDiffusion、SyncDiffusion、DiffCollage、RRR/Du et al. 等把多个扩散采样路径融合，无需训练、可即插即用，但只能用于其设计时的特定任务；Huang et al. (2023) 的 Collaborative Diffusion 需要训练动态融合模块，且模态数量在训练时被固定。DDE 试图用一个通用、参数高效的小型可训练协调器，同时沿两个维度扩展生成域：(1) 生成对象的空间/时间尺寸（如更长音乐、更大图像）；(2) 条件输入的数量与复杂度（如多坐标条件、多块地图条件），并希望协调器在训练时未见过的更大规模上仍能泛化。

Method: 形式化：设预训练条件扩散模型的去噪网络为 D(x,y,t)，目标是在扩展域上求解 p(X_[L]|Y_[L])，假设扩展对象可被分解算子 F 拆成 L 个基域内的子块（[x_1..x_L],[y_1..y_L])，且允许重叠。构建复合去噪器 D_[L](X(t),Y,t) = C_[L]([D(x_i(t),y_i,t)]_{i=1}^L, [y_i]_{i=1}^L, t)：预训练模型在每个子块上独立去噪，一个小型可训练协调器 C 读取这些去噪输出序列与条件序列，输出对整体 X(0) 的估计；训练目标为标准扩散去噪 L2 损失（EDM 框架，式 (1)(5)），在规模 L_train 的大对象数据集上优化，采样时用复合去噪器沿反向 ODE/概率流积分即可生成任意 L 的对象。协调器架构：基于 ViT（Peebles & Xie 2023），把子块去噪输出做 patchify 并加入相对于大对象的全局位置编码（音乐用 1D RoPE，卫星图使用作者推广的 2D RoPE：把 token 隐向量一分为二，分别按行/列坐标做旋转，保证注意力只依赖相对位移）；前向结束后对重叠区域取均值做融合（借鉴 MultiDiffusion 的重叠平均），以兼顾长程依赖与局部一致性；条件以 one-hot 2D 图或条件 token 形式输入 MLP（地图任务中模型输出 token 与条件 token 使用不同 MLP 权重）；可在协调器层面施加 classifier-free guidance（式 (6)，训练时以 10% 概率置空条件）；训练使用 EMA 权重、Heun 二阶校正采样器、Karras σ 调度。

Result: 1) 音乐（Slakh2100，基座 UNet1d 405M、300 epoch、约 12 秒片段）：训练用 L_train=5，测试 L_test=13（48 秒/120 秒）。FAD：DDE 2.112(4l)/2.142(10l)，RNN 4.223/4.081，RNN+重叠 4.447/4.424，拼接 4.623/4.596，MultiDiffusion 4.732/4.796，显著优于所有基线。2) CLEVR 多条件生成（基座 UNet 42M、1050 epoch）：训练仅见 L_train=2，测试 L_test=5。Heun 采样下条件满足准确率 DDE 为 96.5/94.1/86.3/66.8/44.5（L=1..5），优于 RRR（98.0/94.5/83.2/63.3/33.6）与 MultiDiffusion（96.9/94.1/76.2/43.0/25.0）；协调器仅 38M 参数、约 20 epoch 收敛（远少于基座 1050 epoch）。3) 卫星图条件生成（Google Maps API 新数据集，基座 270M、200 epoch）：训练 N=96，OOD 测试 N=128（甚至展示 256）。FID：DDE 31.753/27.373 vs MultiDiffusion 37.815/35.016；协调器 26M 参数。总体上 DDE 参数量远小于基座模型、收敛更快，且能外推到训练未见的更大尺寸。附录中个别细节与正文存在不一致（如地图协调器训练 epoch 正文写 20、附录写 100；CLEVR 协调器正文 20、附录 40）。

Conclusion: DDE 用一个通用而紧凑的可训练协调器（ViT + 相对位置编码 + 重叠平均融合）在预训练扩散模型的去噪输出层面实现「协调」，可同时扩展生成对象的尺寸与条件输入的数量，并能泛化到训练时未见过的更大规模，在音乐长片段、CLEVR 多条件生成、卫星图条件生成三个领域均以定性与定量（FAD、条件准确率、FID）优于拼接、MultiDiffusion、RRR 等基线；相较从头训练或大规模微调，协调器参数量小、收敛快（单张 A100 可完成）。主要局限是需要额外的数据与时间来训练协调器；未来方向包括长程连贯蛋白质序列生成、由短视频生成视频，以及探索协调器的替代监督方式。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Diffusion Domain Expansion (DDE), a method that efficiently extends pre-trained diffusion models to generate larger objects and handle more complex conditioning beyond their original capabilities. Our method employs a compact trainable network designed to coordinate the denoised outputs of pre-trained diffusion models. We demonstrate that the coordinator can be universally simple while being capable of generalizing to domains larger than those observed during its training time. We evaluate DDE on long audio track generation and conditional image generation, demonstrating its applicability across domains. DDE outperforms other approaches to coordinated generation with diffusion models in qualitative and quantitative evaluations.

</details>


### [191] [Reinforcement Learning for Microcanonical Graph Ensemble with Assortativity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23285)
*Hoyun Choi,Junghyo Jo,Deok-Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DMGG，一种基于强化学习的微典范图生成器，通过度保持重连精确控制同配性，完全消除了ERGMs中的软约束问题。方法上采用策略引导搜索直接修改联合度矩阵；结果上在保持构型多样性的前提下加速超过一个数量级；结论上为研究结构-功能关系提供了无伪影的精确零模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional ERGMs impose constraints only in expectation, allowing realizations to fluctuate; microcanonical ensembles with hard constraints have been impractical beyond degree sequences, limiting the ability to generate exact null models for isolating specific structural observables.

Method: DMGG uses a policy-guided search that maximizes alteration of the joint-degree matrix through degree-preserving rewirings, eliminating exhaustive parameter tuning required by ERGMs.

Result: DMGG achieves at least one order of magnitude speedup over Metropolis-Hastings dynamics while exactly reaching target assortativity and preserving configurational diversity. It generalizes across various graph sizes, sparsities, and topologies.

Conclusion: DMGG establishes RL as a practical paradigm for hard-constrained graph generation, enabling artifact-free investigation of structure-function relationships.

Abstract: How network structure determines function is a fundamental question, and it can be investigated by graph ensembles with precisely controlled structural properties. Canonical approaches, formulated as exponential random graph models (ERGMs), enforce constraints only in expectation, allowing individual realizations to fluctuate around the target. Conversely, microcanonical ensembles impose hard constraints exactly, but practical sampling methods beyond fixing the degree sequence have remained out of reach. Here we introduce the Deep Microcanonical Graph Generator (DMGG), a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that transforms any given graph through degree-preserving rewirings to exactly reach a prescribed assortativity, which characterizes the degree--degree correlation of adjacent nodes. Instead of relying on the entropically dominated Metropolis--Hastings dynamics of the ERGM, DMGG employs a policy-guided search that maximally alters the joint-degree matrix. This eliminates exhaustive parameter tuning and accelerates generation by at least an order of magnitude while preserving configurational diversity. As DMGG generalizes across various graph sizes, sparsities, and topologies, it provides exact null models that allow for the quantitative isolation of secondary observables, such as the clustering coefficient. These results establish RL as a practical and powerful paradigm for generating hard-constrained graphs, opening avenues to investigate structure-function relationships free from ensemble artifacts.

</details>


### [192] [Contrastive Distribution Matching for Amortized Sequential Monte Carlo in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23346)
*Jaihoon Kim,Taehoon Yoon,Prin Phunyaphibarn,Seungjun Kim,Morteza Mardani,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散扩散模型中从奖励倾斜分布高效采样这一核心难题，本文提出 Contrastive Distribution Matching (CDM) 框架：通过学习参数化的最优扭函数（twist function）把 Twisted SMC 的推理成本摊销为一次前向传播的常数时间操作。方法用前向 KL 匹配目标分布，其梯度天然呈现对比结构（正样本项提升 log ψ、负样本项压低 log ψ）；关键技巧是利用离散扩散闭式前向核把中间目标分布分解为 p*_0(x_0)·p_base(x_t|x_0)，从而只需采样少量干净正样本并通过前向核重加噪，在多个时间步上复用同一奖励评估，实现对昂贵奖励的可扩展训练。扭函数被参数化为预训练去噪骨干网络末层特征上的轻量标量头（MLP / MLP+PE / Transformer），推理开销仅占骨干前向的 0.5%–5%。在毒性文本生成、调控型 DNA 序列设计、蛋白质设计性、扩散大语言模型对齐四类任务上，CDM 在同等墙钟时间下全面超越 Best-of-N、SMC(M=1,4)、SMC+Grad 与回归式 Soft Value，并能叠加 d1/DRAKES 等微调提案获得协同增益，同时缓解微调方法的模式崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散（尤其是掩码扩散模型 MDM）已成为生成结构化类别数据的有力框架，其核心应用之一是奖励对齐：从预训练先验 p^base 采样自奖励倾斜分布 p*_t ∝ p^base_t · ψ*_t。KL 正则化目标的最优解由最优扭函数 ψ*_t = exp(V_t/β) 给出，Twisted SMC 可渐近精确地采样该分布，但每一步的增量重要性权重 w_{t-1} = (ψ*_{t-1}/ψ*_t)·(p^base/q) 都要求评估扭函数。在连续扩散中该量可用 Tweedie 公式（后验均值）廉价插值估计，而离散空间不存在类似关系（离散扩散无 Tweedie 估计），只能采用昂贵的蒙特卡洛估计（每个状态抽 M 个干净样本平均 exp(r/β)），在奖励评估昂贵（如折叠模型计算设计性、API 奖励模型）时构成严重的推理瓶颈；增大 M 可提升精度但推理时间线性增长。同时，梯度引导类方案（一阶泰勒展开的最优提案）需依赖 Gumbel-Softmax 近似，存在梯度偏差与优化不稳定，且完全无法处理不可微奖励。因此需要一种把 SMC 推理成本摊销化的方法：在训练阶段预先学习扭函数，使其在推理时成为常数时间操作。

Method: 框架：CDM（Contrastive Distribution Matching）。设 p^φ_t(x_t) = p^base_t(x_t)ψ^φ_t(x_t)/Z^φ_t 为被参数化扭函数调制的中间分布，训练目标为最小化时间平均前向 KL：L_CDM(φ) = E_t[KL(p*_t ‖ p^φ_t)]。其梯度具有显式对比结构：-∇_φ L = E_t[ E_{p*_t}[∇log ψ^φ] (正项，上调高奖励区域的 log ψ) − E_{p^φ_t}[∇log ψ^φ] (负项，压低次优样本) ]。正项通过扩散特有的分解 p*_t(x_t) = Σ_{x_0} p*_0(x_0)·p^base(x_t|x_0) 改写为 E_{p*_0}E_{p^base(x_t|x_0)}[∇log ψ^φ]，得到无偏的前向核梯度估计器；由此可维护干净正样本缓冲区 B（用蒙特卡洛扭估计 + SMC 采样得到，按 n_update 步刷新），对样本重复施加闭式前向核获得任意时间步的 x_t^*，实现单样本多梯度更新，大幅摊销奖励评估成本。负样本通过将重要性权重中的最优扭替换为参数化扭 ψ^φ 的 IS/SMC 采样 p^φ_t 得到（实验发现负采样用 IS 更高效）；训练采用 EMA 软目标更新 φ̄ = stopgrad(φ) 稳定在线优化（Alg. 1 Twisted SMC/IS、Alg. 2 CDM 训练）。参数化：扭函数不是独立训练的新网络，而是预训练骨干最后隐层上的轻量标量头（MLP、MLP+PE、Transformer 三种），与 logit 头共享骨干特征并行输出，单次前向同时给出 logits 与扭值，推理开销 <5%（最低 0.5%）。SMC 细节：马尔可夫假设下增量权重为 ψ*_{t-1}/ψ*_t × p^base(x_{t-1}|x_t)/q(x_{t-1}|x_t)，按 ESS 阈值自适应重采样（ESS_thres=0 退化为 IS），IS 时扭比沿时间步望远镜式相消、仅需在 t_stop 计算一次。提案可选 base 或微调后的 p^FT（此时权重含 p^base/p^FT 修正），扭函数与提案解耦、可任意组合。

Result: 在四个应用上以匹配墙钟时间的 scaling 实验中，CDM 全部建立新的 Pareto 前沿，优于 Best-of-N、SMC(M=1,4)、SMC+Grad 与 Soft Value（回归式扭学习）：（1）毒性文本（DiT-MDM/OpenWebText，毒性分类器奖励 + 多语分类器 held-out 奖励）；（2）调控型 DNA 设计（CNN-MDM/70 万增强子数据，Enformer HepG2 奖励 + 独立验证集 Enformer held-out 奖励），仅 SMC M=4 在给定奖励上接近但 held-out 明显更差；（3）蛋白质设计性（DPLM-2，scRMSD 奖励 + scTM held-out 奖励），奖励评估耗时 1356 ms/次，SMC 缩放过慢需单独插图，CDM 仍优于 BoN 与 Soft Value；（4）扩散 LLM 对齐（LLaDA-8B-Instruct + RewardBench，Skywork 偏好奖励 + ArmoRM held-out 奖励），CDM 一致优于 BoN 且在 held-out 上优势更明显。扭头运行时分析（RTX Pro 6000）显示相对骨干开销为 0.710%/2.94%/0.425%/4.637%。兼容性：与 d1（GRPO）、DRAKES（Gumbel-Softmax 反传）叠加后进一步提升性能（毒性文本：Self-BLEU 0.015、PPL 124.5，优于 d1 的 0.051/332.3 与 DRAKES 的 0.027/322.4；蛋白质：FoldSeek clusters 17、inner-TM 0.499，优于 d1 的 14/0.851），说明 CDM 可缓解微调方法的模式崩塌并保持奖励水平。训练效率：同等优化器/架构/批大小下，CDM 在墙钟时间上比任意 M 的 Soft Value 收敛更快、最终奖励更高（如毒性 -0.4614 vs Soft Value M=1 的 -1.8060、M=4 的 -0.9030；蛋白 -1.6950/-1.5725 vs -1.8237/-1.6895）。消融：CDM 对蒙特卡洛样本数 M 不敏感（性能保持一致），正样本采样用 SMC 明显优于 IS（蛋白 -1.5725 vs -1.8049），缓冲区更新间隔 n_update=4~10 均可稳定工作（奖励昂贵时取更大值）。

Conclusion: 本文指出离散扩散中 Twisted SMC 推理的核心瓶颈在于最优扭函数的蒙特卡洛估计，并提出 CDM：通过对比式分布匹配（前向 KL + 正负样本）预先学习扭函数，利用离散扩散的闭式前向核把中间目标分解为干净目标与前向核，使一个干净正样本可在多个时间步、多次梯度更新中复用，从而把 SMC 推理摊销为常数时间（<5% 开销）的轻量头前向。实验证明 CDM 在墙钟时间匹配的 scaling 上稳定超越现有基线，训练收敛快于回归式 Soft Value，对昂贵与不可微奖励同样有效，可与任意（含已微调）提案组合获得协同增益并缓解模式崩塌。局限与展望：复杂奖励可能需要更复杂的扭头结构；极稀疏信号下的期望未来回报建模仍困难；未来将扩展到原生支持二值/类别型奖励结构。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have emerged as powerful frameworks for generating structured categorical data. However, efficiently sampling from reward-tilted distributions remains a fundamental challenge. While Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) offers asymptotic exactness for this task, estimating the optimal twist function in discrete state spaces necessitates costly Monte Carlo approximations, resulting a severe computational bottleneck at inference. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contrastive Distribution Matching (CDM), a novel framework that amortizes the cost of SMC inference by learning a parameterized twist function via positive and negative samples. For efficient training, we reformulate the gradient estimator to leverage the closed-form forward kernels of discrete diffusion models. In practice, evaluating our learned twist function incurs less than 5% additional computational overhead compared to a single forward pass of the base model. Through extensive empirical evaluations, we demonstrate that CDM consistently outperforms existing baselines under matched wall-clock time. We validate the effectiveness and versatility of our approach across a diverse range of applications, including toxic text generation, regulatory DNA sequence design, protein designability, and diffusion large language model alignment.

</details>


### [193] [Prudent-Banker: No Extra Fees for Baseline Safety in Adversarial Bandits With and Without Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23351)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 **Prudent-Banker**：面向带（任意）延迟反馈的对抗多臂老虎机算法，在保持 minimax 最优最坏情形遗憾 Õ(√T+√D) 的同时，对指定"安全"基线策略仅付出 Õ(1)（更精确为 O(1+log D)）的遗憾；并证明该安全—鲁棒性权衡在安全约束下（除对数因子外）不可改进，首次在延迟反馈设定下实现"零学习成本"的安全在线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 现实系统（网络、视频流、机器人、多智能体、广告与临床决策）的反馈普遍延迟，而现有延迟对抗 bandit 理论只关注 minimax 遗憾，未刻画"高危场景"中的安全要求：学习者通常已被赋予一个经过验证的"安全基线策略"（如标准医疗方案、合规控制规则、保守投资策略），学习代价应被限制。
2) 三个根本障碍：(A) Lattimore 的不可能性结果指出 bandit 反馈下对确定性比较器做到 O(1) 遗憾必然招致 Ω(T) 最坏遗憾，故必须将基线放松为全支撑（uniformly exploratory）分布；(B) FTRL/OMD/Exp3 类算法的势函数望远镜结构依赖即时反馈，延迟使更新使用陈旧信息、隐藏损失，标准"安全包装器"（Prod-MWU 变体、参数自由方法）与延迟版 OMD 无法直接拼接；(C) 分阶段激进（phased aggression）的重启判据依赖"阶段间隙统计量"，延迟下未观测反馈形成"隐藏债务"，最坏扭曲可达 O(√D/δ)，足以抵消标准阈值给出的证据，造成保守—激进切换时机错乱、破坏安全保证。
4) 核心张力：给阈值加上 ξ≈O(√D) 的松弛可中和"幽灵损失"，但总延迟 D 事先未知，用最坏上界（D≈T²）会得到 ξ≈O(T) 与空洞的线性遗憾。

Method: **问题设定**：oblivious 对手，损失 ℓ_t∈[0,1]^A，延迟 d_t，总延迟 D=Σd_t；伪遗憾 ℛ_T=max_x EΣ⟨ℓ_t, x_t−x⟩；次目标为相对安全基线 x^c 的遗憾 ℛ(x^c)（假设 x^c(a)≥δ，全支撑）。
**算法（Algorithm 1，三层嵌套）**：
① Stage 层（延迟自适应）：维护延迟估计 D̂_s（初值 1），若阶段内累计延迟超过估计则 D̂_{s+1}≈2D_t^{(s)} 并执行 Hard Restart（重置 Banker 状态、α 重新计算）；阶段数 S≤⌈log₂D⌉+1。
② 核心层（Banker-OMD，Huang et al.）：用"步长额度/记账"机制，只在反馈到达时用重要性加权估计 ℓ̂_u=ℓ_u(A_u)/x_u(A_u)·e_{A_u} 构造镜像下降更新；每单位学习率预算只用一次，缺失反馈以默认分布 x_0=1/A 补足，恢复望远镜结构。
③ Phase 层（安全/分阶段激进）：混合策略 x_t=α_k x̂_t+(1−α_k)x^c；用已到达数据计算相位间隙 max_x⟨g_t, x^c−x⟩；当其超过**延迟校准重启阈值** B(D̂_s)=2R̂(D̂_s)+ξ̂(D̂_s)（R̂(D)=√(C₁C₂)(3√T+7√(2D ln(D+1)))，ξ̂(D)=(√(8D+1)−1)/δ ≈ Θ(√(D/δ))）时进入新阶段：k←k+1，α_k=min{2^{k−1}/R̂_s,1}，并 Soft Restart（重置为 1/A）。两类重启分工明确：Soft Restart 调整"基线—探索"配比，Hard Restart 校准延迟预算。
**证明架构**：按 Stage/Phase 分解遗憾；Lemma 4.1（延迟质量守恒，𝔇≤D）、4.2（D̂_{s+1}<2D，阶段数对数）、4.3（Banker-OMD 阶段内 Õ(√(T_s+D))）、4.4（缺失反馈贡献 ≤O(√D)：由 m(m+1)/2≤D 得 m≲√D 且单条 ⟨ℓ̂,x^c−x⟩≤2）、4.5（正常阶段：任意 x 遗憾 ≤4R̂+O(√D)，对 x^c 遗憾 ≤2^{k−1}）、4.6（灭绝阶段：对 x^c 遗憾 ≤−2^{k−1}，即"负遗憾信用"）。
**下界**：先证分块（batched）bandit 的安全下界（Lemma E.2，两环境 E₊/E₋ + KL/Pinsker 论证得 Ω(δ^{-1/2}√(V/L₁))），再用"贪心桶"分解把延迟实例归约为分块实例（Lemma E.3 逐路径 regret 相等），结合 V_j≥D_{S̄_j} 得延迟侧下界。

Result: **上界（Theorem 4.1）**：任意延迟序列下，ℛ_T ≤ Õ(√(C₁C₂)(√T+√D))，且 ℛ_T(x^c) ≤ O(1+log D)=Õ(1)；负熵正则化时为 Õ(δ^{-1/2}(√T+√D)) 与 O(log D)。无延迟时 ℛ_T ≤ Õ(√(δ^{-1}T log A))、ℛ(x^c)=O(1)，闭合了 Müller et al. 留下的上下界间隙。臂依赖方面：T 项与无约束基准 Ω(√(AT)) 相当（仅差对数），D 项多出 √A 因子，源自 Banker-OMD 骨架。
**下界（Theorem 4.2/E.1）**：存在延迟序列与满足 min_i x^c(i)≥δ 的比较器，使 ℛ_T(x^c)≤log D ⟹ ℛ_T ≥ Ω~(δ^{-1/2}√D)；与时间侧 Ω(√(δ^{-1}T)) 合并得 ℛ_T ≥ Ω(√(δ^{-1}T)+√(δ^{-1}D)) − O(δ^{-3/4}(T^{1/4}+D^{1/4}))，说明 δ^{-1/2} 是安全约束的内在代价，且上界在延迟/非延迟两种情形下对数意义内最优。
**实验**：T=50,000、A=100、500 个非平稳块、δ=10⁻³ 的全支撑比较器，三种延迟（固定一步 D=1545、几何 D=3927、Pareto 重尾 D=2065）。Prudent-Banker 与比较器的差距有界且在延迟下多次为负；α_t 在无延迟时单调升至 1，在延迟下随延迟估计被突破而反复回撤保守；最坏情形优于 Safe-EXP3-IX，而 Conservative-UCB 在"默认臂恰为后见最优臂"的诊断设定下表现更紧（该优势不可在线获得）。

Conclusion: Prudent-Banker 是首个在（带或不带）任意延迟反馈下同时实现 Õ(√T+√D) minimax 最坏遗憾与 Õ(1) 基线遗憾的算法，其安全—鲁棒性权衡在安全约束下除对数因子外不可改进（延迟侧代价 δ^{-1/2}√D 被证明是内在的）。方法论要点有三：(i) 以显式松弛 ξ=Θ(√(D_s/δ)) 消除"安全认证缺口"，避免因未观测损失造成虚假重启；(ii) 通过与阶段耦合的调度而非朴素几何重启，使延迟自适应在安全界上"几乎免费"（朴素重启会丢失已积累的负遗憾信用，导致 √T 或 √D 量级的重复支付）；(iii) 把 Banker-OMD 的"额度记账"结构复用为安全认证工具，实现延迟鲁棒性与安全性的统一分析。作者指出后续方向为自适应/学习型基线，以及向上下文、线性 bandit、强化学习与多智能体设定的推广；本工作不涉及具体应用领域（医疗、法律、推荐等）的落地讨论。

Abstract: We study adversarial multi-armed bandits with and without delayed feedback under a safety-aware goal: achieving minimax-optimal worst-case regret while keeping nearly constant regret relative to a designated "safe" baseline policy. Existing approaches can balance this trade-off with immediate feedback for smooth comparators, but arbitrary delays can mistime transitions between conservatism and exploration, endangering the safety guarantee. To bridge this gap, we propose Prudent-Banker, a novel algorithm that combines a delay-adapted variant of Online Mirror Descent with a modified phased-aggression mechanism. Its key technical contribution is a delay-calibrated restart threshold that rigorously accounts for the worst-case distortion induced by unobserved feedback and reliably detects comparator suboptimality. We also establish new lower bounds for safety-constrained adversarial delayed bandits, showing that the regret guarantees of Prudent-Banker are unimprovable, up to logarithmic factors, under the baseline-safety requirement. To the best of our knowledge, Prudent-Banker is the first algorithm to achieve the optimal safety--robustness trade-off: pseudo-regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$ together with $\widetilde{O}(1)$ regret against the safe comparator, both with and without delays. Experiments across diverse delay distributions show that, unlike standard delay-robust baselines, Prudent-Banker effectively balances safety and learning.

</details>


### [194] [Instance-Optimal Estimation with Multiple LLM Judges on a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23362)
*Junghyun Lee,Sanghwa Kim,Yassir Jedra,Alexandre Proutière,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束下多裁判LLM评估的优化分配问题，提出EST-IVWE自适应算法实现方差未知时的最优估计，建立了局部 minimax 下界证明算法实例最优性，采用Assouad型论证技术保留局部分布结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型评估越来越依赖LLM-as-a-judge协议，但评估成本高昂。不同裁判有不同的价格和可靠性，查询难度也差异显著。在固定预算下，如何最优分配评估查询以获得最准确的分数估计，是一个基础性的资源分配问题。

Method: 1. 分析了逆方差加权估计量(IVWE)，推导出最小化误差率的oracle分配；2. 提出EST-IVWE自适应算法，利用乐观偏倚的方差估计来稳定经验分配；3. 采用Assouad型in-expectation论证而非Fano型方法，以保留局部分布结构。

Result: 1. 证明了EST-IVWE在预算主导项上与oracle IVWE速率相同；2. 建立了匹配的下界，证明了算法的实例最优性；3. 数值实验验证了相比均匀分配方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 论文在预算约束下的异方差多裁判估计问题上取得了完整的理论成果，建立了估计误差与预算分配之间的最优性等价关系，为自适应算法设计提供了理论基础，并验证了方法在合成数据和真实数据上的优越性。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models increasingly relies on LLM-as-a-judge protocols, but such evaluations remain costly: different judges have different prices and reliabilities, and the difficulty of each prompt-response pair can vary substantially. This raises a basic allocation question: under a fixed budget, how should one distribute evaluation queries across heterogeneous judges and instances to obtain the most accurate score estimates? We formalize this question as *budgeted heteroskedastic multi-judge estimation*. Given $K$ prompt-response pairs, $J$ judges with known costs, and unknown query-judge variances, the goal is to estimate a bounded score vector while minimizing an $\ell_p$-error. Our first contribution is to analyze the inverse-variance weighted estimator (IVWE) and to derive the oracle allocation that minimizes its error rate. Since this allocation depends on the unknown variances, we then address the practical unknown-variance setting by proposing EST-IVWE, an adaptive algorithm that constructs and leverages *optimistically biased* variance estimates to stabilize the empirical allocation. We prove that EST-IVWE matches the oracle IVWE rate up to lower-order terms in the budget. Our second and central theoretical contribution is a matching *local* minimax lower bound, which establishes the instance-optimality of the proposed algorithms. A key technical insight is that Fano-type high-probability arguments are too coarse for this problem: their packing construction loses the local variance structure that governs the optimal allocation. We instead use an Assouad-type in-expectation argument, based on local perturbations, which preserves this structure and yields the sharp allocation-dependent lower bound. Finally, we numerically validate the superiority of our approach over naïve uniform allocation on synthetic and HelpSteer2 datasets.

</details>


### [195] [Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23365)
*Kyungyoon Kim,Donghyeon Ki,Hee-Jun Ahn,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOM proposes an actor-critic algorithm that enables one-step policy generation in online RL by constructing target velocity fields from Q-functions via score estimation, achieving better performance and efficiency than multi-step diffusion/flow-matching approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing diffusion and flow-matching policies suffer from multi-step denoising overhead at inference time, which is problematic for online RL. MeanFlow offers single-step generation but requires target distribution samples for constructing the velocity field—data unavailable in online RL.

Method: SOM constructs the target velocity field directly from the Q-function via score estimation and probability flow ODE, concentrating probability mass on high-value modes. It is an actor-critic algorithm designed for the fully online RL setting.

Result: SOM achieves state-of-the-art performance on locomotion tasks with only a single generation step, while substantially reducing both training and inference time compared to prior diffusion- and flow-matching-based policies.

Conclusion: SOM enables efficient single-step policy generation in online RL by avoiding the computational overhead of multi-step denoisers while maintaining strong performance on locomotion tasks.

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching have emerged as expressive policy classes in reinforcement learning, but their reliance on multi-step denoising imposes substantial computational overhead at inference time, which is particularly problematic in online RL. MeanFlow offers a promising alternative by learning an average velocity field that maps noise to data in a single network evaluation. However, MeanFlow typically requires samples from the target distribution to construct its target velocity field, which are unavailable in online RL. We propose Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization (SOM), an actor-critic algorithm that resolves this by constructing the target velocity field directly from the Q-function via score estimation and a probability flow ODE, thereby concentrating probability mass on high-value modes. In the fully online RL setting, SOM achieves state-of-the-art performance on locomotion tasks with a single generation step, while substantially reducing both training and inference time compared to prior diffusion- and flow-matching-based policies.

</details>


### [196] [Curriculum reinforcement learning with measurable task representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23372)
*Yongyan Wen,Siyuan Li,Mingjian Fu,Yiqin Yang,Xun Wang,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACRL（Automatic Curriculum with Representation Learning）：用 VAE 把任务上下文编码到可度量的隐空间，学习到"奖励+状态转移"层面的任务相似性度量，再据此在隐空间做插值（LSP）或在探索边界加噪外推（EBU），自动生成从初始任务分布到目标任务分布的中间课程，在 MiniGrid 与 U-Maze 上优于 CURROT、Goal GAN、PLR、VDS、ALP-GMM 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程强化学习（CRL）通过一系列由易到难的任务让智能体积累知识，最终解决稀疏奖励、长时序的困难目标任务。自动课程生成的主流范式是"任务分布插值"（如 CURROT 使用 Wasserstein 距离、PLR/ALP-GMM/Goal GAN 等），但它们隐含假设上下文（任务）空间中的欧氏距离可以刻画任务难度/相似性。在迷宫、U-Maze 等具有复杂几何结构的导航任务中，墙体阻挡使直线插值产生的中间任务不可达（例如插值路径正好撞墙），该假设失效；同时这些不考虑目标任务的基线在目标任务上的学习效率不稳定。此外，基于 GAN 的方法与任务相似性度量脱节，无法给出平滑的任务过渡。因此需要一个能把非欧氏任务空间映射到可度量空间的表示机制，从而自动生成平滑、逐渐逼近目标的中间任务分布。

Method: 框架基于上下文马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）：上下文 c 决定奖励函数 R 与转移函数 P，"任务"即由具体上下文实例化的 CMDP。整体包含两部分：
(1) 任务表示学习（VAE）。执行当前策略 π(s,c) 收集轨迹 τ_c = {(s_t,a_t,r_t,s'_{t})}；转移编码器 q_φ(z|τ_c,t) 对单条转移输出高斯分布（μ, σ²I），轨迹级任务表示由所有转移嵌入相乘 q_φ(z|τ_c)=∏_t q_φ(z_t|τ_c,t) 得到，从而获得排列不变（permutation-invariant）的任务嵌入；解码器由奖励解码器 p^R_θ(r|z) 与转移解码器 p^T_θ(s'|z) 构成，联合优化 ELBO（重构项 + KL）；另加辅助任务解码器 p^c_θ(z)，把隐变量映射回上下文 c，以 L=||c − p^c_θ(z)||₂ 训练，用于在隐空间插值/外推后生成新上下文（兼容参数化与经验分布，兼顾离散与连续上下文空间）。表示学习与策略学习联合训练（策略梯度更新不回传到表示部分）。
(2) 自动课程生成。维护任务缓冲区 T（非参数中间分布）与 FIFO 的 VAE 缓冲区 B。以策略在当前任务上的评估平均回报 Ĝ 作为更新门控：达标后先更新任务表示，再在目标任务的评估回报与目标阈值 δ_tar 比较——未达标用 EBU，达标则切换为 LSP。
- LSP（隐空间预测）：从 B 中取回报 ≥ δ 的 (c_i, τ_i)，计算 z_i 与目标轨迹嵌入 z_tar，按 d(c,c_tar)=||z − z_tar||₂ 排序，取 z' = α z_tar + (1−α) z，再解码为上下文 c' = p^c_θ(z')，加入 T。需目标任务上至少一条成功轨迹。
- EBU（探索边界更新）：用回报最高的目标轨迹作为 z_tar，将已学任务按到目标的距离降序排列，用指数分布 P(ξ;β)=βe^{−βξ} 采样索引选取边界任务，在上下文空间加维度独立高斯噪声 N(0, σ²I) 生成新任务（离散上下文需离散化）。
采样时以概率 λ 从 T 采样、否则从上下文空间均匀分布 U(C) 采样，以维持探索多样性与可行性（对不可行任务做拒绝采样）。方法与具体 RL 算法解耦，可与 PPO/SAC 等配合。

Result: 在 MiniGrid 八个难度分级环境（Easy/Medium/Hard A/B/C，147 维部分可观测观测、稀疏奖励、含钥匙/门/岩浆的序列任务）与连续控制 U-Maze（MuJoCo，中心屏障使 l₂ 距离失效）上，以 PPO 为底层算法、5 个随机种子平均，与 Default、Random、ALP-GMM、Goal GAN、VDS、PLR、CURROT 比较。ACRL 在到达阈值（回报 ≥0.75）的 time-to-threshold 上明显更快，且在阈值之后表现更稳定；CURROT 等基于插值的方法因欧氏假设失效，中间分布可能撞墙而难以迁移；Goal GAN 渐近性能尚可但缺少目标引导故达标更慢；PLR/ALP-GMM 不以目标任务为中心，目标任务上学习不稳定；Random 在简单任务上因上下文样本少反而表现不差。课程可视化显示 ACRL 生成的中间分布会沿环境几何结构从初始位置先移向缺口（可通行区域）再逐步逼近目标，而 CURROT 常把智能体引向被墙阻挡的目标。表示分析：隐空间嵌入的相对关系与任务真实难度一致（尽管上下文空间上更远的任务因需绕过墙反而更相似），MDS 谱分解显示嵌入的 Gram 矩阵仅前两个特征值显著为正、其余近零，表明数据实际落在近似二维欧氏流形上（该性质是涌现的而非先验假设）。消融显示：λ=0.25 最优，λ 过大（0.75/1.0）因解码器损失增大、遗忘与探索不足而性能明显变差，λ=0 退化为均匀采样；δ、δ_tar、G 等课程参数在合理范围内鲁棒；去掉奖励解码器或转移解码器均导致表示退化与性能下降（U-Maze 尤其明显），二者作用相当；仅用 LSP 或仅用 EBU 都会使表示不稳定、效率下降，其中 EBU 影响更大。

Conclusion: 可度量的任务表示学习能有效刻画非欧氏上下文空间中的任务相似性，从而支撑平滑、面向目标的自动课程生成；ACRL 无需外部环境奖励，兼容任意 RL 算法与离散/连续上下文空间，在多个复杂导航任务上取得更优的样本效率与渐近性能。主要局限是假设上下文空间是参数化的（可用数值参数表示并可被解码重构），无法直接处理符号化或语言描述的非参数任务上下文；作者提出可引入语义编码器扩展，并对隐空间的欧氏结构给出经验性观察但未做形式化保证。

Abstract: In curriculum reinforcement learning (CRL), an agent incrementally accumulates knowledge over a sequence of tasks (i.e., a curriculum), and the learning process is aimed at using the accumulated knowledge to finally solve a challenging target task. While early CRL works focus on sequencing candidate tasks, recent research explores automatic curriculum generation. Among the rich CRL literature, the interpolation-based CRL paradigm is a main body, which automatically generates intermediate tasks by interpolating between the initial task distribution and the target task distribution in task space with meaningful distance metrics (i.e., can measure the task similarity). However, in challenging navigation tasks, the non-Euclidean context (task) space invalidates this assumption. To achieve automatic curriculum generation in complex task, we propose a novel automatic curriculum generation approach based on measurable task representation learning. To better measure the similarity, we propose to transform the task space to a latent space. Through a variational autoencoder structure that encodes the reward and the state transitions, we achieve a latent task representation with a task similarity measurement property, and two close task embeddings correspond to two similar tasks in terms of rewards and state transitions. Based on the learned task representation, we further develop an automatic curriculum generation scheme, which can effectively generate new tasks more and more similar to the target task. We evaluate our method in a variety of challenging navigation tasks, and the experiment results indicate that the proposed approach surpasses state-of-the-art CRL approaches based on interpolation and generative adversarial networks.

</details>


### [197] [Coupling-Robust Accuracy in Multiphysics Physics Informed Neural Networks via Kronecker-Preconditioned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23391)
*Youngjae Park,Jaemin Kim,Junghwa Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用 NTK（神经切核）谱分析解释了多物理场 PINN 中"方程间耦合越强、精度越差"的原因，并给出结构性解法：块对角 Gauss–Newton (GN) 预条件将预条件 NTK 的谱半径限制在网络数 S，与耦合强度无关；工程上用 Kronecker 预条件优化器 SOAP + 逆梯度范数加权（GradNorm）在 222 次实验、4 个基准系统中实现"耦合鲁棒"精度，并在 2D 电渗流（解析双电层，ε=0.01）上取得 Adam 类方法完全失败的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物理场 PINN 在方程耦合增强时出现系统性的精度退化（强耦合/弱耦合误差比可达 10² 以上，Adam+GradNorm 甚至直接失败），而已有工作多针对单方程问题：要么改表示/训练动态（Fourier 特征、域分解、因果训练），要么事后做损失重加权（GradNorm、自适应注意力），这些启发式方法均未针对"方程间耦合导致的失衡"。已有耦合处理方式（混合变分形式、逐场顺序训练、分离式网络）同样是启发式的架构选择，耦合本身未被当作分析对象。作者的目标是：(i) 系统刻画精度如何随耦合强度退化；(ii) 证明并实现"为什么是预条件结构（而非损失加权）能消除这一依赖"。

Method: 理论部分：设 S 个参数互斥的分离式网络、M 个 PDE 残差，构造残差 Jacobian J=[J_{i,θ_k}] 与块对角 GN Hessian H=blkdiag(J_θk^T J_θk)。定理1（GD）：线性耦合下 λ_max(K=JJ^T) ≥ γ²σ_max²(C_ik)，故 η_max^GD=O(1/γ²)。引理1+定理2（块对角 GN）：K_P=JH⁺J^T 可分解为 S 个正交投影之和，λ_max(K_P)≤S，与 γ、PDE 结构、参数化无关；过参数化时取等 K_P=SI。命题1：SOAP 的逐层 Kronecker 预条件在 Assumption 3 下 λ_max≤SL。命题2：Adam 的对角预条件破坏投影结构，trace=p 而 rank≤MN_r，故 λ_max≥p/(MN_r)≫S，且该迹恒等式与权重无关（对任意 Λ>0 成立）。命题3：Adam 残差动力学核 K_{1/2}=JD^{-1/2}J^T 的 λ_max=Θ(γ)，η_max^Adam=O(1/γ)。命题4+推论1（单向耦合下的类级分离）：任意正对角预条件（固定或自适应）在驱动残差上需 Ω(γ²)（固定）或 Ω(γ) 迭代才能减半，而块对角 GN 只需 O(1)；要靠重加权逃逸需 λ_T/λ_u=Ω(γ²)，超出梯度范数均衡的设计目标。数值验证：对三个 1D 系统在全雅可比上数值计算 K、K_P^GN、K_P^{lw-GN}、K_P^Adam、K_{1/2}^Adam 的 λ_max，并跟踪训练过程中的谱演化。实验部分：4 个基准（1D 热弹性单向耦合、1D 三物种反应扩散双向耦合、1D Nernst–Planck–Poisson 非线性、2D NP+P+Stokes 电渗流六残基），分离式 MLP（tanh + Xavier），比较 Adam / SOAP / Adam+GradNorm / SOAP+GradNorm，固定学习率、采样点与 epoch（1D 3 万、2D 5 万），3 seed，指标为末轮相对 L2 及强/弱耦合退化比。

Result: 谱验证：λ_max(K) 随 γ 呈 γ² 增长（如热弹性 γ=0.1→100 时 1.0e4→9.8e7），λ_max(K_{1/2}^Adam) 呈 Θ(γ)；λ_max(K_P^GN)=S 在所有系统、所有训练快照中精确成立（过参数化、满行秩），λ_max(K_P^{lw-GN})≈9.3<SL=10，λ_max(K_P^Adam)≈1.2e4 ≫ p/(MN_r)≈63 且与耦合无关。精度：SOAP+GradNorm 的退化比在热弹性 0.6×、反应扩散 0.9×、NP+P 2.3×，是唯一在所有情形下降化有界的配置；Adam 分别为 16×、9.6×、失败（>0.1 阈值），Adam+GradNorm 在三者上失败，SOAP 在 NP+P 上退化 118×。2D EOF：SOAP+GradNorm 在 ε=0.2/0.1/0.05 时 L2 分别为 2.7e-4/5.1e-4/2.4e-3，而 Adam+GradNorm 全部失败（0.32~0.47，比值 194–1202×）；ε=0.01 时分离式网络两者均失败，改用单网络 (S=1) 后 SOAP+GradNorm 达到 L2=1.3e-2（较分离式约 50× 改善，属"从失败到成功"的转变），Adam 两种架构均失败。机制层面：K_{1/2}^Adam 的谱能量集中于慢方向，R_T 占比衰减 O(1/γ²)（γ=10 时实测 0.3%），RD 中 R_A 主导特征向量占比由 22.3% 降至 5.0%。开销：SOAP 相对 Adam 每 epoch 墙钟约 1.6×。

Conclusion: 耦合导致的 PINN 精度退化本质上是预条件器结构问题而非损失加权问题：决定性杠杆在于曲率是"跨场分块求逆"还是仅"对角缩放"。块对角 GN/SOAP 预条件使 λ_max(K_P)≤S 与耦合强度无关，而梯度下降 Ω(γ²)、Adam 瞬态 Θ(γ)、Adam 稳态 p/(MN_r) 保留对 γ 或维度的依赖；单向耦合下这一差距是类级的（无任何对角预条件可达到 O(1) 迭代复杂度）。因此可用"预条件结构判据"替代逐案例损失权重调参。局限：谱界基于线性化残差动态，与末轮精度只有经验对应而无收敛定理；瞬态结论假设均匀权重，一般加权扩展仅覆盖单向情形（推论1）；Assumption 3 中 SOAP 与逐层 GN 的对应未数值验证（命题1 描述的是精确逐层 GN 形式）；2D 基准按设计为 x-不变参考，轴向非均匀边界条件未测试；NP+P 中仍残留 2.3× 退化（源于 O(ε) 边界层逼近难度与线性化分析本身的局限）。未来工作：时变多物理场、真正二维几何、超出谱条件性的收敛性保证。注意摘要与正文存在不一致：摘要写 234 次实验、方法称 SOAP+GN 与 Adam+GN 对比，正文为 222 次实验且方法为 SOAP+GradNorm / Adam+GradNorm。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for coupled multiphysics systems suffer systematic accuracy degradation as inter-equation coupling strengthens. We provide a theoretical explanation for this phenomenon through neural tangent kernel (NTK) analysis: for linearly coupled systems, we prove that the standard NTK's spectral radius grows as $Ω(γ^2)$ with coupling strength $γ$, shrinking the stable learning rate, while block-diagonal Gauss--Newton (GN) preconditioning yields a preconditioned NTK $K_P = J H^{+} J^\top$ (where $H$ is the block-diagonal GN Hessian) whose spectral radius is bounded by $S$ ($S$ = number of networks), independent of $γ$. We verify the $Ω(γ^2)$ growth numerically across symmetric, asymmetric, and nonlinear coupled PDE systems, and confirm $λ_{\max}(K_P) = S$ with equality in all cases. Combining the Kronecker-preconditioned optimizer SOAP with inverse-gradient-norm loss balancing (SOAP+GN) yields coupling-robust accuracy: across 234 experiments spanning three 1D systems of increasing nonlinearity and a 2D electroosmotic flow benchmark, SOAP+GN maintains final-epoch $L_2$ degradation $\leq 1.1\times$ (ratio of strong- to weak-coupling error) even as coupling parameters vary over one to two orders of magnitude, compared with $> 10^2\times$ for Adam+GN. SOAP+GN further scales to a 2D, 6-PDE electroosmotic flow system at EDL-resolved conditions -- a regime that all prior PINN electrokinetics studies have avoided through simplified physics -- where Adam+GN fails entirely ($L_2 > 0.9$).

</details>


### [198] [CoSPlay: Cooperative Self-Play at Test-Time with Self-Generated Code and Unit Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23491)
*Zhangyi Hu,Chenhui Liu,Tian Huang,Jindong Li,Yang Yang,Jiemin Wu,Zining Zhong,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoSPlay addresses the GT UT bottleneck in LLM code generation by introducing a GT-free, training-free framework that jointly evolves code candidates and unit tests through cooperative self-play. Using the Code-UT execution matrix for bidirectional refinement and output-consensus for final selection, it achieves SOTA results comparable to RLVR methods purely at inference time.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs) represent a critical bottleneck in LLM code generation: state-of-the-art RLVR methods require them for costly training, while existing Test-Time Scaling (TTS) methods lose competitiveness without them. Existing GT-free approaches suffer from noisy self-generated UTs that are spuriously coupled with incorrect code, creating a chicken-and-egg problem where neither UT quality nor code reliability can be validated independently.

Method: CoSPlay employs a cooperative self-play mechanism with three core components: (1) Diverse solution exploration with failure mode identification to generate discriminative UT ideas; (2) Bidirectional refinement using the Code-UT execution matrix to iteratively prune/fix weak codes and refresh/replace unreliable UTs, enabling co-evolution of both pools; (3) Output-consensus clustering for final code selection among tied candidates.

Result: On four challenging benchmarks, CoSPlay on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct achieves: BoN improvement from 22.1% to 33.2% and UT accuracy improvement from 14.6% to 78.3%, matching or surpassing the RLVR model CURE-7B. Applied to CURE-7B, it further improves BoN by 5.7%. The method generalizes across diverse backbones, outperforms GT-free TTS baselines under comparable token budgets, and shows continued gains with scaled budgets.

Conclusion: CoSPlay demonstrates a viable path toward competitive code generation at inference time without relying on ground-truth data. The method scales effectively across different model backbones and continues to improve with increased token budgets, offering a practical and generalizable solution for GT-free code generation scenarios.

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and Test-Time Scaling (TTS) have advanced LLM code generation through executable verification. Yet Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs) remain a bottleneck: SOTA RLVR methods require them for costly training, while existing TTS methods lose competitiveness without them. This motivates GT-free TTS, where existing methods directly use self-generated UTs to refine and select code candidates. Yet such UTs are often noisy or spuriously coupled with wrong code, and UT quality in turn cannot be validated without reliable code. The key challenge is therefore to jointly improve both. To this end, we present CoSPlay, a GT-free, training-free framework that jointly improves codes and UTs through cooperative self-play. It first explores diverse solution ideas and identifies their potential failure modes to produce discriminative UT ideas. It then uses bidirectional pass-count signals from the Code-UT execution matrix to iteratively prune or fix weak codes and refresh or replace unreliable UTs, letting the two pools co-evolve. Finally, when multiple codes remain tied at the highest pass count, it picks the final code from the largest output-consensus cluster, since correct codes agree on the same inputs while wrong codes diverge. Experiments on four challenging benchmarks show that CoSPlay on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct improves average BoN from 22.1% to 33.2% and UT accuracy from 14.6% to 78.3%, matching or surpassing the RLVR model CURE-7B. When applied to CURE-7B, it further improves BoN by 5.7%. CoSPlay also generalizes across diverse backbones and outperforms GT-free TTS baselines under comparable token budgets, with continued gains as the budget scales up. These results suggest a scalable inference strategy for competitive code generation without any GT data.

</details>


### [199] [Every Component is a Lookup: Token Attribution and Composition from a Single Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23393)
*Po-Kai Chen,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper introduces Unpack, a novel interpretability method that decomposes transformer computations by exploiting the key-value structure in attention and MLP sublayers. The method recovers known circuit structures and demonstrates consistent mechanistic behavior across model scales from 160M to 6.9B parameters, without requiring ground-truth labels or auxiliary training.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mechanistic interpretability of transformers requires understanding not just which components matter, but how they compose into the computational route that produces a prediction, going beyond identifying individual important components.

Method: Unpack, a backward recursion that decomposes credit through attention and MLP sublayers by exploiting the shared key-value template φ(S)U, producing interaction strengths, end-to-end paths with K/Q/V composition labels, and per-token attribution from a single forward pass without intervention, gradients, or auxiliary training.

Result: The method successfully recovers all three composition connections from Wang et al. (2023) including mode-specific K/Q/V routing on GPT-2 small. Token-level attribution shows first mention retains strong credit while duplicate-detection positions are suppressed, a pattern absent in control prompts and consistently recovered across all Pythia model scales.

Conclusion: The method can track mechanistic structure without ground-truth circuit labels, demonstrating consistent behavior across the Pythia model family (160M to 6.9B parameters), with code publicly available.

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of transformers requires identifying not just which components matter but how they compose into the computational route that produced a prediction. Both attention and MLP follow a shared key-value template $φ(S)U$. We exploit this structure to develop Unpack, a backward recursion that decomposes credit through both sublayers, producing interaction strengths between any two components, named end-to-end paths with K/Q/V composition labels, and per-token attribution from a single forward pass, without intervention, gradients, or auxiliary training. We evaluate on the indirect object identification task. On GPT-2 small, the method recovers all three composition connections described by Wang et al. (2023), including the mode-specific routing of each connection (K, Q, or V). To test token-level attribution beyond trivial copying, we compare two occurrences of the same name in the same decomposition: the first mention retains strong credit while the duplicate-detection position is suppressed, a pattern absent in matched control prompts. Across the Pythia family from 160M to 6.9B parameters, this suppression pattern is consistently recovered at every scale, demonstrating that the method tracks mechanistic structure without ground-truth circuit labels. Code is available at https://github.com/Fun-Cry/unpacklm.

</details>


### [200] [DiLaDiff: Distilled Latent-Augmented Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23605)
*Jean-Marie Lemercier,Tomas Geffner,Karsten Kreis,Morteza Mardani,Arash Vahdat,Ante Jukić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiLaDiff是一种掩码扩散语言模型的变体，通过结合连续潜在空间（从掩码扩散语言模型微调的自动编码器学习）、潜在扩散模型学习编码器分布的先验、以及一致性模型蒸馏，实现了三组件协同。该方法在加速推理的同时超越了掩码扩散基线性能，且一致性蒸馏使潜在生成时间可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型本质上无法捕获解码token之间的相关性，导致采样质量与吞吐量之间的严峻权衡。

Method: 提出DiLaDiff，包含三个组件：(1) 通过从现有掩码扩散语言模型微调的自动编码器学习具有语义能力的连续潜在空间；(2) 学习编码器分布先验的潜在扩散模型；(3) 将学习到的先验蒸馏为少步潜在生成模型的一致性模型。

Result: 即使不进行蒸馏，潜在引导扩散模型也优于掩码扩散基线，同时显著加速推理。一致性蒸馏进一步降低了连续扩散的计算开销，使潜在生成时间与离散解码相比可忽略不计。

Conclusion: DiLaDiff成功解决了扩散语言模型中token相关性捕获的难题，通过连续潜在空间和一致性蒸馏实现了质量与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion language models intrinsically fail to capture correlations between decoded tokens, which leads to a harsh trade-off between sampling quality and throughput. To solve this issue, we propose DiLaDiff, a variant of masked diffusion language models with three components: (1) a continuous latent space with semantic capabilities, learned by an auto-encoder fine-tuned from an existing masked diffusion language model; (2) a latent diffusion model learning the prior over the encoder distribution; (3) a consistency model distilling the learned prior into a few-step latent generative model. We show that, even without distillation, our latent-guided diffusion model outperforms the masked diffusion baseline while significantly accelerating inference. Consistency distillation further lowers the computational overhead of continuous diffusion, such that the latent is generated in negligible time compared to discrete decoding.

</details>


### [201] [Convex Compositional Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23395)
*Meir Roketlishvili,Semyon Semenov,Maksim Bobrin,Viktor Kovalchuk,Albert Baichorov,Abduragim Shtanchaev,Fakhri Karray,Dmitry V. Dylov,Martin Takáč,Arip Asadulaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Convex Compositional Energy Minimization（CCEM）：把组合推理中的每个局部约束因子参数化为对决策变量凸的输入凸网络（ICNN/PICNN），利用非负加和保持凸性，使组合后的全局能量在紧凸松弛（Birkhoff 多面体/单纯形）上仍为凸，从而用确定性的投影一阶方法（PGD/投影 Adam）替代扩散链或粒子采样。训练分两阶段：因子级对比学习（InfoNCE）塑形局部能量盆地，再通过展开的投影求解器端到端精化。在 N-Queens、图着色、3-SAT 上达到或优于基于粒子的组合 EBM（8-Queens 100/100 正确），并能从单一小规模实例零重训迁移到更大规模，推理预算大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合推理（组合式能量模型 EBM）的通用做法是：学一个局部约束的能量因子 f_θ，在测试时通过 E_θ(x,y)=Σ_k w_k f_θ(y_{S_k}; c_k) 复用到任意大规模实例，从而把学习与问题规模解耦。但论文指出真正的瓶颈并非组合结构本身，也不是采样器的选择，而是**因子能量参数化导致的非凸几何**：即使各因子单独看行为良好，非凸因子求和后仍会产生稳定但全局错误的局部极小点（Prop 3.1 给出一维构造 f1=(y²−1)²、f2=α(y−1)² 的反例，梯度下降会收敛到伪极小点 ȳ≠1）。已有工作用长扩散链、Parallel Energy Minimization（PEM）等多粒子加噪采样来"逃出"这些坏盆地，属于治标不治本。论文因此主张：若每个因子在决策变量上凸且权重非负，则组合松弛能量必然全局凸，不可能出现非全局局部极小，从而可用确定性投影一阶优化替代昂贵的采样。同时作者严格区分"松弛优化"与"离散恢复"：凸性本身不保证整数性或解码有效，需引入解码器感知的能量边界（decoder-aware margin）作为充分条件。

Method: 【模型】因子 f_θ 采用部分输入凸网络 PICNN：非凸上下文通路 u 与凸作用域通路 y/z 交错，权重 W^z=softplus(W̃^z) 非负、门控 softplus 保持 z 通路非负，由凸非减激活复合保证 f_θ(·;c) 对 y 凸；上下文 c 编码掩码、有效长度等因子信息。【推理松弛】N-Queens 用 Birkhoff 多面体 𝒴={X∈[0,1]^{N×N}, 行和=列和=1}（行/列/列交替投影）、对角线用单纯形 {Σy_i≤1}；图着色用乘积单纯形松弛；投影算子几乎处处可微，梯度可穿过求解器。【求解器】投影梯度/投影 Adam，y_{t+1}=Π_𝒴(y_t − η_t A_t ∇_y E_θ)，步长余弦调度；加小幅均匀 tie-breaking 场 ξ（保凸，仅打破置换对称）；多起点 S 个可行热启动，按能量选最低者；S=1 为真正的单轨凸推理模式。【两阶段训练】阶段一：因子级 InfoNCE 对比预训练（正样本为凸包顶点，负样本用 under-satisfied/overloaded/two-peak/重归一化混合等因子特定提案）；阶段二：展开投影求解器端到端反传，损失为回归 + 能量间隔 + 沿轨迹硬负挖掘（α‖x̂−x*‖²+β·softplus(E(x*)−E(x̂)+ρ)+γ·硬负项），只对最低能量起点回传，并加入 warm-start/凸混合等硬负。【理论】Prop 3.1（非凸因子导致稳定伪极小）；Lemma 3.2（凸性与连续性下 Γ^D>0 ⟺ argmin∩ℬ=∅，即能量低于该边界即保证解码有效）；Thm 3.3（若 2Δ+δ<γ_dec 则有限精度求解输出有效，Δ≤Σ w_k ε_k）；Thm 3.4/Cor A.5（一阶最优性条件即充分，误差 ε 时 ε-全局最优）；Cor 3.5/A.6（PGD O(1/T) 能量误差，强凸时线性收敛）；Thm A.3（严格梯度边界 m>0 ⇒ 唯一极小且分块 argmax 精确解码，Frank–Wolfe 间隙可作可检验证书）；Prop A.2（复杂度边界：若存在一致构造的逆多项式边界证书将给出 NP-hard 问题的多项式算法，故不作此类主张）。【诊断】报告投影残差 r_opt、分数度 φ、top-2 间隙、解码前后能量差、松弛/精确违约等指标以区分优化误差、松弛松弛度与解码不稳定。

Result: 8-Queens（100 采样盘）：CCEM（T=50, S=64）100/100 正确、放置皇后 8.00±0.00，超过 EBM-Diff+PEM（P=1024）的 97/100、Fast T2T 的 41/100 及 GFlowNets/DIFUSCO；作者复现 EBM-Diff 仅 75/100。图着色：冲突边数在全部 8 个分布上大幅下降（如 Erdos–Renyi 8.60→2.20，Holme Kim 2 59.0→14.0，Paley 34.8→1.8，完全图 3.4→0.0）；离散精确有效着色在 6/8 分布上优于 EBM+PEM（Erdos–Renyi 11→29、Paley 32→59、完全图 57→100），在最难的 Holme Kim 2 与 Regular Expander 2 上打平（均为 0）。3-SAT（20 变量/91 子句）：满足子句比例 0.9959（修正版表述，较投稿版 0.9665 明显提升），接近 EBM+PEM 的 0.9985；完全求解比例随 S 从 66.0% 升至 69.7%。泛化：仅在 N=8 上训练即零重训迁移到 N∈{4,…,10}，S 增大单调提升有效率（如 N=10 在 S=64 达 82%），PEM 用 P=128 与投影 Adam S=32 相当。推理效率：单轨 51 次前向（N-Queens）/141 次（图着色、3-SAT）对比 EBM+PEM 的 201 次；总前向 3,264 vs 205,824（8-Queens）、9,024 vs 25,728（图着色）；评估耗时 60s vs 340s（8-Queens）、60s vs 2000s（3-SAT），训练时间亦更短。消融：Phase-2 各损失组合在 S=64 下均全解，说明增益主要来自组合能量结构而非某一项损失；锐度诊断显示 PICNN 能量在 Birkhoff 多面体上给出单一列的"决断性"盆地（top-2 间隙≈1、熵≈0、和误差<1e-3），无需温度式解码。

Conclusion: CCEM 表明组合式能量模型的核心障碍是因子级几何而非采样器：把因子换成输入凸网络并使用紧凸松弛，可将组合推理转化为一个全局良态的凸程序，从而以远低于粒子式方法（尤其在 3-SAT、图着色上的时间/前向开销）的确定性投影求解替代扩散采样与多粒子重采样，并在三项组合推理任务上匹配或优于基线，同时实现从单一小规模训练实例到更大实例的零重训迁移。理论层面论文严格限定边界：凸性只保证松弛目标的全局最优（其一阶条件即充分），离散正确性需额外满足实例相关的解码器感知边界，并明确指出这不蕴含 NP-hard 问题的多项式时间可解性。局限包括：凸约束可能过强，产生平坦区域、分数化解或对多峰离散解空间拟合不佳；多起点仍被用于从近等能量松弛极小中挑出可正确解码者（不用于逃离伪局部极小），且在极大规模实例（N=10 时 S=64 仅 82% 有效）上有效率仍受有限步优化与解码限制。作者结论是：控制因子级几何是高效神经推理一个有前景的方向，前提是松弛与解码器充分对齐。

Abstract: Compositional energy-based models can generalize to larger combinatorial reasoning problems by reusing a learned factor energy across many local constraints. In our paper, we show that a key bottleneck in compositional reasoning is not composition itself, but the non-convex geometry of the learned energy landscape. To solve this problem, we introduce Convex Compositional Energy Minimization (CCEM), a framework that parameterizes each factor with an input-convex neural network and optimizes the composed energy over a tight convex relaxation of the feasible set. Because convexity is preserved under summation, the global relaxed objective remains convex, enabling deterministic projected first-order optimization. CCEM is trained in two stages: factor-level contrastive learning to shape local energy basins, followed by end-to-end refinement through an unrolled projected solver. Our experiments show that our models trained on small subproblems or a single problem size transfer to larger instances without retraining.

</details>


### [202] [Strong Teacher Not Needed? On Distillation in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23857)
*Taiming Lu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 挑战了知识蒸馏中"强教师必然产生好学生"的传统假设。研究表明，通过适当的损失混合策略，即使是规模较小或训练不足的教师模型也能有效提升大学生成质量；而过强的教师（更多参数或更长训练）可能无法带来额外收益甚至适得其反。此外，蒸馏带来的泛化提升（OOD和下游任务）比域内拟合更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏领域长期假设强教师必然产生更好的学生模型，该研究对这一假设在LLM预训练中的有效性提出质疑。

Method: 通过变化模型参数规模（架构大小）和训练token预算，构建三种教师-学生关系：强到弱、同级别、弱到强。采用语言建模损失与知识蒸馏损失的适当混合策略进行训练。

Result: 1. 弱教师也能提升大学生成质量；2. 更强的教师（更多参数或更多训练tokens）带来的收益会饱和甚至反转；3. 蒸馏对分布外泛化和下游任务表现的提升效果优于域内拟合。

Conclusion: 挑战了蒸馏预训练必须使用强教师模型的传统认知。表明教师强度与学生表现之间并非简单的正向关系，弱教师配合适当的损失混合策略同样有效，甚至更强的教师可能适得其反。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation generally assumes a strong-to-weak relationship where stronger teachers yield better students. In this work, we examine this assumption about distillation in large language model pretraining. By varying architecture sizes and training token budgets, we create strong-to-weak, same-level, and weak-to-strong teacher-student relationships, and study distillation's effectiveness under each. We find that the teacher need not be strong: with proper mixing of the language modeling and knowledge distillation losses, even small and undertrained teachers improve larger students. At the same time, a stronger teacher is not always better: pushing the teacher further, through more parameters or more training tokens, can saturate or even reverse the distillation gains. We further observe that distillation improves generalization (out-of-distribution and downstream performance) more readily than in-domain fitting. Together, these results challenge the common belief that distillation pretraining always requires a strong teacher.

</details>


### [203] [Parametric Prior Mapping Framework for Non-stationary Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23402)
*Jinglin Li,Jun Tan,QI Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPM是一种将参数化结构先验注入生成式建模的概率多元时间序列预测框架，通过动态自适应先验平衡了参数化方法的效率和生成模型的表达能力，在非平稳数据上实现高精度与低计算成本的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化方法具有强归纳偏置但缺乏灵活性，而深度生成模型难以捕捉复杂时间依赖且需大量数据和计算资源。

Method: PPM将参数化结构先验注入生成式建模过程，利用参数估计器推导动态自适应先验，通过可学习映射引导复杂预测分布学习，采用混合目标函数训练。

Result: PPM在非平稳数据处理上优于现有基线，具备精确预测和良好校准的不确定性估计能力。

Conclusion: PPM提供了一种精度与计算效率的优越权衡，在非平稳数据处理上优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Effectively modeling non-stationary dynamics in probabilistic multivariate time series(MTS) forecasting requires balancing expressiveness with robustness. Existing parametric approaches benefit from strong inductive biases but lack flexibility, whereas deep generative models struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies without extensive data and computation. We introduce Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM), a framework that injects parametric structural priors into a generative modeling process. Specifically, PPM utilizes a parametric estimator to derive a dynamic, adaptive prior that guides the learning of a complex predictive distribution via a learnable mapping. This design allows the model to retain the efficiency of parametric methods while exploiting the expressive power of generative models. Trained with a hybrid objective, PPM yields precise forecasts with well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Empirical results show that PPM outperforms existing baselines in handling non-stationary data, offering a superior trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM.

</details>


### [204] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Corrective Diffusion Modeling for Meteorological Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23403)
*Rui Wang,Edoardo Pasetto,Amer Delilbasic,Morris Riedel,Kristel Michielsen,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种将变分量子电路层嵌入扩散UNet瓶颈的混合量子-经典纠正扩散模型，用于气象统计降尺度，在保持物理结构完整性的同时提升预测精度，但面临硬件限制和泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计降尺度是气象建模的重要环节，需从粗分辨率输入重建高分辨率输出，但完整动态细化成本高昂，因此探索混合量子-经典方法以提升降尺度性能。

Method: 将变分量子电路层嵌入扩散UNet最压缩的瓶颈位置，同时保留回归分支完全经典；在10米风分量上评估了通道内和跨通道ansätze；采用MAE和CRPS评估指标，以及动能谱和风速分布等结构诊断方法。

Result: 混合模型在2020验证集上保持稳定，保留了大尺度空间组织结构，在多种配置下MAE和CRPS均优于纯经典纠正扩散模型；结构诊断显示其保留了动能谱和风速分布，同时对尾部行为、极端风速定位和联合风场分量结构产生了可控变化；模拟噪声影响可忽略，但真实硬件部署受制于量子比特可用性和执行保真度；2021分布外测试显示分布内收益不能均匀转移。

Conclusion: 瓶颈层级量子混合化可为气象统计降尺度做出非平凡贡献，但电路规模和硬件部署仍是关键限制因素，分布外泛化能力亦有待提升。

Abstract: Statistical downscaling is a crucial component of the weather modeling field, where high-resolution outputs must be reconstructed from coarse-resolution inputs with the full cost of dynamical refinement. In this work, we investigate a hybrid quantum-classical corrective diffusion model for probabilistic statistical downscaling of weather fields. The proposed model inserts variational quantum circuit layers into the most compressed bottleneck of the diffusion UNet while leaving the regression branch fully classical. This placement tests whether quantum circuits can act as compact nonlinear feature maps for latent-channel mixing. We evaluate intra-channel and cross-channel ansätze on 10m wind components. On the 2020 validation set, the hybrid models remain stable, preserve the large-scale spatial organization of the generated wind fields, and improve both MAE and CRPS relative to a classical corrective diffusion model in several configurations. Structural diagnostics further show that the hybrid variants preserve kinetic-energy spectra and windspeed distributions similar to its classical counterpart while producing controlled changes in tail behavior, extreme-windspeed localization, and joint wind field components structure. Backend studies on the 2020 validation set show negligible impact from simulated device noise at the tested circuit scale, whereas real-hardware deployment remains limited by qubit availability and execution fidelity. The 2021 out-of-distribution test shows that these in-distribution gains do not transfer uniformly under temporal shift, revealing a generalization gap that motivates future mitigation through stabilization and regularization. These results show that bottleneck-level quantum hybridization can make a nontrivial contribution to weather statistical downscaling, while also highlighting that circuit scale and hardware deployment remain key limiting factors.

</details>


### [205] [What Linear Probes Miss: Multi-View Probing for Weight-Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23410)
*Eunwoo Heo,Kyeongkook Seo,Jaejun Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MVProbe addresses the "Model Jungle" problem by introducing a multi-perspective probing framework that combines first-order signals with interaction-aware (Gram-based) views, theoretically grounded via scaling law analysis to ensure balanced branch contributions, and demonstrated to outperform ProbeX across diverse architectures on the Model Jungle benchmark.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The explosive growth of open-source model repositories has created a "Model Jungle" where checkpoints lack adequate documentation. While weight-space learning can identify models from parameters, processing full-scale weights is computationally prohibitive. Existing probing-based methods (lightweight alternatives) only capture first-order structures, missing higher-order correlation patterns in row-column interactions.

Method: MVProbe proposes a multi-perspective probing framework that synthesizes first-order signals with interaction-aware (Gram-based) views. The approach is theoretically grounded through analysis of scaling laws for different probing orders, deriving a principled standardization and fusion strategy to ensure balanced contributions from all branches.

Result: Evaluated on the Model Jungle benchmark, MVProbe consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art ProbeX across diverse architectures including discriminative backbones (ResNet, SupViT, MAE, DINO) and large-scale generative LoRA adapters (Stable Diffusion LoRA).

Conclusion: MVProbe consistently outperforms state-of-the-art ProbeX on Model Jungle benchmark across diverse architectures, including ResNet, SupViT, MAE, DINO, and Stable Diffusion LoRA, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining first-order signals with interaction-aware views.

Abstract: The explosive growth of open-source model repositories has created a Model Jungle, where checkpoints are frequently shared without adequate documentation or metadata. While weight-space learning offers a pathway to identify and analyze these models directly from their parameters, processing full-scale weights is computationally prohibitive. Probing-based methods have emerged as a lightweight alternative, extracting permutation-equivariant representations via learnable probe vectors.
