Extensive experiments show that MetaEvaluator produces stable and accurate performance estimates at substantially reduced cost compared to conventional approaches, making scalable benchmarking of emerging models on unlabeled data practical.

</details>


### [228] [Preisach Attention: A Hysteretic Model of Sequential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23603)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAL (Preisach Attention Layer) is a novel sequence modeling architecture that replaces softmax attention with a hysteresis-based binary relay operator. It achieves Turing-completeness with O(1) depth, computes certain functions that transformers cannot without auxiliary state, and operates with O(n log n) inference cost. The architecture is particularly suited for tasks with long episodic memory and weak positional dependence.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard softmax attention mechanisms have fundamental limitations: they require O(n^2) inference cost and O(log n) depth for certain computations. There is a need for architectures better suited to tasks with long-term dependencies and rate-independent functional requirements.

Method: PAL replaces the softmax attention mechanism with a binary relay operator parameterized by learned activation/deactivation thresholds, maintaining a stack of local extrema as its internal state. A single-layer PAL-Transformer with O(1) depth is proven Turing-complete through simulation of a two-stack pushdown automaton.

Result: 1. Turing-completeness achieved with O(1) depth (compared to O(log n) for standard hard-attention transformers); 2. PAL and transformers have incomparable function classes - PAL excels at historical range statistics while transformers support random-access retrieval; 3. Rate-independence property demonstrated, with the extremum stack proven as a minimal sufficient statistic for all rate-independent functionals.

Conclusion: PAL offers O(n log n) total inference cost versus O(n^2) for standard attention, making it particularly suitable for tasks requiring long episodic memory with weak positional dependence.

Abstract: We introduce the Preisach Attention Layer (PAL), a novel sequence modelling architecture grounded in the classical Preisach hysteresis operator from mathematical physics. PAL replaces the softmax attention mechanism with a binary relay operator parameterised by learned activation and deactivation thresholds, maintaining a stack of local extrema as its internal state. A single-layer PAL-Transformer with O(1) depth is Turing-complete under arbitrary precision arithmetic, achievable through simulation of a two-stack pushdown automaton -- in contrast to the O(log n) depth required by standard hard-attention transformers. Second, we prove that the function classes computable by PAL and by the transformer are incomparable: PAL computes historical range statistics in O(1) layers that require O(log n) layers for transformers, while transformers support random-access retrieval that PAL cannot perform without auxiliary state. The separating property is rate-independence -- PAL responds only to the sequence of local extrema, not to absolute token positions or temporal spacing. Third, we show that the extremum stack constitutes a minimal sufficient statistic of the input history for all rate-independent functionals, providing a formal analogue of the wiping property in classical hysteresis theory. PAL is thus an efficient architecture for tasks with long episodic memory and weak positional dependence, with O(n log n) total inference cost versus O(n^2) for standard attention.

</details>


### [229] [How Hard is it to Rig a Benchmark? A Social Choice Analysis of Leaderboard Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23628)
*Polina Gordienko,Georg Schollmeyer,Frauke Kreuter,Christoph Jansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将机器学习基准测试中的数据选择操纵问题形式化为计算社会选择中的移动贿赂问题，证明了其在常见聚合规则下的计算复杂性，并量化了不同聚合方法对操纵的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务基准测试已成为ML研究的核心，但不断增长的影响力激励了基准测试博弈（benchmark gaming）——即通过策略性行动提升特定模型在排行榜上的排名。现有研究缺乏系统性的框架来理解和量化这种操纵行为。

Method: 将基准测试中的数据集建模为"投票者"、模型建模为"候选人"，基准测试特定训练对应选举操纵中的移动贿赂问题。通过复杂性理论证明NP-hard下界，并推导各投票规则下最小攻击成本的解析表达式。在MMLU/HELM和BIG-Bench Hard/Open LLM Leaderboard上进行实证验证。

Result: 该问题在Borda计数和平均胜率聚合下是NP-hard的。平均胜率最难操纵：在BBH上其中位鲁棒性为22个任务（92%），而算术平均和中位数/成对多数仅为12-13个任务（50-54%）。

Conclusion: 作者建立了基准测试特定训练与选举操纵理论（移动贿赂）的形式化联系，证明了该问题在Borda计数和平均胜率下的NP-hard性，并引入实例级鲁棒性度量，发现平均胜率是最难操纵的聚合方法。

Abstract: Multi-task benchmarks have become a central pillar of machine learning research, yet their growing influence has incentivised benchmark gaming -- strategic actions taken to improve the leaderboard rank of a specific model. Treating datasets as voters and models as candidates, we consider benchmark-specific training -- the inclusion of benchmark data in training -- as a form of election manipulation. For any ordinal benchmark, the problem of choosing datasets to train on so that a target model becomes top-ranked corresponds to shift bribery, a class of manipulation problems from computational social choice. Leveraging this identification, we show that the benchmark-specific training problem is NP-hard under Borda count and mean win rate. Complementing this worst-case perspective, we introduce the instance-level robustness, the minimum number of datasets a model developer must include in training to top a given leaderboard, and derive expressions for it under arithmetic mean, median, mean win rate and pairwise majority. We evaluate these expressions on MMLU under HELM and on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) under the Open LLM Leaderboard. Across both suites, mean win rate is hardest to manipulate: this gap is clear on BBH (24 tasks, 4507 models), where its median robustness is 22 tasks (92%), compared with 13 (54%) under arithmetic mean and 12 (50%) under median and pairwise majority.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Through Noise: Why Subliminal Learning Works and When It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23645)
*Vincent C. Brockers,Roman D. Ventzke,Valentin Neuhaus,Belén Hidalgo-Ogalde,Viola Priesemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Subliminal learning in neural networks is governed by compatible output heads rather than matched initialization. Through controlled experiments with split output architectures on MNIST, the authors demonstrate that auxiliary head compatibility enables knowledge transfer even across architectures, and they develop a theoretical framework predicting when subliminal learning succeeds or fails.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work attributed subliminal learning (knowledge/bias transfer via task-unrelated distillation) to shared or closely matched teacher-student initialization, but this explanation was incomplete. Understanding when and how subliminal learning occurs is critical for controlling knowledge transfer in neural networks.

Method: Controlled MNIST experiments with split output architecture (auxiliary head for task-unrelated noise signals, class head for classification). Systematic testing includes random initialization, layer removal/addition, and architecture conversion (MLP-to-CNN). Theoretical analysis to explain mechanism and derive failure bounds.

Result: Subliminal learning occurs even with random initialization, layer modifications, and cross-architecture transfer (MLP-to-CNN). Compatible auxiliary heads enable transfer of recoverable teacher signals, aligning student representations with teacher. With compatible class heads, students trained only on noise can match teacher-level performance. Upper bounds on failure conditions were derived.

Conclusion: The paper transforms subliminal learning from a surprising transfer effect into a theoretically grounded mechanism with predictable limits, enabling better understanding and control of knowledge distillation in neural networks.

Abstract: In the context of artificial neural networks, subliminal learning refers to the transfer of task-relevant knowledge or unintended biases from teacher to student models through distillation on task-unrelated input$\unicode{x2013}$output pairs. Prior explanations tie this effect to shared or closely matched teacher$\unicode{x2013}$student initialization. We show that a closely matched initialization is not necessary. Instead, subliminal learning is governed by compatible output heads. Using a controlled MNIST setting, we split outputs into an auxiliary head (for auxiliary, task-unrelated noise signals) and a class head (for classification) to demonstrate subliminal learning occurs$\unicode{x2014}$even when we randomly initialize hidden layers and remove layers, add new layers, or change the architecture (MLP-to-CNN). Compatible auxiliary heads enable transfer of a recoverable teacher signal, bringing the student's representations closer to the teacher's. When the class heads remain compatible as well, students trained only on task-unrelated noise can approach, and in favorable regimes match, teacher-level task performance. Our setting enables us to develop a theory that explains the mechanism of subliminal learning and to derive upper bounds on when subliminal learning fails. Together, our results turn subliminal learning from a surprising transfer effect into a theoretically grounded mechanism with predictable limits.

</details>


### [231] [Valid and Expressive Copulas for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23632)
*Christian Klötergens,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopFITi is a novel copula model for irregular multivariate time series forecasting that combines normalizing flows with Gaussian Mixture Copula, achieving marginalization-consistency and state-of-the-art performance in joint density modeling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Irregular multivariate time series (IMTS) forecasting requires modeling both accurate univariate marginals and flexible joint dependency structures, but existing approaches often fail to decouple these components effectively.

Method: Combines normalizing flows (for univariate marginals) with Gaussian Mixture Copula (for joint dependency structure).

Result: Copula-based approaches that decouple marginals from joint yield better marginal models than architectures directly fitting the full joint.

Conclusion: CopFITi establishes a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling, being the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction.

Abstract: We introduce CopFITi, a copula model for probabilistic forecasting of irregular multivariate time series (IMTS). Our model combines the expressivity of normalizing flows for univariate marginals with the consistency and flexibility of a Gaussian Mixture Copula for the joint dependency structure. Our experiments show that copula-based approaches, which decouple the marginals from the joint, yield better marginal models than architectures that directly fit the full joint. With CopFITi, we propose the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction and establish a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling.

</details>


### [232] [It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23825)
*Stuart Bladon,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Geopolitical bias in language models originates from post-training (alignment) rather than pre-training, shifting models toward the country/region of their developers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: It has been assumed that geopolitical bias originates from pre-training data; this assumption needed empirical validation.

Method: Tested seven open-weight LLM pairs (base vs. chat models) from seven labs using a paired-scenario forced-choice probe across 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese.

Result: Six of seven labs showed geopolitical bias shifts toward the developer's country/region after post-training. Qwen 2.5 exhibited the largest shift (18x in odds). Bias magnitude depended on the prompt language (e.g., Mistral became pro-France only under French prompting).

Conclusion: Geopolitical preferences in LLMs are actively shaped during post-training rather than inherited from pre-training data, necessitating greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes.

Abstract: It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.

</details>


### [233] [Relevant Walk Search for Explaining Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23673)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Michael Gastegger,Grégoire Montavon,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于 max-product（Viterbi）消息传递的多项式时间高阶解释算法，用于在 GNN 中高效搜索最相关的"行走"(walk)：EMP-neu 在神经元级精确搜索 top-K 相关行走，AMP-ave 在节点级近似搜索。二者将 GNN-LRP 原本随网络深度指数增长的相关行走识别开销降为多项式（时间 O(LM²N̄²+K̃L²MN̄)），在流行病学、分子、自然语言等任务上验证了高准确性（γ≥0.2 时精度/召回良好），并比穷举 GNN-LRP 快数个数量级（Infection 数据集上 86 秒 vs >10^11 秒），代码见 github.com/xiong-ping/rel_walk_gnnlrp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络的可解释性对安全性、公平性与鲁棒性至关重要。GNNExplainer、PGExplainer 等主流实例级方法只给出节点/边级别的低阶解释，忽略高阶交互；而 Schnake 等人 (2022) 提出的 GNN-LRP 以"行走"(walk) 为归因单元，可刻画跨层的信息流，属于更优的高阶解释。然而 GNN-LRP 的相关行走识别需要评估 M^(L+1) 量级的全部行走，计算复杂度随网络深度指数增长，仅能处理小规模问题。作者此前的工作 (Xiong et al., 2022) 已用 sum-product 消息传递解决了子图归因的指数复杂度问题，但"找出最相关的行走"这一环节仍是指数级的，因此本文聚焦解决该瓶颈。

Method: 核心洞察：行走相关性与马尔可夫链联合分布具有相同的可分解性(Xiong et al., 2022)，因而可将求和边缘化替换为求最大化的 max-product（Viterbi）消息传递，从而导出局部消息传递规则。两个难点阻碍 max-product 分解：相关因子可能为负；节点级行走搜索中包含求和操作。作者的解决方案是：\n\n(1) EMP-neu（精确 max-product 神经元级搜索）：先最大化绝对相关度 |R| 使各因子非负以获得可分解性，再采用 Nilsson (1998) 的搜索空间分裂策略——将剩余搜索空间按"在第 i 层与首解不同"划分为 L+1 个不相交子集，对每个子集重新做 max-product，从而逐步获得 top-K~ 绝对相关行走；并通过消息复用把复杂度从 O(K̃L²M²N̄²) 降到 O(LM²N̄²+K̃L²MN̄)，内存 O(LM²N̄²)。定理 3.1 给出：若 top-K~ 绝对行走中包含 K 条正相关行走，则 EMP-neu 精确找出 top-K 正相关行走。\n\n(2) AMP-ave（近似 max-product 节点级搜索）：节点级相关性需对神经元边缘化，max 无法与 sum 分配。作者提出"列相似性假设"——将传播矩阵的各列近似为平均列（用激活矩阵 H 与修正权重矩阵 W↑ 实现，无需显式存储 T），使 max 可逐层分布；消息传递为 m̂_l = argmax_{m_l} Σ_{n_{l-1},n_l} T·μ̂（近似最大化）与 μ̂ 的求和更新（精确处理 n_{l-1} 依赖）。采用与 EMP-neu 相同的搜索空间分裂获得 top-K；不使用绝对值（因对 |T| 边缘化与对 T 边缘化差异大）；若部分行走相关度为负，则增大 K~ 直至找到 K 条正相关行走。AMP-ave 内存降至 O(L·max(M,N̄)²)，比存储全部传播矩阵的 O(LM²N̄²) 小约三个数量级。

Result: 近似精度：与穷举真值 top-K* 对比，AMP-ave 在 LRP-γ（γ≥0.2）下精度/召回良好，推荐设置 γ=[3,…,0] 下尤佳；LRP-0 精度差但该规则本身不适用于 GNN 解释。列相似性诊断显示四个数据集上传播矩阵列向量与其平均的余弦相似度平均>0.8，解释了高精度来源；EMP-neu 的 top-100 排序与穷举完全一致（3.4 秒 vs 穷举 3 小时以上）。\n\n定性结果：Infection 数据集(M=1000, L=4, GCN 准确率 82.51%，接近 oracle 83.1%)上，AMP-ave 的 top-3 行走恰好对应 3 条真实感染链，而 Node-IG/Edge-IG 未能定位；Mutagenicity 上 top-10 行走即定位到已知致突变基团(NH2+芳香环)与非稠合碳环等负面证据，效果与全量 GNN-LRP 相当；Graph-SST2 上能定位决定情感的关键子句（如"the value and respect"）。\n\n定量结果：作为感染链检测器，top-5 行走对真实感染链召回率达 94.22%，接近 oracle，明显优于基于 Edge-IG 的 sum/prod 启发式；BA-2motif 基序边检测中，AMP-ave（取包含该边的最大行走相关度作为边分数）达到 100% 召回的速度快于所有基线（Edge-IG、GNNExplainer、边级 GNN-LRP、梯度热图）。\n\n效率：BA-2motif 上 AMP-ave K=1/25/1000 分别 0.003/0.123/2.574 秒，对比 GNN-LRP 穷举 15.879 秒；Infection 上 0.137/1.217/86.006 秒，而穷举 >10^11 秒，Edge-IG 0.514 秒、GNNExplainer 60.6 秒。随深度 L 与图规模 M 增长的对比曲线显示优势随规模急剧扩大。

Conclusion: 本文给出首个多项式时间的高阶 GNN 解释算法族：EMP-neu 在神经元级精确、AMP-ave 在节点级近似地搜索 top-K 相关行走，使 GNN-LRP 的高阶解释能力可用于大规模问题，且在流行病学、分子、自然语言三类基准上验证了精度与效率。max-product 思想可推广到任意前馈网络（神经元级/块级行走搜索），有望用于概念级分解、疫情研究与软件漏洞检测等场景。局限：AMP-ave 的近似误差缺乏理论保证，仅由经验与列相似性诊断支撑（可考虑对神经元聚类以强化假设，代价为 N^L）；方法仅适用于能写出式(5)型传播规则的模型（如 MPNN 及其扩展），更一般的 GNN 架构仍需设计相应传播规则。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become important machine learning tools for graph analysis, and its explainability is crucial for safety, fairness, and robustness. Layer-wise relevance propagation for GNNs (GNN-LRP) evaluates the relevance of \emph{walks} to reveal important information flows in the network, and provides higher-order explanations, which have been shown to be superior to the lower-order, i.e., node-/edge-level, explanations. However, identifying relevant walks by GNN-LRP requires {\em exponential} computational complexity with respect to the network depth, which we will remedy in this paper. Specifically, we propose {\em polynomial-time} algorithms for finding top-$K$ relevant walks, which drastically reduces the computation and thus increases the applicability of GNN-LRP to large-scale problems. Our proposed algorithms are based on the \emph{max-product} algorithm -- a common tool for finding the maximum likelihood configurations in probabilistic graphical models -- and can find the most relevant walks exactly at the neuron level and approximately at the node level. Our experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithms at scale and their utility across application domains, i.e., on epidemiology, molecular, and natural language benchmarks. We provide our codes under \href{https://github.com/xiong-ping/rel_walk_gnnlrp}{github.com/xiong-ping/rel\_walk\_gnnlrp}.

</details>


### [234] [Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23861)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-CGM框架，将预训练基础模型（如大语言模型和扩散模型）融入因果生成建模，实现端到端视觉因果推理。该框架通过概念提取、操纵和反事实生成三阶段流程，结合CSG语义引导机制，在零样本设置下完成因果发现、干预和反事实生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果生成建模方法将因果约束融入生成模型训练，但缺乏统一框架来发挥预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力。

Method: FM-CGM框架包含三个核心组件（概念提取器、概念操纵器、反事实生成器），利用大型推理模型进行因果推理，文本到图像扩散模型进行生成；另提出因果语义引导(CSG)机制，通过交叉注意力确保语义干预传播至后继概念同时保留不变区域。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够识别合理的因果结构，并生成忠实于因果机制的反事实图像。

Conclusion: FM-CGM successfully unifies foundation models with causal reasoning, enabling end-to-end zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation with semantic consistency.

Abstract: Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.

</details>


### [235] [Optimization of randomized neural networks for transfer operator approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23689)
*Mohammad Tabish,Stefan Klus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机神经网络 RaNNDy 无法优化其随机基函数这一局限，提出了一种在保持隐藏层权重与偏置固定的前提下，通过优化激活函数超参数（以及权重/偏置采样分布的尺度）来改进随机字典的算法。该方法以 Rayleigh 商变分原理构造损失函数（自伴 Koopman 算子用 tr(Ĉ₀₀⁺Ĉ₀₁)，非自伴用前向-后向算子 tr(Ĉ₀₀⁺Ĉ₀₁Ĉ₁₁⁺Ĉ₁₀)），可用梯度下降或贝叶斯优化调节激活参数 ω，最终仍以闭式解求解输出层。数值实验表明：初始随机基常给出较差近似，优化后收敛迅速（<20 轮），能准确恢复图元的谱隙与一致集、Bickley 射流的一致集，以及蛋白 NuG2 的折叠/未折叠两态；且相比全训练网络（VAMPnet）该方法收敛更稳健、可容忍更大学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的传递算子（Koopman 算子、Perron–Frobenius 算子、重加权算子）逼近需要选择合适的基函数字典（dictionary），其最优选择高度依赖具体系统，是开放问题。神经网络可作为字典，但其反向传播训练代价高昂，存在收敛慢、局部极小、梯度爆炸/消失、对超参数敏感等问题。RaNNDy 通过固定隐藏层权重与偏置、仅训练输出层（闭式解）来降低成本，但固定随机初始化意味着随机基无法被优化；若随机基无法表示算子的特征函数，将导致近似质量很差。因此需要一种在保留随机化训练优势（免反向传播、闭式解、低成本）的前提下改善随机字典的机制。

Method: 核心思想：把随机基参数化为 ψ(x,ω)=σ(W(ω_W)x+b(ω_b),ω_a)，其中 ω=[ω_a,ω_W,ω_b]^T 为待优化超参数（包括激活函数参数与权重/偏置采样分布的尺度），隐藏层具体权重与偏置保持固定。损失函数基于 Rayleigh 变分原理（引自 Wu/VAC 框架）：在每个 epoch 用当前 ω 构造投影算子矩阵 A(ω)=C₀₀⁻¹C₀₁（自伴 Koopman 情形）或前向-后向复合算子 Â(ω)=Ĉ₀₀⁺Ĉ₀₁Ĉ₁₁⁺Ĉ₁₀（非自伴情形），并最大化 tr(A(ω))；积分用数据近似：Ĉ₀₀=Ψ₀Ψ₀ᵀ/m，Ĉ₀₁=Ψ₀Ψ₁ᵀ/m，Ψ₀=ψ(X,ω)、Ψ₁=ψ(Y,ω)（另需 Ĉ₁₀、Ĉ₁₁）。对 ω 求梯度并以学习率 η 做梯度上升（也可用贝叶斯优化），迭代直至收敛；随后固定 ω*，通过拉格朗日乘子法与广义特征值问题 Ĉ₀₀⁺Ĉ₀₁W_o=W_oΛ（自伴）或 Ĉ₀₀⁺Ĉ₀₁Ĉ₁₁⁺Ĉ₁₀W_o=W_oΛ²（非自伴）求输出层闭式解，取前 n 个特征向量/奇异向量作为主特征函数/奇异函数近似。

Result: 1) 图元实验：初始 ω=0.001 的随机基不合适，优化在 20 轮内收敛；得到前 5 个 Koopman 特征值并在第 3 个特征值后出现谱隙，对应三个亚稳集合，并可秩-3 重构图元 g 与转移密度 p。2) 分布与网络对比：网格搜索显示偏置分布影响可忽略；损失面表明优化器在 VAMPnet 上陷入局部极小且 lr>10⁻² 时发散，而 RaNNDy 在 lr=0.5 时仍收敛。3) Bickley 射流：τ=40、m=5000 条件下 <10 轮迭代收敛，优化后 9 个主奇异函数聚类为 9 个簇，成功识别一致集。4) 蛋白 NuG2（56 残基，lag=50 帧，接触图 1431×18585）：初始随机基近似质量差，优化后第二主特征函数清晰区分折叠与未折叠两态，并能给出稳定的接触图频率统计。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法使 RaNNDy 变得“可优化”：仅通过少量激活函数/采样分布超参数即可显著提升随机基质量，同时保留免反向传播、输出层闭式解、低训练成本等优势，可视为完全随机网络（RaNNDy）与完全训练网络（VAMPnet）之间的折中；且损失景观更简单、更易用贝叶斯优化等黑箱方法求解。后续工作方向：考察具有更多超参数的不同激活函数及其调优效果，并在更复杂动力系统上做系统性的运行时对比，以评估随机化神经网络方法的优劣。

Abstract: RaNNDy is a randomized neural network architecture for the data-driven approximation of transfer operators associated with complex dynamical systems. The weights and biases of the hidden layers of the network are randomly initialized and kept fixed, only the output layer is trained. This has several advantages over fully optimized neural networks, notably a closed-form solution for the output layer and significantly lower training costs. Despite these advantages, RaNNDy is restricted to the initial selection of weights and biases that parametrize the basis functions required for the operator approximation. Since the basis functions are determined by the activation function, choosing an appropriate activation function for the hidden layers is crucial. In this work, we propose an algorithm that optimizes the activation function itself, while keeping the weights and biases in the randomized neural network fixed, providing a more suitable dictionary. We illustrate the efficacy of the approach using various benchmark problems, including stochastic differential equations and random walks on graphons.

</details>


### [236] [Graph-based Complexity Forecasts in UK En Route Airspace Using Relevant Aircraft Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23696)
*Edward Henderson,George De Ath,Nick Pepper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"相关航空器对"(relevant aircraft pairs)数量的概率化空域复杂度预测框架：在伦敦中扇区(LMS, FL215–305)通过持证ATCO的迭代反馈改造已有相关航空器滤波算法(F1由0.69提升至0.84、精度/召回0.84、准确率0.95)；结合2024全年历史航路构建的重采样航路图(365节点/980边，航段5–10 NM)、航路点到达时间服从正态分布(σ=5 min)的航段占用概率模型，以及按月统计的"航段对—相关性"经验先验，可提前30–45分钟预测未来相关交互对数。该预测与实际相关交互数的Spearman ρ=0.68，显著优于基于航路点到达时间直接预测扇区交通流量的基线(ρ=0.55，差值0.134，95% CI [0.064, 0.211]，p=0.0004)，且笔记本上单次预测耗时<1秒，具备实时部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空管管制员(ATCO)的工作负荷管理直接关系运行安全，扇区组长(group supervisor)需要提前预判扇区复杂度以决定扇区构型与值班排班。问题在于:(1)复杂度具有强烈的扇区特异性(几何结构、程序、流量流向各异),数值指标难以跨扇区泛化,只能通过ATCO的主观工作负荷直接度量;(2)行业标准模型采用启发式度量(扇区内航空器数、上升/下降航空器数叠加热点因子),其底层轨迹预测是确定性的,无法刻画飞机性能上的认知不确定性;它们只度量特定航路的占用,缺乏对未来扇区交通构型的分辨率,在低流量但恰好占用关键航路时会高估复杂度;(3)模型假设飞机全程沿申报航路飞行,而实际中因对流天气等因素常被引导(vectored)或改航进入相邻扇区;(4)系统级扰动(对流单体、机场大面积延误)会突发性抬升复杂度,仅数分钟的到扇时间偏差就可能凭空产生一对需要解冲突的航空器。因此需要一种融合历史数据分布与实时业务数据流、可随运行态势实时修正的概率化方法。

Method: 方法分三部分。(1)相关航空器滤波器的扇区化改造:以 Vijay Kumbhar et al. 针对 Brussels West 提出的时空交互滤波算法为基础(参数:垂直间隔ΔFL、当前水平距d、轨迹预测前瞻ΔT、最近接近点水平距d_CPA与时间t_CPA),与Swanwick 中心两名持证ATCO开展多轮问卷反馈(每轮50个静态交通样本,取自2019年8月高流量时段6–23架、中位15架),以F-β(β=1.5)偏向降低漏检为准则调参并扩展四项逻辑:①尾流同向判定(位于主飞机后方60°锥体内且地速相同或更低者不视为相关);②航路发散(始终保持≥5 NM水平间隔则不相关);③基于Bluebird数字孪生中概率轨迹预测器(2σ可信区间)给出的爬升/下降垂直剖面锥体(加10 FL缓冲)替代原"当前/指定/退出高度层跨度相交"的过保守判据;④取消状态型/意图型双分支,改为依据ATCO最后一次指令做单一轨迹预测,并额外生成一条直飞扇区出口航路点的轨迹,两条轨迹按d_CPA缓冲后扫掠区域,任一路径判定相关即判相关。最终在新的50个ATCO标注样本上评估。(2)图式航路表示:用2024年1–12月穿越LMS的全部航班申报航路构建初始图(556节点/1356边),经线性细分(最长5 NM)与凝聚式层次聚类重采样为365节点/980边、航段统一在5–10 NM,并建立原始航路点到重采样图的查找表。(3)概率化复杂度预测:将航路点到达时间建模为T_i ~ N(μ_i, σ²)(σ恒取5 min),用CDF差分计算某时刻飞机占据各航段的概率P(L_k,t);由2024年每2分钟采样的静态场景(逐月)统计各航段对被判定为相关的经验条件期望E[X|O_i,O_j] = N(relevant ∩ (O_i,O_j)) / N(O_i,O_j)(无观测则置0);预测时对所有航段对求和Σ P(O_i)P(O_j)·E[X|O_i,O_j]得到未来相关对数期望。评估使用2025年5月某日NAS FDP实时数据流,06:00–16:00每分钟生成30/45分钟前瞻预测,以Spearman ρ与移动块自助法(10,000次重采样,块长15 min)与"直接预测扇区飞机数"的基线比较。

Result: 滤波性能:更新后的算法在50个ATCO标注样本上全面优于原算法——精度0.84、召回0.84、F1=0.84、准确率0.95,而原算法为0.67/0.72/0.69/0.90。示例场景中,原算法漏检ATCO判定的相关航空器BEE451E,并误将垂直间隔6700英尺且间隔持续增大的BCS6892判为相关;新算法因引入受限垂直剖面而正确排除BCS6892。预测性能:30分钟与45分钟前瞻下,预测相关对数与实际相关对数的Spearman ρ均为0.68(中等偏强正相关),峰谷走势基本吻合,12:00–13:00最繁忙时段的对数上升被正确捕捉;交通流量基线对应ρ分别为0.54与0.55。45分钟前瞻时两者ρ差异经移动块自助法检验为0.134,95% CI [0.064, 0.211],p=0.0004;30分钟前瞻p=0.004,均统计显著。计算效率上,45分钟前瞻预测在笔记本电脑上耗时不足1秒,可在单个雷达扫描周期(约6秒)内完成多扇区预测。σ=5 min的取值经经验检验较为稳健。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了以"相关航空器对数量"作为ATCO工作负荷代理量、通过图表示与概率化到达时间模型预测空域复杂度的可行性:改造后的扇区专用滤波器与ATCO判断高度一致(F1=0.84),并能较行业标准的交通流量预测更准确地预判未来45分钟的复杂度变化,可为扇区组长提供扇区构型调整与ATCO排班的决策支持。局限与未来工作:(1)仅在LACC的单一扇区(LMS)验证,跨扇区需重新标定滤波参数并扩展航路图;(2)评估仅覆盖2025年5月的单日数据,需在多季节、多交通流型态下检验稳健性;(3)图表示目前仅为二维,垂直交互信息隐含在历史先验中,需引入垂直维度以支持FL305以上扇区的并行预测;(4)σ恒定与正态分布假设可替换为随距离变化的不确定度或其他到达时间分布模型。落地前仍需在真实实时数据上评估,并与扇区组长充分磋商以验证运行效益。

Abstract: Effectively managing Air Traffic Control Officer (ATCO) workload is crucial in maintaining operational safety. Group supervisors use tools that estimate upcoming traffic load to aid decision-making. However, industry-standard models can fail to capture the nuances of upcoming air traffic complexity. This study presents a probabilistic approach to forecast the complexity of an airspace sector using the number of relevant aircraft pairs, i.e., those that require monitoring or deconfliction by a controller, as a proxy measure for ATCO workload. We adapted an existing filter algorithm to make it suitable for use in London Middle Sector (LMS), a complex airspace sector with multiple flows of traffic above some of the busiest airports in Europe. Through iterative feedback with ATCOs, the algorithm was refined and extended to handle specific geometric and operational considerations. The updated algorithm outperformed the original, with an F1-score of 0.84 compared to 0.69 on a labelled set of 50 traffic scenarios. To produce forecasts of future numbers of relevant aircraft pairs in the sector, a graph representation of the LMS route network was constructed, standardising the spatial fidelity of route legs. The forecasting method accounts for uncertainty in aircraft arrival times by modelling the probability of each aircraft occupying route segments at future query times. When combined with historic distributions of relevant interactions and a live operational data stream, predictions of upcoming ATCO workload could be made up to 45 minutes in advance. The proposed method to forecast upcoming workload showed a significantly stronger correlation with actual relevant interactions (Spearman's $ρ= 0.68$) than a standard traffic volume prediction ($ρ= 0.55$). The resulting data-driven tool shows promise for use by group supervisors to inform sector configuration and ATCO rostering decisions.

</details>


### [237] [LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23901)
*Xu Ouyang,Deyi Liu,Yuhang Cai,Jing Liu,Yuan Yang,Chen Zheng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Yiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Shannon Scaling Law，利用香农-哈特利定理将LLM训练建模为噪声信道上的信息传输过程，通过将模型参数映射为信道带宽、训练token映射为信号功率，揭示了足够信噪比(SNR)的重要性。理论预测：仅扩展模型规模或数据而不维持足够SNR会导致性能从单调改善转变为U型退化。实验在Pythia和OLMo2上验证，通过高斯噪声、量化及数学/QA/代码任务微调扰动，Shannon Scaling Law一致优于经典scaling law，在≤6.9B模型≤180B token数据上拟合后，可预测12B模型在307B token时的性能(pooled R²=0.847)，而单调基线方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing monotonic power law scaling laws fail to explain critical non-monotonic phenomena: catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation where performance deteriorates with increased compute.

Method: Shannon-Hartley theorem application: mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, analyzing SNR dynamics during scaling.

Result: Outperforms classical and perturbation-aware scaling laws with strong R² scores; accurately captures loss basins; extrapolates to 12B model (unseen) at 307B tokens with pooled R²=0.847, while monotonic baselines collapse.

Conclusion: Shannon Scaling Law provides a unified framework explaining both monotonic improvement and non-monotonic degradation in LLM training, with strong predictive power (R²=0.847) for unseen model scales.

Abstract: Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute.
  We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation.
  We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.

</details>


### [238] [Learning Dynamic Stability Landscapes in Synchronization Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23708)
*Christian Nauck,Junyou Zhu,Michael Lindner,Frank Hellmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes learning stability landscapes from graph topology using a novel graph-to-image prediction paradigm (GNN + CNN decoder). Releases datasets of 10,000 graphs with per-node landscape labels. Demonstrates learnability of stability landscapes that exceed conventional network science approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional scalar per-node stability indices provide limited insights into synchronization behavior. Deeper characterization of synchronization robustness is needed.

Method: Graph-to-image prediction paradigm: GNN encodes graph topology, CNN decoder renders per-node image-like landscapes, learned end-to-end. Two datasets of 10,000 graphs each (20 and 100 nodes) released based on oscillator model.

Result: Good in-distribution accuracy achieved. Model generalizes across graph sizes and to realistic power grid topologies.

Conclusion: Stability landscapes are learnable from topology and open new avenues for moving beyond scalar stability indices in biology, neuroscience, and power grids.

Abstract: The robustness of synchronization is typically characterized by scalar, per-node stability indices whose dependence on topology is studied via network science or graph neural networks (GNNs). We propose a novel upstream task, learning stability landscapes, which provide deeper insights into synchronization behavior and from which many such scalar indices can be derived. Crucially, we pioneer a graph-to-image prediction paradigm: learning image-like landscapes as per-node targets directly from graph topology, a formulation we are not aware of having been established elsewhere in the literature. To support this task, we release two datasets of 10,000 graphs each at 20 and 100 nodes with per-node landscape labels, based on a conceptual oscillator model, capturing power grid synchronization behavior. A GNN encodes topology and a CNN decoder renders per-node images, learned end-to-end with good in-distribution accuracy, generalizing across graph sizes and to realistic power grid topologies. This demonstrates that stability landscapes, while beyond the reach of conventional network science, are learnable from topology and open new avenues for moving beyond scalar stability indices in biology, neuroscience, and power grids.

</details>


### [239] [Contrast to Detect: Dynamic Graph Contrastive Regularization for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23744)
*Yunhua Pei,Zixing Song,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ContrastAD（Contrast to Detect）是一个面向多变量时间序列（MTS）的无监督异常检测框架，核心创新是把动态图结构演化本身当作学习信号，而不是像传统图对比学习方法那样强制跨视图不变性。它由三部分组成：多视角嵌入器 MPE（时间/属性/结构三个视角）、频率感知注意力混合器 FAM（在注意力之前做频谱 top-K 硬滤波以抑制噪声进入 query-key 相似度）、以及动态图对比学习器 DGCL（用批次级 DTW 距离构造幂律启发的稀疏图快照，选择结构最不相似的两个快照与稳定锚点做对比正则化）。在 SWaT、SMD、MSL、PSM、SMAP 五个基准上对 8 个 SOTA 基线，F1 全部第一，AUC 在 3 个数据集第一（SWaT 93.60、SMD 98.66、PSM 97.79），SWaT 与 PSM 上 F1/AUC 提升具有统计显著性。消融显示三模块互补，λ 敏感性表明对比项应作为"软正则"、负号（λ<0）才是关键设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测面临三大障碍：(1) 变量间依赖关系随时间动态漂移（结构性漂移），静态图假设易产生误报；(2) 频域噪声导致的特征纠缠，注意力机制中噪声会污染 query-key 相似度；(3) 真实场景中异常标签极度稀疏，监督方法不可用。现有重构类检测器（如 Transformer 预测+拓扑重构）会把异常也重构得同样好，削弱检测能力；现有图对比学习方法（FedCAD、GCLAD、GraphTNC、CGSTA 等）普遍依赖"跨视图不变性"假设与静态图/固定增广，在结构漂移下会抹掉真实的结构性变化信号，忽略图结构演化本身可作为异常线索这一特性。此外，训练数据本身无标签且被视为完全干净，进一步限制了方法设计。

Method: 整体为编码器—混合器—对比正则化—残差评分的四段式架构，最终总损失 L = L_forecast + β·L_reconstruction + λ·L_graph（β=0.1，前两项均为 RMSE；λ 为负，见下）。1) MPE：用 WaveNet 式膨胀因果卷积在时间轴（得 z_seq）与转置的属性轴（得 z_fea）两条正交路径编码，再加一个结构视角的时间图注意力（把每个变量当作全连通图节点，用 GAT 式注意力 + 1D 卷积得 z_gat），三视角拼接成统一嵌入 Z，避免了此前最多双视角方法（如 GDN 仅结构、DTrans 时间-属性）需要的额外对齐阶段。2) FAM：先加位置编码，沿时间轴做实数 FFT，取幅度 top-K（K=6）保留、其余置零后 IFFT 重构（等价于二值频率掩码的直通估计器），再做多头注意力（2 层、128 维、4 头）与 FFN；强调"频谱筛选必须在注意力之前"，否则噪声只能在输出端被抑制；最终用门控权重融合两路 FFN 输出并加残差，得到趋势/季节聚合的统一嵌入。3) DGCL：在滑动窗口（窗长 100、步长 1）内把时间序列切成 S=10 个长度 δ=10 的非重叠快照，每个变量为一个节点，计算节点段之间的成对 DTW 距离，选取距离最大的 K 对变量连边（"最不相似的关系边"）；每快照边预算 K = ⌊N·E(X)/2⌋ 由 Zipf 幂律度先验决定（度分布 P(n)∝n^{-γ}，γ 为度指数），只固定预算而不强制精确幂律拓扑，从而保持稀疏（密度 1.14%–3.33%）。随后在所有快照 {G_1..G_S} 中，用归一化度分布之间的对称 KL 散度 D_sym 找出最不相似的快照对 (G_p, G_q)；以其余 S−2 个快照平均图为"稳定锚点" z_a（GCN 编码），z_p、z_q 分别为两个最异质快照的 GCN 嵌入。定义 InfoNCE 风格的三项 score 形式正则项：两项分别将 z_p、z_q 与锚点对齐（分母为批内所有 j），第三项显式把 z_p 与 z_q 推开。理论动机基于 alignment-uniformity 视角：最大化该 score 可保持两个异质快照与锚点兼容同时互不塌缩；由于总损失取最小化，需要 λ<0，且 |λ|≤1 以使其仅作辅助正则项（标准 InfoNCE 的正负样本结构不适用）。此外 DTW 硬关系边 + 图级难负样本共同扩大决策边界、抑制表示塌缩。4) 训练与评分：只用无标签训练集（5 个数据集训练集均无异常），预测+重构+对比正则联合训练；异常分数由预测/重构残差导出；阈值按 Xu et al. (2022) 用验证集分数聚类选取；λ 由训练/验证集先验试验确定（SWaT/MSL −0.1，SMD −0.9，PSM −1.0，SMAP −0.4），事后 λ∈[−1,1] 扫描仅用于敏感性报告。效率方面 DGCL 作用于固定节点数的稀疏快照，内存随变量数 N 而非窗口长度 N_L 增长；N=55 时每窗 DTW 比较上限 1485 次，可用 Sakoe-Chiba 带状约束把 O(δ²) 降到 O(δw)。

Result: 五个数据集（SWaT/SMD/MSL/PSM/SMAP，5 个随机种子 {0,1,2,3,42}，统一 point adjustment）上与 GDN、CSTGL、FuSAG、MTG、MSHTrans、DTrans、MemStr、Catch 八个基线对比：F1 均值在全部 5 个数据集上第一（SWaT 88.24、SMD 78.87、MSL 89.95、PSM 91.17、SMAP 70.04），AUC 在 3 个数据集第一（SWaT 93.60，较 Catch +1.66；SMD 98.66，较 MSHTrans +0.16；PSM 97.79，较 MSHTrans +1.53），其中 SWaT 与 PSM 的 F1/AUC 优势在 95% 置信区间不重叠意义下统计显著；MSL/SMAP 的 AUC 分别落后 MSHTrans 约 0.16 与 0.63 个百分点但 F1 仍领先。多个基线出现退化的 P-R 折衷（GDN 在 SMD 上 P 86.39% 但 R 3.41%、F1 仅 6.55%；CSTGL 在 PSM 上 R 99.95% 但 P 27.77%、F1 43.46%），ContrastAD 在所有数据集上均规避了这两种失效模式。消融：去掉 DGCL（SWaT F1 88.24→73.84，等价 λ=0）、去掉 MPE（SMD 78.87→38.01，跌幅最大）、FAM 换成普通多头注意力（SMD/SMAP 退化最明显），三模块作用互补、增益可叠加。λ 敏感性：λ=0 在所有数据集 F1 最差（SMD 掉 28.86 分），任意非零 λ 即大幅恢复，负值整体优于正值，最优 F1 均落在负 λ；非零区间内 F1 标准差多低于 2 分，说明关键是 λ 的符号而非幅度。效率表：ContrastAD 是唯一同时具备并行训练、O(1) 推理、O(N_L) 内存且处于高性能档的方法（132.16 s/epoch，对比 CSTGL 384.53、MSHTrans 634.72）；MSHTrans 与 Catch 因自注意力需 O(N_L²) 内存。SWaT 案例研究（10,000 步、含三次攻击）：ContrastAD 是唯一在三次起始点均尖锐起跳、攻击间快速回落、正常段无噪声的方法，而 GDN/CSTGL 几乎漏检、FuSAG 出现误报、MTG 无分离度、DTrans 只响应第三次、MemStr 基线常年抬升、Catch 只对第三次响应、MSHTrans 恢复缓慢。

Conclusion: 结论：ContrastAD 通过把结构演化转化为学习信号，在非平稳 MTS 上实现了稳健的无监督异常检测。其贡献在于 DGCL 不再强制跨视图不变性，而是"对齐稳定关系模式 + 允许结构漂移"地做软正则化（λ 取负值最大化对比得分、|λ|≤1 保持辅助地位），使嵌入保持对关系变化的敏感性，从而区分良性的时序漂移与真正的拓扑偏离；实验证明严格不变性在动态环境下是次优选择。局限与未来工作：训练集被假定完全无异常（异常污染的训练集留待未来）；图构造仅用 DTW 距离，可对比相关性或可学习邻接；快照差异度仅用度分布 KL，可扩展到谱距离；λ 为逐数据集固定值，未来可做随结构稳定性自适应调整的对比加权；并可推广到金融、网络安全等具有动态关系结构的领域。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series (MTS) is hindered by dynamic inter-variable dependencies and feature entanglement under spectral noise, and in practice, is further complicated by the absence of anomaly labels. Existing reconstruction-based detectors tend to recover anomalies as faithfully as normal patterns, while prevailing graph contrastive methods enforce invariance across views and thus assume a stationary relational structure, an assumption that breaks under structural drift in real systems. We propose ContrastAD, an unsupervised framework that turns structural evolution itself into a learning signal rather than suppressing it. A Multi-Perspective Embedder encodes inputs from temporal, attribute, and structural perspectives. A Frequency-Aware Attention Mixer then performs spectral top-K filtering before attention, preventing noise from leaking into query-key similarities. The core component, a Dynamic Graph Contrastive Learner, builds power-law-inspired sparse graph snapshots from batch-level DTW distances and contrasts the most divergent pair against a stable anchor, regularizing the latent space without imposing rigid invariance. Across five real-world benchmarks, ContrastAD attains the highest mean F1 on all five datasets and the highest AUC on three (SWaT 93.60, SMD 98.66, PSM 97.79), with statistically significant F1 and AUC margins over the strongest baseline on SWaT and PSM. On MSL and SMAP, it trails the AUC leader by under 0.7 points while still leading on F1. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm that the contrastive objective works best as a soft regularizer, supporting our claim that strict invariance is suboptimal under non-stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [240] [Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23751)
*Pál András Papp,Aleksandros Sobczyk,Anastasios Zouzias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM注意力机制的I/O复杂度进行优化，通过借鉴近似注意力框架，将传统O(n²)的I/O开销降低至接近O(n)的线性水平，并证明了对应lower bounds验证算法接近I/O最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FlashAttention及其变体的I/O开销与n呈二次关系，但trivial lower bound仅为Ω(nd)，理论上应可优化。

Method: 借鉴Alman和Song的近似注意力框架，开发了I/O高效算法。

Result: 在大多数参数配置下，I/O成本仅接近线性依赖于n，相比现有方法有显著改善。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法接近I/O最优，解决了理论计算复杂度与实际实现之间的gap。

Abstract: We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.

</details>


### [241] [SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23753)
*Hamed Shirzad,Frederik Wenkel,Dominique Beaini,Danica J. Sutherland,Emmanuel Noutahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeedER是一种知识图谱检索框架，通过迭代、低成本的扩展策略解决多跳组合查询难题。先用密集检索播种核心节点，再用强化学习训练的图感知策略选择性扩展，在紧凑候选集上实现高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱结构不规则，传统方法面临挑战：自我图扩展增长迅速，密集嵌入方法难以处理多跳组合查询，现有基于智能体的图探索方法虽然表达力强但在大规模检索中成本过高。

Method: SeedER采用两阶段策略：1）使用轻量级密集检索和实体检索播种核心节点集；2）通过强化学习训练的图感知策略选择性扩展节点集。该策略将全局推理分解为可重用的局部决策，从而有效控制扩展成本。此外，论文从组合泛化和图约束子模优化角度建立了理论优势。

Result: 实验表明，SeedER在紧凑候选集上显著提升了召回率，优于强基线的密集检索和图增强方法。

Conclusion: SeedER提出了一种可扩展的知识图谱检索框架，通过种子节点选择和图感知策略扩展相结合，在紧凑的候选集上实现高召回率，为知识密集型推理系统提供了有效的第一阶段检索器。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.

</details>


### [242] [LLM-driven design of physics-constrained constitutive models: two agents are better than one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23754)
*Marius Tacke,Matthias Busch,Kian Abdolazizi,Jonas Eichinger,Kevin Linka,Roland Aydin,Christian Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向本构建律模型生成的多智能体LLM范式：Creator（生成器）提出针对数据的模型，Inspector（审查器）依据九项超弹性物理约束逐一审计并在发现违反时退回修改，只有通过审查的模型才被导出。以CANN为验证载体，在脑组织、实验橡胶、合成橡胶（各向同性）与猪皮（横各向异性）四个基准上，用Claude Opus 4.7与Kimi K2.5两种骨干对比"仅Creator""Creator+Inspector""Creator+带数值工具的Inspector"三种配置：数值验证器作为真值显示，加入Inspector后全部九项约束的通过率由Opus的90%提升至95%、Kimi的47%提升至60%（带工具时Kimi可达83%，Opus因基线已高反降至93%）；同时模型精度与专家设计的CANN持平甚至更优（猪皮平均R² 0.90 vs 0.83），在Treloar不变量平面上表现出可靠的泛化与外推。代价方面，每次完整运行新增成本均低于1美元，远低于节省的专家工时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一个能在任意载荷下准确描述材料变形的本构建律模型，传统上需要连续力学、机器学习与科学编程的多年专门训练，成本极高。已有LLM驱动的工作（SGA、CSGA、GenCANN）虽能按需生成模型，但均为单智能体流水线，没有任何机制系统检验生成模型是否真正满足基本物理定律（如客观性、材料对称性、热力学一致性、弹性稳定性等）。CANN类模型的"by-construction"物理保证只在实现正确时才成立，一旦实现交给LLM生成，其正确性必须被显式检验。此外，多智能体LLM文献（辩论、自洽性、生成-批判分工）已表明"生成者+审查者"分工可显著提升事实性与推理可靠性，但尚无面向物理约束本构建律生成的多智能体系统。

Method: 范式：Creator-Inspector双智能体流水线。Creator被提示实现符合材料类别与数据集的本构建律模型（提示中列出九项物理约束及架构层面的实现提示，如用输入凸子网络保证多凸性、用标量势梯度求应力保证热力学一致性），返回完整Python实现；先经自动句法检查（可执行、可实例化、可训练、可预测、可保存/加载），失败则连同失败原因退回Creator修正；通过后交由Inspector逐条判定九项约束是否满足并给出理由，若有违反则连同理由退回Creator修改；全部通过则导出。随后进入两轮精化：把训练集预测表与R²/MSE反馈给Creator生成改进版本，再走同样的句法检查与Inspector审查，每次完整运行产出三个被批准模型，取最准确者作为输出。

九项物理约束（沿用Linden等2023）：热力学一致性、应力张量对称性、客观性、材料对称性、多凸性（数值上以秩一凸性/椭圆性检验）、体积增长条件、能量归零、应力归零、应变能非负。独立于Inspector，配套确定性采样式数值验证器作为"真值"：约35个（各向异性时扩充至约53个）平面应变+面外剪切的变形梯度采样点、9组三维旋转与镜像、Fibonacci分布的200个方向对配对构成秩一形式；TI情形需先由模型自身响应数值反演纤维方向（两种探针族、2阶与4阶Fourier模态提取），再在缩减的横向各向同性对称群下检验不变性；TI的能量非负性还额外在旋转对称梯度全集上检验（可达I₄≈e⁻³的强纤维压缩）。相对容差10⁻³、绝对下限10⁻⁴，椭圆性判据按状态量级相对化以避免浮点抵消误判。

消融：(1)仅Creator（所有句法有效模型直接导出）；(2)Creator+Inspector；(3)Creator+可调用数值工具的Inspector（提示仅在真正不确定时调用工具），用于区分LLM自身理解与验证工具能力；另在合成橡胶上以"最小提示"（仅列出约束名称、不作解释）重复全部配置。

基准与骨干：脑组织（Budday等2017a，皮质灰质，单轴拉/压与简单剪切，每模式仅17点）、实验橡胶（Treloar 1944）、合成橡胶（Linka等2021，闭式真值可算任意变形应力，沿用原文训练/测试划分）、猪皮（Taç等2022b，402点，5种双轴协议共10条应力-伸长曲线，随机留出20%测试）；骨干为Claude Opus 4.7与MoonshotAI Kimi K2.5；每配置10次独立运行、每运行3轮精化。基线为文献中经超参搜索得到的CANN（脑组织取Pierre等2023a主伸长基模型）。

Result: 1）约束遵守率（数值真值）：Opus仅Creator 90%、加Inspector 95%、带工具93%；Kimi仅Creator 47%、加Inspector 60%、带工具83%。即Inspector带来5个百分点（Opus）与13个百分点（Kimi）的提升，两个骨干均成立。2）审查可靠性不对称：Opus审查器对"标记为违反"的模型中97%其实合格（偏保守，宁可疑不轻易放过）；Kimi则仅37%（不合格）/63%（不合格），只在确信或工具确认时才标记；工具使用上Opus多数只调1–2个工具（遵循"仅不确定时调用"），Kimi呈双峰（全调或全不调）。3）状态转移：Opus首次提案85%即被判合格，违规中90%在下一轮解决、仅5%重复违规；Kimi起始78%合格、72%直接解决、17%重复、12%转为新违规。4）精度：橡胶两数据集上基线与生成模型均近乎完美；脑组织基线（主伸长）R²为0.96/0.99/1.00（拉/压/剪），Kimi为0.614/0.651/0.829、Opus为0.453/0.876/0.981，在本文统一的不变量基下与专家不变量模型具竞争力；猪皮基线平均R²=0.83，Opus达0.90、Kimi为0.80，三者共同弱区为几乎不受拉方向应力分量的strip-axial协议。5）泛化与外推：合成橡胶上在Treloar不变量平面上，生成模型从训练三条路径向中间及更远区域外推时精度无明显下降，优于基线的可见外推误差（<5%但可辨）。6）可靠性：橡胶数据上10次运行间R²方差近零；脑组织与猪皮方差中等，Kimi仅Creator在猪皮上均值0.59、标准差0.47（存在严重失败运行），而加入Inspector后Kimi精度反而提升。7）精化循环有效：橡胶无明显趋势（用于探索），脑组织第一次精化小幅提升，猪肉皮（最难）逐轮均值上升且方差略降。8）网络规模：Opus自主选出的网络规模与基线相当（脑组织12,12；橡胶20,20；合成橡胶24,12），Kimi偏大（128×4、10,10、256,256等），但均未过拟合小训练集。9）成本：Opus无工具Inspector每次运行增加约1.7万输入+0.65万输出token（+58%，约0.25美元）、平均多触发约1次CANN训练；带工具+79%（约0.24美元）、额外训练降至0.08次；Kimi无工具+52%（约1美分）、额外训练0.2次；带工具+229%（约5美分）、额外训练1.6次；最贵配置单次运行仍低于1美元。10）最小提示消融：所有配置均低于充分提示版本；Opus"仅Creator"由90%降至83%，而"Creator+Inspector"由100%崩至0%——原因是Inspector对 ellipticity、增长条件、应力归零、能量非负过度质疑，Creator几乎每轮都用基于det(F)的体积项修补，反而系统性破坏材料对称性，30个导出模型全部违反该约束；带工具时同一失败消失、回到90%。Kimi在最小提示下从0%提升到30%（Inspector干预净收益为正）。

Conclusion: 生成与审查相分离使LLM驱动的本构建律建模成为可信流程：两个行为迥异的骨干（Opus与Kimi）都因引入专职Inspector而获得一致且可测量的物理有效性提升，说明收益来自范式本身而非特定模型。生成模型同时具备物理有效性、高精度与可靠外推能力，可直接用于实际工程（含粘弹性等更多材料类别的推广）。论文强调三点定位：(1) 自治度是刻意"校准"的——Creator-Inspector的交替是预设的、验证工具是硬编码的，因为在当前LLM水平下不能让Creator自行决定是否需要检查（Opus约每十个提案就产生一个物理无效模型）；未来Creator能自估合规性后可放宽为按需调用，工具代码可靠后硬编码工具亦将失去意义。(2) 该范式技术无关：CANN只是演示载体，同一Creator-Inspector循环可无概念改动地包裹PANN、CKAN或符号定律，仅需改提示；切换骨干也只是代码一行改动，从而能自动"搭便车"受益于LLM能力进步（呼应Sutton的苦涩教训）。(3) 提示与编排是核心资产：最小提示下Opus配对出现灾难性失败，说明系统的可靠性很大程度取决于对约束的详尽告知；长期有价值的产物不是某个具体模型，而是生成模型的智能体编排与提示工程。数值验证器为采样式而非严格数学证明，故通过率只可能偏乐观（检测到违反即为反例因而确定，未发现违反仅记为通过），这是该方法的固有局限。

Abstract: Developing constitutive models that capture how materials deform under load traditionally requires years of specialized expertise in continuum mechanics, machine learning, and scientific programming. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been shown to lower this barrier by generating constitutive models on demand, but existing single-agent pipelines lack systematic checks that the resulting models respect fundamental physical laws. To close this gap, we introduce the first multi-agent LLM-driven approach for constitutive model generation: a Creator agent proposes a model tailored to the data, while an Inspector agent critically audits each proposal against nine physical constraints and returns it for refinement whenever a violation is detected. We demonstrate this concept with constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) and benchmark it on brain tissue, experimental rubber, and synthetic rubber, using two different LLM backbones (Claude Opus 4.7 and Kimi K2.5). Adding the Inspector raises the share of exported models that truly satisfy all physical constraints from 91% to a perfect 100% for Opus and from 37% to 56% for Kimi, while preserving near-baseline accuracy and remarkable generalization to unseen loading paths. In combination, the generated models are physically valid, highly accurate, and extrapolate reliably beyond the training data - properties that together make them directly usable in practice. Separating generation from inspection thus turns LLM-driven constitutive modeling into a genuinely trustworthy process. The paradigm is deliberately technique-agnostic and scales automatically with advances in LLM capability, opening a promising path toward automated, physics-aware model discovery.

</details>


### [243] [Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23797)
*Bo Peng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang,Zhen Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的分布外检测（OOD）问题，提出通过修正负样本标签的采样偏差来提升检测性能。核心创新是将去偏负样本挖掘转化为基于ID标签和无标注数据的蒙特卡洛采样问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后验OOD检测方法依赖启发式规则从无标注数据中挖掘负样本标签，但存在严重的"假负样本"问题，导致VLM的OOD检测能力未被充分释放。

Method: 建立理论框架，通过间接近似负样本标签分布来修正采样偏差；将去偏负样本挖掘问题转化为基于ID标签和无标注野生数据的蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 在多种OOD检测设置下取得新的最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 所提出的去偏负样本挖掘方法能有效提升基于预训练VLM的OOD检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.

</details>


### [244] [Entrywise Error Bounds for Spectral Ranking with Semi-Random Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23854)
*Dongmin Lee,Anuran Makur,Japneet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在"半随机对手"（每条边可被任意提升采样概率，下界为 p）模型下，用谱方法（rank centrality）估计 Bradley–Terry–Luce 分数向量的逐项（ℓ∞）误差界。结论：未加权谱方法在规范 Markov 矩阵谱隙（1−|λ₂(S)|）以高概率保持常数下界、且边采样概率向量满足适度"变化条件"时，可得到与均匀（Erdős–Rényi）采样一致的 O(√(log n/(npk))) 误差界；这一结果可推广到一类满足动态范围条件的随机块模型（SBM）。为克服谱隙退化，作者引入边重加权的谱方法：通过单调耦合论证证明半随机图中必存在具有 Erdős–Rényi 谱性质的子图，而边重加权是取子图的连续松弛；采用 MMWU 近线性时间 1/2-近似 SDP 算法求解最大化加权 Laplacian Fiedler 值的优化问题，可得误差界 c₁√(log n/(npk)) + c₂√(n log n/((np)³k))，在稠密图 (np ≳ c√n) 下简化为最优界 C√(log n/(npk))。数值实验支持了理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) 模型的谱估计（rank centrality）在 Erdős–Rényi 均匀采样图上的理论已较完善，但其分析严重依赖度集中等"均匀性"性质，难以推广到更一般的随机图。而真实偏好数据往往呈簇状、非均匀（如国际象棋手多来自同一国家），或由"先小样本、后扩大集合"等机制产生，导致某些边被过度采样。已有一般性结果（如 Chen 2023、Li et al. 2022）对 ℓ∞ 误差的界不如均匀采样情形紧。Yang et al. (2024) 已针对 MLE 方法建立了半随机对手模型的分析，但谱方法（BTL 估计的另一大范式）在此模型下的性能分析此前是空白。此外，一个反直觉现象是：加边可能反而降低归一化 Laplacian 的谱隙（Fiedler 值），因此"信息更多"未必意味着估计更好，这正是需要研究的关键点。

Method: 建立半随机观察图模型：每条边 (i,j) 以概率 q_ij∈[p,1] 独立被采样，每对观测边重复 k 次比较得到经验胜率 p̂_ij。分两部分：(1) 未加权谱方法：证明 ℓ² 误差引理（Lemma 5.1，误差 ∝ 1/(γ√(npk))），再通过 leave-one-out 分解把 ℓ∞ 误差拆分为多个盒装项（δ_i 中含 leave-one-out 差项、偏差项 a/b、留一残差项），用 Hoeffding/Bernstein 集中不等式逐项控制并用 union bound 合并，得到 Theorem 3.1（需满足变化条件 Eq.12：n∑q_ij² ≤ s(∑q_ij)²；以及谱隙条件 Eq.13：P(1−|λ₂(S)|≤γ) ≤ n⁻⁵）。再结合 Löwe & Terveer (2025) 的 SBM 谱结果与 Negahban et al. (2017) 的谱隙比较引理，得到满足 np ≥ c₀log⁵n、q_m ≤ rp 的 SBM 推论。(2) 加权谱方法：定义加权 Laplacian L^W 及其 Fiedler 值 λ_{n-1}(L^W)，推导对任意权重都成立的通用误差界（三项分别为 λ^{-1}、λ^{-2}、λ^{-3} 尺度），证明用 π 加权 Laplacian 与矩阵 Bernstein 不等式控制误差项；再用单调耦合（共享均匀随机数 U_ij，构造 G_E ⊆ G_S）证明引理 5.3：任何 Erdős–Rényi 图以高概率成立的图性质，在半随机图中必存在满足该性质的子图；边重加权是取子图的连续松弛，故存在权重使 λ_{n-1}(L^W) ≥ np/2；最后把最大化 λ_{n-1}(L^W) 的问题写成 SDP，用 MMWU（矩阵乘性权重更新）近线性时间 1/2-近似求解，结合 Yang et al. (2024, Thm 10) 得到 Theorem 4.2。实验部分用 Python 实现 MMWU（贪心 1/2-近似 oracle），每组实验跑 25 次取中位数，比较谱隙与相对 ℓ∞ 误差。

Result: 主要理论结果：(1) Theorem 3.1：在变化条件与谱隙条件下，未加权 rank centrality 满足 ‖π̂−π‖∞/‖π‖∞ ≤ (c₁/γ + c₂)√(log n/(npk))，概率 ≥ 1−O(n⁻⁵)；其中 c₀ ≥ 10240h²/γ²，k ≥ 5。(2) Corollary 3.2：在 Erdős–Rényi 图下恢复 Chen et al. (2019) 的 c₁√(log n/(npk)) 一致界。(3) Proposition 3.4：若 SBM(n,m,p,q) 满足 np ≥ c₀log⁵n 且 q_m ≤ rp（动态范围条件），则误差同样为 c₁√(log n/(npk))。(4) Theorem 4.1（通用加权）：误差被三项之和控制，即 (c₁/λ_{n-1}(L^W))√(n log n p/k) + (c₂/λ_{n-1}(L^W)²)√(n² log n p²/k) + (c₃/λ_{n-1}(L^W)³)√(n⁴ log n p³/k)，要求 d_min≥1、d_max≤2np、k ≥ 640h²n log n p/λ_{n-1}(L^W)²。(5) Theorem 4.2（MMWU 加权）：对半随机图，误差 ≤ c₁√(log n/(npk)) + c₂√(n log n/((np)³k))；Corollary 4.3：当 np ≥ c√n（稠密）时简化为 C√(log n/(npk))，即渐近最优。实验：3 块 SBM（块内密度差异大、d_max/d_min 不有界）中，无权重谱隙随 n 从约 0.03 衰减到约 0.01，而 MMWU 加权后谱隙稳定在约 0.07，且加权方法 ℓ∞ 误差下降更快（n=135 时 0.46 vs 0.48）；Erdős–Rényi 情形下加权仍能提升谱隙但误差无明显改善，权重分布近乎均匀。

Conclusion: 未加权谱方法在半随机对手下的表现强烈依赖生成图的谱性质：当规范 Markov 矩阵的谱隙以高概率被常数下界控制时，可获得与均匀采样完全一致的逐项误差界，这一结论覆盖 Erdős–Rényi 图以及满足动态范围条件的随机块模型；而当图结构（如过度采样的 SBM）导致谱隙随 n 衰减时，未加权谱方法失效。通过对观测边做适当重加权（MMWU 求近线性时间的 1/2-近似 SDP，最大化加权 Laplacian 的 Fiedler 值），可以抵消对手对谱隙的破坏，在稠密半随机图上恢复渐近最优的 O(√(log n/(npk))) 误差界；其原理是半随机图中必存在 Erdős–Rényi 谱性质的子图，而重加权是取子图的连续松弛。作者强调工作以理论为主，稀疏半随机图上能否取得最优界仍需新的证明技术，是有前景的后续方向；SBM 结果亦可推广到 m 随 n 增长的更一般情形。

Abstract: Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) model estimation is a well-established strategy to rank a collection of items given a dataset of pairwise comparisons. Although the theoretical performance of BTL estimation methods, such as spectral and maximum likelihood estimation, is well studied in the regime of uniformly sampled graphs, generalizing such results to a wider class of random graphs has proved challenging. In this work, we investigate the entry-wise error of spectral algorithms against a semi-random adversary that can arbitrarily boost the sampling probabilities of certain edges. We find that the performance of the unweighted spectral method is heavily dependent on the spectral properties of the generated graph. Furthermore, we show that asymptotic performance approaching that of uniformly sampled graphs can be recovered by appropriately reweighting the observed edges to counteract the adversary and restore the spectral gap. Finally, we provide numerical simulations that support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [245] [Training-Free Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23872)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无需训练的循环transformer方法，将预训练模型的中间层块在推理时循环应用，通过ODE近似的细化视角替代朴素重应用，在多个模型家族上验证了性能提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环transformer方法需要端到端训练循环结构，该研究首次将循环机制直接改装到预训练模型上实现推理时增强。

Method: 采用轻量级推理时包装器，对冻结检查点的连续中间层块进行循环应用。将pre-norm transformer块视为ODE上的前向欧拉步骤，将循环视为同一近似的细化，用较小的阻尼子步骤替代一次大更新。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上MMLU-Pro提升2.64pp，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct上CommonsenseQA提升1.14pp，Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct上OpenBookQA提升1.20pp。朴素层重应用通常导致性能下降，循环应用策略至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法在七种密集型、稀疏MoE和MLA+MoE模型家族中均有效提升性能，无需额外训练即可增强预训练模型能力。

Abstract: We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.

</details>


### [246] [Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23893)
*Hongwu Peng,Ohiremen Dibua,Yuanjun Xiong,Yifan Gong,Jianming Zhang,Yan Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Complete-muE是一个超参数迁移框架，通过双桥系统解决从Dense FFN到各种MoE配置的超参数迁移问题。Bridge I处理Dense到Dense MoE的迁移，Bridge II处理Dense MoE到Sparse MoE的迁移。核心实践准则是"一次调参，迁移全部"——在单个Dense参考模型上调优后，可高效迁移到所有MoE配置，避免昂贵的超参数搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing hyperparameter transfer tools have critical limitations: μP requires fixed architecture, while SDE requires fixed per-step token count. Neither can directly solve transfer problems in MoE setups where Dense to MoE transitions or total expert scaling involve simultaneous changes in both architecture and tokens per expert. This gap prevents efficient hyperparameter transfer when scaling model capacity to MoE configurations.

Method: Complete-muE employs a two-bridge transfer system: (1) Bridge I maps dense FFN to Dense MoE using active-width μP with normalized router scale; (2) Bridge II maps Dense MoE to sparse MoE via activated-expert scaling, where first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while bounded residual σ₀ shift remains. The framework handles architectural and token-level variations that existing methods (μP or SDE) cannot address independently.

Result: Experiments on language model and diffusion model pretraining demonstrate that Complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts. The observed drift is minor and consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge II. Hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations, enabling MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling capacity.

Conclusion: Complete-muE enables efficient hyperparameter transfer from dense models to various MoE configurations through a two-bridge system, achieving the practical principle of "tune dense once, transfer to all". This framework supports diverse architecture changes (activated experts, capacity, granularity, shared/group-balanced hybrids) and general transformer changes (width/depth, batch size, duration), accelerating MoE model convergence without costly hyperparameter search.

Abstract: We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale. Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Whose Good, Whose Place? The Moral Geography of Agentic AI for Social Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22995)
*Poli Nemkova,Haeshitha Indukuri,Jaedon Charles*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对2015-2026年间发表的112篇关于社会公益智能体AI的论文进行了系统调查，发现存在"道德-地理不对称"现象：论文在最需要本地政治、法律和文化背景的领域（如制度性目标SDG 16）反而最不明确地理背景（仅13%），而健康或生态领域则有37-40%。73%的论文未指定任何地理背景，75%未报告任何真实世界部署或小规模测试。作者识别出五个问责缺口并提出最低报告标准建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI系统常被宣称用于社会公益并与联合国可持续发展目标(SDGs)挂钩，但这些社会公益声明并未建立对目标社区的问责机制，亟需系统评估现有研究的地理特异性与实践落地情况。

Method: 对112篇智能体AI社会公益论文进行结构化调查分析，按SDGs类别进行分类统计，重点考察地理背景说明率和真实世界部署情况。

Result: 发现严重的道德-地理不对称：73%论文无地理背景说明；健康/生态相关SDGs论文37-40%说明地理背景，而制度/社会政策SDGs仅13%；SDG 16（和平、正义与强大机构）覆盖最多但地理说明率最低；仅25%论文报告真实世界部署或小规模测试。

Conclusion: 智能体AI社会公益研究普遍存在"道德抽象化"倾向，将制度性公益视为普适真理而非需要情境化理解的事项。作者识别出五个 accountability gaps（问责缺口），并提出针对智能体AI社会公益应用的最低报告标准框架，以促进更具情境特异性、参与性和问责性的研究实践。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly proposed for social-good domains, often invoking the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) as a vocabulary of global benefit. Yet claims of social good do not establish accountability to the communities a system claims to serve. We present a structured survey of 112 papers on agentic AI for social good published between 2015 and 2026.
  We find a moral-geographic asymmetry: papers are least likely to specify geographic context in precisely the domains where local political, legal, and cultural context matters most. Across the corpus, 82 of 112 papers (73%) specify no geographic context. Papers aligned with health or physical/ecological SDGs specify geography 37-40% of the time, while papers aligned with institutional and social-policy SDGs do so only 13%. SDG 16, peace, justice, and strong institutions, is both the most-covered goal in the corpus and the one with the lowest geographic-specification rate.
  We interpret this as moral abstraction: agentic AI for social good often treats institutional good as universal in ways it does not treat health or ecological good. A second finding compounds this: only 28 of 112 papers (25%) report any real-world deployment or small-scale test. We identify five accountability gaps and propose a minimal reporting standard for more context-specific, participatory, and accountable agentic AI for social good.

</details>


### [248] [Opportunities and Risks of Generative AI through the Health Information Journey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23026)
*Matthew R. DeVerna,Harry Yaojun Yan,Kai-Cheng Yang,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper introduces a conceptual framework examining how AI transforms public engagement with health information and formal healthcare, identifying both opportunities (access, comprehension, care continuity) and risks (inaccurate content, manipulation, opaque automated decisions).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI systems are increasingly integrated into both health information consumption and clinical decision-making, creating dual opportunities for improved access/comprehension and risks of unreliable or opaque content.

Method: Conceptual framework development; proposes a four-stage model categorizing AI applications as the public transitions from information environments to clinical care settings.

Result: N/A - This is a conceptual/framework paper introducing a research agenda rather than reporting empirical findings.

Conclusion: A four-stage framework is proposed to map opportunities and risks across the continuum from information seeking to formal healthcare encounters.

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is fundamentally changing how health content is encountered and acted upon across both the information and healthcare ecosystems. AI systems now generate claims, curate information, interpret symptoms, synthesize evidence, and guide decisions, with significant opportunities and risks for the public. Potential benefits include improvements in access, comprehension, and continuity of care. At the same time, AI can introduce inaccurate or manipulative content that is difficult to distinguish from reliable guidance, and encourage automated decisions that affect care with little transparency or recourse. We introduce a four-stage framework to examine how these opportunities and risks unfold as the public moves through the information environment and into formal healthcare.

</details>


### [249] [Defining AI Fatigue in Academic Contexts: Dimensions, Indicators, and a Stage-Based Model Using Grounded Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23123)
*John Paul P. Miranda,Emmanuel B. Parreño,Jovita G. Rivera*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究针对学术场景中AI工具使用引发的独特疲劳现象，基于菲律宾三所高校1,054名学生的质性数据，通过扎根理论识别出AI疲劳的五维结构（认知、动机、道德、身体、注意力），并构建了AI Fatigue Model来解释各压力源如何随反复交互而累积与相互强化，为该构念的进一步量化验证和应用奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论框架（如技术压力technostress、数字疲劳digital fatigue）尚未充分解释AI工具在学术场景中引发的新型疲劳形式，亟需建立针对AI使用情境的独特构念。

Method: 基于1,054名菲律宾三所高校学生的开放式回答，采用扎根理论分析法（grounded theory analysis）构建概念模型，从认知、动机、情绪、身体和注意力五个维度分析学生在AI辅助学术工作中的压力体验。

Result: 识别出AI疲劳的五个核心维度：认知超载（Cognitive Overload）、动机脱离（Motivational Disengagement）、道德焦虑（Moral Unease）、身体疲劳（Physical Strain）和注意力分散（Attentional Drift），每个维度包含两个基于参与者描述的指标。

Conclusion: 研究建立了AI疲劳作为独立构念的概念基础，并提出AI Fatigue Model（阶段式框架），为未来量表开发、跨情境验证及AI辅助学习环境中的学生心理健康研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The integration of AI tools in academic settings has introduced a distinct form of strain that existing frameworks like technostress and digital fatigue have not yet fully addressed. This study develops a conceptual model and identifies the dimensions that define AI fatigue as a form of strain arising from sustained academic use of AI tools. Using grounded theory analysis of open-ended responses from 1,054 university students across three universities in the Philippines, the study examined the cognitive, motivational, emotional, physical, and attentional pressures students experienced during AI-supported academic work. Analysis produced five dimensions of AI fatigue, namely Cognitive Overload, Motivational Disengagement, Moral Unease, Physical Strain, and Attentional Drift, each consisting of two indicators grounded in participant accounts. The findings also yielded the AI Fatigue Model, a stage-based framework that explains how these pressures accumulate and reinforce one another across repeated AI interaction in academic tasks. These contributions establish a conceptual and exploratory foundation for AI fatigue as a distinct construct and provide a basis for future instrument validation, scale development, and cross-contextual inquiry in academic settings where AI now mediates student learning.

</details>


### [250] [SolarChain: Bridging Physical Law, Verifiable Trust, and Sustainable Markets for Urban Energy Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23162)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Zhenshan Zhang,Luyao Zhang,Ming-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: SolarChain 是一个“物理有界验证 + 链上市场结算”的三层原型：离线物理引擎用气象数据与第一性原理公式为每块光伏板算出最大可能出力 P_max，超限报告在上链前被拒；经审批的电量进入强制“20% 产者奖励 / 80% 市场流动性”划分的智能合约市场，消费时 SOLR 销毁；全部决策写入前驱哈希链接的事件日志可回放。核心结论其实是“边界”而非“胜利”：物理合理性不等于数据真实性——近界欺诈、界内欺诈、天气欺骗、协同近界攻击完全未被覆盖；聚合分类上 IQR/MAD（F1=1.000）反而优于可解释的自适应验证器（F1=0.988）；20/80 分配比例只是该模拟器假设下的治理选项；哈希回放证明的是软件处理一致性，而非传感器真值或合约安全性。因此本文应被读作“受控实验台与可复现基准”，而非部署就绪证明（另注意：arXiv 条目摘要比正文更乐观，正文明确否认部署就绪与资本门槛下降的实证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市分布式光伏要在数百万碎片化产者间规模化以支撑去碳，但存在两个耦合的失效模式：(1) 物理测量可被操纵——账本能忠实保存一条上链记录，却无法证明其物理可信，虚假数据注入（FDIA）可凭空制造供给、奖励与结算事件；而传统残差检验可被“保持期望残差”的攻击绕过；(2) 市场设计依赖显式假设——把更多价值分配给个体奖励就会抽走共享流动性，链上激励易脱钩于物理活动，退化为投机与囤积，区块链本身不能消除“链下测量+预言机”的信任边界。作者自陈贡献不在组件新颖性（网格异常检测、链上能源市场、DePIN 各有独立文献），而在于一个跨层系统问题：能否把物理有界的 PV 报告贯通到可协调的市场结算，同时保留可追溯、可复现的证据链；并把各层的失败模式与失效传播暴露出来。

Method: 三层架构 + 持久多智能体 + 受控基准。
1) 离线物理引擎：由 Open-Meteo 城市级气温与短波辐射（外部来源，非面板遥测）推太阳位置与晴空包络，结合节点面积 A、效率 η、温度系数 β、参考温度算 P_max = AηG[1−β(T−Tref)]；容差 τ=1.03，报告超过 τ·P_max 即判物理不一致。
2) 规则化自适应验证器（无学习成分）：残差 r=(P_rep−0.978·P_max)/d、归一化 q=P_rep/d；物理界 B（>1.03P_max+25，或 P_max≤1 时 >50）；时间规则 T（与至多 12 个历史残差比差 >0.24）；白天邻居规则 N（偏离城市同时刻中位数 >0.32）；记忆规则 M（24 步前窗，均值/当前值双阈值）；复合判据 C = B ∨ M ∨ (T∧N) ∨ (|r|>0.20 ∧ N)。SolarAgent 保留 48 步残差、≥6 次观测后更新校准、按反馈更新信任并映射为 active/probation/suspended；PlannerAgent 给出批准/拒绝/升级；Factory/Market 回传需求与滑点。
3) 链上层：6 个模块化合约（SolarToken、SolarPanels、FactoryRegistry、EnergyExchange、PowerReward、Shop）；updateMarketStep 强制 20/80 划分，buyEnergyForFactory 燃烧 SOLR；事件总线对 6 类事件做 SHA-256 前驱链接，前端展示“注册/购买前的证据”。
4) 基准与协议：2026-04-01~05-01（Asia/Shanghai），5 城、50 个模拟节点、每小时步长 1 h（kW×1h 折算为 kWh）、36,000 条记录；1,800 条原始 FDIA 标签 + 10 类脚本攻击（共 11 类），固定种子（数据集 20260511、分类法 20260617）；三类数据来源严格区分（外部来源 / 物理建模 / 合成）；攻击为脚本化、非自适应；所有检测器共享同一有序记录与标签，攻击为正类，FAR=攻击被接受比例、FRR=正常被拒比例；30 次预热全批运行测运行时。
5) 分配敏感性：41 个比例（步长 2.5pp）× 7 个场景扰动（低/正常/高需求 0.5/1.0/1.5、阴天发电 0.6、10%/20% FDIA 损失、高波动 sin），S=0.50F+0.20R+0.15L+0.10U+0.05D，稳健分 0.75·S̄+0.25·min(S)，可行判据（平均履约≥0.90、最差≥0.80、平均滑点≤40%、平均奖励≥5,000 kWh）。
6) 24 小时端到端 trace（2026-05-01，1,200 条节点-小时记录 + 60 个显性异常）：城市聚合 → 分配 → 工厂购买 → 事件日志，并做 20/80 与 50/50 参考的市场状态对比；local-EVM 5 用例覆盖注册、访问控制、比例边界、燃烧与比例变更后结算。

Result: 检测器（36,000 条月度记录）：IQR/MAD P/R/F1=1.000/1.000/1.000、FAR=FRR=0、中位 7.859 ms/批（0.218 μs/记录），聚合最优；物理界 F1=0.926（召回 0.862）；自适应 SolarAgent P=0.976、R=1.000、F1=0.988、FAR=0、FRR=0.001，中位 52.159 ms/批（1.449 μs/记录）、p95 53.769 ms——代价是更高时延与偶发误拒，且非全面占优。攻击类能力分层：原始 FDIA、夜间不可能值、超界、邻居不一致为强覆盖（F1 0.972–1.000）；传感器漂移 0.927、重放 0.788（召回仅 0.654）、间歇性突发 0.941 为部分覆盖；近界欺诈、界内欺诈、天气欺骗、协同近界攻击 F1 均为 0.000，属当前声明范围之外。物理容差敏感性显示 τ 升高可消除正常误拒但牺牲近界召回（τ=1.00 时近界召回 0.572、FRR 0.008），实现值 1.03 非全局最优。
分配：41 点扫描下可行奖励份额区间 12.5%–22.5%，20% 在该集内稳健分最高（0.628，平均/最差场景 0.651/0.556，平均履约 0.956/最差 0.836，奖励 8,976 kWh，滑点 32.569%，未满足需求 2.111 MWh）；50/50 履约降至 0.782、滑点 33.297%、未满足 9.520 MWh、综合分 0.592；0/100 与 10/90 分数更低（0.617/0.640）。24 小时市场状态对比（20/80 vs 50/50）：平均流动性 +70.3%、峰值 +63.6%、流动性面积 +70.3%、平均滑点代理 −40.0%（34.33% vs 57.18%）、日间滑点 −39.4%；作者强调这些是模拟量而非福利估计。
端到端：5 城共验证发电 2,028.13 kWh（成都容量最大但验证输出最低，上海/杭州容量较小而输出较高，源于城市级天气与合成硬件）；42 笔合成购买共 0.6052 MWh、燃烧 609.77 SOLR、exergy 字段 59.63 MJ（示例值均非真实交易/减排）；审计方面 144,721 个事件全部通过前驱哈希复现（canonicalize JSON→清空 record_hash→重算 SHA-256），EVM 5/5 用例通过；不变量检查确认被拒发电不进入奖励/流动性、奖励+流动性等于被验收的一小时值。
作者对结果做了分层解读：IQR/MAD 适合批级偏差筛查，自适应代理保留残差与状态变更以解释规划者决策，但两者共享同一建模包络，其一致性不构成独立佐证；应同时报告分类指标与“升级/否决/排除结算”等运营后果指标，把错误追溯进分配与结算。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一个可复现的跨层决策链与开源基准，而非新组件或部署级结论，作者自我定位为“实验台”：(1) 物理有界验证可拒明显不可能的报告，但近界操纵、协同伪造、天气欺骗仍可穿透，因此自适应验证器是“给出理由的规划者检查点”而非更优检测器；近界欺诈等四类攻击仍需认证上下文或额外检测机制。(2) 20/80 是治理决策而非普适最优或福利最优，需公布评分权重、场景区间与更新间隔，并同时披露产者奖励、履约、未满足需求、滑点、回撤的完整权衡；合约的基点参数可替换为观测行为。(3) 哈希链接日志证明软件处理了什么，不证明传感器校准、资产控制或环境额外性；离链引擎与规划者是受信任组件，合约所有者可改比例，尚未实现去中心化预言机共识、持币方投票与争议解决；治理需分离模型维护、比例变更、审批申诉的权限与版本化理由，并保留申诉时的生效参数。(4) 效度威胁：构念效度受建模发电与滑点代理限制；内部效度受合成硬件/需求/交易/攻击与固定种子限制；外部效度受单月天气、缺少通信丢包、时钟漂移、遮挡、限幅、停机、策略性报价、隐私成本、链拥堵与适应性对手限制；IQR/MAD 的满分是分布特异的。后续验证议程：回放同步的逆变器与智能电表遥测 → 影子运行 → 有治理的微电网试点；测试季节性故障、缺失数据、适应性攻击、端到端时延与隐私/通信开销；按时间顺序区分冷启动与稳态，并把每次错误决策传播到结算以量化下游价值风险；现场协议须定义电表校准、比例治理、申诉流程及天气/网络不可用时的行为。部署就绪性主张尚需同步遥测、适应性攻击评估与受治理试点三项支撑。

Abstract: Urban decarbonization requires scaling rooftop solar across millions of fragmented producers, yet cities face a fundamental tension: energy data is easily manipulated, and economic incentives often reward speculation rather than actual infrastructure deployment. We present SolarChain, a platform that resolves both problems by anchoring digital accountability to the thermodynamic limits of solar energy conversion. Using real-time meteorological data, geospatial coordinates, and first-principles calculations of solar yield, the system establishes a hard physical boundary for every panel's maximum possible output; any reported generation exceeding this limit is automatically rejected before entering the shared ledger. This trustless verification enables a peer-to-peer marketplace with programmatic reward structures that continuously reinvest value into equipment maintenance and market liquidity, preventing the speculative hoarding that typically destabilizes blockchain-based marketplaces. When electricity is consumed, the corresponding digital credits are permanently retired in direct proportion to physical energy dissipation, creating an auditable one-to-one mapping between urban consumption and carbon accounting. Deployed across heterogeneous city nodes, the prototype demonstrates resilience against data injection attacks while lowering capital barriers for community-level solar expansion. Beyond energy, the framework offers a general model for coordinating economic activity with physical law in any domain where distributed infrastructure demands both data integrity and sustainable investment. We release the data and code as open-access on GitHub.

</details>


### [251] [Cognitive offloading and the speedup illusion in human-AI interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23177)
*Sunny Yu,Myra Cheng,Ahmad Jabbar,Ilia Sucholutsky,Katherine M. Collins,Dan Jurafsky,Robert D. Hawkins*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究发现用户系统性地低估AI辅助任务的完成时间，存在"加速幻觉"偏差，但对人类辅助无此偏差；同时主观努力与客观时间分离，提示效率评估需考虑时间与感知的双重维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM有潜力提升人类生产力，但前提是用户能够准确判断何时应将认知工作卸载给AI。现有研究缺乏对用户在估计AI潜在时间节省方面校准程度（calibration）的系统分析。

Method: 一项预先注册的大规模行为研究（N=1237），聚焦简单认知任务，对比独立完成、AI辅助完成与人类辅助三种条件下的预期时间与实际完成时间。

Result: 发现"加速幻觉"现象：实际完成时间在独立与AI辅助条件下无显著差异，但用户预测AI将显著更快；该偏差在想象另一个人类帮助时未出现。此外，时间与努力感知分离：用户报告AI辅助时主观努力更低，尽管实际完成时间相当。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了用户对AI辅助效率的认知偏差——"加速幻觉"，即用户系统性地低估AI辅助任务的完成时间，同时将主观努力与实际时间分离。这表明仅用完成时间来衡量效率增益是不充分的，需要考虑用户感知的多维性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to boost human productivity by speeding up task completion -- provided users know when to offload cognitive work to them. But we do not know if users are well-calibrated in estimating these potential time savings. We conducted a preregistered large-scale behavioral study (N = 1237) to characterize mismatches between expectations and reality, with a focus on simple cognitive tasks. While actual completion times between independent completion and AI-assisted completion did not differ, participants predicted AI to be significantly faster. The same bias was not observed when imagining help from another human participant. We identify a speedup illusion where people have accurate forecasts of independent completion times but significantly underestimate AI-assisted times. Additionally, time and effort dissociate: participants reported lower subjective effort with AI despite equivalent completion times. This suggests that completion time itself is not sufficient to characterize efficiency gains.

</details>


### [252] [IyàwóBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Clinical Triage Accuracy on Undifferentiated Febrile Illness in Nigerian Primary Health Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23465)
*Anthonio Oladimeji Gabriel,Dimeji Abdulsobur Olawuyi,Oloruntoba Ajayi,Temiloluwa Aderemi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究引入IyàwóBench v1.0评估工具，用于测试大型语言模型在尼日利亚初级卫生环境中的临床分诊能力。研究发现LLMs在分诊安全性上表现良好（100%安全率），但分诊准确性差异显著（39.0%-67.5%），集成WHO指南的临床工程系统优于通用模型约28.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Undifferentiated febrile illness is the leading cause of primary care outpatient visits in Nigeria, yet no validated benchmark exists for evaluating LLM clinical triage reasoning in West African primary health settings.

Method: Six LLMs were evaluated on structured triage classification across two metrics: triage accuracy and safety score, using 200 synthetic clinical vignettes derived from statistical distributions of 1,200 real patient encounters at 19 PHCs in Oyo State, Nigeria.

Result: All six models achieved 100% safety scores (never downgrading critical cases), but triage accuracy varied substantially (39.0%-67.5%), with two models showing near-zero accuracy due to structured output non-compliance. Clinically engineered systems outperformed general-purpose models by up to 28.5 percentage points.

Conclusion: IyàwóBench provides the first reproducible evaluation framework for LLM clinical decision support in West African primary care. Modern LLMs exhibit safe triage behavior but vary substantially in structured clinical accuracy. Clinically engineered systems with embedded WHO guidelines outperform general-purpose models by up to 28.5 percentage points.

Abstract: Background. Undifferentiated febrile illness is the leading cause of primary care outpatient visits in Nigeria, yet no validated benchmark exists for evaluating large language model (LLM) clinical triage reasoning in West African primary health settings. Methods. We introduce IyàwóBench v1.0, a dataset of 200 synthetic clinical vignettes across eight febrile illness categories derived from statistical distributions of 1,200 real patient encounters at 19 primary health centres (PHCs) in Oyo State, Nigeria. Six LLMs were evaluated on structured triage classification across two metrics: triage accuracy and safety score. Results. All six models achieved 100% safety scores (95% CI: 96.4-100.0%), never downgrading a critical REFER NOW case to TREAT HERE. Triage accuracy varied substantially: Claude Sonnet (claude-sonnet-4-5) 67.5% (95% CI: 60.8-73.7%), Llama 4 Scout 59.5% (52.5-66.2%), Llama 3.3 70B 43.0% (36.2-50.0%), and Llama 3.1 8B 39.0% (32.4-45.9%). Two models demonstrated near-zero accuracy attributable to structured output non-compliance. Conclusions. Modern LLMs exhibit safe triage behaviour but vary substantially in structured clinical accuracy. Clinically engineered systems with embedded WHO guidelines outperform general-purpose models by up to 28.5 percentage points. IyàwóBench provides the first reproducible evaluation framework for LLM clinical decision support in West African primary care.

</details>


### [253] [Strategic Stalemates: The Paradox of Export Controls in the U.S.-China AI Race](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23475)
*Jingwen Liu,Jyh-An Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了美国对华AI领域出口管制的战略有效性及法律合规性，认为虽然出口管制是美国遏制中国AI发展的战略工具，但长期效果存疑且可能适得其反，并进一步从WTO/GATT视角论证商业或军民两用AI产品不符合国家安全例外的法律标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中美AI竞争背景下，美国出口管制政策的战略有效性存疑，且可能产生反效果；同时，过度管制面临WTO法律挑战，需要明确安全例外条款的适用边界。

Method: 通过追踪2022年10月以来的政策演变，对比中美双方措施，从法律层面分析WTO合规性，并结合GATT条款评估出口管制的正当性。

Result: 研究表明：（1）长期看，出口管制可能无意中增强中国的自主研发能力；（2）过于严格或随意的管制可能违反WTO义务，阻碍AI发展。核心法律结论：商业或军民两用AI产品不符合GATT第XXI(b)条的安全例外标准。

Conclusion: 过度使用出口管制可能违反WTO义务，应克制对国家安全例外的解释，商业或军民两用AI模型和半导体不符合GATT第XXI(b)条的安全例外标准。

Abstract: Export control is a policy and legal tool to protect national interests by regulating exports of sensitive goods and technology to foreign nations. It has become central to U.S.-China tech rivalry, especially in AI. Controls cover advanced chips, capital, personnel, and critical minerals for semiconductors. Since October 2022, the U.S. BIS has progressively tightened restrictions on advanced computing components to China. China responded with export curbs on critical minerals and filed a WTO complaint against the U.S. under GATT. This article argues that while export controls are strategic in U.S.-China AI competition, their long-term effectiveness is questionable. They often unintentionally boost China's self-reliance and R&D. Moreover, overly strict or arbitrary controls may violate WTO obligations, complicating dispute resolution and hindering AI progress. The study further examines legal implications of overusing export controls.
